<a id="paper-1"></a>

### 1. [Recurrent Vision Language Models Solve the Shell Game](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25210v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25210

**作者：** Dingqiang Ye、Jiacong Xu、Wen Zhang、Yiqun Mei、Vishal M. Patel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25210v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25210v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 VLM 在「猜壳游戏」类隐藏物体追踪上受限于串行计算深度，提出让预训练 VLM 的每层注意力在时间上循环（用本层输出计算已完成帧组的 key/value）的 RecurrentVLM，并以单个答案 token 在 VET/MET-Bench 上取得近满分结果及超出训练长度的泛化。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉状态追踪、固定深度下的状态转移次数与长度泛化，其「每增加一次交换就多需一层注意力」「状态在前四层形成」「循环保留最新状态但牺牲早期细节」等观察与 latent 状态中的视觉细节保留、信息坍缩及贡献分配问题高度相关，值得优先核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Recurrent Vision Language Models Solve the Shell Game

**abstract/A1：** Tracking a hidden object through a video is effortless for humans, yet it remains a bottleneck for vision language models (VLMs). In the shell game, where a ball is hidden under one of several identical cups that are then swapped, VLMs that answer directly stay near chance, thinking models fail on long games, and fine-tuning learns only the lengths it has seen. We argue that the limit is serial computation: each swap must be composed with the last result, and a VLM of fixed depth has only a fixed number of state transitions for it. To address this, we propose RecurrentVLM, which makes every attention layer of a pretrained VLM recur over time by computing the keys and values of finished frame groups from the layer's own output. With a single answer token, RecurrentVLM reaches 100% on VET-Bench and 99.6% on MET-Bench, and 99% on 50-swap games after training on at most 30 swaps. We also observe several interesting phenomena: length generalization emerges suddenly at an aha moment; without recurrence, each swap costs an attention layer; the ball's state forms in the first four attention layers; and recurrence keeps the latest state at the cost of details of earlier moments. These results suggest that recurrent VLMs are a potential solution for tracking the states of unobserved objects in video. Code will be released.

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [Learning to Think with Images Faithfully via Adaptive Data Selection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26199v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26199

**作者：** Yu Zhao、Jiaxin Zhang、Rohit Saxena、Fazl Barez、Pasquale Minervini、Yiwen Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26199v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26199v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文诊断「Thinking with Images」工具推理的不忠实问题：VLM 虽会裁剪放大，却常不真正依赖返回区域，并将成因归于训练数据；据此提出随模型能力自适应的数据选择、细粒度可验证问题的合成流程，以及「无需放大时倾向不用工具」的自适应奖励，在 V\* 与 HR-Bench 上同时提升忠实性与准确率。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应主线中「答案是否真正使用视觉证据、视觉旁路与信息坍缩」的问题，其「准确率提升不等于忠实使用证据」的诊断和能力自适应数据筛选，可供核查 LOOK 类证据获取与停止决策的监督设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Think with Images Faithfully via Adaptive Data Selection

**abstract/A1：** Thinking with images (TwI) empowers vision-language models (VLMs) to overcome perceptual bottlenecks by actively cropping and zooming into fine-grained visual details, promising both higher accuracy and interpretable, region-grounded reasoning. However, our analysis shows that the improved accuracy of TwI models does not guarantee faithful tool use; they frequently invoke tools without actually relying on the zoomed region, undermining the reliability of tool-assisted reasoning. We trace this unfaithful behaviour to the training data: when questions are answerable without zooming, the model can answer correctly while ignoring the returned evidence, and this problem is exacerbated during training as the model becomes better at answering without tools. To keep zooming useful throughout training, we adapt the training data to the model's evolving capability and train on questions where correct zoomed evidence can still help the model answer correctly. This selection is supported by a data synthesis pipeline that localises small objects and constructs verifiable questions about their fine details, and complemented by an adaptiveness reward that favours tool-free answers when zooming is unnecessary. The experimental results show that our method substantially improves reasoning faithfulness and achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual search benchmarks (+11.6% on V\*, +7.9% on HR-Bench 4K, and +9.4% on HR-Bench 8K). On multimodal hallucination benchmarks, our model also outperforms the compared TwI baselines. These results suggest that improving TwI faithfulness does not sacrifice accuracy and that both can be learnt from the right training data without directly supervising the reasoning process.

</details>

<a id="paper-3"></a>

### 3. [VisAlloc: Allocating Visual Supervision for On-Policy Self-Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25069v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25069

**作者：** Haoran Xu、Tian Chen、Yuhe Wu、Yanbo Zhao、Junchi Liao、Hongyu Wang、Jiaze Li、Wentao Wang、Yanfeng Li、Rui Qian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25069v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25069v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** VisAlloc 提出一种双层视觉监督分配框架：在样本层用「证据中心裁剪 vs 空间错位裁剪」的成对教师筛选样本，在 token 层用两个 EMA 教师的 log-prob 差异度量视角敏感性，并对敏感 token 施加额外的蒸馏权重，仅在训练期使用、推理只需全图学生模型。

**相关性简评：** 它把「视觉证据是否被真正使用」转化为可筛选的监督信号与 token 级贡献分配，与 LVR 关心的视觉旁路、信息坍缩和视觉证据筛选直接相关；可核查其视角敏感性门控能否作为诊断模型是否依赖视觉 latent 的代理指标。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VisAlloc: Allocating Visual Supervision for On-Policy Self-Distillation

**abstract/A1：** On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers a promising approach to improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, not all samples benefit equally from privileged visual guidance, and uniform token supervision can dilute visually grounded signals. We propose VisAlloc, a dual-level framework combining sample-level visual filtering with token-level counterfactual reweighting. A fixed paired-teacher filter retains samples answered correctly from an evidence-centered positive crop but incorrectly from a spatially displaced negative crop. At the token level, both EMA teachers score the same student-generated response, and their absolute log-probability differences measure view sensitivity. Additional supervision targets tokens admitted by sensitivity or teacher-distribution disagreement, with shared frozen-prior, template-bigram, and student--teacher divergence gates. Inverse-frequency redistribution preserves the total additional weight within each micro-batch, while the positive teacher remains the sole distillation target. Inference requires only the full-image student. Across eight benchmarks, our 4B and 9B models achieve macro-averages of 81.44 and 84.92, outperforming Vision-OPD reproductions by 0.97 and 0.52 points, respectively. Code on GitHub: https://github.com/Kaelyn01/VisAlloc

</details>

### 4. [MKA: Evaluating Multimodal Knowledge Agents on end-to-end professional tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25228v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25228

**作者：** Anima Singh、Suhas Yogin、Weicheng Kuo、Ivor Rendulic、Ivan Jurin、Antoine Yang、Oran Kelly、Rohan Doshi、Hammad Ayyubi、Ioana Bica、Sergi Caelles、Matthew Conniff、Dan Graur、Anastasija Ilić、Ahmet Iscen、Chao Jia、Tomasz Kępa、Chunyuan Li、Noah Ó Donnaile、Filip Pavetic、Goran Petrovic、Khalid Salama、Zhicheng Wang、Polina Zablotskaia、Yiqian Zhang、Ming Zhao、Benjamin Caine、Mario Lučić、Melvin Johnson、Radu Soricut、Jean-Baptiste Alayrac

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25228v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25228v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MKA 基准，含 200 个覆盖 10 个经济部门的端到端专业任务，要求 agent 从 CAD 图纸、电路图、影像、图表、地图等视觉密集文件中提取并整合信息，再产出 xlsx/pptx/pdf 等交付物；评分由专家加权 rubric 与 agentic autorater 完成，并报告 Climb-WSR 与 token 成本。

**相关性简评：** 多文件视觉证据的提取、筛选与跨模态整合正是主线中的证据获取问题，且「移除看图工具不改变得分」的诊断可供核查视觉旁路与模型是否真正使用视觉证据；但任务是交付物生成，未触及内部状态或 latent 生成，机制层面的可用信息有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MKA: Evaluating Multimodal Knowledge Agents on end-to-end professional tasks

**abstract/A1：** We introduce the Multimodal Knowledge Agent (MKA) benchmark, which measures how well autonomous agents perform end-to-end professional knowledge work that depends on visual understanding. Each task requires an agent to extract and combine information from multiple visually dense files, such as CAD blueprints, electronics schematics, radiographs, complex charts and geospatial maps, and to produce a standalone deliverable in .xlsx, .pptx, .pdf, .docx, .html or .png format. Existing benchmarks leave this capability largely untested: removing image-viewing tools leaves Gemini 4 Carbon's score on GDPval unchanged, and single-format evaluations such as GDP$.$pdf and GDP$.$xlsx do not require reasoning across files or modalities. MKA contains 200 tasks across 10 economic sectors. Deliverables are graded against expert-weighted rubrics by a sandboxed agentic autorater that agrees with human domain experts on 96.2% of criterion judgments in our expert-graded study (Cohen's $κ= 0.91$), scored by weighted satisfaction rate (WSR) overall and on frontier criteria unsaturated by prior-generation models (Climb-WSR). Across the frontier models we evaluate (Gemini 4 Carbon, Claude Fable 5.2, Claude Opus 5.5, and GPT-6 Astra), Claude Fable 5.2 (56.6% Climb-WSR, 73.3% Overall WSR) and Gemini 4 Carbon (54.3% Climb-WSR, 71.9% Overall WSR) achieve the highest scores, while Carbon uses $5.7\\times$ fewer tokens. Even for the strongest models, Climb-WSR remains below 57%, leaving substantial headroom for multimodal agents.

</details>

### 5. [Keel: A Frontier Model Teaches a Vision-Language Model by Rewriting Its Weights](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25257v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25257

**作者：** Jiajun Fan、Neil He、Bangji Yang、Qinglong Zheng、Ziwen Wang、Chaoran Cheng、Changyi Yang、Hanqi Mao、Ge Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25257v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25257v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Keel，一种面向图像任务的 agentic 权重编辑方法：由前沿多模态 LLM 充当编辑代理，仅凭「thinking 模式下反复自重复直到 token 上限」这一症状，借助失败普查与注意力可视化定位病灶并直接改动 VLM 权重，且只保留不损害留出题准确率的编辑，无需训练、不改变模型规模与单 token 成本。

**相关性简评：** 直接触及 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益与代价问题：编辑代理把循环归因于中间层注意力输出，并观察到较小模型在循环中不再查阅图像，可供核查「视觉旁路/注意力坍缩」假设；但它是权重层面的失败修复而非 latent 推理状态生成，与长度泛化、停止决策的机制联系需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Keel: A Frontier Model Teaches a Vision-Language Model by Rewriting Its Weights

**abstract/A1：** Can a frontier model repair a vision-language model (VLM) by rewriting its weights? Small reasoning VLMs such as Qwen3.5 need such a repair: in thinking mode they fall into loops on many image questions, repeating themselves until the token limit, spending the whole budget and losing the answer. The usual remedies act on the decoding, leaving the looping model as it was, or must be told what to change. Yet the cause is written in the weights. Keel, the first agentic weight-editing method for VLMs on image tasks, finds and removes it there. Told only the symptom, an editor, a frontier multimodal LLM acting as an agent, locates the cause, edits the weights and tests them, with a failure census and instruments showing it the failing images and where the VLM attends; among several editors, the harness keeps only edits that clearly reduce the failure without lowering accuracy on questions no editor saw. The repair needs no training, keeps the model's size and per-token cost, and applies to already-edited weights. The editors expose where the loops live: from the original weights, independent editors on both models trace them to the attention outputs of the middle layers, and the smaller VLM stops consulting the image inside a loop. On Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-2B, across six image benchmarks from visual mathematics to GUI grounding, the edit removes most loops on every benchmark and shortens answers, and the edited models are significantly more accurate than the thinking models on average; the larger one gains most on GUI grounding, a task absent from the practice questions. Because the repair lives in the weights, it combines with budget forcing where held-out questions favor it: on the best available weights, Keel has the best average accuracy on both models, significantly above every training-free baseline we compare on the larger one.

</details>

<a id="paper-6"></a>

### 6. [ScaleDiag: Diagnosing the Limits of Test-Time Scaling in GUI Grounding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25297v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25297

**作者：** Chen Liu、Zhen Fang、Xin Yu、Dadong Wang、Ling Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25297v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25297v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ScaleDiag 受控诊断框架，在多个基准和最多八个 VLM 上分别改变视觉尺度与上下文、指令信息量、候选采样与选择，分析 GUI grounding 测试时扩展的瓶颈与饱和区间。

**相关性简评：** 直接诊断视觉证据聚焦、指令筛选和重复采样的收益边界，其中「目标占画面比例」与「正确坐标难选择、几何共识近乎无效」可供核查 LVR 的视觉证据筛选及候选验证策略。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ScaleDiag: Diagnosing the Limits of Test-Time Scaling in GUI Grounding

**abstract/A1：** GUI grounding maps a screenshot and a natural language instruction to the coordinates of the intended interface element. Existing approaches improve grounding through visual scaling, instruction scaling, and sampling scaling, yet it remains unclear which grounding bottlenecks these strategies address and where their gains saturate. To address this gap, we introduce ScaleDiag, a controlled diagnostic framework that isolates the effects of visual input, instruction information, and prediction sampling on GUI grounding by independently varying target scale and surrounding context, target-specific information and auxiliary descriptions, and candidate coverage and selection, respectively. We evaluate ScaleDiag across five benchmarks and up to eight vision-language models and find that each scaling strategy operates within a much narrower regime than commonly assumed. (1) Visual scaling is governed by target occupancy rather than a fixed crop ratio, with full-screen errors typically rescued once the target occupies only 0.32% of the cropped view, while further zooming eventually becomes harmful. (2) Instruction scaling is driven by information that identifies the target rather than description richness itself, as even ground-truth-derived attributes such as color, size, and shape are often neutral or harmful, while appearance and spatial descriptions account for over 90% of the achievable gain. (3) Repeated sampling exposes a different limitation, as correct coordinates are frequently generated but remain difficult to select, leaving 5.1--11.0 points of oracle headroom while geometric consensus provides almost no improvement. Together, these results show that test-time scaling is constrained by distinct operating regimes rather than benefiting monotonically from more visual focus, richer instructions, or additional samples. Our code is available at https://github.com/YenanLiu/ScaleDiag.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by ICDM@D2AI 2026 Best Paper Award

</details>

<a id="paper-7"></a>

### 7. [Search or Scoring? Diagnosing Why Visual World-Model Planners Fail](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25347v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25347

**作者：** Haoxuan Jia、Yang Liu、Shang Luo、Chenhao Wu、Zixiang Ni、Yingguang Yang、Feng Zhang、Chongyang Zhang、Hao Zheng、Kefu Xu、Congjing Ran、Jifeng Zhu、Bin Chong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25347v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25347v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文区分视觉世界模型规划器的两类失败：优化器没采到代价本会偏好的动作（搜索失败）与预测上的代价把坏动作排第一（评分失败），通过执行候选、用规划器自身代价给已知解打分并比较预测与真实结果的代价来判别，再据误差模型（DemoProp）决定用演示窗口暖启动搜索还是绕过模型直接平均窗口。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测代价排序是否可靠」的诊断（AUROC 在真实结果上高、在预测上接近随机）为核查 LVR 中 latent 内容是否被真正用于判断以及视觉证据检索的使用条件提供了可借鉴的评价思路，但对象是世界模型动作规划而非 VLM 推理，迁移需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Search or Scoring? Diagnosing Why Visual World-Model Planners Fail

**abstract/A1：** Visual world models such as DINO-WM and JEPA-WM plan with an optimizer that searches for actions whose predicted outcome has the lowest cost, its distance to a goal image. A plan can thus fail in two ways: the optimizer never tries a good action that the cost would prefer (a search failure), or the cost, computed on predictions, ranks a bad action first (a scoring failure). The two need different remedies, more search or a better model, but a planner does not record which one occurred. We tell them apart by executing the optimizer's candidates, scoring a known solution with the planner's own cost, and comparing costs on predicted and real outcomes. An error model explains when each arises and so how to use retrieved demonstration windows (DemoProp): start the search from them (a warm start) when prediction errors are small, and average them without the model when large errors make the optimizer pick overrated actions. Across four simulated environments and three world models, on short tasks whose goals the planner can represent, PushT and Wall fail mostly by search, while Metaworld and RoboCasa fail by scoring because the cost, sound on real outcomes (AUROC 0.94 on Metaworld), is at chance on predictions (0.54). The diagnosis tells which remedy pays off: a four times larger search lowers the real-outcome cost only where failures come from search (clearly for DINO-WM), and where they come from scoring, averaging the windows without the model solves 127 of 168 new Metaworld tasks, against at most 87 when the model chooses among them. Within PushT, degrading only the model flips which use is better, and our rule, frozen in advance, follows the flip and solves 678 of 960 new episodes, against 530 for the best fixed use.

**comment/C1：** 43 pages, 10 figures, 30 tables; supplementary material included

</details>

<a id="paper-8"></a>

### 8. [Fold the Loop: Decoding-Time KV Memory for Looped Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25369v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25369

**作者：** Kun Zhou、Wenmian Yang、Zhensheng Wang、Yanzeng Li、Weijia Jia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25369v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25369v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对 looped VLM 在解码时随循环深度线性增长的视觉 KV 缓存，提出 FoldCache：在模块内把循环步分组，让组内各步共享该组最后一个视觉 KV，从而在保留完整 prefill 的前提下压缩缓存，并可叠加 token 淘汰与量化。

**相关性简评：** 直接触及循环生成中视觉证据的保留与计算成本权衡，可供核查各循环步复用末尾视觉 KV 是否损失任务必要视觉细节、以及分组粒度对长循环和图像尺寸泛化的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fold the Loop: Decoding-Time KV Memory for Looped Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Looped vision-language models (VLMs) deepen visual reasoning by repeatedly applying weight-tied modules. However, their decoding memory grows with recurrent depth and limits the image size and batch size that fit in a fixed memory budget. Each recurrent step produces its own key-value (KV) states, so the cache holds several copies of the same image. We find that, after an exact prefill, earlier steps still need access to visual information but can reuse the final visual KV within the same module. We propose FoldCache, which compresses visual KV along the recurrent axis while preserving the full prefill computation. FoldCache partitions recurrent steps within each module into groups and retains the final visual KV of each group for its members to share during decoding. Sharing stays within module boundaries, while question and answer KV remain separate for each step. The grouping granularity controls the degree of compression, and folding can be combined with token eviction and quantization to further reduce memory use. On LoopVL, FoldCache approximately halves prefix KV memory while matching full-cache accuracy on five open-ended benchmarks. These memory savings support batch sizes up to 1.7 times those of a full cache under the same GPU memory budget, and up to 2.6 times with the more aggressive FoldCache-Lite.

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [TuringSpace: Grounding Spatial Intelligence in Action with Interactive CAPTCHAs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25387v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25387

**作者：** Xuwei Ding、Skylar Zhai、Linxin Song、Jiarui Feng、Dongqi Fu、Huajun Xi、Junnan Li、Jingcheng Liang、Jieyu Zhao、Yinglong Xia、Hong Li、Jian Kang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25387v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25387v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 TuringSpace：125 个需点击拖拽交互完成的合成网页 CAPTCHA，覆盖五类空间能力，每道题由程序生成并自带 checker 与 oracle，可作可扩展训练环境；评测显示人类成功率 94.1%，最强模型仍失败 25.9%，失败主因是误读截图，且模型倾向编造证据为错误信念辩护；用该环境微调 Qwen3.5-9B 使成功率由 1.6% 升至 33.1%，并在七个分布外基准上均有提升。

**相关性简评：** 其「动作—观察—修正」交互回路与错误信念不更新、幻觉证据的现象诊断，直接关联 LOOK/STOP 的收益成本与视觉证据筛选问题；但关注屏幕操作与空间判断，未涉及 latent 推理状态生成，自我修正机制与检查器驱动的监督方式可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TuringSpace: Grounding Spatial Intelligence in Action with Interactive CAPTCHAs

**abstract/A1：** Humans act in the visual world by inferring the 3D layout behind a 2D view, predicting how an action changes it, and correcting themselves after seeing the result. Can multimodal large language models (MLLMs) do the same? CAPTCHAs offer a natural test, since humans solve them through this very loop, clicking and dragging on the screen and adjusting after each result. Yet existing CAPTCHA benchmarks are largely solved and rarely spatial, while spatial benchmarks rarely require action. We therefore present TuringSpace, a synthetic web environment of 125 interactive CAPTCHAs that span five spatial abilities and must be solved through clicks and drags on the screen. Every task is a program that generates new instances with a checker and an oracle, so the tasks also form a scalable training gym. Humans solve 94.1% of the tasks, whereas the best model, GPT-6 Astra, still fails 25.9% of them, and the best open-weight model, Kimi K3, fails 68.0%. Our further analysis shows that most failures come from misreading the screenshot, and that model reasoning rarely corrects a wrong belief but hallucinates evidence to defend it. To test whether such spatial abilities can be learned, we fine-tune Qwen3.5-9B on this gym. Its success rate rises from 1.6% to 33.1%, above Kimi K3, and it improves on all seven out-of-distribution benchmarks, including a 9.9-point gain on the real-world indoor videos of VSI-Bench. These results show that grounding spatial intelligence in screen actions exposes what MLLMs lack and offers a scalable way to teach it, with gains that transfer to real-world scenes. We will release TuringSpace to support further study.

**comment/C1：** 45 pages. Project page: https://xuweid.com/turingspace/

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [FG-GraphBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Fine-Grained Visual Understanding with Multimodal Scene Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25408v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25408

**作者：** Sicheng Liu、Xunkai Li、Ziyu Han、Xingnan Zhang、Ru Zhang、Rong-Hua Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25408v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25408v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建了 FG-GraphBench，用两阶段流程抽取细粒度实体及其关系生成多模态场景图，并配套实现三个任务（细粒度问答、图像描述、图像描述生成）；同时提出 FiGRA，把实体与关系特征编码为连续 graph tokens 注入 MLLM，并采用两阶段训练。

**相关性简评：** 连续 graph tokens 直接关系到「视觉细节与实体关系能否保留在模型内部状态并被答案使用」，其图结构注入方式与细粒度实体覆盖基准可供核查对视觉证据筛选及信息坍缩诊断的借鉴价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FG-GraphBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Fine-Grained Visual Understanding with Multimodal Scene Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Fine-grained visual understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to recognize small visual entities and understand their relationships. Scene graphs offer a natural way to represent this information, organizing visual entities as nodes and their relationships as edges. However, we identify two challenges: existing scene graph benchmarks provide insufficient coverage of fine-grained entities and the relationships among them, and existing methods for fine-grained visual understanding have yet to effectively leverage such graph-structured information in MLLMs. To address these challenges, we introduce Fine-Grained Multimodal Scene Graph Benchmark (FG-GraphBench) and FiGRA. FG-GraphBench is a benchmark comprising three complementary tasks: fine-grained visual question answering (FG-QA), image captioning (FG-Caption), and image description generation (FG-Description). We develop a two-stage construction pipeline that discovers and grounds fine-grained entities and establishes their relations to construct the fine-grained multimodal scene graphs (MSGs) in FG-GraphBench. Building on these MSGs, we propose FiGRA, a Fine-grained Graph Representation Augmentation method for MLLMs. FiGRA adopts a two-stage training strategy. It encodes multimodal entity and relation features as continuous graph tokens, and integrates them into the MLLM. Experiments show that FiGRA significantly outperforms strong baselines across three tasks of FG-GraphBench. Further analyses confirm the effectiveness of FG-GraphBench and highlight the importance of preserving fine-grained entity and relation information for visual understanding.

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning for Mammography Vision--Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25418v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25418

**作者：** Zhengbo Zhou、Dooman Arefan、Shandong Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25418v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25418v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BESR：在共享 token 预算下用双边引导的 token 选择器同时保留病灶外观及其局部与对侧上下文，并施加双边奖励促使诊断评估真正利用所保留的对比证据，在乳腺钼靶数据集上验证证据保留与诊断表现。

**相关性简评：** 论文明确指出「保留证据不等于模型会使用它」，与视觉证据筛选、视觉旁路及贡献分配的问题直接对应，其选择器与奖励设计可作为 LVR 中按任务必要性筛选视觉细节并检验其因果作用的核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning for Mammography Vision--Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Medical vision--language models (VLMs) often need to identify small, subtle lesions within high-resolution images containing extensive normal anatomy and redundant visual information. Visual-token compression improves efficiency, but preserving salient regions alone can discard the context needed for diagnostic interpretation. This context can extend across images: in bilateral mammography, the opposite breast provides a patient-specific anatomical reference. Yet retaining such evidence does not ensure that the model uses it effectively. We propose Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning (BESR), which connects evidence preservation with comparative assessment through a bilateral-guided token selector and a bilateral reward. Under a shared token budget, the selector jointly prioritizes lesion appearance and its local and bilateral context, while the reward encourages assessments that benefit from the preserved comparison. Experiments on a bilateral dataset constructed from CBIS-DDSM and on VinDr-Mammo demonstrate improved evidence retention and diagnostic performance under constrained visual-token budgets. Inference benchmarking further shows reduced prefill latency.

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [ActMem: From Latent Memory Reasoning to Grounded Robotic Action](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25461v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25461

**作者：** Linfeng Dong、Jianwei Niu、Jiuyi Zhao、Ruibing Feng、Xinghao Wu、Wu Yang、Shuai Tao、Wulong Liu、Mu Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25461v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25461v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ActMem：在单一视觉语言骨干内将记忆推理与感知接地解耦，由带时间戳的交互历史推断当前子任务，并把该推断形成的隐藏状态保留为 Latent Memory CoT 来条件化 flow-matching 动作专家，同时用感知注意力让专家直接看当前画面，并复用跨决策计算以加速长历史推理。

**相关性简评：** 其 latent 隐藏状态承担记忆推理并驱动下游决策、感知注意力构成视觉旁路，与 LVR 关心的 latent 承载内容、视觉证据筛选与训练-使用一致性直接相关；跨决策计算复用且声称不改变输出，可供核查缓存对 latent 状态比较的影响，但需确认该 latent 是真正推理状态而非历史摘要。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ActMem: From Latent Memory Reasoning to Grounded Robotic Action

**abstract/A1：** Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising results in real-world long-horizon manipulation, where memory plays a crucial role. However, existing memory-augmented VLAs either retrieve past observations without reasoning about what they imply, or reason over the full history in a shared context where it crowds out current perception. Cognitive science treats them as distinct systems: memory reconstructs past experience to decide what to do, while perception guides how to act. We therefore propose ActMem, an in-context memory framework that decouples memory reasoning from perceptual grounding within a single vision-language backbone. Built on a vision-language model with native video understanding, ActMem infers the current subtask from a timestamped interaction history and keeps the hidden states formed during this inference as a Latent Memory Chain-of-Thought (Memory CoT), which conditions a flow-matching action expert. Perception Attention further lets the expert attend directly to the current view. To keep inference fast under long histories, Efficient Memory CoT Inference reuses computation across consecutive decisions without changing the output. ActMem achieves 71.1% task success and 80.8% completion success on RoboMemArena, surpassing the strongest baseline by 7.5 and 8.7 percentage points, and 73.8% average success on MIKASA-Robo, the best among all compared methods. In the real world, it reaches 68.8% average success on exogenous, endogenous, and traceless memory tasks, 10.0 percentage points above the strongest memory-augmented VLA. Code and checkpoints will be released at https://github.com/LigphiDonk/ActMem and https://huggingface.co/donk111/ActMem.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 11 figures, 6 tables. Code and checkpoints: https://github.com/LigphiDonk/ActMem

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [Contrast Where It Matters: Visual Evidence-Guided Decoding for Mitigating Hallucinations in LVLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25524v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25524

**作者：** Yiheng Li、Wenhao Wang、Xingyi Li、Mingda Jia、Zichang Tan、Yinhang Tang、Zhen Lei、Yang Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25524v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25524v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的视觉证据引导解码 VEGD：聚合定位头找出与查询相关的图像证据，在辅助图像中削弱该证据，引导两分支的视觉载体头关注同一位置，并按分布差异自适应融合或对比预测，以缓解 LVLM 幻觉。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉证据筛选、注意力结构以及语言先验压制视觉信息的失败分析直接相关，可供核查解码期证据定位和头级干预能否稳定保留任务必要的视觉内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Contrast Where It Matters: Visual Evidence-Guided Decoding for Mitigating Hallucinations in LVLMs

**abstract/A1：** Despite strong multimodal capabilities, large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate when linguistic priors outweigh visual evidence or when predictions are grounded in irrelevant or misleading image regions. Training-free contrastive decoding mitigates hallucinations by contrasting the original prediction with an auxiliary one that exposes linguistic priors. Yet existing methods focus on altering the auxiliary input or computation and do not ensure that either branch uses query-relevant visual evidence during decoding. We propose Visual Evidence-Guided Decoding (VEGD), a training-free framework that identifies query-relevant visual evidence and guides its use throughout contrastive decoding. VEGD locates this evidence by aggregating localization heads with strong visual responses and coherent spatial structure, then selectively weakens it in the auxiliary image. During generation, VEGD directs visual-carrier heads in both branches toward the same evidence location, strengthening access to intact evidence in the main branch while anchoring the auxiliary branch to its weakened counterpart. An adaptive distribution fusion strategy then combines or contrasts their predictions according to distribution divergence. Experiments across diverse LVLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that VEGD consistently reduces hallucinations.

**comment/C1：** preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [Support-Guided Triadic Decoding for Hallucination Mitigation in Multimodal Dialogue with LVLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25535v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25535

**作者：** Yiheng Li、Wenhao Wang、Xingyi Li、Zhilin Tang、Zichang Tan、Zhen Lei、Yang Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25535v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25535v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出免训练的 SGTD 解码框架，用于多模态多轮对话中的幻觉抑制：先估计历史回答相对于同类型候选的视觉支持度，据此通过注意力干预削弱低支持历史的影响，再将精炼预测、全历史预测与无答案视觉证据三路融合，并用视觉一致性与历史支持度约束候选词。

**相关性简评：** 其按视觉支持度筛选历史内容并在解码期融合多路证据的做法，与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、答案是否真正使用视觉信息以及错误传播的诊断问题直接相关，可供核查支持度估计能否作为潜在状态的质量信号。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Support-Guided Triadic Decoding for Hallucination Mitigation in Multimodal Dialogue with LVLMs

**abstract/A1：** In multimodal multi-turn dialogue, dialogue history plays a dual role: reliable responses provide useful context for subsequent turns, whereas hallucinated responses can propagate errors across turns. Existing training-free methods globally adjust the effect of dialogue history on generation, making it difficult to suppress misleading information without weakening useful context. To address this trade-off, we introduce Support-Guided Triadic Decoding (SGTD), a training-free framework that selectively controls the influence of unreliable history and fuses complementary predictions during decoding. Specifically, Historical Answer Support Estimation measures the visual support of historical responses against type-matched alternatives and identifies low-support content that may mislead later generation. Support-Targeted Attention Intervention then weakens the influence of this content while preserving the surrounding dialogue, producing a support-refined prediction. Triadic Decoding combines this prediction with the full-history prediction and answer-free visual evidence, while the Support-Adaptive Plausibility Constraint filters implausible tokens using visual agreement and historical support. Experiments on MMHalSnowball and VisDiaHalBench across representative LVLMs show that SGTD consistently achieves the highest accuracy under hallucinated histories while preserving the benefits of factual context.

**comment/C1：** preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [Query-Aware vs. Query-Agnostic Training-Free Video Token Pruning in Multimodal LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25537v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25537

**作者：** Divya Kothandaraman、Timo Kumkel、Jaclyn Pytlarz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25537v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25537v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在统一的 Qwen-3-VL 骨干、token 预算与解码设置下，系统比较 21 种免训练的视频视觉 token 选择策略（查询无关、浅层查询条件、结构化查询条件与整体式），并提出把问题分解为语义锚点的 Structured Query Pruning 及其结合视觉多样性的 Pareto 变体，在 Video-MME、MVBench、LongVideoBench 上评估。

**相关性简评：** 该研究直接检验「哪些视觉证据被保留/丢弃」这一视觉旁路与信息保留问题，其结论——原始问题嵌入的相关性不可靠、结构化锚点更稳、强查询无关覆盖法仍有竞争力——可供核查 LVR 中证据筛选与压缩预算下的细节保真问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Query-Aware vs. Query-Agnostic Training-Free Video Token Pruning in Multimodal LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Video Large Language Models process large numbers of visual tokens, making token pruning important for efficient inference. Training-free pruning methods differ in whether and how they use the user query, and the value of query conditioning remains unclear. We present a controlled study of training-free, pre-LLM video token selection. Using a shared Qwen-3-VL backbone, token budgets, and decoding settings, we compare 21 pruning principles spanning query-agnostic, shallow query-conditioned, structured query-conditioned, and holistic strategies. To instantiate the last two categories, we introduce Structured Query Pruning, which decomposes the question into semantic anchors, and a Pareto variant, which combines anchor relevance with query-agnostic visual diversity. Across Video-MME, MVBench, and LongVideoBench, relevance computed from the raw question embedding is unreliable (8--16 points below the best methods), whereas anchors derived from query decomposition avoid this failure. However, query access is not inherently advantageous: strong query-agnostic coverage methods remain competitive or superior in the full-field comparison. Relative to a redundancy-only baseline, structured selection helps most on evidence-specific and long-context tasks at tight budgets; on broad tasks, the holistic selector improves over either relevance or redundancy alone. Further, pruning reduces cost and memory. These results support a practical design rule: preserve visual coverage by default, and add structured relevance when the task and token budget demand selective evidence retrieval.

</details>

<a id="paper-16"></a>

### 16. [Efficient Latent Reasoning for Vision-Language Model via Dynamics-Aware Quantization and Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25670v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25670

**作者：** Yanjing Li、Sheng Xu、Baochang Zhang、Xianbin Cao、Yawei Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25670v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25670v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 VLM 中 latent CoT 的低精度部署：分析连续思考对量化误差的敏感性与方向依赖效应，提出 QL-VLM，用保持 rotary-key 结构的非均匀码本量化与对齐连续思考、latent 读出及预测的蒸馏，在 ScienceQA-IMG 上以 4-bit 报告 90.43% 准确率。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及连续思考状态在承载视觉检索信息时的保真与训练监督，可供核查量化扰动如何影响 latent 内容使用、信息坍缩与后续推理一致性，以及蒸馏与量化各自贡献。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient Latent Reasoning for Vision-Language Model via Dynamics-Aware Quantization and Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Efficient chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning in vision-language models (VLMs) requires reducing both intermediate reasoning steps and their execution cost. Latent CoT methods compress textual rationales into continuous thoughts, yet their full-precision (FP) models retain substantial storage and computation costs. Lowering numerical precision could reduce these remaining costs, yet direct quantization severely degrades reasoning accuracy, particularly with 4-bit weights and activations. Operator and perturbation analyses reveal key sensitivity and direction-dependent predictive effects of continuous-thought errors, motivating preservation of information retrieval and subsequent reasoning beyond reconstruction accuracy. We therefore introduce QL-VLM, a dynamics-aware Quantization framework for efficient Latent reasoning in Vision-Language Models (VLMs). First, Dynamics-Guided Quantization (DGQ) uses nonuniform codebooks that preserve rotary-key structure, mitigating quantization-induced distortion in vision-language information retrieval. Second, Dynamics-Preserving Distillation (DPD) aligns continuous thoughts, latent-query readouts, and predictions with a full-precision teacher, maintaining predictive consistency through subsequent low-precision reasoning. On ScienceQA-IMG with Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct, 4-bit QL-VLM achieves 90.43% accuracy and improves baseline by 40.33%, with 16.64x fewer bit-operations and 6.40x lower measured latency than explicit CoT.

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [Beyond Entity Recognition: Vision-Centric Knowledge Visual Question Answering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25680v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25680

**作者：** Jiatong Ma、Yuchen Liu、Longteng Guo、Yichen Yuan、Yuze Wu、Dongze Hao、Ru Zhen、Yanhao Zhang、Haonan Lu、Jing Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25680v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25680v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VC-VQA 基准，按视觉信息来源（内部／外部图像）与介入阶段（检索前／检索后）刻画视觉信息在知识推理中的作用，要求模型检索外部视觉证据、定位并核验视觉线索，并在四种知识获取设置下评测 21 个 MLLM。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉证据筛选、检索前后线索定位及多步获取决策的兴趣直接相关，其失败分析（细粒度视觉知识获取为主要瓶颈、agentic 检索收益）可供核查 LOOK 步骤的必要性与成本。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Entity Recognition: Vision-Centric Knowledge Visual Question Answering

**abstract/A1：** We propose VC-VQA, a large-scale benchmark for vision-centric knowledge reasoning, aiming to systematically evaluate the capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in fine-grained visual knowledge acquisition and complex multi-stage reasoning. Unlike existing benchmarks that primarily treat visual information as entity recognition and rely on text-based external knowledge, VC-VQA explicitly models the role of visual information by considering both its source (internal vs. external images) and stage of involvement (pre-retrieval vs. post-retrieval). Specifically, VC-VQA requires models to retrieve external visual evidence, identify visual clues before retrieval, and localize, verify, or match retrieved knowledge against the internal image. We further construct a controlled multimodal knowledge base with traceable textual and visual evidence for each sample. We evaluate 21 representative MLLMs under four settings: no external knowledge, with provided evidence, knowledge base retrieval, and open-web search. Experimental results lead to three key findings: current models still struggle with vision-centric knowledge reasoning; fine-grained visual knowledge acquisition remains the primary bottleneck; and agentic retrieval highlights the value of multi-step planning and cross-modal knowledge integration. Overall, VC-VQA provides a more challenging benchmark for evaluating vision-centric knowledge reasoning in MLLMs. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/CASIA-IVA-Lab/VC-VQA.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to EACL2027(Main)

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [Interpreting DeepStack-Style Vision-Language Models: Do Input or Deeper-Layer Visual Tokens Drive Answers?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25713v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25713

**作者：** Yucheng Du、Yi Nian、Ruixue Lian、Yue Zhao、Xiyang Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25713v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25713v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文通过只改变单一物体颜色、削弱或恢复某一入口的视觉特征，比较 DeepStack 类 VLM 的答案究竟跟随输入层还是深层视觉 token，并考察深层特征能否纠错及分流推理的成本。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉证据在不同深度内部状态中的保留与使用，并用因果冲突实验区分主入口和深层入口的贡献；其「单入口干净即可预测可丢失哪一入口」的发现可供核查深层视觉信息对答案的因果作用与冗余假设。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpreting DeepStack-Style Vision-Language Models: Do Input or Deeper-Layer Visual Tokens Drive Answers?

**abstract/A1：** DeepStack-style vision-language models feed visual tokens to the language model at its input, and again at deeper layers. We call these the main entry and the later entries. What do the later entries add when the model already has the image? We give the entries versions that differ in one object's color and see which one the answer follows. On photographs, the original DeepStack follows the later entries, while Qwen3-VL-8B/32B follow the main entry. Does weakening the main entry give the later features more influence? On synthetic images, we keep this conflict and reduce color saturation at the main entry only. The unchanged later features gain a stronger effect on the relative scores of the two colors, in all four models, including Granite Vision. This holds even under mild desaturation, when accuracy without the conflict stays at 100%. To test whether later features can also correct errors, we degrade both entries and restore only the later ones. The same blur costs both Qwen models 50 percentage points of accuracy on stripe orientation. The 32B model recovers 48 points and the 8B model only 8. Accuracy with one clean entry predicted which entry each model can lose, and five of six tested predictions held on new GQA attribute questions. With the other entry zeroed, Qwen3-VL-8B loses only 0.3 points on its main entry alone, and DeepStack at most 3.45 points on either entry alone. In split inference on these questions, DeepStack can then send only its main-entry features at higher precision. This takes 49% fewer bytes than all entries at a shared precision, at similar accuracy.

**comment/C1：** Includes the supplementary material. Code and numerical results: https://github.com/yucheng-du/interpreting-deepstack-style-vlms

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [Suppressing the Shortcut Hidden Behind the Gains of VideoLLM Fine-Tuning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25776v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25776

**作者：** Youngwoo Shin、Jongsuk Kim、Junmo Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25776v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25776v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 VideoLLM 经 SFT 后会依赖训练数据的文本规律作答、绕开视频内容，且这种捷径在训练分布内被准确率上升所掩盖；作者提出 VETO，在基座激活中校准文本单独占据的子空间，并把微调更新投影到其正交补，从而在捷径形成时予以压制。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应「答案是否真正使用视觉证据、视觉旁路导致信息坍缩」这一核心问题；其按子空间干预更新的做法可能为诊断与抑制 LVR 中视觉证据未被使用的状态提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Suppressing the Shortcut Hidden Behind the Gains of VideoLLM Fine-Tuning

**abstract/A1：** Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is the stage in which Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) advance their capability to reason over video. However, the VideoLLM also learns to answer from textual regularities of its training data, a shortcut that bypasses the video. The regularities behind the shortcut are hard to define at the data level, and the shortcut forms regardless of training epoch, data size, or backbone. The shortcut is hidden behind a rise in accuracy within the training distribution, yet it is not required for the rise and even costs SFT the gain the video offers under distribution shift. We therefore propose Video-Evidence Training via Orthogonal projection (VETO), which intervenes on the fine-tuning update where the shortcut is written rather than on the data. VETO calibrates the subspace that the text alone occupies in the activations of the base model and projects the update onto its orthogonal complement. The VideoLLM is thus left to ground its answer in the full input, with the shortcut suppressed as it forms and never defined. Across seven VideoLLMs, VETO preserves the gain of SFT on the training distribution and suppresses the shortcut. On six benchmarks under shift it gains more than SFT and answers from the full input rather than the shortcut. Code is available at https://github.com/Youngwoo-git/VETO.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-20"></a>

### 20. [When Safety Rationales Hallucinate: Diagnosing and Grounding Multimodal Safety Guards](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25793v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25793

**作者：** Yingshuo Wang、Weixiang Zhao、Guancheng Li、Jiahe Guo、Yu Zhang、Yanyan Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25793v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25793v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 系统诊断多模态安全护栏生成理由中的视觉事实幻觉与安全归因幻觉，并提出 TCG-RL，仅在工具使用能提升理由视觉落地时给予奖励。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉证据筛选、工具调用收益与理由忠实性；其「相对无工具基线判断工具贡献」的奖励设计，可供核查 LOOK 的收益归因与推理轨迹监督。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Safety Rationales Hallucinate: Diagnosing and Grounding Multimodal Safety Guards

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal safety guards increasingly generate rationales alongside safety predictions, yet whether these rationales are reliable remains largely unexplored. We systematically study \*rationale hallucination\* from two perspectives: visual rationale hallucination, where rationales contain unsupported visual facts, and safety attribution hallucination, where grounded content is assigned an incorrect safety interpretation. Across rationale-generation methods, multiple vision-language backbones, and safety datasets, we find that rationale hallucination is widespread and does not consistently diminish with higher classification accuracy or increased model scale. Further analysis of the Think-with-Image paradigm shows that hallucination is concentrated in fine-grained attributes and relations, while visual tools are often invoked infrequently or without sufficient spatial focus. Based on these findings, we propose \*Tool-Contrastive Grounding Reinforcement Learning\* (TCG-RL), which rewards tool use only when it improves rationale grounding over a tool-free reference. TCG-RL substantially reduces rationale hallucination while maintaining competitive safety prediction performance. By revealing the gap between prediction correctness and rationale reliability, our study motivates a shift in multimodal safety guarding from outcome-only evaluation toward safety decisions that are both accurate and verifiably grounded.

</details>

<a id="paper-21"></a>

### 21. [What Does Anti-Hallucination Training Buy? Saying Less, Cheap Pruning, and the Rest](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25814v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25814

**作者：** Lingkai Bu、Qian Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25814v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25814v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文拆解视觉语言模型 anti-hallucination 偏好训练带来的 CHAIR 分数下降：先与同 recall 的 base 模型截断输出对比以剥离「说得更少」的收益，再用冻结的十一特征后处理探针在 500 张标注图上判定剩余增益能否被复现，并在多个主干与自建 DPO checkpoint 上做同样的归因，同时用该度量给训练信号定价。

**相关性简评：** 提供一种把「回答更短」与「真正使用视觉证据」分离的归因与评测框架，可供核查 VLM 推理中答案是否依赖图像证据而非语言先验或捷径，也对齐 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中收益来源的拆解思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Does Anti-Hallucination Training Buy? Saying Less, Cheap Pruning, and the Rest

**abstract/A1：** Preference training is the standard recipe for reducing object hallucination in vision-language models, and each new method reports a lower CHAIR score. We ask what that reduction consists of. Every trained system is compared with its base model's caption truncated to the same object recall, which removes the part of the gain that comes from saying less; the remainder is compared with what a deliberately weak, frozen, eleven-feature post-hoc probe fitted on 500 annotated images can reproduce. All criteria were specified in the analysis code and frozen before the outputs they judge existed. For eight public methods on LLaVA-1.5-7B, the probe reproduces at least two thirds of what training removes beyond saying less for 7 of 8 methods on COCO, 8 of 8 on AMBER and 6 of 8 on Object HalBench, with RLAIF-V the consistent exception; a later public method released for both LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen2.5-VL (SENTINEL) meets the same bar on both. On our own DPO checkpoints the accounting holds on Qwen2.5-VL but fails on InternVL2.5 on COCO (2 of 4 training designs). At matched recall, the base model plus the probe beats truncation for 8 of 9 LLaVA-1.5 systems on COCO and 9 of 9 on AMBER, on the two newer backbones, and when realized as actual sentence deletion. The same measure prices training signals: DPO on the model's own samples labelled by the probe or by an object-level CLIP score recovers 0.70 and 0.87 of the beyond-brevity gain of ground-truth-labelled DPO on Qwen2.5-VL (the probe 0.73 on InternVL2.5), both beat length-matched random labels, and CLIP labels buy more than probe labels on COCO. Human judgements show that the sentences the probe deletes are wrong more often than those truncation would delete; a human check of the CHAIR evaluator's precision falls below our pre-set bar, which we report as a limitation.

</details>

<a id="paper-22"></a>

### 22. [Harnessing Medical Visual-Language Models with Agentic Evidence Management](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25840v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25840

**作者：** Qiming Huang、Hanze Dong、Jipeng Zhang、Mike Zheng Shou、Xinxing Xu、Furu Wei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25840v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25840v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 Mask-Evidence MedVQA 基准（2.5K 例、四类医学图像、同一真值掩码多种渲染），诊断直接调用分割工具时证据呈现与取舍的问题，并提出 TUNE 证据管理框架，显式决定获取何种证据、如何呈现以及是否采纳答案更新。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 关注的视觉证据获取、呈现方式对内部状态的影响及何时采纳或停止直接相关；同一掩码不同渲染的可控设计可供核查视觉旁路与答案更新的因果作用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Harnessing Medical Visual-Language Models with Agentic Evidence Management

**abstract/A1：** Medical vision-language agents increasingly rely on segmentation tools, and recent work has focused on acquiring the right mask, often treating a good mask as the end point of tool use. But do these agents use the resulting evidence effectively? To answer this question, we need a benchmark that separates evidence use from segmentation quality, spans multiple medical image types, and allows the same mask to be presented in different ways. Existing medical VQA datasets do not jointly provide these properties. We therefore construct Mask-Evidence MedVQA, a 2.5K-example benchmark spanning four image types, where each question is paired with an answer-relevant ground-truth mask and multiple possible renderings. Our study reveals two limitations of direct tool use. First, evidence presentation matters. Different renderings of the same fixed mask can substantially change model behavior. Second, masks introduce an evidence trade-off. Highlighting a relevant region can expose diagnostic evidence the model previously missed, but the added visual cue can also induce incorrect associations. As a result, even a ground-truth mask can correct some answers while turning initially correct ones into incorrect ones. Together, these findings show that acquiring the right mask is not enough. An agent must also decide how to present the evidence and whether it should change the answer. We therefore introduce TUNE (Tool Use is Not Enough), an evidence-management harness that explicitly manages what evidence to acquire, how to present it, and whether to accept the resulting answer update. Across three public medical VQA benchmarks, evidence management improves the fine-tuned Qwen3.5-9B backbone's mean accuracy by 4.0 percentage points. Full TUNE improves over direct tool use by 9.1 points on average across seven benchmarks and achieves competitive results against existing medical VLMs and agents.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 3 figures. Qiming Huang, Hanze Dong, and Jipeng Zhang contributed equally

</details>

<a id="paper-23"></a>

### 23. [EgoWitness: Benchmarking Instance-Level Spatial Memory and Reasoning in Egocentric Video Streams](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25849v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25849

**作者：** Zhengru Fang、Zehao Liu、Mingyu Tsoi、Yaoyu Zhu、Junlong Li、Fengmao Lv、Sijie Cheng、Yuanliang Sun、Fang Yang、Jun Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25849v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25849v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EgoWitness，一个面向第一视角视频的实例级空间记忆与推理基准（约 324 小时录制、8,453 个经人工校验问题，覆盖七类能力），并给出 EgoClue 空间记忆 agent：用五个工具检索候选时段、核验实例身份、计算时空关系，经 SFT 加 GRPO 将 4B 控制器从 23.64% 提升到 72.65%。

**相关性简评：** 该工作直接诊断多模态模型能否保留并调用跨时段、跨人物的视觉实例证据，可作为视觉证据筛选与检索式推理的对照；其外部工具加证据轨迹的做法也可能提供核查线索，用来比较它与内部 latent 状态在证据获取和计算分配上的差别。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EgoWitness: Benchmarking Instance-Level Spatial Memory and Reasoning in Egocentric Video Streams

**abstract/A1：** Recalling where and when an object was last seen and who saw it requires recognizing it across days and people. Existing long-video benchmarks focus on events, object categories, or relative locations, leaving instance-level spatial memory largely untested in multimodal models. To address this gap, we present EgoWitness, a benchmark for instance-level spatial memory and reasoning in egocentric video. Its 8,453 human-verified questions span approximately 324 hours of week-long and short-horizon recordings and test seven abilities, from recognizing an object or room to recalling who saw it and reasoning about room visits. Over one million reviewed masks of 34 registered landmarks and 21 movable-object labels are linked through room timelines. Among twelve direct vision-language models (VLMs), open-weight models reach at most 46.97% accuracy, and none exceeds 35% on identifying who saw an object last. We therefore propose EgoClue, a spatial-memory agent that retrieves candidate intervals, verifies instance identity, and computes spatial and temporal relations with five tools. Two-stage training with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) raises its 4B controller from 23.64% to 72.65% accuracy on 1,320 held-out questions. Evidence-trace synthesis matches the best direct VLM at 74.85% on week-long questions and achieves 80.56% in environments excluded from training.

**comment/C1：** 43 pages, including supplementary material. Zhengru Fang and Zehao Liu contributed equally. Project page: https://egowitness.github.io/ ; Code: https://github.com/egowitness/EgoWitness

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [Thinking in Streams: Proactive Spatial Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25865v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25865

**作者：** Junyoung Hong、Minsu Kim、Jaehyun Kim、Jeonghwan Cho、Jiwoo Lee、Seon Joo Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25865v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25865v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究流式视频中的主动空间推理，即预给查询后由模型自行判断空间条件何时成立并决定是否、何时作答；构建了覆盖物体、相机与场景推理的九任务基准 ProSpace-Bench 及训练集 ProSpace-Train，并提出 ProSpace-VLM，将因果估计的几何编码注入 VLM 中间层，用 KV cache 充当空间记忆并以空间 token 选择实现高效流式处理。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及「何时作答／正确弃答」的停止决策与跨视角内部空间状态维护，其 KV cache 作记忆、中间层几何注入与 token 选择可供核查 LVR 的状态更新、证据筛选与停止成本问题；分析部分关于历史空间信息表征的结论也值得对照 latent 内容是否被真正使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Thinking in Streams: Proactive Spatial Reasoning with Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Recognizing when a requested spatial condition holds in streaming video can require maintaining and updating spatial knowledge across changing viewpoints. We study this capability through proactive spatial reasoning, where a model receives a query in advance and determines whether and when to respond as the stream unfolds. We introduce ProSpace-Bench, comprising nine tasks across object, camera, and scene reasoning, and ProSpace-Train for supervised training. To support spatial decisions, we propose ProSpace-VLM, which injects explicit geometric encodings estimated causally from incoming frames into intermediate VLM layers, using the existing KV cache as spatial memory and spatial token selection for efficient streaming. Experiments reveal a substantial gap between VLMs and humans, while ProSpace-VLM improves timely responses and correct abstention over fine-tuned baselines. Analyses examine how ProSpace-VLM represents previously observed spatial information across changing viewpoints.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-25"></a>

### 25. [EvoMem: Self-Evolving Skills for Time-Aware Long Video Memory Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25889v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25889

**作者：** Yutong Guan、Meixing Shi、Haoran Sun、Bing Sun、Yuxiang Cai、Yankai Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25889v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25889v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EvoMem，面向长视频问答构建按时间对齐的分层记忆，把已获取证据中的时间线索转为约束后续访问的显式信号，并以奖励引导的自进化检索技能在不更新参数的情况下调整检索内容、记忆操作与停止时机。

**相关性简评：** 与「获取证据、停止决策」主线直接相关，其证据状态驱动的时间约束检索和「过早停止」诊断可供核查；但它作用于外部记忆检索而非 latent 推理状态，对内部表示的启示需读原文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EvoMem: Self-Evolving Skills for Time-Aware Long Video Memory Agents

**abstract/A1：** Although memory-augmented architectures have expanded the ability of large vision-language models (LVLMs) to process long videos, existing retrieval processes still largely rely on global semantic matching and lack state-driven temporal control, often returning fragmented or redundant evidence and failing to maintain a coherent evidence chain. To address this challenge, we propose EvoMem, a time-aware long-video memory agent guided by self-evolving retrieval skills. EvoMem constructs a hierarchical time-indexed memory, where different types of evidence are aligned onto a unified video timeline; during retrieval, temporal cues from acquired evidence are further converted into explicit signals that constrain subsequent memory access. To address recurring procedural errors in long-video question answering, such as unclear retrieval targets, improper memory access paths, or premature stopping, EvoMem introduces retrieval skills to provide process-level guidance, enabling the agent to dynamically adjust retrieval content, memory operations, and target temporal ranges according to the current evidence state, while deciding when to stop. Furthermore, inspired by reinforcement learning, we formulate skill evolution as a reward-guided policy improvement process. Each candidate skill is treated as an external policy patch, and rewards are assigned according to answer-correctness state transitions on the validation set. In this way, effective skills are accepted and low-quality skills are rejected without updating model parameters. Experiments on four long-video question answering benchmarks show that EvoMem significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving an average accuracy of 60.1% and an average improvement of 8.6 percentage points over the previous best methods, demonstrating its effectiveness for long-video reasoning.

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [ForeSeek: Foresight-Driven Reasoning for Long-Form Video Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26024v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26024

**作者：** Yuchen Sun、Siyuan Cheng、Shi-Zhe Chen、Yixin Qin、Jinwen Luo、Zheng Wei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26024v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26024v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ForeSeek，将长视频推理从被动检索转为前瞻驱动的预测式探索：先在未观测时间段形成视觉可验证的预期，再有针对性地观测验证并依证据修正搜索，失败预期用于回溯，并配套两阶段训练。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及「看哪里、何时停」的证据获取与决策问题；其「预期—验证—回溯」循环可能为 LVR 的预测工作区以及 LOOK/STOP 的成本权衡提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ForeSeek: Foresight-Driven Reasoning for Long-Form Video Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Long-form video understanding, as a fundamental yet crucial vision task, is being revolutionized by multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based video agents. However, the retrieval-driven nature inherent to these agents creates a significant bottleneck, forcing them to reactively fetch clips based on local saliency without a global temporal plan. To address this, we present ForeSeek, a foresight-driven video agent that transforms long-video reasoning from reactive retrieval into predictive exploration. Rather than blindly probing the timeline, ForeSeek forms visually grounded expectations over unseen temporal regions, verifies them through targeted observations, and continuously refines its search based on emerging evidence, where failed expectations serve as informative signals for principled backtracking. Furthermore, we develop a two-stage training framework that equips MLLMs with foresight-guided reasoning and efficient decision making. Extensive experiments on five long-video benchmarks demonstrate that ForeSeek consistently improves the efficiency--accuracy trade-off of long-video reasoning, achieving competitive or superior accuracy while observing $2.5$--$4.4\\times$ fewer frames than the strongest search-based agent. Code is available at https://github.com/harry0021/ForeSeek

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [SpatialScout-V2: Can VLM Agents Actively Explore Real-World 3D Scenes and Localize Objects?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26177v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26177

**作者：** Zhaoxi Zhang、Hao Liang、Linxuan Wu、Yiming Xu、Yifu Yuan、Kun Liang、Chenming Tang、Meiyi Qiang、Wentao Zhang、Weikang Li、Yunfang Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26177v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26177v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SpatialScout-V2 真实住宅 3D 场景主动探索基准，要求 VLM 代理在固定交互预算内选择记录视角、整合标定 RGB 观测并迭代估计物体中心；覆盖 100 个家庭、3171 个目标、29 个类别，并用平均 IoU、命中率和目标覆盖率评测七个前沿 VLM。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 中 LOOK 和证据获取直接相关：该基准将视觉证据筛选、部分观测整合及预算约束下的探索决策放到真实场景中检验，可供核查精度—覆盖率权衡与失败模式，但不涉及 latent 推理状态的生成机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpatialScout-V2: Can VLM Agents Actively Explore Real-World 3D Scenes and Localize Objects?

**abstract/A1：** Spatial intelligence requires agents to acquire visual evidence and integrate partial observations into coherent three-dimensional representations. We introduce SpatialScout-V2, which extends SpatialScout's active mapping task from simulation to real residential scenes through three advances: real visual observations, preservation of original scene content, and interactive exploration of recorded views. Built from 100 homes in Realsee3D, the benchmark covers 3,171 target objects across 29 categories. Given a fixed target inventory, agents autonomously select recorded viewpoints and viewing directions, integrate calibrated RGB observations, and iteratively estimate and revise object centers within a fixed interaction budget. We evaluate the localization accuracy and completeness of object-center maps using mean IoU, IoU-threshold hit rates, and target coverage. Evaluating seven frontier vision-language models (VLMs), we find that six achieve an all-target mean IoU below 20\\%. Even the best-performing model, Claude Opus 5.5, reaches only 22.52\\%, with just 18.36\\% of targets exceeding an IoU of 0.5. Further analysis shows that broad submission coverage can coexist with limited localization accuracy, while target omissions further reduce overall scores. These results highlight substantial challenges in constructing accurate and complete object-center maps through active exploration of real scenes.

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [ResVision: Spectrum-Matched Resizing and Context-Preserving Inspection for Visual Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26318v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26318

**作者：** Guojian Zhan、Jiahao Xu、Yahui Liu、Yao Hu、Chuan Mu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26318v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26318v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向 agentic 视觉推理中的证据放大，指出高频细节在视觉 token 形成时衰减、空间分散证据难以被语言模型取回、孤立裁剪破坏全局对应三个瓶颈，提出按图像复杂度自适应选择全局采样尺度的 Spectrum-Matched Resizing 与每次返回局部裁剪加完整工作视图的 Context-Preserving Inspection，并在 4 个前沿 VLM、29 个基准上评测。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LOOK 环节的收益与成本：以裁剪式证据获取改善视觉细节保留，同时用全局视图缓解尺度与位置错配，可供核查视觉证据筛选、细节信息保真与工具轮次成本之间的权衡；但方法止于输入采样与上下文组织，未涉及 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ResVision: Spectrum-Matched Resizing and Context-Preserving Inspection for Visual Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Agentic visual reasoning improves vision-language models by selectively enlarging evidence without adding new scene content, but the benefit depends on whether fine structure remains accessible and whether local observations stay grounded in the full scene. We identify three recurring bottlenecks: high-frequency detail can be attenuated during visual-token formation; spatially distributed evidence can remain encoded yet difficult for the language model to retrieve; and isolated crops can weaken whole-detail correspondence through scale and positional mismatch. ResVision addresses these limitations with two complementary components. Spectrum-Matched Resizing (SMR) estimates image complexity and selects an adaptive global sampling scale, avoiding unnecessary enlargement on simple inputs while allocating more visual tokens to detail-rich images. Context-Preserving Inspection (CPI) returns each inspected crop together with the complete working view, preserving local detail and global context across tool turns. We evaluate Direct and Agentic variants on four frontier VLMs and 29 benchmarks spanning six visual-reasoning families. Complete ResVision (Agentic Base plus SMR plus CPI) is best for every model, improving by 3.36 to 4.94 points over Direct Base and 1.87 to 2.94 points over Agentic Base. These results show that adaptive visual sampling and context-preserving inspection address distinct, complementary constraints in multimodal reasoning.

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [ViDecide: Teaching Video Diffusion Models to Reason and Plan Step by Step](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26389v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26389

**作者：** Kaiwen Zhang、Yiming Gao、Zeqi Xiao、Xintao Wang、Pengfei Wan、Kun Gai、Xingang Pan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26389v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26389v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ViDecide 提出以决策为中心的框架，训练视频扩散模型逐步因果预测状态转移并自行判断何时停止，并用纠正性微调（将失败决策的噪声映射到模型自身的成功延续）替代策略梯度优化，在抽象游戏、规划任务与多步机器人规划基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应「STOP」停止决策、逐步状态转移与轨迹级监督问题，其纠正性微调也提供了无需策略梯度的贡献分配线索；但推理状态位于视频扩散生成器中，需核查其 latent 是否真正承载任务必要的视觉证据而非仅生成合理画面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ViDecide: Teaching Video Diffusion Models to Reason and Plan Step by Step

**abstract/A1：** Current leading video generation models can synthesize highly realistic videos, while still frequently violating physical and logical constraints in multi-step reasoning and planning, even after training with large-scale reasoning data or existing diffusion RL methods. We attribute these failures to the conventional video training paradigm, which treats a solution as a fixed-length clip rather than a chain of decisions. We present ViDecide, a decision-centric framework that trains video diffusion models to predict state transitions causally and decide when to stop, with a decision-focused training schedule. We further propose Corrective Fine-tuning (CFT), which corrects failed decisions by mapping their noise to the model's own successful continuations, achieving stable improvement without policy-gradient optimization. Across abstract games, planning tasks, and a new benchmark of multi-step robot planning, ViDecide achieves an average strict success rate of 83.8%, far above 23.3% for standard fine-tuning and 67.8% for the strongest prior method, and generalizes to larger puzzles and unseen layouts. Our framework therefore unlocks greater potential for video generation models as reliable reasoners and planners in the visual world. Project page: https://kevin-thu.github.io/ViDecide.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://kevin-thu.github.io/ViDecide

</details>

<a id="paper-30"></a>

### 30. [MM-SystemOneBias: Evaluating Bias in Multimodal Jev-Style System One Decision Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26557v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26557

**作者：** Jinzhe Li、Qi Wang、Dandan Guo、Li Liu、Yuan Wu、Yi Chang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26557v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26557v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MM-SystemOneBias，在 9 个公开数据集、11 类任务场景的 5,323 个实例上，用六种配对干预（选项顺序、选项标签、外部判断、图文描述、无关信息、评分量表）评测 13 个多模态决策模型及其骨干模型的稳定性与准确性变化。

**相关性简评：** 外部判断、一致/冲突描述与无关信息三类干预可用于核查多模态模型是否真正筛选视觉证据、还是走文本旁路或迎合提示；「决策不变但答案概率仍移动 4.56–11.55 个百分点」提示评价 LVR 时需超越准确率，测量内部状态对呈现变化的敏感度。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MM-SystemOneBias: Evaluating Bias in Multimodal Jev-Style System One Decision Models

**abstract/A1：** When multimodal decision models determine actions, moderation outcomes, or quality judgments, their reliability depends on preserving semantic decisions across presentation changes, ignoring irrelevant additions, and distinguishing information consistent with task evidence from information that conflicts with it. Existing evaluations study decision-interface biases and multimodal model behavior in separate settings, leaving no common protocol to compare multimodal decision models with their backbones on practical tasks. We introduce \\textbf{MM-SystemOneBias}, which evaluates 13 models on 5,323 original instances from nine public datasets and 11 task scenarios using six paired interventions: option order, option label, external judgment, image--text description, irrelevant information, and rating scale. Across seven matched decision-model--backbone pairs, decision models are more stable under option-label, irrelevant-information, and rating-scale changes in all seven pairs; six pairs also show lower option-order flip rates. They incur smaller accuracy losses under incorrect external judgments in all seven pairs and conflicting descriptions in six. In six pairs, they gain less accuracy from correct external judgments and show less favorable accuracy changes under consistent descriptions. Among pairs with unchanged semantic decisions, mean absolute changes in answer probabilities range from 4.56 to 11.55 percentage points, revealing input effects that accuracy alone misses.

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [Probing the "Conscious" Mind: Selective Access and Global Workspace in Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26590v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26590

**作者：** Jiahao Huang、Jun Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26590v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26590v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Multimodal Concept Lens（MC-Lens），一种多模态 Jacobian lens，将视觉编码器与语言解码器的中间状态映射到解码器词表定义的共享语义空间，比较跨模态概念的可读性与按需选择性访问，并用反事实干预检验被访问概念是否参与下游计算；另提出两个 J-space 指标追踪跨模态概念对齐的发展。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LVR 关心的「视觉信息是否保留在内部状态」与「答案是否真正使用 latent 内容」：其概念可读性探针和干预方法可供核查视觉旁路、信息坍缩与贡献分配，J-space 指标也可能用于观察跨模态对齐与表示几何。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Probing the "Conscious" Mind: Selective Access and Global Workspace in Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) integrate visual and textual information, but their outputs do not reveal internal concept representations. Global workspace theory (GWT) proposes that information is selected from parallel brain processes, integrated in a shared workspace, and made available to specialised processes. We introduce the Multimodal Concept Lens (MC-Lens), a multimodal Jacobian lens, to study these functions in VLM concept processing. Its vision encoder and language decoder lenses map intermediate states to a shared semantic space defined by the decoder's output vocabulary to compare concept accessibility across modalities. MC-Lens supports internal concept interventions to examine the role of accessed concepts in downstream computation. (i) Accessibility and selective access. Multimodal concept information is readable in intermediate states and selectively accessible as tasks require. We extend "readout carriers" from one name token to a token set to better capture and express concept information and support counterfactual experiments. (ii) Concepts in downstream computation. Counterfactual experiments show that accessed concept information participates in downstream computation and reasoning. (iii) Development of cross-modal alignment and selective access. We develop two J-space metrics tracking the development of cross-modal concept alignment more clearly than hidden state cosine in controlled VLM training. The experiment also shows that reliable selective access develops later than concept accessibility. (iv) Shared functions across models. Across VLM families and sizes, we find shared patterns of concept accessibility, query-dependent selectivity and cross-task intervention effects despite differences in internal organisation. These findings offer an experimental basis to study GWT in multimodal artificial systems and how it can guide cross-modal alignment improvements.

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [Stop Going in Circles:Loop-Triggered Routing for System 1 Decision Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25363v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25363

**作者：** Zeming Yu、Huamin Ma、Yingyu Chen、Yuan Sun、Peng Hu、Yang Qin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25363v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25363v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 LoopRoute：默认由 System 1 决策模型（Jev）选动作，当检测到重复 state-action 对形成的循环时，调用 LLM 给出简短反馈式协助再交回控制权；在 ALFWorld、MapReader、TextWorld cooking 三个基准和七个 LLM 上提升成功率，同时降低 LLM 调用时间与成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「用停滞信号触发昂贵推理、完成后恢复廉价策略」的免训练路由规则，可能与 LVR 中 THINK/STOP 的计算分配与切换判据相关，可供核查；但该工作为纯文本 agent 任务、无视觉表征或 latent 状态，迁移有效性需自行检验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Stop Going in Circles:Loop-Triggered Routing for System 1 Decision Agents

**abstract/A1：** System 1 decision models (represented by Jev) provide fast, low-cost action selection, but existing agent integrations do not reliably translate these local advantages into task completion. At certain points during execution, an agent may repeatedly select the same actions without making progress toward its goal. LLM-based policies offer strong reasoning capabilities that support planning within an agent, but invoking them at every step incurs substantial overhead. The effective coordination should therefore preserve Jev's fast local action selection while introducing LLM reasoning when execution stalls. We observe that recurring state-action pairs can signal stalled execution. We therefore propose LoopRoute, a simple but effective action router that uses this signal to allocate control within an agent. The decision model acts by default. When a loop is detected, the LLM provides brief, feedback-driven assistance and then returns control to the decision model. Later loops can request further assistance. The rule requires neither additional training nor model-specific confidence calibration. Across three benchmarks and seven LLMs, LoopRoute improves task success over standalone Jev with only limited use of the LLM policy. The mean gains are 21.0%, 60.1%, and 30.1% on ALFWorld, MapReader, and TextWorld cooking, respectively. Compared with full LLM control, LoopRoute also substantially reduces the mean cumulative API time and API-call cost per task. The same rule also improves the success rates of six frozen decision models.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages,3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-33"></a>

### 33. [COVERAGE-TRACE: Source-Side Counterfactual Tracing for Detecting Omissions in LLM Summaries](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25539v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25539

**作者：** Ali Karkehabadi、Jamshid Hassanpour、Rafael E. Banchs

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25539v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25539v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 COVERAGE-TRACE：在源文档侧把指定原子事实替换为类型匹配的替代事实、固定候选摘要，测量其 teacher-forced 长度归一化似然变化，以检测摘要是否遗漏该事实，并检验多反事实聚合与改写不变性的影响。

**相关性简评：** 「源侧反事实替换＋观察输出似然响应」的依赖度测量可供核查用于判断 VLM 回答是否真正使用了某个视觉细节，而非仅语义相近；其发现同义改写会显著削弱判别力，也与 latent 相似度中内容与措辞/位置混杂的问题存在可类比之处。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** COVERAGE-TRACE: Source-Side Counterfactual Tracing for Detecting Omissions in LLM Summaries

**abstract/A1：** Omissions are difficult to verify because missing information leaves no output span to inspect: a summary may be factually supported while still excluding a decision, qualification, quantity, or condition needed to preserve the source's meaning. We introduce COVERAGE-TRACE, a source-side counterfactual framework that evaluates omission as dependence on a designated atomic source fact. It replaces only the target fact with type-matched alternatives, holds the candidate summary fixed, and measures the resulting change in its teacher-forced, length-normalized likelihood. A summary covering the target should respond more strongly than one omitting it, while aggregation across multiple counterfactuals reduces sensitivity to replacement choice. We evaluate COVERAGE-TRACE on 80 matched covered--omitted QMSum pairs spanning several fact types and four open-weight models.
