Multi-CF achieves 0.764--0.825 AUROC, compared with 0.640 for the strongest evaluated text-only baseline. On Falcon-7B, targeted interventions achieve 0.779 AUROC, whereas shuffling counterfactual assignments reduces performance to 0.533; target deletion is effective while unrelated deletion is not. However, adjustment for meaning-preserving paraphrases reduces AUROC to 0.591--0.624. COVERAGE-TRACE therefore provides a practical, complementary signal of target-specific source--summary dependence, but not a wording-invariant semantic coverage probability. These results establish source-side intervention as a promising approach to omission detection while identifying semantic invariance as the central remaining challenge.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at LCFM @ NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-34"></a>

### 34. [Diagnosis is not a policy: a state-transition audit of caoability-conditioned test-time inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25566v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25566

**作者：** Yuchong Hu、Zhao Zhang、Kainan Liu、Haoyu Wang、Siyan Zhang、Jing Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25566v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25566v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TERRA 审计协议，把 test-time 干预结果分解为适用性、目标状态实现、端点翻译、危害与推理成本，并通过固定输入状态的配对比较与跨模型比较，在可执行金融推理任务上说明诊断能带来的准确率上限并不等于可部署的收益。

**相关性简评：** 其「状态是否实现」与「证据到答案翻译」的分离及成本记账，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 干预是否真正改写答案、THINK/LOOK 收益来自何处；但实验为文本金融推理，能否迁移到多模态与视觉证据筛选仍需阅读正文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Diagnosis is not a policy: a state-transition audit of caoability-conditioned test-time inference

**abstract/A1：** Test-time actions can satisfy retrieval, state-realization, program-consistency, or rendering objectives without correcting an answer. Test-time Error Response and Realizability Audit (TERRA) is an evaluation/auditing protocol, not a new inference algorithm. It uses a unified decomposition of intervention outcomes into applicability, target-state realization, endpoint translation, harm, and resource/inference cost. Paired comparisons fix the incoming state and evaluation conditions; cross-model analyses vary the receiving configuration. Executable financial reasoning experiments yield three findings: the same context expansion can harm one configuration and benefit another; sufficient evidence does not ensure correctness because configurations differ in evidence-to-answer translation; and diagnostic headroom---accuracy attainable with idealized diagnostic information over the measured baseline---plus local consistency does not ensure practical gains. Action value is $V(a\\mid x,m)$, not diagnosis alone; diagnosis is not a deployable policy $π(a\\mid x,m)$.

</details>

<a id="paper-35"></a>

### 35. [DriftGuard: Online Memory-Drift Detection via Causal Attribution for LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25583v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25583

**作者：** Licheng Pan、Yuzhou Chen、Zhixuan Chu、Yichen Sun、Han Zheng、Zhiyu Wang、Hao Wang、Yan Wang、Bingyu Zhu、Haiwen Hong、Longtao Huang、Kui Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25583v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25583v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DriftGuard，用于 LLM agent 的服务期记忆漂移检测：离线通过配对反事实重放（从共享动作前状态出发，中和单条记忆后继续执行）标注因果贡献并聚合为记忆漂移分数，再训练在线风险模型在动作前估计该分数、标记致漂移记忆。

**相关性简评：** 虽属纯文本 agent 记忆安全，但其「困惑度、检索相关性等内容特征不足以判断因果作用，须做反事实中和」的判断与视觉证据筛选同构，且「离线反事实监督＋蒸馏为在线估计器」的做法可能可供核查，用于低成本分配视觉证据或 latent 状态的因果贡献而免去逐候选重放。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DriftGuard: Online Memory-Drift Detection via Causal Attribution for LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents rely on persistent memory to plan tool calls and complete multi-step tasks, which exposes them to memory drift: an adversarial or misleading memory alters the execution path and final outcome. In this work, we investigate online memory-drift detection, which estimates each retrieved memory's causal contribution to potential drift at serving time. We identify two fundamental challenges. (1) Content-level features are insufficient for causal memory-drift attribution, as perplexity or retrieval relevance alone cannot determine which retrieved memory would change agent behavior if neutralized; (2) Counterfactual attribution is prohibitively expensive for online detection, as it requires replaying downstream agent and tool execution for each candidate memory, exceeding real-time serving budgets. To address these challenges, we propose DriftGuard, a two-stage framework that decouples offline causal supervision from online estimation. Specifically, DriftGuard first constructs causal supervision through repeated paired counterfactual replays, continuing the original and single-memory neutralized trajectory suffixes from a shared pre-action state. The resulting annotations are aggregated by component-wise majority voting into a memory drift score (MDS) that captures action change, attack removal, violation removal, and task recovery. Guided by this supervision, DriftGuard then learns an online risk model that estimates MDS from pre-action information and flags drift-inducing memories via its maximum component risk, without online counterfactual rollouts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DriftGuard accurately identifies drift-inducing memories and enables efficient online defense. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DriftGuard-7F0B.

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [RelGeo: Pair-Conditioned Relational Calibration for Frozen Diffusion Correspondence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25628v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25628

**作者：** Zixuan Dai、Zhiliang Zhang、Daosong Hu、Tang Zheng、Xinkai Liu、Tianyu Lei、Wei Zhan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25628v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25628v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文诊断冻结扩散描述子在语义对应中的候选选择瓶颈：多数 Top-1 错误在原始 Top-5 空间峰内仍存在 PCK 有效匹配，并提出 RelGeo 将互惠扩散 Q/K 亲和与 RoMa 双向传输端点编码为共享源-目标坐标，以内积校准描述子相似度且不额外训练语义对应。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示中已有正解但读出选错」的瓶颈与 LVR 中答案是否真正使用 latent 内容、视觉证据筛选的问题相通；把扩散注意力与传输结构作为关系证据做校准的思路，可供核查 latent 相似度中注意力结构的作用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RelGeo: Pair-Conditioned Relational Calibration for Frozen Diffusion Correspondence

**abstract/A1：** Frozen diffusion descriptors retrieve strong semantic correspondence candidates, yet cosine nearest-neighbor matching can select incorrect locations despite valid high-ranking alternatives. Across DIFT-SD2.1 and DiTF-FLUX on SPair-71k, AP-10K, and SOCO (CC, SOC, Cross-SOC), 41-69% of Top-1 errors retain a PCK@0.10-valid match among the original Top-5 spatial peaks, revealing a persistent candidate-selection bottleneck. We find that descriptor rankings and current-pair relational evidence can favor different candidates: reciprocal diffusion Query/Key associations and pretrained RoMa transport exhibit distinct, partly complementary preferences. To incorporate this evidence into matching decisions, we introduce RelGeo, a Pair-Conditioned Relational Calibration framework expressing heterogeneous relations as shared source-target coordinates. Spectral Relational Coordinates (SRC) encode reciprocal diffusion affinities, while Warp Identity Coordinates (WIC) encode reliability-weighted bidirectional transport endpoints. Their inner products augment frozen descriptor similarity while preserving full-domain cosine nearest-neighbor matching without additional semantic-correspondence training. RelGeo improves all ten evaluations by an average of 5.51 PCK@0.10 points; fixed-candidate reranking and pair-misalignment controls corroborate improved selection and dependence on current-pair relational alignment.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 13 figures, including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-37"></a>

### 37. [LATER: Learning Action-Conditioned Temporal Evidence Retrieval for Predictive World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25671v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25671

**作者：** Zihan Zhou、Wenxi Xu、Qilin Wang、Hao Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25671v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25671v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LATER，一个基于 CF-JEPA 的动作条件世界模型：将已完成的真实转移写入分区记忆，并在候选动作 rollout 中保持固定，由 response bridge 结合当前状态与候选动作选择性读取，并用 multi-cut 辅助监督训练记忆写入；在 Two-Stage PushT 上评估成功率、物理量读取误差及历史证据依赖。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留与选择性读取」的记忆分区、固定缓存及早期视觉证据干预，可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选、跨步状态保持和缓存/读取结构提供核查线索；但研究的是世界模型预测而非 VLM 推理状态生成，联系需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LATER: Learning Action-Conditioned Temporal Evidence Retrieval for Predictive World Models

**abstract/A1：** Early interactions can reveal physical properties that later observations no longer show. These properties can still gov- ern the effects of future actions. Inspired by the distinction between retention and selective readout in working mem- ory and cognitive science, we introduce LATER, an action- conditioned world model built on CF-JEPA. LATER stores completed real transitions in partitioned memories that remain fixed during candidate rollouts. A response bridge queries these memories using the current state and candidate action, while multi-cut auxiliary supervision trains the memory writ- ers. On distracted Two-Stage PushT, LATER raises terminal success from 15.5% to 25.0% over reconstructed CF-JEPA. In a separate comparison across six models and two scratch- training seeds, LATER achieves the lowest mean physical readout error in both clean and distracted views, reducing along-push mean absolute error (MAE) under distractors by 20.2% relative to CF-JEPA. Controlled history interventions show that later predictions depend on earlier physical evi- dence even when query observations and actions are fixed, with larger effects during pushing than waiting.

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [RouteVerify: Routing Interventions for Step-Level Verification in Mixture-of-Experts Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25780v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25780

**作者：** Yucheng Du、Ryan A. Rossi、Xiyang Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25780v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25780v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** RouteVerify 在九个 MoE 检查点上做受控干预：把一条解法轨迹的 router logits、MoE 输出或 post-MoE 残差激活替换为另一条轨迹的对应激活，输入不变，测量步骤级验证 margin 的变化。结果显示 router logits 探针能区分正确与错误步骤，替换也确能改变判断，但判断移动很少随来源步骤的正确性而变，即「可读出」不等于「被使用」。

**相关性简评：** 该文给出「内部信号可解码 ≠ 下游判断真正依赖它」的因果干预与 norm-matched 随机对照范式，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容是否真正参与答案生成、步骤验证与贡献分配，以及把可读性误当因果作用的陷阱；但研究对象为纯文本 MoE，迁移到多模态 latent 状态需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RouteVerify: Routing Interventions for Step-Level Verification in Mixture-of-Experts Models

**abstract/A1：** Routing states in mixture-of-experts (MoE) models can distinguish correct from erroneous reasoning steps. If changing routing also changes a model's step-level verification judgment, it is tempting to conclude that the judgment follows what routing encodes. We test this inference with RouteVerify, a controlled intervention study that replaces selected internal activations from one solution trace with activations from another, while keeping the evaluated input unchanged. Across nine MoE checkpoints, we compare three intervention interfaces: router logits, MoE outputs, and post-MoE residual states. We vary the layers and token positions at which activations are replaced. We measure changes in a verification margin that quantifies the model's preference for INCORRECT over CORRECT. We also evaluate the same input with activations from correct versus erroneous steps to test whether the resulting judgment depends on the source step's correctness. We report four linked results. First, probes on router logits separate correct from erroneous steps in eight checkpoints (AUROC 0.80-0.88). Second, replacing router logits across all routed layers changes the judgment in all nine checkpoints, and by more than a norm-matched random replacement. Third, this change rarely depends on whether the source step is correct. At one layer, routing from erroneous steps and from correct steps produces indistinguishable margins in all nine checkpoints. Under all-layer routing, their difference is resolved and positive in only one. Fourth, the same difference is positive for local residual replacements in eight of nine checkpoints, so the measure does detect such dependence where it exists. Correctness is therefore readable from routing, and routing replacements move the judgment, but the movement rarely follows correctness.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages, 5 figures. Code: https://github.com/yucheng-du/RouteVerify

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [Generalist Meets Specialists: An Evolving Multimodal Agent with Persistent Tool Orchestration for Traffic Scenario Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25868v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25868

**作者：** Linjia Kang、Binhan Jiang、Juan Yang、Zhiqin Song、Xiangsen Wu、Qionglin Ren、Jiang Liu、Zhongjiang He、Hao Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25868v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25868v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向交通场景理解的多模态 agent StarTV-Agent，用角色敏感的过程优化配合弱项驱动的教师示范训练，并在推理期以工作流复用走快路径、以 ReAct 慢路径处理新任务，结合无标注反馈更新记忆，在三个基准上提升性能并降低 token 消耗。

**相关性简评：** 过程优势与终止奖励的结合、快慢双路径的选择与工作流复用，可为核查多模态推理中贡献分配、计算开销与「何时复用而非重新推理」的停止决策提供方法线索，但其收益基于外部工具编排而非内部 latent 状态，与 latent 表示层面的联系仍需阅读正文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generalist Meets Specialists: An Evolving Multimodal Agent with Persistent Tool Orchestration for Traffic Scenario Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Traffic scenario understanding requires combining fine-grained visual evidence with spatial and temporal reasoning. Tool-augmented vision-language models (VLMs) can use specialized perception tools, but effective collaboration faces two challenges: terminal rewards provide limited guidance for intermediate decisions and error recovery, while repeated planning adds unnecessary cost across similar tasks. Echoing the fast-slow dichotomy of human cognition, we propose StarTV-Agent, which combines policy learning with experience evolution to support adaptive reasoning when needed and efficient reuse of prior experience when possible. During training, Role-Sensitive Process Optimization (RSPO) combines process advantages with terminal advantages, while Weakness-Driven Demonstration Learning (WDL) provides validated teacher demonstrations targeting the student's weak questions. During inference, Lightweight Adaptive Workflow Reuse (LAWR) enables a fast path by selecting suitable workflows and binding task-specific arguments and resource references, while a slow ReAct-based path handles tasks without suitable workflows. We further design label-free feedback based on execution completion, workflow adoption, and cost savings to guide workflow reuse and memory updates. Across three challenging benchmarks, StarTV-Agent achieves up to 8.0% relative performance improvement, while reducing token consumption by up to 52.1%.

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [Budget Internalization: Fixed-Budget RLVR Trades Test-Time Scaling for In-Budget Accuracy](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26172v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26172

**作者：** Ilia Zavidnyi、Alexander Kovrigin、Arina Puchkova、Kirill Gelvan、Sergey Titov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26172v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26172v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文发现 RLVR 训练时的固定 rollout 长度上限会被模型「内化」为学习到的约束：训练后模型几乎不超出该预算，在预算附近优于基座模型，但更大预算下准确率停滞甚至低于基座，作者称之为 budget internalization，并用多个模型、两类任务及 SFT 截断轨迹对照实验定位其源于长度截断式后训练而非 RL 特有目标项。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本工作，但其关于长度预算被内化为停止倾向、以及在 THINK/STOP 上以低预算收益换取测试时扩展能力的结论，可为 LVR 中停止决策、动态计算分配与长度泛化的收益-成本分析提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Budget Internalization: Fixed-Budget RLVR Trades Test-Time Scaling for In-Budget Accuracy

**abstract/A1：** Reasoning models often become more accurate when they can generate longer answers at test time. This behavior is known as test-time scaling. However, RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) usually trains them with a fixed rollout cap. We find that this cap changes model behavior, not only training cost. After training, models use few additional tokens beyond the training budget. They outperform the base models near that budget, but their accuracy stops improving and falls below the base model at larger budgets. The rollout cap thus becomes a learned constraint, that limits model's test-time scaling. We call this effect budget internalization. We observe it across Qwen3.5-4B, Gemma4-E2B, and Nemotron-3-Nano-4B and two reasoning training tasks: mathematical reasoning and diverse, procedurally generated problems. This effect also transfers from math training to science and coding tasks. Training-objective level ablations on Qwen3.5-4B show that most changes recover little test-time scaling. Stronger KL regularization and lower training-data truncation preserve more scaling, but stronger KL reduces in-budget accuracy. Forcing an answer on truncated training rollouts largely removes budget internalization, but also removes most of the in-budget gain. SFT on artificially truncated traces reproduces budget internalization, whereas SFT on naturally short traces does not, which shows that the effect is not specific to RL. This result links budget internalization to length-censored post-training rather than to an RL-specific objective term. Thus, a rollout cap does not only control training cost. It can trade low-budget gains for lower performance when additional inference compute is available.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 26 figures, 16 tables. Checkpoints: https://huggingface.co/collections/JetBrains-Research/budget-internalization

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [Training LLMs to Follow Reasoning Instructions with Minimal Edits](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26342v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26342

**作者：** Alexandra Yakovleva、Luat Gia Khoi Nguyen、Juho Kannala、Harri Valpola、Alexander Ilin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26342v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26342v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 edit-based training 框架：从模型自身采样的完整推理轨迹出发，由编辑器定位给定准则下的首个失败点并给出最小替换片段，只对该片段施加监督，并有离线与在线（持续适配演化中的失败模式）两种版本，在 ReasonIF 上用 Qwen3-32B 与全轨迹模仿、轨迹级 RL 对比。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本、不涉及视觉 latent 状态，但其「定位首个失败点、仅做最小片段修正」的细粒度监督可能是 LVR 轨迹训练与贡献分配的可用线索，可供核查如何在施加局部纠正的同时不破坏已有推理模式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Training LLMs to Follow Reasoning Instructions with Minimal Edits

**abstract/A1：** Large language models increasingly rely on extended reasoning traces, yet the training signals used to shape this behavior remain coarse. Supervised fine-tuning on full trajectories forces the model to imitate an entire demonstration, overconstraining its output distribution, while trajectory-level reinforcement learning provides only weak credit assignment, rewarding or penalizing whole completions without identifying where they succeed or fail. We propose edit-based training, a general framework for learning from a model's own trajectories via localized textual corrections. Given a sampled completion, an editor identifies the first point of failure under a specified criterion and proposes a minimal replacement span. The model is then supervised only on these edited spans. By design, this concentrates supervision where the model's reasoning first breaks down and aims to preserve its existing reasoning patterns elsewhere. The method applies both offline and online, with the latter continually adapting supervision to the model's evolving failure modes. We instantiate this approach on reasoning instruction following with Qwen3-32B on the ReasonIF benchmark. Compared to full-trajectory imitation and trajectory-level reinforcement learning baselines, edit-based training substantially improves compliance with the reasoning instruction while largely preserving the model's skills on untargeted tasks. These results suggest that localized correction is an effective complement to these approaches for shaping how LLMs reason.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Transitioning from Pre-training to Post-training

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [World Models Favor Learned Priors over Visual Evidence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26366v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26366

**作者：** Han Xiao、Zhiyong Bao、Xiaotong Liu、Yanhao Chen、Qingqiang Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26366v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26366v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究跨环境世界模型中的「视觉校准」：用短视频中的物体运动与交互作为证据推断当前物理规则，并迁移到新初始状态预测；提出 TailWorld 基准，以「演示规则」和「常见规则」两条参考未来来度量预测向常见动力学的偏向，以及 RIFT 方法显式控制训练频率对规则推断的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其「视觉证据 vs 学习先验」的偏向诊断与 RIFT 对训练频率的显式控制，可能对应 LVR 中视觉细节是否真正进入推理状态、长尾或语言先验压制证据的问题，可供核查作为缓解视觉旁路的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** World Models Favor Learned Priors over Visual Evidence

**abstract/A1：** World models are increasingly used to simulate physical interactions, but training a separate model for each environment limits scalability. Cross-environment prediction requires identifying the current physical rule, which appearance alone may not reveal. We therefore study visual calibration: using object motion and interactions in a short video as direct evidence to infer the current rule and predict motion under that rule from a different initial state. However, differences in data availability across physical conditions can yield a long-tailed training distribution, biasing models toward common rules even when evidence supports rare ones. To study this challenge, we introduce TailWorld, which evaluates the transfer of demonstrated rules across initial states while independently controlling training imbalance and calibration informativeness. The benchmark compares predictions with two reference futures from the same prediction initial state, one following the demonstrated rule and the other a common rule, to measure prediction bias toward common dynamics. We further propose Rule Inference for Transfer (RIFT), which learns how different rules affect object motion, infers the current rule from calibration interactions, and applies it to prediction from a new initial state. RIFT explicitly controls the influence of training frequency on rule inference, allowing sufficiently informative observational evidence to support rare rules. Experiments on TailWorld show that RIFT improves prediction accuracy and physical consistency across varying training imbalances and distribution shifts, with gains in predicting rare dynamics.

</details>

<a id="paper-43"></a>

### 43. [GLiDE: A Fast, Calibrated Decision Model Distilled from Reasoning Teachers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26509v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26509

**作者：** Sahibzada Allahyar、Julia White、Dhruv Atreja、George Hurn-Maloney、Urchade Zaratiana

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26509v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26509v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GLiDE 系列决策模型（0.4B–27B），通过在 full-attention 层去掉 causal mask 让上下文、问题与候选答案双向交互，并用多个教师（含推理模型）的 soft-target 蒸馏与渐进式后训练，把推理收益压进单次前向的答案打分中；还尝试无参数的循环深度在不生成 token 的情况下增加推理期计算，并报告校准误差与延迟。

**相关性简评：** 其「能否不生成推理文本而保留推理收益」的问题设定，以及用循环深度替代 token 级推理来增加计算、并以校准与延迟衡量成本的做法，可为 LVR 中把推理轨迹蒸馏进内部状态、以及推理期计算的收益-成本核算提供可核查线索，但对象是纯文本决策任务，尚无视觉证据筛选方面的证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GLiDE: A Fast, Calibrated Decision Model Distilled from Reasoning Teachers

**abstract/A1：** Decision models enable fast, confidence-aware decisions by scoring candidate answers in a single forward pass, without generating text or intermediate reasoning. Yet reasoning can substantially improve decision quality, raising the question of whether its benefits can be retained without the cost of generating it. We investigate this question with \\textbf{GLiDE}, a family of decision models spanning 0.4B to 27B parameters. Building on Qwen hybrid language models, GLiDE enables bidirectional interactions between the context, question, and candidate answers by removing causal masking from full-attention layers. To transfer reasoning capabilities into single-pass inference, we combine soft-target distillation from multiple teachers, including a reasoning model, with progressive post-training on diverse decision tasks. We further explore parameter-free recurrent depth to increase inference-time computation without generating tokens. Across five model sizes, GLiDE lies on the performance--parameter Pareto frontier of the Hugging Face \\DI{}, with each model outperforming evaluated alternatives of equal or smaller size. \\dmtwo{} (27B) achieves a hidden-set score of 71.52, with an expected calibration error of 2.0\\% and a median latency of 92.9\\,ms on a single GPU. Further checkpoint averaging and parameter-free recurrent depth yield GLiDE~27B, which reaches 73.37 with an expected calibration error of 2.1\\%.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-44"></a>

### 44. [AeroBelief: Budgeted First-Action Verification for Evidence-Guided Aerial Sensing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25568v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25568

**作者：** Tianren Li、Dongbo Li、Hongkai Chen、Jie Liu、Guoliang Xing

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25568v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25568v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** AeroBelief 面向航空感知，把「下一个可执行动作」作为验证单元：先用廉价 proposer 按首个动作分组扫描候选视角，再有选择地查询结构化世界模型，并用校准的组界与六次查询预算下的控制器串联信念更新、接受检查与按需图像处理。

**相关性简评：** 以动作级选择性验证、校准下界和预算上限来分配证据获取，与 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的收益成本权衡及「证据充分即提交」的停止准则可能同构，其 first-action certificate 与查询预算设计可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AeroBelief: Budgeted First-Action Verification for Evidence-Guided Aerial Sensing

**abstract/A1：** Aerial sensing must combine uncertain overhead context with current observations while budgeting prediction, movement and image processing. AeroBelief uses the next executable action as the unit of verification against reference scores. It groups candidate views by first action, scans them with an inexpensive proposer, and selectively queries a structured world model (WM). Calibrated group bounds support strict commitment; a six-query controller connects selective prediction with belief updates, acceptance checks and scheduled sensing. Across four layout families, 40 worlds and 960 closed-loop executions, the AeroBelief controller completes 75.42% of tasks, compared with 73.33% for exhaustive WM scoring and 65.83% for line-of-sight geometry, using 91.81% fewer reference-candidate evaluations than exhaustive scoring. In fixed-reference replay with a six-query cap, proposer-scaled anchored bounds raised first-action certificate availability from 36.25% to 54.69% and reduced mean queries per state by 13.54% relative to unit-scale anchored bounds. A complete ground-hosted pipeline processes 2,685 self-collected 20-megapixel unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) images from seven DJI Mavic 3M routes. Context-guided admission and feedback retain all eight independently supported target confirmations with 76.05% fewer processed images and 70.02% less accumulated active processing service than uncapped all-frame processing; feedback saves another 5.17% of images relative to static context. The studies connect budgeted action selection with evidence-driven processing of recorded flight imagery.

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [EgoPass-Bench: Identifying Objects That May Affect the Wearer's Passage in Egocentric Video](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24995v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.24995

**作者：** Hongyu Wang、Chuhan Li、Congjin Xu、Xuandong Zhao、Hua Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24995v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.24995v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EgoPass-Bench：以智能眼镜采集的 284 段第一视角片段和 2201 个问题，从运动感知、指定实体的交互判断到无目标提示的相关实体发现三个层面，评估多模态基础模型能否找出影响佩戴者通行的实体。

**相关性简评：** 该基准诊断「能识别物体运动却选不准通行约束实体」的失败模式，与 VLM 推理中的视觉证据筛选、任务必要几何与实体关系是否保留在内部状态相关，可供核查其错误类型与评价设计是否适用于 LVR 研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EgoPass-Bench: Identifying Objects That May Affect the Wearer's Passage in Egocentric Video

**abstract/A1：** Understanding an egocentric environment requires reasoning about how surrounding entities affect the observer's actions. Existing evaluations of multimodal foundation models (MFMs) examine observer localization, motion trajectories, and action feasibility, but leave the discovery and interpretation of evolving observer-entity interactions insufficiently assessed. We introduce EgoPass-Bench, a benchmark for observer-relative interaction understanding in real-world egocentric video. EgoPass-Bench comprises 284 manually reviewed evaluation clips recorded using Ray-Ban Meta (Gen 2) smart glasses and 2,201 distinct questions across five tasks assessing motion perception, interaction judgments about specified entities, and relevant-entity discovery without target cues. Our evaluation of eight frame-based and six native-video configurations reveals a pronounced discrepancy between motion perception and interaction understanding. The strongest evaluated model, GPT-6 Astra, correctly recognizes object motion in 91.37% of 394 comparisons involving the same targets, but correctly identifies changes in passage constraints in only 66.50%. Across all 574 entity-discovery questions, it trails the two-person human baseline by $23.65 \\pm 7.45$ percentage points (paired 95% CI), while exceeding the always-empty baseline (36.76%) by at least 18.9 percentage points. Further analysis reveals frequent inclusion of entities that neither pose a plausible physical conflict nor constrain passage, showing that accurate motion recognition does not ensure reliable relevant-entity discovery.

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [VidToC: Rethinking Video Token Compression for Multimodal Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25574v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25574

**作者：** Zihan Xiao、Boshen Xu、Pei Fu、Jianzhong Ju、Zhenbo Luo、Jian Luan、Qin Jin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25574v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25574v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VidToC 受控评测框架，在三种视频理解任务上统一比较代表性视觉 token 压缩算法，并考察压缩位置、时间编码策略与 Transformer/Mamba 混合骨干在免训练和微调设定下的影响。

**相关性简评：** 压缩本质上决定保留哪些视觉证据，其任务依赖的压缩行为可供核查视觉信息坍缩如何影响多模态推理所需细节与计算节省；但未涉及 latent 推理状态或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 决策，需读原文确认是否分析推理类任务。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VidToC: Rethinking Video Token Compression for Multimodal Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Extending multimodal large language models (MLLMs) from images to videos faces substantial memory and latency challenges due to the massive number of visual tokens. These challenges have driven the development of numerous visual token compression methods and more efficient LLM architectures. However, existing compression methods are often evaluated with different training setups, tasks, and models, making fair comparison difficult. Meanwhile, the growing use of hybrid Mamba-Transformer architectures raises questions about whether compression strategies transfer across backbones. We present VidToC, a controlled evaluation framework for representative compression algorithms, LLM backbones, and time encoding strategies, covering training-free and fine-tuned settings on three complementary video understanding tasks. Our results reveal task-dependent compression behavior and characterize the effects of compression placement, time encoding, and backbone choice. These findings provide empirical guidance for designing and selecting video token compression strategies across tasks and training settings.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 7 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [DACA: Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent for Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25722v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25722

**作者：** Jiaming Liu、Linghe Kong、Guixu Zhang、Huaxi Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25722v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25722v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出双锚点协作智能体 DACA：语义锚用光流与 VLM 局部验证跟踪目标，几何锚用体素化和多关键点投影做全局空间推理，二者经工具调用协议双向交换信息并自主决定下一步感知动作，实现零样本 3D 视觉定位。

**相关性简评：** 其闭环的「感知—动作」决策、双向信息交换与失败恢复，与 LVR 关注的视觉证据筛选、何时继续 LOOK 及停止判断直接相关；但推理发生在显式工具与外部点云/图像上而非 latent 状态，可为核查决策收益与表示作用的对照工作。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DACA: Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent for Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding

**abstract/A1：** 3D visual grounding (3D-VG) is a task that bridges the natural language and 3D space, serving as a foundational capability for embodied intelligence and human-computer interaction. Although recent 3D-VG agents overcome the reliance on pre-processed 3D point clouds, they essentially employ a unidirectional, pipeline-style tool-calling architecture. This architecture separates semantic understanding from geometric reconstruction, leading to error accumulation and an inability to autonomously recover from failures. To address this flaw, this paper proposes the Dual-Anchor Collaborative Agent (DACA), which reinterprets 3D-VG as a semantic-geometric dual-anchor collaborative reasoning paradigm. DACA comprises two complementary specialized tools: the semantic anchor tool holds the visual semantic features of the target and achieves efficient target tracking via optical flow and VLM local verification; the geometric anchor tool holds the 3D spatial geometric features of the target and performs global spatial reasoning via voxelization and multi-keypoint projection. The two components exchange information bidirectionally via a standardized tool call protocol, autonomously deciding on the next perception-action step to form a closed-loop optimization. Experiments on the ScanRefer and Nr3D benchmarks demonstrate that zero-shot DACA achieves an overall Acc@0.25 of 73.6\\% on ScanRefer and 70.4\\% overall accuracy on Nr3D, far surpassing the latest zero-shot 3D-VG methods. The code and models are available at https://github.com/liujia99/DACA

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [SpatialMem: Building Quantitative Spatial Reasoning Agents with Hierarchical Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25925v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25925

**作者：** Zinan Zheng、Nuo Chen、Jia Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25925v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25925v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SpatialMem，一个双层记忆框架（任务记忆记录工具编排、场景记忆记录专家选择），配合双状态轨迹优化，让 VLM agent 积累工具使用经验来解决定量空间推理；同时发布 MetricBench 基准。

**相关性简评：** 定量空间推理中的工具编排与记忆化证据获取贴近「获取证据与动态计算」议题，但推理状态外化在工具流水线而非内部 latent；其成功/次优轨迹的保留与精炼做法可供核查 LVR 的轨迹监督与贡献分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpatialMem: Building Quantitative Spatial Reasoning Agents with Hierarchical Memory

**abstract/A1：** Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in qualitative spatial reasoning, but remain limited in quantitative spatial reasoning. Current work leverages the VLM agent to invoke external vision tools to perform computation. However, these methods usually bind each functional requirement to a single predefined vision specialist, implicitly resulting in fixed and rigid tool pipelines. In practice, addressing diverse spatial scenarios and tasks demands a broader diversity in pipeline orchestration and vision specialist selection to achieve robust spatial reasoning. However, such diversity leads to a combinatorial execution space, making it challenging for the agent to orchestrate the optimal pipelines and specialists for different scenarios. In this work, we propose SpatialMem, a memory framework that enables a VLM agent to accumulate tool-use experience and leverage that memory to orchestrate vision specialists to solve the spatial reasoning problem. SpatialMem builds a two-level memory structure, with task memory capturing tool orchestration and scene memory capturing tool selection. It further introduces a dual-state trajectory optimization process to preserve successful tool experiences and refine suboptimal ones. Moreover, existing spatial reasoning benchmarks suffer from quality issues and limited coverage of scene types and tasks. To address these gap, we propose MetricBench, a benchmark covering both indoor and outdoor scenes and three quantitative spatial reasoning tasks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art results on MetricBench, Q-Spatial, and SpatilRGPT-Bench, outperforming best VLM baselines by 32% on MetricBench. Additionally, the memory design yields a 9.1% absolute gain over a memory-free agent on MetricBench.

</details>

<a id="paper-49"></a>

### 49. [PsyWorld: Profile-Conditioned World Modeling for Human Behavior Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24999v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.24999

**作者：** Ziyuan Qin、Yang Qiao、Summer B. Frandsen、Michael T. Treadway、Liang Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24999v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.24999v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出一种以心理测量画像为条件的隐状态世界模型：训练时用后验编码器量化真实的环境—行为状态转移，再由先验从画像与转移前上下文预测离散编码，并用预测的下一状态驱动宏观行为与模拟器执行，在 VEET 与 AgentSociety 上验证对未见个体的泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其「后验编码器量化真实转移 + 先验从上下文预测隐编码」的结构与离散 latent 状态的生成及监督方式相近，消融中连续状态建模与离散编码的贡献差异可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容的贡献分配与信息坍缩问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PsyWorld: Profile-Conditioned World Modeling for Human Behavior Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Understanding how stable psychometric traits and time-varying states jointly inform behavior is an important problem in computational behavioral science. Existing personalized agents commonly condition actions directly on a static profile, leaving realized state transitions unused as an explicit source of supervision. We introduce a profile-conditioned state world model that uses observed environment--behavior state transitions to learn transition-informed representations for behavioral prediction. During training, a posterior encoder quantizes realized transitions, while a prior predicts the resulting codes from the psychometric profile and pre-transition context. The prior code and predicted next state then condition macro-level behavior and simulator-grounded execution. Thus, the world model provides structured transition supervision instead of treating personality as a direct action descriptor. We evaluate the framework in VEET, a controlled behavioral simulator with human trajectories, and in AgentSociety as a structurally different synthetic environment. A participant-disjoint split of 240 training, 25 validation, and 48 test participants evaluates generalization to unseen people. On VEET, the model reaches 0.904 Top-1 accuracy (95\\% CI: \[0.895, 0.913\]) and 0.0033 Macro KL. Ablations associate continuous next-state modeling with most of the Top-1 gain, while discrete codes and prior alignment mainly improve Top-3 and distributional alignment. These results establish transition-informed world-model supervision as useful for subject-generalized behavior prediction, without implying causal psychological mechanisms or calibrated long-horizon state dynamics.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the KDD 2027 AI for Sciences Track

</details>

<a id="paper-50"></a>

### 50. [Watch Where You Linearize: Predicting and Correcting Model-Merging Interference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25000v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25000

**作者：** Ido Nitzan Hidekel、Dan Raviv

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25000v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25000v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究模型合并中的干扰预测：在接收模型处用任务 A 输出沿任务 B 任务向量的一阶方向导数预测邻近合并的逐任务、逐样本效果，并据此冻结响应构造对系数仿射的无标签代理，提出免反传的凸教师匹配修正方法 LR-Merge。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联；但其逐样本预测输出翻转的方向导数判据和对合并系数的仿射代理，可能可供核查多能力（如 THINK 与 LOOK）合并时干扰预测与贡献分配的做法。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Watch Where You Linearize: Predicting and Correcting Model-Merging Interference

**abstract/A1：** Model merging combines separately fine-tuned models by adding their task vectors to a shared backbone. Interference is typically assessed only after constructing and evaluating a candidate merge, making coefficient search expensive. We show that a first-order directional derivative of task A's outputs along task B's vector can predict interference, but only when evaluated at the appropriate model. At the receiver model, this derivative forecasts nearby merges per task and per example, halves the accuracy error of a no-change baseline, and identifies most examples whose predictions flip. For full multi-task merges, a single evaluation is insufficient; two evaluations along the merge path are needed. At the shared pretrained backbone, however, the derivative is nearly orthogonal to realized changes in our vision experiments and only partly predictive in LoRA language-model pilots. Controls link this failure to displacement along the receiver's own task vector, not simply parameter distance; the mismatch appears within the first few dozen fine-tuning steps. Freezing receiver-local responses produces a surrogate affine in the merge coefficients. We use it to construct LR-Merge, a label-free correction that optimizes coefficients through convex teacher matching on unlabeled probes, without backpropagation through merged networks. LR-Merge improves all tested closed-form, isotropic, and sparsified base mergers, with gains generally increasing as more tasks are merged. Using pretrained rather than receiver-local responses instead degrades the base merge. LR-Merge matches exact teacher matching through a merged network, which takes roughly three times longer to converge and performs similarly at an equal compute budget. Once built, the surrogate answers new coefficient and task-subset queries in seconds.

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [Infer3D: Inverse Generative Modeling Enables Robust 3D Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25061v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25061

**作者：** Amani Kiruga、Mark Hamilton、Joshua B. Tenenbaum、William T. Freeman、Vincent Sitzmann、Yilun Du、Ayush Tewari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25061v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25061v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Infer3D，通过反演生成模型的潜变量使其匹配单目输入，并用冻结的 3D 上采样网络约束优化空间，以提升分布外场景的 3D 重建；同时引入按样本歧义程度分配测试时计算的自适应推理策略。

**相关性简评：** 其依据输入不确定性路由并增配测试时计算的机制，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 决策与动态计算预算提供参照；潜变量反演优化过程也可供核查 latent 状态是否承载必要几何证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Infer3D: Inverse Generative Modeling Enables Robust 3D Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Humans can understand the 3D structure of scenes even from highly unfamiliar or extreme viewpoints. Cognitive science suggests this generalization stems from a top-down generative inference strategy. In contrast, state-of-the-art 3D reconstruction neural networks typically rely on amortized, feedforward inference. While fast, this pattern-matching approach degrades when faced with out-of-distribution (OOD) scene conditions unseen during training. Inspired by the generative nature of human vision, we propose Infer3D, an inverse generative modeling framework that recovers the latent variables of a generative model to best match the monocular input. To prevent degenerate flat outputs, we couple the generative image prior with a frozen 3D lifting network that constrains the optimization space to a valid, multi-view-consistent 3D manifold. Across a variety of controlled synthetic and real-world tasks, Infer3D demonstrates superior OOD reconstruction accuracy compared to feedforward networks trained on equal or larger datasets. Furthermore, mirroring how human perception requires more time to process confusing stimuli, we introduce an adaptive inference strategy that expends more test-time compute on ambiguous OOD examples while efficiently routing in-distribution inputs. Project website: https://amanikiruga.github.io/infer3d-site/

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 12 figures, 15 tables. Includes references and appendices. Project page: https://amanikiruga.github.io/infer3d-site/

</details>

<a id="paper-52"></a>

### 52. [DSFlow: A Neuro-Symbolic Data Workflow Agent with Statistical Diagnostic Guardrails](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25111v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25111

**作者：** Junfeng Nie、Xiaohui Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25111v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25111v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DSFlow，一个基于 LangGraph 的神经-符号数据分析智能体，将 LLM 选择的可复用技能与确定性统计学诊断护栏结合，由符号验证决定接受方法、要求澄清与重规划或强制安全回退，并用回归与聚类案例展示决策记录。

**相关性简评：** 其符号护栏在「采纳／重规划／回退」间做门控决策，可能为 LVR 中的答案验证、贡献分配与停止—重规划控制提供可供核查的线索；但该工作面向表格数据流程，无视觉输入或 latent 状态，联系属于机制类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DSFlow: A Neuro-Symbolic Data Workflow Agent with Statistical Diagnostic Guardrails

**abstract/A1：** Data science workflows often require tools that presume non-trivial technical knowledge. Large language models (LLMs) reduce this barrier, but a gap remains between flexible general-purpose agents and statistically controlled pipelines. We introduce DSFlow, a LangGraph-based neuro-symbolic data-agent platform that combines reusable, LLM-selected skills with deterministic diagnostic guardrails. Skills provide extensible task knowledge. Symbolic validation can accept a proposed method, request clarification or replanning, override an unsuitable method, or supply a safe fallback. Regression and clustering case studies illustrate how DSFlow uses diagnostic evidence to correct method choices and documents those decisions in auditable reports.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 8 figures. Presented as a poster at AIAS+ 2026, San Francisco, California, USA

</details>

<a id="paper-53"></a>

### 53. [Do Deep Stereo Models Learn Shape Priors?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25113v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25113

**作者：** Linbo Tang、Aneel Damaraju、Todd Zickler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25113v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25113v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建形状高度受限的合成双目数据集，检验多种先进立体匹配模型在局部图像与视差线索薄弱时是否仍产生符合形状先验的边界，发现即使从头训练仍存在系统性边界误差，并提出联合预测视差与表示物体边界距离场的生成式立体模型以改善边界定位。

**相关性简评：** 其中「弱局部证据下边界与全局几何结构」的诊断，以及用距离场作为辅助几何表示的联合预测，可能为 LVR 中几何与任务必要信息如何保留在内部状态、以及辅助监督提供可供核查的线索，但该文不涉及多模态推理或 latent 推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do Deep Stereo Models Learn Shape Priors?

**abstract/A1：** Localizing object boundaries is important for embodied visual systems, since small boundary errors can change the inferred geometry of physical surfaces. We study whether deep stereo models produce boundaries consistent with simple shape priors when local image and disparity cues are weak. We create a synthetic stereo dataset consisting of objects drawn from a highly constrained family of shapes, with randomized depth, slant, color and texture. Despite the simplicity of this shape space, we find that several state-of-the-art stereo models retain systematic boundary errors, even when finetuned or trained from scratch on the dataset. We then introduce a simple generative stereo model that jointly predicts disparity and a distance field representing object boundaries. The model improves boundary localization and produces contours that adhere more closely to the underlying shape space. These results expose limitations in the boundary predictions of current stereo architectures when local evidence is weak, and motivate further study of whether generative models can better combine regional grouping evidence with global geometric structure.

</details>

<a id="paper-54"></a>

### 54. [Fast and Memory-Light Evaluation of Density-Based Clusterings with the DISCO Index in R](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25114v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25114

**作者：** Amin Entezari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.CO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25114v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25114v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 介绍 R 包 discoCVI（0.2.0）：将密度聚类内部效度指标 DISCO 改为在互可达最小生成树的单链接合并树上精确计算所需全部量，使内存随点数线性增长、时间复杂度仍为平方级，并修正单点簇评分；基准显示显著提速降内存。

**相关性简评：** 不涉及视觉或多模态推理；但其用合并树替代 n×n 全对相似度矩阵做精确计算的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 相似度/缓存的存储与计算成本，其对噪声标签的评分也可能类比「是否给出有效证据或弃权」的评价方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fast and Memory-Light Evaluation of Density-Based Clusterings with the DISCO Index in R

**abstract/A1：** DISCO is a recent internal cluster validity index for density-based clustering that, unlike earlier indices, also evaluates the quality of noise labels. The discoCVI package makes DISCO available in R. This article describes the design of the package and a typical workflow, in which DISCO is used to compare clusterings and to choose the parameters of HDBSCAN without ground truth, followed by the implementation introduced in version 0.2.0. DISCO is defined through density-connectivity distances between all pairs of points, so direct implementations store several n-by-n matrices. We show that every quantity DISCO needs can be read off the single-linkage merge tree of the mutual-reachability minimum spanning tree, so that the index can be computed exactly with memory that grows linearly with the number of points, while the running time remains quadratic as in the original definition. The implementation is tested against the previous version, agrees with the original Python implementation to within $3 \\times 10^{-13}$, and corrects the score of singleton clusters to match the published definition. In benchmarks, discoCVI is 23 to 138 times faster than its previous version for 500 to 4,000 points and 3 to 9 times faster than the Python reference for 500 to 8,000 points, using a fraction of the memory of both, and it scores 100,000 points in about 50 seconds with less than 200 MB of memory.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 4 figures. R package discoCVI available on CRAN

</details>

<a id="paper-55"></a>

### 55. [TALD: Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement for Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25126v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25126

**作者：** Hui Li、Pengfei Yang、Xinyi Feng、Junhong Liu、Tianyang Zheng、Hui Zeng、Le Dong、Kaibing Zhang、Mengting Zhao、Quan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25126v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25126v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TALD 两阶段跨模态蒸馏框架，在类别对齐的 logit 空间中将教师与目标模态参考学生的 logit 分解为模态通用与模态特有分量，并用样本自适应调制器调节分解约束与融合权重以缓解负迁移。

**相关性简评：** 其按模态拆分表示并做样本自适应融合的思路，可能为 LVR 中区分任务通用信息与视觉特有细节、筛选视觉证据提供可核查线索；但该工作目标是跨模态蒸馏，并非推理期 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TALD: Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement for Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Cross-modal knowledge distillation can incur negative transfer when source-modality supervision conflicts with the target modality's discriminative characteristics. We propose TALD, a two-stage framework for Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement in class-aligned logit space. Guided by task-dependent self-benefiting and cross-modal exclusivity criteria, a Residual Disentangler decomposes logits from a teacher and an independently trained target-modality reference student into modality-general and modality-specific components. A Sample-Adaptive Modulator regulates decomposition constraints and sample-specific fusion weights. The learned modality-general components are then adaptively fused to supervise the final student. Across five benchmarks spanning vision, audio, and text, TALD achieves the largest mean accuracy gain and highest positive-transfer frequency among the compared methods. Across ten transfer directions with five runs each, TALD improves over training without distillation in 84% of evaluations, compared with at most 70% for the baselines, while offering a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. We plan to release the code.

</details>

<a id="paper-56"></a>

### 56. [Temporal Extrapolation of Elastic Seismic Wave Propagation in Layered Media Using Neural Operators](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25130v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25130

**作者：** Dibyajyoti Nayak、Yuze Pu、Kami Mohammadi、Somdatta Goswami

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.geo-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25130v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25130v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究层状弹性介质中地震速度场的时间外推：TI-DeepONet 内嵌四阶 Runge-Kutta 推进速度场，FNO 学习空间残差修正，二者以「预测-校正」方式联合训练并自回归 rollout，在应力条件下外推到训练区间两倍时长。

**相关性简评：** 其内嵌积分器＋残差修正的自回归状态推进与超训练时长泛化，可供核查 LVR 多步 latent 更新的误差累积和长度泛化，但跨领域迁移仅为可能线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Temporal Extrapolation of Elastic Seismic Wave Propagation in Layered Media Using Neural Operators

**abstract/A1：** Temporal prediction of seismic velocity fields beyond the time interval represented in training remains challenging for neural operator models. This study develops a coupled neural operator framework for stress-conditioned temporal prediction in layered elastic media. A time-integrator-embedded Deep Operator Network (TI-DeepONet) advances the velocity field through an embedded fourth-order Runge-Kutta (RK4) scheme, while a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) learns a residual correction to the predicted spatial field. The two components are trained jointly in a predictor-corrector configuration. Prediction is initialized with reference velocity fields at the first two time levels and proceeds autoregressively, with reference stress fields and prescribed excitation supplied throughout rollout, including beyond the training interval. Four cases consider horizontally polarized shear (SH) waves in four-layer soil columns with fixed total depths of 50 and 280 m, excited by third-order Gaussian-derivative (DOG3) wavelets with central frequencies between 0.5 and 6 Hz. Layer thicknesses and shear-wave velocities vary across profiles. Evaluations on test profiles extend beyond the training interval by up to twice its duration. The predictions reproduce principal velocity arrivals and depth-dependent response patterns, with accuracy varying across profiles. Across the four cases, mean peak-normalized root-mean-square errors (NRMSEs) range from 0.53% to 2.31% over the full evaluation windows and from 0.61% to 2.82% over the extrapolation intervals. Both metrics use each profile's peak absolute reference velocity over the full evaluation window as the normalizer. These results demonstrate temporal velocity prediction under reference-stress conditioning for the tested configurations; autonomous continuation of the coupled velocity-stress wavefield remains to be established.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages including appendix, 14 figures, D. Nayak and Y. Pu contributed equally to this work

</details>

<a id="paper-57"></a>

### 57. [Calibrated Decision Rewards: Training Language Models with a Judge That Knows How Sure It Is](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25135v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25135

**作者：** Rudransh Agnihotri、Manasvi Kapoor

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25135v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25135v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 RLCDR：把 LLM 改造成对「答案是否正确」「首个错误步骤是哪一步」这类问题给出概率分布的决策模型，经选项顺序去偏与约百条标注校准后，作为无参考答案的 RL 奖励，在 Qwen3-1.7B 上用 GRPO 训练数学推理，并标出该奖励在分布偏移下过度自信的失效边界。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本的奖励校准工作，但「单次前向读出首错步骤概率并作为密集训练信号」可能为 latent 推理轨迹的步骤级贡献分配与训练监督提供线索，其校准与 reward hacking 诊断也值得核查能否迁移到多模态判据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Calibrated Decision Rewards: Training Language Models with a Judge That Knows How Sure It Is

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) can only teach a language model what its reward can recognize. Answer checkers are exact but need a reference answer; learned reward models and LLM judges work everywhere, but their scores are not probabilities. We study RLCDR (Reinforcement Learning from Calibrated Decision Rewards). An LLM converted into a decision model answers a typed question about a response ("is the final answer correct?", "which step is the first error?") with a probability distribution, read in one forward pass, debiased over option orders and calibrated with about a hundred labels. Training Qwen3-1.7B with GRPO on MATH, this reference-free reward raises MATH500 accuracy from 72.2% to 76.8% (+4.65 points, p &lt; 1e-4), matching training on the answer key (76.9%), while a random reward gains only +0.65. The gain grows with difficulty, from 44.8% to 51.7% on the hardest level. On the policy's own changing samples the reward stays calibrated: its expected calibration error is half that of a softmax judge on the same 27B backbone (0.074 vs. 0.141), and it shows no sign of reward hacking. Without step training, the same model finds the first wrong step better than a dedicated 7B process reward model (ProcessBench F1 0.772 vs. 0.722, 20/20 splits). We also map where the approach stops being enough: trained only on problems the base model mostly fails, the reference-free reward still improves the policy (+1.9 MATH500 points, p = 0.03) but trails the answer key, because its probabilities become overconfident on that distribution. We state six hypotheses in advance and test each with a control designed to falsify it.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 12 figures, 13 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-58"></a>

### 58. [Separating Interpretability and Reconstruction in Sparse Autoencoders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25165v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25165

**作者：** Anh Tien Nguyen、Dimitris Samaras、Mahdi S. Hosseini

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25165v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25165v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DualSAE，将标准 SAE 作为稀疏分支学习主要语义的稀疏码，并增加可插拔的残差分支建模被忽略的非语义信息，配合去相关损失与两阶段训练，在视觉与语言数据上同时提升重建保真度与单义性。

**相关性简评：** 其「语义 / 非语义」分支解耦与去相关约束，可能为分析 latent 推理状态中哪些视觉与任务相关信息被保留、哪些仅用于重建提供探查工具，但论文未涉及 VLM 推理，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Separating Interpretability and Reconstruction in Sparse Autoencoders

**abstract/A1：** Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are effective tools for mechanistic interpretability by decomposing neural representations into sparse and interpretable features. However, a single sparse representation in traditional SAEs is responsible for both reconstruction fidelity and interpretability, which are not guaranteed to align. To separate these objectives, we propose DualSAE, a modular framework that augments standard SAEs with a plug-in Residual Branch. A standard SAE serves as the Sparse Branch, learning a sparse code that contains the dominant semantics of the input. The added Residual Branch models the non-semantic information omitted by the Sparse Branch. We ensure that the contributions of the two branches towards the overall reconstruction have minimal overlap through a decorrelation loss. We also adopt a two-stage training strategy that first stabilizes sparse representation learning before coupling with the Residual Branch. Across diverse encoder-SAE configurations on two vision datasets and two language corpora, DualSAE achieves mean relative improvements of approximately 29% in reconstruction fidelity and 34% in interpretability (measured by monosemanticity score). Moreover, with more interpretable representations, DualSAE yields consistent performance gains in the downstream applications of topic modeling, hypothesis generation, and LLM steering. Qualitative analysis further suggests that the Sparse Branch indeed preserves dominant semantic content, whereas the Residual Branch captures non-semantic information to improve reconstruction fidelity.

**comment/C1：** Code: https://github.com/AtlasAnalyticsLab/DualSAE

</details>

<a id="paper-59"></a>

### 59. [CoRelate: Coordinated Human-Object Motion Synthesis through Interaction Relations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25166v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25166

**作者：** Zhiwen Qiu、Daisy Lin、Diyu Zhou、Jialin Wu、Rosita Ji、Saleh Kalantari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25166v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25166v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoRelate，用一个扩散框架协调生成全身人-物运动：以「手-部件关系」（参与手、部件相对参考位置、参与时间区间）作为生成条件与交互实现程度的度量，并在每个去噪步由联合训练的时间精化器融合关系偏差、任务残差与当前预测的运动特征统一更新人和物体轨迹，生成后再对局部片段做运动修正以缓解残余接触失败。

**相关性简评：** 其把任务必要的关系/接触约束写成显式条件，并在生成过程中按偏差反馈逐步更新状态、事后针对失败片段做约束保持的局部纠正，可能为 LVR 的迭代状态更新、证据或关系保真校验与停止/纠正机制提供线索，可供核查该反馈如何进入内部状态而非仅作后处理打分。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoRelate: Coordinated Human-Object Motion Synthesis through Interaction Relations

**abstract/A1：** Human-object motion synthesis requires coordinated human participation as objects move toward specified goals. However, reaching object targets does not ensure that contact is established and maintained, while adjustments that improve contact may interfere with task completion. We introduce CoRelate, a diffusion framework that coordinates full-body human-object motion through explicit interaction relations and task feedback for rigid and articulated objects. We represent intended participation through hand-part relations that specify the participating hand, its part-relative reference position, and participation interval. These relations serve both as generation conditions and as references for measuring interaction realization. At each denoising step, a temporal refiner trained jointly with the generator combines relation deviations, task residuals, and motion features from the current prediction to jointly update human and object motion across the sequence. After generation, temporal motion correction addresses remaining contact failures by optimizing local motion segments while preserving object trajectories and protected human motion. Experiments demonstrate improved object-goal satisfaction, contact F1, and complete interaction success over the evaluated baselines. Ablation studies support the benefits of relation conditioning and the complementary roles of task and relation feedback.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-60"></a>

### 60. [Game-Theoretic Data Allocation for Expert Specialization and Routing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25167v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25167

**作者：** Mark Lovett、Denis Lim、Soroush Vosoughi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25167v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25167v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 InflEns：在固定 prompt-trait 空间中用位置博弈同时决定 LoRA 专家的训练数据分配与推理路由，无需训练显式 gate；在七个安全基准组成的语料上，最高份额专家在约 90% 的 prompt 上取得最低留出 NLL，并可通过追溯单个专家重训将 HarmBench 攻击成功率从 0.51 降至 0.02。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理无直接关系，但其「由数据分配推导固定可解释路由、按份额选择专家」的做法，可能为 LVR 中并行推理路径/模块的动态选择与贡献归属提供可核查线索，尤其是否可迁移到视觉编码器尚待确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Game-Theoretic Data Allocation for Expert Specialization and Routing

**abstract/A1：** Can the allocation used to train experts also predict which expert will perform best on a prompt? Such a connection would make the intended division of labor inspectable before training and allow the allocation itself to guide inference. We introduce InflEns, a mixture of LoRA experts whose training weights and inference routes come from the same location game in a fixed prompt-trait space. Players choose positions to maximize their expected shares of the prompt distribution, without using expert losses. We utilize the game's kernel-general theoretical criterion for when co-located positions become locally unstable to separation. We evaluate InflEns on a corpus assembled from seven safety-related benchmarks, which demonstrates that the highest-share expert has the lowest held-out response negative log-likelihood (NLL) on approximately 90% of prompts across four kernels under a sharpened simplex representation, compared with approximately 15% after permuting allocations across prompts. We demonstrate that the method performs comparably to published learned gates without having to train an explicit gate on the experts. Because the router is fixed, we can trace harmful behavior to a single expert and retrain only that expert, which lowers HarmBench attack success from 0.51 to 0.02 while keeping instruction following. The game also gives us several controls over how the experts specialize, including the kernel choice and its parameters, the trait-space geometry, and the number of experts. Importantly, InflEns is a general framework that can be extended to any encoder and data distribution.

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [What a Calibrated Probability Hides: Belief-Level Post-Training for Decision Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25168v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25168

**作者：** Xinye Li、Zirui Song、Huaxing Liu、Xiang Wang、Shuai Li、Zixiang Xu、Yinjun Liu、Zhewen Zhang、Xiaojun Chang、Xiuying Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25168v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25168v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Belief-Grouped Policy Optimization（BGPO）：策略先生成一组潜在信念及其占比，并为每个信念预测选择分布；训练时用贝叶斯规则估计观测选择由各信念生成的可能性来加权奖励，并以留出选择筛选稀有信念，从而区分人群分歧与个体不确定性。

**相关性简评：** 其“多潜在信念假设＋证据加权贡献＋留出验证筛选”的机制，可能为 LVR 的多 latent 假设训练、贡献分配及证据获取与停止决策提供可核查线索，但论文未直接研究视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What a Calibrated Probability Hides: Belief-Level Post-Training for Decision Models

**abstract/A1：** A calibrated decision model can predict how often each answer is chosen. The same prediction can reflect disagreement among people who are confident in their choices or uncertainty among people who share a belief. Disagreement calls for identifying whose perspective applies, while uncertainty calls for gathering more evidence. Standard post-training scores a single prediction against one observed choice at a time, learning the pooled distribution without distinguishing these two sources of uncertainty. We introduce Belief-Grouped Policy Optimization (BGPO). The policy proposes a list of beliefs, estimates their prevalence, and predicts a choice distribution for each. During training, BGPO uses Bayes' rule to estimate how likely each belief was to generate an observed choice. These estimates weight the choice's contribution to each belief's log-score reward. To discover rare beliefs, the policy also proposes lists after seeing a few observed choices and retains them only if they predict held-out choices well. At inference, the policy uses only the question and combines the belief-specific distributions by prevalence to produce a calibrated aggregate prediction. On benchmarks covering opinions, conversation safety, emotions elicited by artwork, and photo aesthetics, BGPO achieves the lowest conditional, worst-group, and overall Jensen-Shannon divergence across model initializations. It improves conditional divergence on both text and image tasks. On a synthetic image task with known beliefs, BGPO recovers the underlying groups more accurately than baselines trained with belief labels.

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [A Verifier from the Past: Test-Time Compute for Time Series Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25171v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25171

**作者：** Arjun Choudhry、Aditya Narayanan、Raghav Mallampalli、Po-han Li、Sai Shankar Narasimhan、Raimi Shah、B. Aditya Prakash、Shubhankar Agarwal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25171v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25171v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 BGAA，一种用于时间序列基础模型的 test-time compute 算法：以滚动起点回测在序列自身历史上构造因果验证器，为上下文的结构化视图打分，剔除劣于原始上下文的视图并平均幸存者的预测分布，并在 GIFT-Eval、fev-bench、BOOM 上给出稳定增益及视图选择的空间。

**相关性简评：** 「不许用未来信息时以自身历史充当验证器来筛选候选」以及仅按数据集选视图远远优于逐窗口选视图的差距分析，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、候选状态验证与动态停止提供可核查的方法线索；但对象是时间序列输出集成，并非 latent 视觉推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Verifier from the Past: Test-Time Compute for Time Series Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Test-time compute (TTC) improves language models by spending additional inference compute to generate, verify, and select among candidate outputs, but this recipe has not transferred directly to time series forecasting. A language model can verify a proof or vote across samples, but a forecaster must be causal; it cannot use future information to score a candidate. We propose Backtest-Gated Augmentation Averaging (BGAA), a TTC algorithm for time series forecasting that builds a causal verifier from the series's own history, using rolling-origin backtests to score structured views of the context, discard those that underperform the untransformed context, and average the survivors' predictive distributions. BGAA improves every tested TSFM-benchmark combination, reducing MASE by 0.5-1.0% on GIFT-Eval, 1.3-5.0% on fev-bench, and 1.7-5.9% on BOOM, where a 313M model with TTC outperforms the 1B and 2.5B models in its family. We further show that useful views have structured, model-dependent effects consistent with a prior-transport interpretation. On the 53 cross-fittable GIFT-Eval configs, the deployed policy gains only 0.4-1.3% CRPS, versus 1.4-4.1% when choosing the best view for each dataset configuration and 17-19% when choosing the best view separately for each test window. This gap suggests that a major remaining challenge is window-level view selection.

**comment/C1：** 43 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [PDE-State Conditioning for Learned Optimization Using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25185v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25185

**作者：** Farzana Yasmin、Ricardo Vilalta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25185v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25185v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究学习式 PINN 优化器应观测哪些 PDE 层级信息以及如何据此调节更新，构造 11 维状态描述（算子结构、残差几何、短期更新响应），并通过有界 FiLM 调制元学习优化器；设计比较显示紧凑残差几何状态 G2 加有界 FiLM 最优，在多个 PDE 上优于 Adam 与无条件学习优化器。

**相关性简评：** 其「紧凑状态摘要 + 有界特征级调制」及对状态内容的设计对比，可能可供核查 LVR 中由内部状态筛选与调节 latent 更新这一问题；但属科学计算优化领域，与视觉推理仅有间接方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PDE-State Conditioning for Learned Optimization Using Physics-Informed Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Physics-informed neural network (PINN) optimization depends on global properties of the underlying PDE and how the physics residual evolves across the domain. Standard optimizers such as Adam use fixed gradient-based statistics, while learned PINN optimizers replace the fixed rule with a learned update built from local parameters and optimization-history features. However, none explicitly use PDE-level information. We study two coupled design questions: what PDE-level information should a learned optimizer observe, and how should that information influence its update? We introduce an 11-dimensional PDE state-capturing operator structure, residual geometry, and short-horizon update response, and use it to condition a structured meta-learned optimizer through bounded feature-wise modulation. Repeated design studies identify the compact residual-geometry state G2 and bounded FiLM as the strongest overall choices among those tested. The resulting PDE-State-conditioned optimizer improves all evaluation metrics (relative-L2, L1, solution MSE, and physics MSE) over Adam and the unconditioned learned optimizer on Linear Advection, KdV, and Burgers.

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [When AI Analysts Disagree: Agentic Multiverse Analysis and the Robustness of Empirical Research](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25198v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25198

**作者：** Ruijiang Gao、Steven Chong Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25198v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25198v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 agentic multiverse analysis 框架：让多个 AI 编码 agent 枚举一篇实证论文可能的分岔选择空间，按效度评分表逐项校验后执行规格网格，并在职责分离下相互交叉审查，以此评估结论对分析选择的稳健性。

**相关性简评：** 其「枚举假设空间—评分验证—交叉审查」的多 agent 协议可供核查是否能迁移到 LVR 多条 latent 假设的筛选与验证；agent 分歧在模仿示例后坍缩的现象，也可能为 latent 假设多样性坍缩提供类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When AI Analysts Disagree: Agentic Multiverse Analysis and the Robustness of Empirical Research

**abstract/A1：** Every empirical analysis on observational data rests on dozens of defensible analytical choices ("forks") that its readers cannot see. We develop an agentic multiverse analysis (AMA) framework that makes a conclusion's sensitivity to those choices visible at scale, and we position it as a decision-support instrument for human experts. A pilot study first deploys 150 autonomous AI coding agents on one dataset and one set of research questions and finds that they disagree through the same defensible methodological choices human analysts make, though over a narrower range. AI peer review leaves their dispersion almost unchanged. Exposure to top-rated exemplar reports then collapses it, largely through imitation rather than analysis. Built on those lessons, AMA has agents enumerate a published paper's possible fork space, score every fork against a validity rubric, execute the validated specification grid, and cross-examine one another's work under strict separation of duties. Applied to ten empirical studies from a leading management journal's replication packages (1,755 specification estimates), the framework reproduces every paper's original estimates, and shows that some conclusions hold across every specification the assessment validates, while others are sensitive to individual analytical choices that the published texts do not discuss. The case studies suggest our AMA-based measures can serve as robustness proxies for empirical findings. AI makes multiverse analysis practical, where it once required resources few research teams can afford: our exercises take hours of agent time and tens of dollars per paper. We discuss the implications for replication policy and for AI-assisted peer review in information systems.

**comment/C1：** This paper subsumes arXiv:2603.16744. 45 pages, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [Identity World Model: Recognizing People in an Imagined Latent World](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25204v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25204

**作者：** Dingqiang Ye、Kartik Narayan、Vishal M. Patel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25204v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25204v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 IDWorld 身份世界模型：用单次前向在共享规范条件（同一服装、固定视角、裸轮廓等）下预测人物的反事实 latent 特征，不渲染像素，并对各条件的想象结果按 probe-gallery 对自适应加权融合，用于全身人物识别，在多个基准上取得领先。

**相关性简评：** 反事实 latent 特征预测加按样本对自适应加权，与「内部状态是否保留任务必要视觉信息、如何抑制外观旁路」有方法学线索，可供核查其作为 latent 状态不变性训练的借鉴价值，但该文属识别任务，不涉及推理生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Identity World Model: Recognizing People in an Imagined Latent World

**abstract/A1：** Whole-body human recognition has advanced rapidly with large vision models. However, existing methods remain fragile under real-world variations, as they tie identity to transient cues such as clothing, texture, pose, or viewpoint. We argue that a robust recognizer should not merely learn to ignore these cues, but imagine how a person would be represented if they changed. To this end, we propose IDWorld, an identity world model: just as a latent world model predicts the features of a scene after an action, IDWorld predicts how a person would be represented under a changed condition. In a single forward pass, IDWorld imagines these counterfactual features under shared canonical conditions, such as the same outfit, a fixed view, or a bare silhouette, without rendering any pixels. Compared with explicit imagination in pixels, this latent imagination keeps nearly the same accuracy while being over 11x faster, at 5.9 ms per frame. Since each imagined condition has its own strengths, IDWorld then fuses them adaptively, weighting each condition for every probe-gallery pair. Extensive experiments show that IDWorld achieves state-of-the-art results on CCPG and CCGR-MINI, outperforms the compared methods on MEVID, and generalizes best to CASIA-B\* and SUSTech1K, with the best mAP on CCVID. These results suggest identity world models offer a simple yet effective path to robust recognition. Code will be released.

</details>

<a id="paper-66"></a>

### 66. [Learning to Read Events for Exact State Tracking with Frozen Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25211v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25211

**作者：** Jeonghoon Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25211v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25211v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在冻结语言模型上训练选择器逐事件读取状态转移，用外部整数寄存器按序组合，并用置信规则标记不确定更新或保留多个候选；在五元素置换乘积、房间交换文档和自由格式 issue 评论上评测精确状态跟踪。

**相关性简评：** 将「读取证据」与「状态组合」外部分离，并保留候选/标记不确定更新，可能为 LVR 的状态更新、证据筛选与错误分配提供可供核查的设计线索，但无视觉输入与 latent 表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Read Events for Exact State Tracking with Frozen Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Long event streams require identifying each change and applying the changes in order. We train a selector on a frozen language model to read each transition. With API-only access, we ask the model to list the transitions. An external integer register composes them in order. A confidence rule can flag uncertain updates or retain several candidates. We study products of permutations of five items, a task that fixed-depth, log-precision transformers cannot solve on all input lengths under the standard conjecture that $\\mathrm{TC}^0 \\ne \\mathrm{NC}^1$. On two-event windows, a 27B model answered 3 of 100 correctly without visible reasoning and 96 of 100 when it generated intermediate steps. A frozen 0.8B model with a trained selector read every event in 299 of 299 windows of 43,687 events each. The same package passed 298 of 299 new windows on a fresh machine, missing the target of 0.99 for the one-sided 95% lower confidence bound. A separate follow-up sized to allow one failure yielded 472 of 473. On 1,000-event room-swap documents, three of ten models solved all five documents by reasoning alone. All seven models tested both ways at this length solved all five with extraction and the register, with no chain of thought requested. With concurrent extraction, median wall-clock time fell from 136.1 to 10.4 s for Sonnet 5.5 and from 313.5 to 28.0 s for GPT-6.1 Sol. At fixed per-token rates, mean cost per document fell by 55% for Sonnet 5.5 and 44% for GPT-6.1 Sol. A regular expression with an executor also solved all 15 fixed-format documents. On 500 free-form issue-tracker comments, a separately adapted 4B reader identified the recorded value in 68.8% of cases, compared with 61.0% for a frequency baseline fitted on the same training projects.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 5 figures. Data and code: https://github.com/jeonghoon-ad/learning-to-read-events

</details>

<a id="paper-67"></a>

### 67. [Some Memories Must Fade: Persistence and Decay of User Memories in Model Parameters](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25235v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25235

**作者：** Sanidhya Vijayvargiya、Rahul Lokesh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25235v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25235v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。
