**内容简述：** 论文构建了一套参数化记忆管理系统：以固定容量、k-hot 秩组合的 LoRA 适配器作为可独立控制时间衰减的记忆基底，再训练轻量控制器决定每条用户事件是否写入、写多深、保留多久，并配套长程用户轨迹数据集，考察信息有效期内回忆、过期后弃答。

**相关性简评：** 其「学到的控制器决定写入深度与保留时长、过期即弃答」可供核查是否能借鉴为 LVR 中潜状态证据的持久/淘汰与停止决策机制，但当前文本侧、面向用户事实记忆，跨模态与推理期状态的联系尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Some Memories Must Fade: Persistence and Decay of User Memories in Model Parameters

**abstract/A1：** Parametric memory allows Large Language Models to internalize knowledge directly into their weights, offering a potentially powerful alternative to retrieval-based memory (RAG). However, existing approaches to parametric memory are difficult to deploy as they largely mirror the Continual Learning (CL) objective of retaining information indefinitely. This is neither sustainable nor sufficient for a memory system, which must govern knowledge across its lifecycle by retaining stable facts, reinforcing recurring observations, updating changed beliefs, and allowing transient information to fade. We propose a system for parametric memory management consisting of a memory substrate (handling information storage and forgetting) and a learned policy (what to remember, how deeply, and for how long). Our substrate is a fixed-capacity LoRA adapter that consists of k-hot rank combinations which store facts without catastrophic interference and maintain an independently controlled temporal decay, enabling outdated information to fade while long-term facts persist. On this substrate, we train a lightweight controller that governs each incoming user event to ensure the memory system reflects the user's true, updated belief state. To evaluate how memory systems manage user memory, we introduce a controlled dataset of long-horizon user trajectories with varying persistence of facts based on changing belief states and user-behavioral shifts, and probe queries that test recall while information is valid and abstention after it expires. Our policy learns to effectively manage user memory over its entire lifecycle, balancing recall and abstention to achieve 86% of oracle performance, unlike CL and RAG baselines that primarily default to one of the two behaviors. By providing the first system to manage memory directly in model weights, this work establishes a foundation for long-horizon parametric memory.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-68"></a>

### 68. [Beyond Restoration: Content-Degradation Factor Unfolding for Real-World Concealed Object Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25239v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25239

**作者：** Rihan Zhang、Fengyang Xiao、Yuhan Chen、Sina Farsiu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25239v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25239v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Factor Unfolding Network，将 Retinex 扩展为「内容-退化」因子分解：以内容因子作为分割语义证据的唯一来源，退化因子生成空间可靠度图校准该证据，并通过统一能量的交替更新实现无需干净图像监督的粗到精分割。

**相关性简评：** 其「内容因子独占语义证据、退化因子只输出可靠度权重」的非对称分工与可靠性加权线索，可能对应 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与无关变化抑制，可供核查能否迁移为 latent 状态中证据可靠度的门控机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Restoration: Content-Degradation Factor Unfolding for Real-World Concealed Object Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Reliable visual perception under degradation requires distinguishing task-relevant content from degradation-induced variations, especially in concealed object segmentation (COS), where foreground cues are inherently weak. The central challenge is not merely improving visibility, but identifying reliable content evidence amid degradation-induced variations. RGB-space segmentation often entangles content and degradation cues, while restoration-based methods primarily focus on image recovery and task interaction. This motivates explicit factorization to distinguish content evidence from degradation effects and assess its reliability. Retinex provides a spatially aligned basis for this separation but remains illumination-centric. We therefore extend it to content-degradation formation with task-asymmetric factor roles. The Factor Unfolding Network (FUN) uses the content factor R as the sole source of direct semantic evidence for segmentation, while the degradation factor L captures degradation-related variations and generates a spatial reliability map to calibrate this evidence. A unified energy couples observation formation with reliability-weighted foreground-background separation, and its alternating updates yield a coarse-to-fine network without clean-image supervision. In this way, FUN factorizes degradation for perception rather than removing it. Extensive experiments across clean and degraded conditions and diverse segmentation tasks demonstrate robust performance and generalization. Code will be released.

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [Branch Caching After Step Distillation in Four- and Eight-Step Image Generators](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25248v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25248

**作者：** Mingyu Liang、Dingkun Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25248v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25248v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究少步蒸馏扩散生成器中跨去噪步骤复用 attention 与 MLP 评估的固定分支缓存，并用同一初始噪声下完整轨迹与缓存轨迹的差值给出按分支类型和复用位置标定的标量校正，在 Qwen-Image-Lightning 与 Hyper-FLUX 上报告加速比与图像保真度。

**相关性简评：** 「复用陈旧中间状态 + 用参考轨迹校正误差」的思路可能可供核查多步 latent 推理中的状态缓存与计算节省，但其对象是图像生成质量而非推理证据使用，迁移性尚待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Branch Caching After Step Distillation in Four- and Eight-Step Image Generators

**abstract/A1：** Can a distilled image generator save time by reusing features between its few remaining denoising steps? We study a fixed branch cache that replaces selected attention and MLP evaluations while preserving the student's noise schedule. One full trajectory and one raw-cache trajectory from the same initial noise supply a scalar correction for each branch type and reuse position. At $1024^2$, one internal reuse reduces four-step Qwen-Image-Lightning denoising time from 1.279 to 0.977 seconds. Three reuses yield $1.51\\times$ speedup on eight-step Lightning and $1.53\\times$ on eight-step Hyper-FLUX. We evaluate image quality on all 200 DrawBench prompts. With the reuse positions held fixed, correction improves mean same-seed image fidelity over raw reuse by 0.66 and 0.87 dB on the two Lightning students. On Hyper-FLUX the PSNR difference is 0.01 dB, while LPIPS improves from 0.1625 to 0.1546. These results measure the effect of branch caching and correction while retaining each student's original sampling grid.

</details>

<a id="paper-70"></a>

### 70. [Learning to Verify: Signature-Guided Training for Adaptive Adversarial Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25271v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25271

**作者：** Arash Vashagh、Roozbeh Razavi-Far

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25271v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25271v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在固定原分类器的基础上单独训练一个验证器，利用类别的内部激活「signature」生成既误导分类器、又挑战验证器且内部近似目标类的对抗样本，以提升自适应攻击下的检测鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 以独立验证器读取内部激活来判断原模型输出是否可靠，与 LVR 中核查答案是否真正依赖有效视觉证据的问题可能有共通之处，但该文限于图像分类对抗检测，迁移到多模态推理状态的可检验性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Verify: Signature-Guided Training for Adaptive Adversarial Detection

**abstract/A1：** Adversarial example detectors often fail when attackers explicitly attempt to bypass them. We propose a dual-model approach that keeps the original classifier fixed and trains a separate verifier to assess its predictions. During training, we identify patterns of internal neural network activations that distinguish genuine examples of each class from adversarial examples targeting that class. These patterns, called activation signatures, guide the generation of challenging adversarial examples that mislead the classifier, challenge the verifier, and internally resemble the target class. The verifier learns to recognize these examples while preserving its performance on clean inputs through knowledge distillation. At inference, the verifier evaluates the classifier's prediction without requiring activation signatures. We evaluate the approach on publicly available datasets against several adaptive attacks, including attacks unseen during training. The proposed verifier substantially outperforms directly attacked confidence-based and feature-based detectors under the evaluated L-infinity attacks. Ablation studies show that adaptive verifier training is the main source of this improvement, while signature guidance primarily benefits clean accuracy. Additional L2 evaluations reveal limitations in robustness across perturbation types.

</details>

<a id="paper-71"></a>

### 71. [DS3M: Deep structured state space models for nonlinear system identification on modern hardware](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25274v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25274

**作者：** Marco Roschkowski、Hannes Gernandt

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25274v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25274v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DS3M，将紧凑的非线性动力学组件以并行和串联方式组合，并用 Triton 自定义 GPU kernel 降低串行状态评估的启动与内存开销，在非线性系统辨识任务上比较精度与训练时间。

**相关性简评：** 串行状态更新加自定义 kernel 的构造，可能为 LVR 中循环/序列 latent 状态的训练与推理成本、组合式状态更新设计提供可核查的方法线索；但无视觉或多模态推理验证，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DS3M: Deep structured state space models for nonlinear system identification on modern hardware

**abstract/A1：** Neural state-space identification learns dynamical models from measured inputs and outputs. SUBNET combines this task with an encoder that estimates initial states from short measurement histories. Its continuous-time version requires many successive neural-network evaluations during numerical integration, resulting in slow training speed which is a major limitation for practical use cases. We propose deep structured state space models (DS3M), which combine compact nonlinear dynamical components in parallel and in series. Custom GPU kernels implemented with Triton reduce the launch and memory overhead of their sequential evaluation. We explain this construction, describe a port-Hamiltonian variant, and present various benchmark comparisons of accuracy and training time.

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [MS-Point: Multi-Modal Hierarchical Spectral Learning for Point Cloud Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25282v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25282

**作者：** Hai-tao Yu、Jun Xia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25282v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25282v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MS-Point 跨模态预训练框架，指出不同局部特征模式经池化后会得到相同描述子并收到相同对齐信号，因此在编码器若干阶段先计算邻域均值与残差，将各分支描述子与图像特征分别对齐，并辅以跨层一致性与自适应融合，在分类、少样本、稠密预测和低标签场景上验证迁移效果。

**相关性简评：** 其对「池化导致局部信息坍缩」的针对性监督线索，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节写入 latent 后被压缩丢失的问题，但任务为点云-图像对齐，是否约束推理期状态仍需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MS-Point: Multi-Modal Hierarchical Spectral Learning for Point Cloud Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Point-image alignment learns useful object representations, but different local feature patterns can produce the same pooled descriptor and receive the same alignment signal. We introduce MS-Point, a cross-modal pretraining framework that directly supervises intermediate point features and their neighborhood responses. At selected encoder stages, we compute neighborhood averages and residuals before pooling and align their branch descriptors with image features. Branch-level objectives align each representation with its visual target, while cross-level consistency and adaptive fusion coordinate information across branches. A common interface of intermediate features and spatial neighborhoods supports both graph encoders and point Transformers. Evaluations across object classification, few-shot adaptation, dense prediction, and low-label scene learning demonstrate the transfer value of this design. Same-backbone comparisons and capacity-matched and computation-matched controls connect these improvements to the structure of supervision. Together, our findings support directly supervising intermediate features and neighborhood variation to learn transferable, geometry-aware representations across modalities.

</details>

<a id="paper-73"></a>

### 73. [Cross-Question Reliable Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25292v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25292

**作者：** Hector G. Rodriguez、Marcus Rohrbach

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25292v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25292v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CARE，用一组问答对在各风险容忍度下共同诱导弃答阈值，并据此奖励模型输出的置信度，使 LLM 置信度具有跨题可比性，从而提升选择性预测覆盖。

**相关性简评：** 其按风险约束选择作答或弃答的机制，可能为 LVR 的 STOP 决策与置信度校准提供核查线索，但纯文本设定与视觉推理状态、证据筛选的联系仍需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross-Question Reliable Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** AI systems operating near the limits of their capabilities can maximize utility while minimizing the risk of error by abstaining or deferring questions they are not confident about to a human or a stronger model. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been post-trained to estimate confidence in their own answers. To achieve this, methods reinforce confidence scores based on the correctness signal to each question-answer pair individually, with rewards that encourage assigning confidence 1 to correct answers and 0 to incorrect answers. However, estimating confidence scores is useful in that it enables selecting only the most confident answers, to maximize the share of solved questions while accommodating the user's risk tolerance. Assigning confidence 1 to correct answers and 0 to incorrect ones is theoretically optimal, but unattainable in practice and detrimental for selective prediction with a non-zero risk tolerance: confidences pushed to 0 and 1 carry no information about how answers compare to one another. We therefore introduce CARE (Cross-question Abstention Reward Estimation), which computes rewards over a set of question-answer pairs: At each of several risk tolerances, the confidences across the set induce an abstention threshold, and each confidence is rewarded for lying on the correct side of it. Since the threshold itself comes from the set, every verbalized confidence is reinforced relative to other questions' confidence, not against its own binary correctness label. On math, CARE increases the coverage at 5% risk (C@5) across MATH-500, GSM8K, and Big-Math-Digits from 0.8% to 42.4%, compared to existing pointwise correctness-matching rewards. When trained on multi-hop Wikipedia QA from HotpotQA, CARE improves C@5 in out-of-distribution general-knowledge, reasoning, and math benchmarks (including CommonsenseQA, GPQA, SimpleQA, and TriviaQA) from 12.3% to 23.6%.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages. Code: https://github.com/multimodal-ai-lab/CARE

</details>

<a id="paper-74"></a>

### 74. [Class-Conditional Conformal Triage for ICAO Face-Image Compliance under Class Imbalance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25342v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25342

**作者：** Mustafa Vedat Yildirim、Inna Skarga-Bandurova

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25342v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25342v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在合成 TONO 人脸图像基准上，把 conformal prediction 作为 BioGaze 合规分析器的「接受/拒绝/重拍」筛选层：不重训分析器，仅为 14 项合规标准各拟合 logistic 分类器，比较边际与 label-conditional（Mondrian）覆盖，并对照常规 abstention 规则。

**相关性简评：** 其按类别校准的 conformal 覆盖与 abstention 规则可供核查能否迁移为 LVR 中按假设或视觉证据类别的停止、重查决策校准；结论提示覆盖率保证与分类性能、额外采样成本之间存在权衡，但与人脸合规任务本身无直接机制联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Class-Conditional Conformal Triage for ICAO Face-Image Compliance under Class Imbalance

**abstract/A1：** Automated ICAO/ISO face-image compliance analysers provide criterion-level pass/fail decisions, yet these verdicts do not quantify uncertainty when deciding whether to accept, reject, or retake a photograph. We investigate conformal prediction as a triage layer for BioGaze, evaluating 14 compliance criteria on the synthetic TONO benchmark. Without retraining the analyser, we fit per-criterion logistic classifiers and compare marginal and label-conditional (Mondrian) conformal prediction with conventional abstention rules using subject-disjoint splits. Marginal conformal prediction achieved approximately 0.90 overall coverage but substantially under-covered violations, with a ten-partition mean of 0.37 for one criterion. The corresponding Mondrian means ranged from 0.89 to 0.92 across criteria. However, most of the reduction in criterion-level false positives came from the logistic layer (0.145 to 0.048), while tuned abstention rules achieved lower false-positive rates than Mondrian at comparable retake rates. Bonferroni correction for joint coverage across 14 criteria required retaking nearly all images. These findings show that class-conditional conformal prediction can achieve near-target empirical coverage for minority violation classes and reveal trade-offs between statistical coverage, classification performance, and retake requirements in multi-criterion face-image compliance assessment.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 8 figures, 4 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-75"></a>

### 75. [A Unified Framework for Image Manipulation Localization Under Multi-Type Weak Supervision](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25371v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25371

**作者：** Songlin Li、Hui Ma、Zhiqing Guo、Dan Ma、Gaobo Yang、Xin Liao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25371v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25371v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 UniIML 教师—学生框架，把多种弱标注映射为统一表示来引导 SAM 生成候选掩码，再按弱标注一致性、SAM 质量分和跨视角稳定性排序并聚合选出最终掩码，据此构造软伪标签做可靠性加权蒸馏。

**相关性简评：** 其多准则一致性排序、留一准则重选与选择频率稳定性，可能为 LVR 中多个 latent 候选假设的筛选与验证可信度估计提供线索，但这是像素级掩码选择，是否可迁移需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Unified Framework for Image Manipulation Localization Under Multi-Type Weak Supervision

**abstract/A1：** Existing weakly supervised image manipulation localization (IML) methods typically rely on annotation-specific training strategies, hindering the joint use of diverse annotations within a single model. This limits the scale of usable training data and constrains further progress. We propose UniIML, a teacher-student framework that maps diverse weak annotations into a unified representation to guide the Segment Anything Model (SAM) in generating multiple candidate masks. However, SAM is designed for general-purpose segmentation and has limited sensitivity to manipulation traces; its predicted quality scores alone may therefore fail to reliably identify the candidate that best delineates the manipulated region. We introduce Ordinal Safe Consensus (OSC), which first filters candidates using trusted weak-annotation constraints encoded in the unified representation. It then ranks the remaining candidates separately by consistency with weak annotations, SAM-predicted quality scores, and cross-view stability, aggregating these rankings for an initial selection. To assess how changes in the evaluation criteria affect candidate selection, OSC repeats ranking and selection while omitting one criterion at a time. The most frequently selected candidate is adopted as the final mask, and its relative selection frequency quantifies selection stability. We construct soft pseudo-labels from the selected candidate's predicted probabilities and estimate pixel-level supervision weights based on selection stability, pixel-level agreement among candidates, and predictive certainty. The student receives direct supervision in regions defined by weak annotations and learns from soft pseudo-labels elsewhere through reliability-weighted distillation, enabling unified training across weak annotation types. Experiments show that UniIML achieves superior average performance compared with state-of-the-art IML methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-76"></a>

### 76. [Beyond Weight Updates: Evolving Agent Harnesses for Domain Specialization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25380v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25380

**作者：** BoHao Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25380v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25380v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 LLM agent 的领域特化从权重更新扩展到「agent harness」（系统提示、工具 schema、技能库、规划脚手架）的演化，用 GEPA 反射式进化优化器在冻结策略下构造 RL 式外层循环，并在 SWE-bench Verified、OfficeBench、Terminal-Bench 上比较权重更新、harness 演化及二者组合的效果。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线无直接关联，但其「不动权重、在外层以文本反射搜索推理脚手架」的做法可能为 LVR 提供借鉴线索：可否用类似外层搜索来配置 latent 推理步数、视觉证据注入位置或停止决策结构，仍需核查其可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Weight Updates: Evolving Agent Harnesses for Domain Specialization

**abstract/A1：** Domain specialization of LLM agents is usually framed as a weight-update problem: collect trajectories, run reinforcement learning (RL) or supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and deploy a new checkpoint. We study a complementary axis: evolving the \\textbf{agent harness}---the system prompt, tool schemas, skill library, and planning scaffolding---while keeping the policy frozen. We cast harness evolution as an RL-style outer loop and instantiate it with GEPA, a reflective evolutionary optimizer. The resulting harness adapters require no GPU training, are loadable per vertical domain, and compose with weight-based RL. Experiments with Codev, a Claude Code-style coding agent, on SWE-bench Verified, OfficeBench, and Terminal-Bench show a consistent pattern: weight updates give the strongest single-path gains, harness evolution recovers most of those gains at a small fraction of the compute, and combining both paths is best overall. We also analyze a failure mode of SFT-based specialization---shortened reasoning chains and increased truncation---and argue that harness adapters avoid it by construction. Our results position harness evolution as a practical, non-destructive complement to weight updates for vertical agents.

</details>

<a id="paper-77"></a>

### 77. [Text Lives in the Guidance: Preventing Silent Text Rewriting in Cached Diffusion Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25382v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25382

**作者：** Kun Zhou、Wenmian Yang、Zhensheng Wang、Weijia Jia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25382v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25382v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文发现扩散 Transformer 的特征缓存在加速时会静默改写图像中的文字，并把缓存误差沿 CFG 分解定位到 guidance 项，进而提出 GuideCache：仅对预测 guidance 误差最大的 token 重算两条 CFG 分支，在约 2.4 倍降 FLOPs 下恢复全量计算的文本保真度。

**相关性简评：** 其「缓存状态误差按组成分解，再依据预测误差选择性重算部分 token」的做法，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态复用、计算分配与动态重算决策的线索，但属图像生成加速，与视觉推理主线距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Text Lives in the Guidance: Preventing Silent Text Rewriting in Cached Diffusion Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion transformers (DiTs) now render long, legible text in images, and feature caching is a standard way to accelerate them. Caches are judged by image-level metrics, which do not check whether the rendered text is still correct. We find that caching silently rewrites this text. On Z-Image, TaylorSeer at 2.9x fewer FLOPs lowers word accuracy from 62% to 46%, while ImageReward and CLIP Score barely change. Sampling with fewer steps at the same compute keeps almost all of this text, so the loss comes from how the cache predicts the skipped steps. Decomposing the cache error along classifier-free guidance (CFG), we trace this loss mainly to the guidance term, the difference between the conditional and unconditional predictions, which caches extrapolate worst on text. Correcting the guidance inside the text alone recovers most of the lost text. We therefore propose GuideCache, which recomputes both CFG branches only on the tokens with the largest predicted guidance error, using the guidance terms that the cache already stores. At 2.4x fewer FLOPs, GuideCache restores full-compute prompt fidelity on Z-Image and Qwen-Image, and on Z-Image it renders text as well as or better than ToCa, TeaCache, and FasterCache while staying much closer to the full model's output. GuideCache works with caches of any order, and on top of fewer sampling steps it keeps more text than step reduction at a higher speedup. These results suggest that acceleration methods for text-rendering models should be evaluated on the text they render.

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [Falsifiable Compositional Reasoning in Time Series Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25388v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25388

**作者：** Hao Xue

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25388v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25388v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出时间序列模型以历史回测作开放式搜索与选型，会在非平稳机制切换后失效，主张围绕「可证伪的组合假设」建模，即神经原语编码器、显式耦合约束与训练期校准的证伪检验相结合，并用理论分析与受控多原语基准、德国日前电价数据做诊断。

**相关性简评：** 其「潜在原语耦合假设＋训练期校准的证伪检验」或可为 LVR 中 latent 假设的证据筛选与避免验证集捷径式选择提供方法线索，需核查该检验能否迁移到多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Falsifiable Compositional Reasoning in Time Series Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Time series foundation models as well as the language-guided analytical agents achieve strong accuracy on standard chronological benchmarks. However, models that rank highest on historical validation splits often degrade across non-stationary regime transitions. We hypothesise that this gap arises from using historical backtesting as an open-ended search and model-selection objective instead of using it to test pre-specified compositional assumptions. Real-world time series are generated by recurring latent primitives that co-move within finite historical windows. Under strong historical co-movement, chronological holdout error cannot separate predictors that learn invariant couplings among primitives from predictors that fit transient historical correlations. Searching over large pools of architectures, hyperparameters, or chain-of-thought rules further biases selection toward historical shortcuts. Through theoretical analysis and diagnostic experiments on a controlled multi-primitive benchmark and German day-ahead electricity prices, we show that expanding validation search fails to improve out-of-regime accuracy, and that decomposing primitives without an ex-ante falsification test still breaks down when residual correlations shift. Hence, we propose formulating time series forecasting and reasoning around falsifiable compositional hypotheses that combine neural primitive encoders, explicit coupling constraints, and training-calibrated falsification tests.

</details>

<a id="paper-79"></a>

### 79. [HOPD: Historical On-Policy Distillation for SAR MLLM Continual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25391v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25391

**作者：** Yuxuan Li、Derrick Goh Xin Deik、Zheng-Peng Duan、Qianru Sun、Weijie Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25391v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25391v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向 SAR 多模态大模型持续学习，将各源采集阶段的 LoRA 状态归档为持续教师，对学生自生成前缀做 on-policy 蒸馏，并用原始视角教师指导增强后的历史视图，最终合并为单一适配器。

**相关性简评：** 教师监督学生自生成前缀的 on-policy 蒸馏可供核查 LVR 轨迹训练中的监督信号与贡献分配问题；但该文针对灾难性遗忘与 SAR 域偏移，未涉及内部视觉状态或推理期 latent 生成，需判断其「自生成前缀」与 latent 推理轨迹是否有实质可比性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HOPD: Historical On-Policy Distillation for SAR MLLM Continual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Continual SAR interpretation requires multimodal large language models to learn incoming sources while retaining earlier task abilities. This is difficult when acquisition and geographic shifts alter visual evidence, regionally concentrated categories bias answers, and historical images are scarce. We address this problem with Historical On-Policy Distillation (HOPD), which reuses source-acquisition LoRA states as persistent teachers for one evolving student. Archiving each state before consolidation preserves a stable source reference. The teachers supervise student-generated prefixes, transferring historical behavior to the student's changing generation contexts. To enrich sparse replay, original-view teachers guide augmented historical student views while preserving spatial coordinates. This knowledge is consolidated into a single deployed adapter that requires no source identity. On SARVQA-CL, our source-incremental evaluation protocol, HOPD achieves the highest final mean score of 54.51 among the compared methods, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.96 points. It also improves the final source-macro score in every evaluated task over shared-memory Replay-SFT, with gains across source orders. These results support source-acquisition history as supervision for retaining earlier task performance during continual SAR adaptation. Code and data: https://github.com/zcablii/HOPD.

</details>

<a id="paper-80"></a>

### 80. [Protein Functional Semantic Alignment via Representation Rectification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25396v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25396

**作者：** Chuyang Zhou、Bingxin Zhou、Andi Han、Chang Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25396v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25396v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SAVR，以 Pfam 家族共成员作为同源引导的离散语义信号进行对比后训练，矫正蛋白质语言模型的表示几何：降低方向主导、重分配方差并提升嵌入协方差谱的有效维度，在冻结骨干下改进 EC/GO 功能预测。

**相关性简评：** 其「用离散语义信号做对比后训练以矫正表示几何、提升有效维度」的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的维度坍缩与信息集中问题提供可供核查的训练侧线索，但同视觉推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Protein Functional Semantic Alignment via Representation Rectification

**abstract/A1：** Protein language models (PLMs) are commonly pretrained with likelihood-based objectives such as masked language modeling or denoising. These objectives capture sequence-level regularities, but can produce representations in which functional signals are entangled with non-functional variation (e.g., sequence identity, compositional bias, and frequent local motifs), creating a readout bottleneck for downstream function prediction. We introduce Functional Semantic Alignment via Representation Rectification (SAVR), a contrastive post-training method using Pfam family co-membership as a homology-guided discrete semantic signal to reshape PLM representations. SAVR reduces directional dominance and redistributes variance across embedding dimensions, decreasing variance concentration, and increasing effective dimensionality of the embedding covariance spectrum. Without observing any task-specific labels during post-training, SAVR achieves state-of-the-art results on Enzyme Commission (EC) benchmarks and competitive results on Gene Ontology (GO) benchmarks under frozen-backbone evaluation. These results suggest that homology-guided contrastive post-training can rectify PLM representation geometry and improve sequence-based protein function prediction.

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [Decision Boundaries Matter: Graph-Regularized Residual Adaptation for Cross-Domain Fake Image Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25406v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25406

**作者：** Shichuang Xie、Qishuai Ding、Tong Qiao、Liquan Shen、Guorui Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25406v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25406v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GraRA，在冻结的取证编码器上用无标签目标域数据修正源域决策边界：以不确定度加权的目标特征构造与源分类方向正交的紧凑残差子空间，并用 target-graph diffusion 生成的软标签与邻域一致性约束残差学习，仅优化 32 个系数且不增加推理分支，用于跨域伪造图像检测。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结骨干＋受约束的低维读出修正」线索可供核查 LVR 中收益究竟来自内部视觉状态还是仅决策层/读出适配，邻域一致性软标签也可能供核查 latent 轨迹的筛选与无标签监督；但该文不研究视觉推理状态生成，与主线仅间接相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decision Boundaries Matter: Graph-Regularized Residual Adaptation for Cross-Domain Fake Image Detection

**abstract/A1：** Despite learning transferable features, fake image detectors may struggle on unseen domains due to mismatched decision boundaries. Our analysis shows that features extracted by the frozen forensic encoder remain discriminative, but the source-trained decision boundary does not fully exploit this information. However, correcting this boundary without source data or target labels remains challenging, as unreliable source predictions may lead to incorrect adaptation. To address this issue, we propose Graph-Regularized Residual Adaptation (GraRA), which refines the source decision boundary using only unlabeled target data. To determine which classifier directions to update, GraRA constructs a compact residual subspace from uncertainty-weighted target features and keeps it orthogonal to the source classification direction. To guide these updates, it uses source-score anchors to generate soft labels through target-graph diffusion, while neighborhood consistency regularizes residual learning within the constrained subspace. The learned correction is merged into the source classifier, requiring only 32 optimized coefficients and no additional inference branches. On diverse unseen OpenMMSec domains, GraRA achieves 84.36\\% accuracy and 91.34\\% AUC, improving over the source detector by 4.33 and 3.50 percentage points, respectively. Further analysis shows that reliable source predictions and locally consistent neighborhoods help guide residual learning, allowing GraRA to correct ranking errors that a simple threshold shift cannot address. \\textcolor{cvprblue}{Code will be made publicly available.

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [MIR-Depth: Multi-Range Refinement for Single-View RGB-D Depth Completion of Transparent Objects](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25417v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25417

**作者：** Xinzhe Fu、Qi Shao、Yuanfei Xie、Taoyu Wu、Zhihao Cao、Long Hu、Hong Liang、Xianzhi Li、Anh Nguyen、Baoru Huang、Rongsheng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25417v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25417v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对透明物体单视角 RGB-D 深度补全，先由先验引导的初始深度构造多范围坐标感知假设体（全局逆深度体与深度轴滤波的逐像素局部体），再用多尺度 ConvGRU 配合越界查找掩码与有效性掩码 softmax 融合做选择性循环细化，最后以逐像素空间门控修正全分辨率残差。

**相关性简评：** 「固定采样预算下多范围假设 + 掩码融合 + 循环更新」可供核查 latent 多假设维护、视觉证据筛选与计算预算分配的设计线索，但该文是稠密几何回归任务，与 VLM 推理期 latent 状态无直接关系，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MIR-Depth: Multi-Range Refinement for Single-View RGB-D Depth Completion of Transparent Objects

**abstract/A1：** Recovering accurate visible-surface depth from a single RGB-D image and an object mask is essential for robotic grasping and manipulation of transparent objects. Specular reflections cause missing depth, while refraction corrupts nonzero returns with background depth, making raw measurements unreliable as surface anchors. Under a fixed sampling budget, refining an initial single-view prediction within a single search interval either misses large refraction offsets or sacrifices fine local resolution. To resolve this single-view trade-off, MIR-Depth constructs Coordinate-Aware Multi-Range Hypotheses: a 3D-aggregated global inverse-depth volume and depth-axis-filtered pixel-wise local volumes around a prior-guided initial depth. Selective Recurrent Refinement then introduces out-of-bounds lookup masking and validity-masked softmax fusion to iteratively update inverse depth from these volumes using a multi-scale ConvGRU. Finally, Spatially Gated Correction applies a learned per-pixel spatial gate to the full-resolution residual, preserving accurate initial geometry without imposing raw sensor readings as hard constraints. Experiments on TransCG, TODD, TRansPose, ClearPose, and HouseCat6D show that MIR-Depth achieves lower error than ReMake, DFNet, BP-Net, and PSD. Real-robot evaluations confirm accurate surface geometry for 6-DoF grasp pose prediction. Code and pretrained models will be publicly available.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [DiLE: Discrimination-Aware Latent-Space Learning for Universal Multimodal Embeddings](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25419v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25419

**作者：** Ben Kang、Junda Wang、Yuxuan Yuan、Hang Li、Yuhan Liu、Xin Chen、Wanting Geng、Tong Lin、Pei Fu、Dong Wang、Jian Luan、Huchuan Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25419v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25419v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DiLE，用少量可学习 query 把任务相关信息压缩到紧凑 latent 空间，并以推理指令、答案生成监督、共享-私有专家适配及 on-policy 难负例蒸馏（对齐教师细粒度相关性分布）来训练通用多模态嵌入，在 MMEB-v2 等检索基准上取得领先。

**相关性简评：** 其「嵌入是否保留区分易混候选所需证据」的视角与 latent 状态中的视觉细节保留、信息坍缩问题相通，可学习 query 压缩与难负例反馈监督或可供核查如何为 latent 表示提供判别性监督；但目标是检索嵌入而非推理期状态生成，需人工确认迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DiLE: Discrimination-Aware Latent-Space Learning for Universal Multimodal Embeddings

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models have greatly improved input understanding in universal multimodal embedding models. However, this understanding does not ensure that their embeddings preserve the evidence needed to distinguish relevant items from confusable alternatives. To preserve essential discriminative evidence in embeddings, this paper proposes DiLE, a discrimination-aware latent-space learning framework that combines task guidance with on-policy retrieval feedback. Specifically, a few learnable queries are used to encode task-relevant information in a compact latent space. Reasoning instructions and auxiliary answer-generation supervision shape these representations to support both task understanding and retrieval discrimination. To address task-specific discriminative needs, we design shared-private expert adaptation that integrates cross-task knowledge sharing with task-specific representation learning. Building on this, the on-policy hard-negative distillation is introduced: the student retrieves confusable candidates, and a reverse KL divergence objective aligns its embedding-based similarity distribution with the teacher's fine-grained relevance distribution over these candidates. Candidates are periodically refreshed using updated student snapshots, tying teacher supervision to the student's evolving retrieval behavior and confusions. Across four model configurations that span two backbone generations, DiLE achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMEB-v2, UVRB, and MRMR, demonstrating its effectiveness across modalities and tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-84"></a>

### 84. [From Sequence Uncertainty to Phenotype Hypotheses: Uncertainty-Conditioned Inference over Biomedical Knowledge Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25422v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25422

**作者：** Huixin Zhan、Jason H. Moore

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.GN

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25422v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25422v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「不确定性条件路径能量」框架：将基因组基础模型的变体预测与校准后的预测不确定性，转换为生物医学知识图谱上表型假设的显式概率分布，并用可解释的学习能量为基因—疾病—表型路径打分，其中查询不确定性设定目标温度以调节假设广度。

**相关性简评：** 该工作不含视觉或多模态推理，但其用不确定性调制温度来调节推理假设的宽度、并聚合多条路径概率形成可追溯支持的做法，可能为 LVR 中「证据质量决定候选筛选范围与停止时机」提供可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Sequence Uncertainty to Phenotype Hypotheses: Uncertainty-Conditioned Inference over Biomedical Knowledge Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Genomic foundation models provide variant-level evidence, but connecting that evidence to phenotype hypotheses requires accounting for uncertainty throughout structured reasoning. We introduce an uncertainty-conditioned path-energy framework that converts sequence predictions and calibrated predictive uncertainty into explicit distributions over graph-supported phenotype hypotheses, without patient-level phenotype supervision. An interpretable learned energy scores admissible gene--disease--phenotype routes, query uncertainty controls the target temperature, and path probabilities are aggregated into phenotype hypotheses. This formulation makes evidence quality an explicit determinant of hypothesis breadth while retaining traceable multi-path support. Exact normalization over enumerable candidate sets provides a reference for evaluating a Hastings-corrected learned revision operator. On AlzKB and Hetionet, phenotype-level entropy tracks upstream uncertainty, with Spearman correlations of 0.951 and 0.878, respectively; ablations identify the contribution of uncertainty conditioning. In masked-edge recovery, the model exceeds chance and degree-matched probability-permutation controls. Training the energy under the uncertainty-dependent temperature yields higher held-out recall@10 than training under a constant temperature of comparable magnitude. Under gene hold-out, recall@10 exceeds that of a trained DistMult embedding on 24 of 30 graph masks (0.183 versus 0.123); this difference is attributable mainly to three genes with many variants and is not established for all genes. These results establish an uncertainty-aware interface from genomic predictions to graph-supported phenotype hypotheses that combines interpretable probability allocation with held-out association recovery of explicitly stated scope.

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [Coordinate Basis Linear Bandits with Sublinear Regret over Low-Rank Clusters](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25424v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25424

**作者：** Hantao Yang、Hong Xie、Defu Lian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25424v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25424v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究高维线性 bandit 的计算效率，提出 CBL 算法：用聚类机制在每个簇内维护列空间的精确正交基与低维坐标表示以保留完整协方差信息，并以哨兵触发簇切换，把每轮更新降至 O(r̄²)，避免低秩近似谱尾导致的线性 regret。

**相关性简评：** 其「簇内精确基加低维坐标、按哨兵切换」的压缩与切换机制，或可供核查 latent 推理状态的保信息低秩压缩与表示更新；但线性 bandit 设定与 VLM 距离较远，迁移可行性需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coordinate Basis Linear Bandits with Sublinear Regret over Low-Rank Clusters

**abstract/A1：** We study the problem of computational efficiency for linear bandits in high-dimensional settings with a finite arm set. In linear bandits, the increase in the dimension $d$ of the feature vectors leads to growing computational costs of $O(d^2)$ at each round of update. Traditional low-rank approximation methods such as SOFUL reduce computation via fixed-size matrix sketching, yet run the risk of incurring vacuous linear regret when the spectral tail of the data is heavy and the approximation rank is inadequately selected. To guarantee regret convergence and effectively reduce computational costs, we introduce a clustering mechanism and propose the Coordinate Basis Linear Bandit (CBL) algorithm. Our method preserves the full covariance information in each cluster by maintaining an exact orthonormal basis of the cluster's column space together with a low-dimensional coordinate representation, which guarantees robust sublinear regret without spectral-tail vulnerabilities. It performs cluster switching by assigning a sentinel for each cluster, and reduces per-round update computation to $O(\\bar r^2)$ in the cluster's effective dimension $\\bar r=\\min(r,l)$, where $r$ is the intrinsic rank of the data and $l&lt;d$ is a tunable rank bound.

</details>

<a id="paper-86"></a>

### 86. [CANDOR: Contrastive Self-Denoising Rewards for Label-Free Text-to-Image Post-Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25457v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25457

**作者：** Junhyeok Lee、Byung-Hoon Kim、Kyu Sung Choi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25457v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25457v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文指出文生图模型自身的去噪置信度不能可靠反映提示词忠实度，转而提出 CANDOR：在共享噪声探针下，用无条件下与有条件去噪误差的对数比作为内在奖励做 Flow-GRPO 后训练，无需外部奖励模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「空条件参照 + 同提示组内对比」以抵消与提示无关的难度并分离提示词的真实贡献，这种去除混杂的贡献归因思路可能可供核查：是否可借来构造视觉旁路或语言先验的对照基线，判断 VLM 推理中视觉证据是否被实际使用；但论文本身不涉及多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CANDOR: Contrastive Self-Denoising Rewards for Label-Free Text-to-Image Post-Training

**abstract/A1：** Post-training text-to-image generators with reinforcement learning usually relies on external reward models that need annotated preferences or auxiliary evaluators and are prone to reward hacking. We show that the generator's own denoising accuracy, used by a recent intrinsic reward, is a poor proxy for prompt fidelity. Within a same-prompt group, this absolute self-confidence is dominated by image complexity and ranks correct samples above incorrect ones near chance. We introduce CANDOR (Contrastive Alignment from Null-referenced Denoising lOg-Ratio), the log-ratio of unconditional to conditional denoising errors under shared noise probes, which measures how much the prompt helps the model denoise its own sample. It cancels prompt-independent difficulty that scales both errors and ranks the better of two same-prompt samples higher in 60% and 63% of pairs on compositional generation and text rendering. As the only Flow-GRPO reward for a flow-matching model, CANDOR improves text rendering, aesthetic and human-preference scores over the base model and a prior absolute self-confidence reward, with text-rendering and preference gains that stronger classifier-free guidance does not reproduce. On public Flow-GRPO adapters trained on external rewards, CANDOR further raises PickScore and ImageReward, so it can complement those rewards. Code is available at https://github.com/jhlee0619/candor.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-87"></a>

### 87. [RemoteVLPG: How to Obtain a Specific VLM for Language-Guided Path Generation on Remote Sensing Images?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25458v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25458

**作者：** Junzhe Dang、Mengmeng Zhang、Yuxiang Zhang、Wei Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25458v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25458v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究如何为遥感图像上的语言引导起终点路径生成构建专用 VLM：比较三种常用架构、设计可扩展的预训练数据构建方式并建立三个支持分层评测的基准与指标。

**相关性简评：** 其架构对比与预训练数据构建可供核查 VLM 如何保留起点、终点与几何路径信息，与视觉细节和几何信息是否留在内部状态的问题可能相关，但论文聚焦端到端任务性能，未涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，借鉴线索有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RemoteVLPG: How to Obtain a Specific VLM for Language-Guided Path Generation on Remote Sensing Images?

**abstract/A1：** We study how to obtain a task-specific Vision-Language Model (VLM) for language-guided path generation between start and end points on remote sensing images. We explore this question from three aspects. First, we implement three widely used architectural approaches and compare their performance. Second, we design a scalable method for constructing pre-training data and build an effective pre-training dataset with a degree of diversity. Methods trained on this dataset achieve promising zero-shot performance. Third, we construct three benchmarks that support stratified evaluation and design three metrics for path generation. We investigate this problem through extensive experiments. The results show that the zero-shot performance of methods trained on the constructed pre-training dataset can exceed that of some advanced commercial VLMs. Code and Dataset can be available at https://github.com/jzdang/RemoteVLPG

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-88"></a>

### 88. [A Wolf in Sheep's Pixels: Visual Camouflage Attacks via HDR Gain Maps](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25469v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25469

**作者：** Qi Shen、Yujia Gong、Yue Zhao、Tong Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25469v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25469v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DRJanus：利用 JPEG 的 HDR gain map 在单张图中嵌入两幅独立图像，使自动系统读到 SDR 基图、人眼在 HDR 显示下看到另一幅目标图，从而规避审核服务与 VLM，并操控多个智能体的任务行为。

**相关性简评：** 其「机器所见与用户所见不同」的可控双视图构造，可能为核查 VLM 内部状态究竟编码哪一路视觉证据提供刺激设计线索，跨视图一致性检查也可供思考视觉旁路与证据筛选的检测方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Wolf in Sheep's Pixels: Visual Camouflage Attacks via HDR Gain Maps

**abstract/A1：** An image can pass automated safety checks while showing harmful content to its human viewer. We introduce DRJanus, a visual camouflage attack that exploits the gap between standard dynamic range (SDR) processing and high dynamic range (HDR) display. Automated systems often read only an image's SDR base, whereas HDR viewers reconstruct its appearance using an auxiliary gain map. DRJanus inverts this reconstruction to embed two independently chosen color images in a single JPEG, without querying or optimizing against the target model. It preserves the chosen SDR base exactly and reconstructs HDR targets at an average PSNR of 43.03 dB across 400 images. On a benchmark of 100 sexual-content images, DRJanus evades detection by all six tested commercial moderation services and nine vision-language models. Across five agents, each evaluated on 120 task cases per threat setting, attack success ranges from 31.67% to 100% for moderation evasion and from 95% to 100% for task manipulation. We also explore cross-view consistency checks as a mitigation. These findings expose a gap in visual trust: securing what a machine reads does not secure what a user sees. A demo is available at https://qshen0629.github.io/DRJanus/

</details>

<a id="paper-89"></a>

### 89. [TuringPAI: A Foundation for On-Device Physical AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25474v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25474

**作者：** Qiman Wu、Hanlin Chen、Yuhua Wei、Mingyuan Wang、Shaman Tang、Minghao Li、Rui Xin、Xingye Liu、Zhichao Yang、Yiqi Yu、Hongyan Pei、Xiaowen Tian、YingYing Liu、Shiwen Liu、Jiahui Hu、Yizhao Wang、Yuheng Zhang、Anhua Liu、Yue He、Shuang Su、Da Zhu、Hua Zhou、Pengfei Diao、Haojie Yang、Weichao Huang、Hang Zhang、Honggou Yang、Xianming Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25474v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25474v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向端侧物理AI的0.65B视觉语言基础模型家族 TuringPAI-VL，基于 HybridViT-MLQ 与多尺度 latent query 将空间特征和历史观测压缩后送入语言模型，并以此共享初始化迁移到自动驾驶与机器人操作两个下游模型。

**相关性简评：** 多尺度 latent query 作为紧凑视觉—语言接口压缩空间与时间观测，可能可供核查视觉细节保留与信息坍缩之间的权衡，但论文重点是端侧效率与下游控制而非 latent 推理，联系需进一步确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TuringPAI: A Foundation for On-Device Physical AI

**abstract/A1：** Physical AI requires models that understand spatial relationships and dynamic environments and translate observations into actions under limited onboard compute. We present TuringPAI, a family of models for on-device Physical AI comprising TuringPAI-VL for shared vision-language understanding, TuringPAI-Drive for autonomous driving, and TuringPAI-Embodied for robotic manipulation. TuringPAI-VL is a 0.65B model built on HybridViT-MLQ, which combines convolutional local aggregation with Transformer-based global modeling. Multi-scale Latent Queries integrate spatial features and observation history within the same visual Transformer, providing a compact, structured interface to the language model. Training combines general vision-language data with geometric, cross-view, and temporal supervision. TuringPAI-VL supports general multimodal understanding and improves quantitative geometric perception over Qwen2-VL-2B, while providing a shared initialization for the two downstream VLA models. For autonomous driving, TuringPAI-Drive maintains all evaluated ADE/FDE metrics within 1.2\\% of a full-token HybridViT baseline using the MLQ interface and achieves comparable aggregate scores in an in-house simulation evaluation. Using XPeng Native Runtime (XNRT) on a single NVIDIA Orin-X SoC, TuringPAI-Drive generates trajectories at 12\\,Hz in on-vehicle operation. For robotic manipulation, TuringPAI-Embodied achieves a 96.8\\% average success rate across the four LIBERO suites. These results demonstrate a shared vision-language foundation for driving and robotic manipulation under practical on-device constraints.

</details>

<a id="paper-90"></a>

### 90. [SegAnyFruit: Learning Generalizable and Edge Deployable Fruit Instance Segmentation at Scale](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25475v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25475

**作者：** Yanan Wang、Yuzheng wang、Yibin Ying、Zhenghao Fei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25475v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25475v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 SegAnyFruit，一个数据构建与分阶段训练框架，通过通用掩码预训练、网络图像中训练和人工校验的农业后训练，把基础模型的先验蒸馏进轻量、免提示的实例分割器，并在多基准与边缘部署上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「把大模型视觉先验蒸馏到小模型且免提示」的做法，可供核查视觉细节在压缩后保留多少、以及渐进式训练是否缓解信息坍缩与遗忘，与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选和状态容量问题或许有间接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SegAnyFruit: Learning Generalizable and Edge Deployable Fruit Instance Segmentation at Scale

**abstract/A1：** Deploying fruit instance segmentation in real agricultural settings requires generalizing across unseen species, orchards, and imaging conditions under strict onboard compute and annotation budgets. We introduce SegAnyFruit (SAF), a data construction and progressive training framework that distills foundation-model priors into lightweight, prompt-free segmenters. SAF combines generic mask pre-training, broad mid-training on automatically annotated web imagery from our curated WebFruits dataset, and human-verified agricultural refinement post-training on PaperFruits, together spanning 733K images and 9.66M whole-fruit masks across 165 fruit types. Across seven benchmarks, zero-shot SAF-RF-DETR-XL reaches 69.06 average mask AP50:95 (+4.76 over SAM 3) while remaining deployable on edge devices. SAF initialization also boosts few-shot to full-shot adaptation across five agricultural domains while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we systematically analyze training stages, data scale, model capacity, and generative augmentation, and validate our pipeline's transferability to underwater, cell, and human segmentation. We will release our datasets, code, and SAF models.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [Keystone: Judging Previews at Key Steps for Test-Time Search in Diffusion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25530v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25530

**作者：** Jiajun Fan、Chaoran Cheng、Ziwen Wang、Changyi Yang、Bangji Yang、Qinglong Zheng、Ge Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25530v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25530v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Keystone，一个无需训练的判定器：把 prompt 编译为关于物体、数量、颜色、位置的类型化问题，交由现成多模态决策模型在扩散早期若干「关键步」的 preview 上作答，并据此做自适应搜索与剪枝，在 GenEval 等基准上以不到一半的生成计算超过 best-of-N。

**相关性简评：** 其核心线索是用结构化提问对未完成/含噪的中间状态做可靠验证，并据此决定继续、剪枝或停止，可供核查 LVR 中「对部分成形的 latent 状态判定、视觉证据筛选与 STOP 决策」的验证器设计；但研究对象是扩散图像生成的去噪轨迹，与 VLM 推理状态的联系尚属可能迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Keystone: Judging Previews at Key Steps for Test-Time Search in Diffusion Models

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-image diffusion and flow models yield a preview, their clean-image estimate, at every denoising step. When they miscount, drop or misplace objects, test-time search is in a bind: its verifiers are reward models built for finished images, so judging early misreads blurry previews, and judging late, as best-of-N does, pays to fully denoise doomed candidates. We find that the image is not the problem: the outcome is settled as denoising starts, by the seed or by a stochastic sampler's first steps, and already shows in early previews; what fails on them is the judge, as reward models read them near chance on several kinds of prompts. We introduce Keystone, a training-free judge of previews that compiles a prompt into typed questions about its objects, counts, colours and positions and has an off-the-shelf multimodal decision model answer them. Asked this way, the judge reads counts and positions from early previews better than reward models read them from finished images. Searching at these key steps on GenEval with SD3.5-Medium, Keystone's adaptive search and pruning significantly outperform best-of-8 with every other verifier at under half its generator compute, and as a drop-in verifier there, the Keystone score significantly improves best-of-N, progressive seed pruning and FK steering; without retuning, both searches stay significantly above the strongest other verifier's best-of-N on average over four base models and three benchmarks. Keystone thus moves test-time judgment to the key steps where the outcome already shows.

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>

### 92. [Anchored Inertial Proposals with Objective Verification for Diffusion-Prior Inverse Problems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25551v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25551

**作者：** Minwoo Kim、Seunghyeok Shin、Dabin Kim、Hongki Lim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25551v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25551v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种无需训练的扩散逆问题采样修正：以去噪预测锚定含测量拟合与近端项的目标，用重球动力学生成干净状态候选，再由独立验证器按同一目标接受或回退，并将接受状态重新加噪继续采样。

**相关性简评：** 虽面向图像逆问题而非 VLM 推理，其「候选生成—目标验证—回退—再注入」的状态更新可供核查 latent 推理步的接受准则、位移控制与停止决策是否可借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Anchored Inertial Proposals with Objective Verification for Diffusion-Prior Inverse Problems

**abstract/A1：** Training-free diffusion priors are powerful for inverse problems, but measurement guidance during reverse sampling is local: aggressive updates can improve immediate data fit while disrupting later denoising. We introduce an anchored inertial correction rule that separates proposal generation from acceptance. At each corrected step, the denoiser prediction anchors a frozen clean-space objective combining measurement fit with a noise-level-dependent proximal term. Heavy-ball dynamics with step size and momentum computed from the measured curvature generate a clean-state candidate; a separate verifier evaluates it with the same objective, with the denoiser prediction retained as fallback; both stages are derived from one objective for the whole trajectory, considered one step at a time. The accepted clean state is re-noised to continue sampling. The rule applies to pixel and latent diffusion with differentiable measurement operators, requires no retraining, and adds no denoiser calls inside the correction loop. We analyze how objective verification yields local nonincrease and displacement control. Across natural-image benchmarks, the method achieves competitive reconstruction quality. Code is available at https://github.com/Seunghyeok0715/PIVOT2026.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [TwoPass: Draft-Guided Few-Step Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25553v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25553

**作者：** Yijun Liu、Shijia Ge、Tongtong Feng、Xin Wang、Yuan Meng、Yuwei Liu、Zhi Wang、Wenwu Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25553v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25553v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TwoPass，一个自条件的训练与推理框架：训练时用共享网络产生的干净视频估计作为同一噪声状态下的条件，并加入仅在存在草稿时激活的残差适配器；推理时每次速度预测同时给出求解器更新与下一个草稿，使每个视频片段只需 4 次函数评估，在 Bridge 与 Language Table 上取得较优结果。

**相关性简评：** 草稿复用式迭代细化与少步计算预算，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的循环更新、自条件复用及「多想几步是否值得」的成本权衡提供可供核查的方法线索；但论文本身研究机器人动作条件下的视频预测，不直接涉及视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TwoPass: Draft-Guided Few-Step Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation

**abstract/A1：** Action-conditioned video prediction lets robots anticipate the visual consequences of their actions for planning and policy evaluation. Existing predictors denoise each future from noise without using their own estimate of it, which limits the quality of the predicted future, in particular of the manipulated objects, and requires many network calls. We present TwoPass, a self-conditioned training-and-inference framework that keeps the standard flow-matching objective. Self-Generated Draft Training supplies a detached clean-video estimate from the shared network as conditioning at the same noisy state. A Refinement-Only Late Adapter applies a dedicated residual transformation when a draft is present. At inference, Draft-Reuse Iterative Refinement obtains both a solver update and the next draft from each velocity prediction, requiring as few as 4 function evaluations (NFE) per video chunk. Training samples discrete solver-query noise levels and updates the pretrained backbone. Extensive experiments on Bridge and Language Table show that TwoPass achieves state-of-the-art results with 4 NFE, far fewer than the 50-59 calls of existing predictors. Code: https://github.com/PlantPotatoOnMoon/TwoPass.

</details>

<a id="paper-94"></a>

### 94. [Is a Description Worth a Thousand Demonstrations? Specificity and Reliability of Task Descriptions in In-Context Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25560v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25560

**作者：** Bruce Nguyen、Bei Jiang、Linglong Kong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25560v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25560v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文从贝叶斯视角把 in-context learning 中的任务描述建模为具有特异性和可靠性的先验，用 effective sample size（ESS）衡量描述相当于多少示例，并在上下文线性回归及元训练 Transformer、预训练语言模型上检验其是否按贝叶斯最优方式使用描述，以及推理开启的作用。

**相关性简评：** 虽非视觉工作，但其用 ESS 量化「描述作为先验」的预测价值、并区分可靠性与推理开启后的使用差异，可供核查 VLM 中文字提示或视觉证据是否真正影响 latent 推理状态时借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Is a Description Worth a Thousand Demonstrations? Specificity and Reliability of Task Descriptions in In-Context Learning

**abstract/A1：** A prompt for in-context learning (ICL) can describe a task in addition to, or instead of, demonstrating it through examples. Yet how task descriptions work remains poorly understood theoretically. Through the Bayesian view of ICL, we model descriptions as informative priors characterized by their specificity and reliability. We adapt the effective sample size (ESS) from Bayesian statistics, quantifying a description's predictive value as an equivalent number of demonstrations. For in-context linear regression, we show that a reliable description's ESS grows with its specificity and with the task's dimension. A description that may be irrelevant, however, gains little from its specificity unless demonstrations are given. In practice, do in-context learners use descriptions as a Bayes-optimal predictor would? Our experiments show that meta-trained Transformers come close to the Bayes-optimal ESS when tested on their training distribution. Pretrained language models, however, make too little use of highly specific descriptions when answering without reasoning; most of them closely match the Bayes-optimal predictor only when reasoning is enabled. These results extend the Bayesian analysis of ICL from demonstrations to descriptions.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 6 figures, 17 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-95"></a>

### 95. [Online Mistake Detection in Instruction-Conditioned Procedural Video](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25567v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25567

**作者：** Amit Feldman、Ilan Shimshoni、Rotem Dror

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25567v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25567v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究第一视角程序性视频中的在线、指令条件化错误检测：仅用已观测视频、任务指令与历史预测，实时识别并定位动作尝试并判定其为正确、修改、失误、纠正或多余，并比较模块化流水线与单步联合模型两种设计。

**相关性简评：** 其「联合预测 vs 分阶段流水线」的对照与阶段间误差传播分析，可能为 LVR 中多阶段 latent 状态的贡献分配、监督放置与流水线式推理设计提供核查线索，但论文本身不涉及 VLM 内部表示或 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Online Mistake Detection in Instruction-Conditioned Procedural Video

**abstract/A1：** An assistant monitoring a person performing a procedure is most useful when it can detect mistakes while there is still time to recover. This requires judging actions against task instructions as the video unfolds, identifying the attempted step, locating it in time, and assessing its execution. Existing online detectors do not use the instruction text, while instruction-conditioned methods operate offline. In this paper, we study online, instruction-conditioned mistake detection in egocentric videos. Using only observed video, task instructions, and prior predictions, our model identifies and localizes completed action attempts and classifies them as correct, modified, slip, corrections, or additions; omitted steps are determined after the video ends. We compare a modular pipeline, with separate step identification, temporal localization, and execution verification, to a joint model that predicts all outputs in a single step. To support supervised learning from mistakes, we repartition EgoPER to include erroneous videos in all splits. The approaches achieve similar step-segmentation F1@0.5 (93.6% and 92.1%), while the joint model achieves higher binary mistake-detection F1@0.5 (84.1% vs. 80.7%), with its largest gain on slips (56.4% vs. 37.5%). Component-level analysis suggests that error propagation between stages contributes to the modular approach's lower detection score. On test videos containing mistakes, both designs outperform released AEM and ESTANet checkpoints under our evaluation protocol.

</details>

<a id="paper-96"></a>

### 96. [Reinforcement-Aligned Probabilistic Weather Forecasting with Discrete Tokens](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25570v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25570

**作者：** Jinhan Li、Zhisheng Chen、Yuxuan Li、Yuan Gao、Fan Xu、Hao Wu、Jinlong Du、Kun Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25570v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25570v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 WeatherCraft，用多尺度 coarse-to-fine 离散天气 token 表示大气状态，由 Transformer 建模跨时间与跨尺度的因果依赖并在各尺度内并行预测 token，从而直接采样生成集合预报，并借助 token 精确对数概率做 GRPO 后训练以对齐下游任务奖励。

**相关性简评：** 其分层离散状态表示与「尺度内并行、跨尺度因果」的生成结构，以及用精确 token 似然做序列级奖励优化而无需对评估器求导的做法，可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态组织、并行生成与轨迹监督分配提供方法线索，需核查其是否只适配数值场预报。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reinforcement-Aligned Probabilistic Weather Forecasting with Discrete Tokens

**abstract/A1：** Global medium-range weather forecasting must not only generate ensembles efficiently to characterize uncertainty in future states, but also serve downstream applications such as extreme-event warnings and cross-sphere coupled simulations. We introduce WeatherCraft, a framework that unifies probabilistic generation and reinforcement learning alignment through discrete weather tokens, bringing the GPT-style pretraining--post-training paradigm to global medium-range weather forecasting. A multiscale tokenizer represents atmospheric states as a coarse-to-fine token hierarchy, while a Transformer models causal dependencies across time and scale and predicts tokens within each scale in parallel. Direct sampling generates diverse ensembles, and exact token log-probabilities enable Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to maximize downstream task rewards without differentiating through evaluators or coupled models. On the ERA5 benchmark, WeatherCraft's eight-member ensemble mean reduces day-15 normalized RMSE by 17.6% relative to the strongest evaluated deterministic baseline and, with KV caching, achieves an approximately $18\\times$ inference speedup over GenCast. Direct fair-CRPS adaptation improves ensemble probabilistic skill, while reward-based post-training improves forecasts of persistent land temperature extremes and coupled ocean heat extremes. This work establishes discrete autoregressive modeling as a general foundation for scalable, task-alignable Earth system foundation models.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 11 figures, 15 tables. Code: https://github.com/Jinhan-Li/WeatherCraft

</details>

<a id="paper-97"></a>

### 97. [Measuring Hallucination in Large Language Models for Cyber Threat Intelligence: An Exploratory Study](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25590v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25590

**作者：** Seungho Song、Donggyu Lee、Jaehan Kim、Jiyoung Choi、Jinwoo Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25590v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25590v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 HalluVision 框架，用微调 NER 抽取领域实体并结合多种相似度指标，在 4,940 篇网络安全文章中测量两个商用与一个开源 LLM 在 CTI 摘要和问答中的幻觉，并以 100 条人工标注和两个忠实性检测器验证；发现语义相似度与人类判断更一致，实体级指标一致性有限。

**相关性简评：** 其「语义度量优于实体级度量」的证据可供核查 LVR 中答案是否真正依据视觉证据、以及实体/属性级 grounding 指标可靠性的借鉴线索，但对象为纯文本 CTI，无 latent 状态或视觉通路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Measuring Hallucination in Large Language Models for Cyber Threat Intelligence: An Exploratory Study

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in cyber threat intelligence (CTI) tasks, such as summarization and question answering. However, they may produce hallucinations, false or misleading content not grounded in the source text, which can mislead analysts and corrupt downstream security decisions. This paper presents an exploratory measurement study of hallucinations in LLM-based CTI. We introduce HalluVision, a reproducible framework that generates LLM outputs, extracts domain-specific entities using a fine-tuned named entity recognition model, and evaluates factual consistency using multiple similarity metrics. Using 4,940 real-world cybersecurity articles, we measure hallucinations in outputs from two commercial LLMs and one open-weight LLM. Measurements are validated against 100 human-annotated outputs and two established faithfulness detectors. Results show that semantic similarity aligns with human judgments, whereas entity-based metrics exhibit limited agreement. Differences between models are concentrated in question answering, while entity extraction quality limits what entity-level metrics can reveal.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for publication in Computers and Electrical Engineering

</details>

<a id="paper-98"></a>

### 98. [From Data Alignment to Parameter Generation: Few-Shot Cross-City Traffic Prediction with Conditional Diffusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25592v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25592

**作者：** Shengchun Wang、Yuhang Lin、Tong Li、Pengyuan Zhou、Andy Chi Lok Tai、Wenjie Chen、Zhu Xiao、Zhu Han

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25592v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25592v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对少样本跨城市移动流量预测，提出 CDPG：不再对齐原始数据，而是学习源城市高性能预测器参数的条件分布，并利用卫星影像、LLM 语义描述与时间模式聚类构成的多模态上下文，为目标城市各基站直接生成参数。

**相关性简评：** 在参数空间而非数据空间做条件扩散生成，可供核查是否能迁移为「以视觉与语义证据为条件生成或更新 latent 推理状态」的机制，但其节点级参数生成与 LVR 的状态使用、证据筛选之间的关系仍待阅读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Data Alignment to Parameter Generation: Few-Shot Cross-City Traffic Prediction with Conditional Diffusion

**abstract/A1：** Accurate mobile traffic forecasting is crucial for network service management, supporting proactive capacity provisioning, congestion mitigation, and energy-efficient operation. However, many cities and regions are data-scarce (e.g., newly deployed or poorly instrumented areas), thus making traffic prediction a few-shot learning problem and limiting the practicality of standard data-driven models. Existing cross-city solutions primarily rely on data-space alignment to match traffic distributions between data-rich and data-scarce cities, but such alignment is often brittle under real-world distribution shifts, measurement noise, and city-specific bias. To address these limitations, we propose a Conditional Diffusion-based Parameter Generation (CDPG) framework. Instead of aligning raw data, CDPG learns the conditional distribution of high-performing predictor parameters for source cities and directly generates node-specific model parameters for the target city. To support reliable adaptation for network traffic prediction, CDPG conditions parameter generation on multi-modal spatio-temporal context. Specifically, CDPG captures spatial environment cues by combining satellite imagery with large language model (LLM)-derived semantic descriptions, and summarizes complex temporal behaviors via unsupervised pattern clustering. These contextual components guide CDPG in producing parameters tailored to each base station's operational context, thereby improving robustness under cross-city and cross-region shifts. Extensive experiments on three large-scale datasets show that CDPG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cross-city few-shot baselines by up to 9.72% on standard forecasting metrics and remains robust for few-shot multi-step prediction. The code is available at https://github.com/LingoodBoy/CDPG.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [Bradley-Terry Training Can Distort Outcome Probabilities of Initially Accurate Language World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25594v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25594

**作者：** Xinze Shi、Litian Zhang、Zhenjie Su、Zelong Yu、Yuting Zhang、Qihua Feng、Zifeng Kang、Chaozhuo Li、Zhengxuan Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25594v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25594v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文检验用 Bradley-Terry 目标从比较反馈训练语言世界模型是否会歪曲本已准确的结果概率：在模拟机器人推动任务中，该目标把 33–43 个百分点的概率质量转到枚举分支都产生不了的结果上，并定位为目标函数最优点所致而非比较数据本身。

**相关性简评：** 其「比较式偏好监督的最优解可能系统性偏离真实结果分布，而知晓评判规则的似然训练不会」的结论，可供核查 LVR 中以比较反馈或偏好目标监督 latent 状态与推理轨迹时的概率校准和贡献分配风险。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bradley-Terry Training Can Distort Outcome Probabilities of Initially Accurate Language World Models

**abstract/A1：** Comparative feedback, in which a judge says which of two candidate outcomes is closer to what happened, is a practical way to train language world models that assign probabilities to an action's outcomes. We show that, fitted with a Bradley-Terry objective, it need not teach the true outcome probabilities and can distort accurate ones. On a simulated robot push with enumerated outcome distributions, this objective takes a model that is accurate on unseen layouts (hazard error 2.4 percentage points, pp) and moves 33 to 43 pp of its probability onto outcomes that none of a condition's enumerated branches produces. The cause is the objective, not the comparisons: its optimum, computed before training, predicts where the model ends up, and a likelihood that knows the judge's rule learns from the same comparisons without the shift. The shift is robust: it recurs on unseen conditions, under five candidate laws and a three-fold denser enumeration, and about 29 pp remain without the physically impossible events. Its cost is conditional: with those events it inflates hazard probabilities, and a threshold inherited from the pre-feedback model costs about 3 pp of utility per decision on trained and 1 to 2 pp on unseen conditions, through false rejects; without them the utility loss averages below 1 pp over six seeds, though a small regret in action choice remains. Audits of readout, response and decision trace the effect from probabilities to actions.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures, 1 table

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [LoopedWAM: Looped World Action Models for Elastic Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25595v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25595

**作者：** Anh Dao、Xinxin Liu、Hang Yu、Jeffrey A Chan Santiago、Yuzhang Shang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25595v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25595v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LoopedWAM 与 LoopedWAM-E：视频与动作专家各自循环复用共享的六块核心，视觉特征每查询算一次而动作在每个采样步重复计算，并用弹性自蒸馏在动作深度与采样步预算上训练单一 checkpoint，以获得多个精度—延迟工作点。

**相关性简评：** 权重共享的循环深度与按模态不对称分配计算深度，可能为 LVR 中动态计算预算、迭代状态更新及长度/深度泛化的训练监督提供可核查线索，但其任务为机器人策略与视频世界模型，未涉及视觉语言推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LoopedWAM: Looped World Action Models for Elastic Inference

**abstract/A1：** World action models (WAMs) couple generative video modeling with robotic action prediction but typically require large video and action transformers, limiting deployment efficiency. We present LoopedWAM, a compact WAM that recurrently applies a shared six-block core in each expert, executing 30 transformer layers while storing only 12 blocks per expert. To account for their asymmetric inference costs, the video and action experts can operate at different depths: visual features are computed once per query, whereas action computation repeats at every sampling step. We further propose elastic self-distillation, which trains a single elastic checkpoint, LoopedWAM-E, across action-depth and sampling-step budgets. With only 0.58B policy parameters and no robot pretraining, LoopedWAM achieves 96.1% success on LIBERO, 91.9% on RoboTwin 2.0, and 52.2% on RoboCasaGR1, while using $7.4\\times$ less peak GPU memory than Fast-WAM under matched inference measurements. On LIBERO, LoopedWAM-E achieves 96.0% success in 27.6 ms or 95.2% in 24.7 ms on an H100, providing multiple accuracy-latency operating points from a single checkpoint; with two sampling steps and four action loops, it stays within 0.5 points of LoopedWAM on all three benchmarks. Ablations demonstrate that weight-shared depth preserves competitive performance, while aggressive action-depth reduction requires explicit budget-aware training. On a real AgileX PiPER arm, LoopedWAM succeeds in 71.3% of 80 trials across four tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [Decision-Focused Reinforcement Learning for LLM Post-Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25597v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25597

**作者：** Haotian Zhai、Leqi Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25597v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25597v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DFRL，以下游决策后悔的负值作为奖励对 LLM 做后训练，使其预测的优化参数直接降低决策成本；并用近似奖励热启动与随机精确评估的奖励校正来减少昂贵奖励求解。

**相关性简评：** 其「廉价近似奖励加少量精确验证」的思路可能可供核查 LVR 中推理轨迹监督、贡献分配与验证成本之间的权衡，但需确认能否迁移到视觉证据筛选或 STOP 决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decision-Focused Reinforcement Learning for LLM Post-Training

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise as predictors across various tasks, benefiting from their ability to use information from unstructured context. However, when predictions serve as parameters of downstream optimization problems, higher prediction accuracy does not necessarily translate into lower decision costs. We propose decision-focused reinforcement learning (DFRL), a framework for post-training LLMs to predict optimization parameters using negative downstream decision regret as reward. DFRL trains LLM predictors to minimize the costs of the decisions induced by their predictions, without requiring downstream convexity or differentiability, and integrates readily into existing RL post-training frameworks. To address expensive reward evaluation, we develop two approaches. Heuristic warm-starting uses inexpensive approximate rewards during an initial phase before switching to refinement with exact rewards. Reward correction combines inexpensive estimates with randomized exact evaluations, preserving the expected policy gradient while reducing the expected number of exact solves per update. Experiments on three synthetic optimization problems demonstrate improvements over prediction-loss baselines, while a larger knapsack experiment shows faster early improvement under short training budgets. On inventory planning with H&amp;M transactions and product descriptions, our best configuration, in which the LLM reasons before predicting, reduces regret by 31.4% relative to the SFT baseline and 12.4% relative to the strongest evaluated conventional forecasting baseline.

</details>

<a id="paper-102"></a>

### 102. [PSTO: A PDE-structured transport operator with shared implicit updates for multiscale CDR systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25607v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25607

**作者：** Kaiwen Li、Dezhi Zhou、Kun Wu、Xingcai Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25607v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25607v1>)
