**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PSTO，将 PDE 结构嵌入网络，仅用无反应数据学习对流—扩散动力学，并用单一共享输运算符块配合松弛不动点迭代在 latent 空间做隐式式修正，以在多步递归预测中保持稳定，在三个多尺度 CDR 基准上优于神经基线。

**相关性简评：** 其跨变量共享的迭代细化块与 latent 空间不动点修正是可核查的方法线索，可能对应 LVR 中循环/共享计算的状态更新与长程 rollout 稳定性问题，但属科学计算场景，迁移到视觉推理尚需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PSTO: A PDE-structured transport operator with shared implicit updates for multiscale CDR systems

**abstract/A1：** Multiscale convection-diffusion-reaction (CDR) systems couple spatial transport with local stiff reaction kinetics, making their numerical simulation computationally demanding. Conventional neural surrogates require costly coupled CDR training data, and their parameter counts scale with the number of transported variables, limiting scalability to high-dimensional CDR systems. Here we propose a PDE-Structured Transport Operator (PSTO) that learns convection--diffusion dynamics solely from reaction-free data. By embedding the PDE structure into the neural architecture, PSTO generalizes to full CDR systems at inference without retraining. A single transport operator block is shared across all transported variables and refined through relaxed fixed-point iterations, performing an implicit-like correction in latent space. This improves stability during recursive multi-step prediction without adding trainable parameters. We evaluate PSTO on three benchmarks: a canonical 2D CDR system, a multiscale Robertson counterflow flame, and a multiscale hydrogen planar jet flame. In every case, PSTO reduces the mean relative $L\_2$ error after 100 coupled steps by at least 50% compared to the strongest neural baseline. Ablation studies on the fixed-point iterations and detailed error decomposition further confirm that PSTO achieves both high one-step accuracy and long-term stability in coupled rollouts. Time-step sensitivity analysis shows that PSTO maintains accuracy even with a tenfold larger time step, whereas baseline accuracies decay rapidly as the time step increases. This framework enables efficient, modular modeling of multiscale CDR dynamics by separating transport learning from the treatment of local kinetics.

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [Can an LLM Fix a Language Model Without Training It?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25613v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25613

**作者：** Bharath Kumar Bolla、Raghavender Surya Upadhayula、Vishnu Surya Reddy Nandi、Ramesh Nandipalli

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25613v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25613v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在受控的 Transformer 修复任务中，让外部 LLM 依据预测、激活摘要和权重统计选择无梯度的投影编辑，并与固定投影、脚本化及等预算黑盒搜索、LoRA 对比，评估交互选择是否带来增益。

**相关性简评：** 「外部控制器读取激活摘要后决定干预」这一范式可能为 LVR 中由控制器检查并修改 latent 推理状态提供可核查线索；但该作无视觉输入、目标是权重修复而非推理状态生成，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can an LLM Fix a Language Model Without Training It?

**abstract/A1：** Although an external language model can select and test repairs to another model's weights, behavior can also be recovered through fixed numerical edits. We assessed the value of interactive selection in a controlled study of Transformer repair. After inspecting predictions, activation summaries, and weight statistics, the editor selects projection recipes to apply to the target without gradient updates. We analyzed 20 recorded sessions involving five masked and causal language models, comparing the selected edits with fixed projections, scripted and matched-budget black-box search, and low-rank adaptation. Under the core two-layer fault, live selection matches black-box search on SmolLM2 and Qwen2.5. For Pythia, live selection achieves 65.62% completion accuracy, compared with 0% for black-box search and 66.15% for a fixed projection. Under a separate compound fault affecting attention and feed-forward weights, both scripted and LLM-guided selection increase the descriptive five-checkpoint mean from 24.89% to 71.25% using the same operator family. On unmodified checkpoints, scripted projection yields small gains or losses on a separate set of framed questions. The experiments identify cases in which feedback changes an edit decision, operator coverage accounts for recovery, or supervised adaptation performs better. Combined with measurements of parameter restoration and preservation, these comparisons establish the scope of controller-guided repair for the tested faults and completion tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [Don't Lose the Future: Latent-Aware 4-Bit Quantization of World Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25614v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25614

**作者：** Yichen Guo、Yanlong Chen、Xinyue Zhang、Mohan Liu、Song Zhang、Yawei Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25614v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25614v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 LAQ，面向世界动作模型（WAM）的 4-bit 权重量化框架，用衡量未来误差对动作影响的「reader metric」与按去噪步读取次数加权的「read count」闭式分配激活与 KV cache 精度，并对动作关注的 token 在去噪与缓存中做第二遍 4-bit 精修，以保住预测未来。

**相关性简评：** 其按下游读取次数与动作影响来分配表示精度的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态保真、信息坍缩诊断与视觉证据筛选提供核查线索；但对象是机器人策略量化，非 VLM 推理，联系需较多判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Don't Lose the Future: Latent-Aware 4-Bit Quantization of World Action Models

**abstract/A1：** World action models (WAMs) predict how a scene will evolve and condition their actions on this predicted future. Deploying them on a robot's onboard computer calls for 4-bit weights, activations and key-value caches (W4A4KV4). Existing 4-bit quantizers for WAMs preserve the generated action, and estimate sensitivity one denoising step at a time. However, they fail to preserve the fidelity of the predicted future: once the action pathway is protected, the remaining action error enters mostly through a degraded future that none of them deliberately protects. We propose LAQ, a latent-aware quantization framework that preserves the predicted future where it is written, stored and read. Specifically, we introduce a reader metric, which measures future error by its effect on the action, and a read count, which weights each rounding error by the number of denoising steps that read it. Together they give the allocation of precision over activations and caches a closed-form solution. We further develop gaze-guided refinement, which gives the tokens the action attends to a second 4-bit pass in the denoising loop and in the cache. All weights stay at 4 bits, with no high-precision branch and no per-task recalibration. On the LIBERO-Plus benchmark, LAQ reaches 76.4% and 83.3% average success on Fast-WAM and Cosmos-Policy, within 1.2 percentage points of the 16-bit models and all above the baselines with 16-bit KV caches. It compresses the model by 3.8x, reduces the peak GPU memory of the whole policy by 2.3x, and runs 1.66x faster than the 16-bit model. On a real AgileX PiPER arm, it reaches 47.0% average success over three tasks, against 52.0% for the 16-bit model and 30.0% higher than QuaRot.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [Physical Laws without Physics Loss? Probing Learned Dynamics in Video World Models through Forced Response](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25626v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25626

**作者：** Yutao Li、Chen Wang、Wangmeng Zuo、Hui Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25626v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25626v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用受迫响应实验（激励悬吊物体支撑）配合视觉-数值双条件，在仅用 flow-matching loss 的情况下改造六个图像到视频骨干，检验其能否预测未见的激励、物体与位姿，并以基于 Taylor 展开的三级测试区分线性响应、共振与高阶非线性动力学（如软化），最后扩展到双摆与摇摆块。

**相关性简评：** 其受迫响应、反事实测试与分阶动力学探针，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否保留任务必要的物理量，以及状态估计与传播失败如何导致内部推理偏离，可作为诊断表示与状态更新的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physical Laws without Physics Loss? Probing Learned Dynamics in Video World Models through Forced Response

**abstract/A1：** Do video world models learn physical laws without a physics loss, or merely imitate motion? Overall motion and appearance scores cannot settle it. We investigate this through nonlinear forced response: an excitation shakes the support of a suspended object. We introduce dual visual-numeric conditioning and adapt six image-to-video backbones to predict this motion using only the flow-matching loss. Wan predicts motion accurately and generalizes to unseen excitations, objects, poses, and scenes. Counterfactual tests suggest capabilities beyond replaying training videos. But what laws have they learned? We design three tiers of tests using Taylor expansions of the dynamics and geometry. All six reproduce the linear response, including resonance; five capture higher-order dynamics, including nonlinear softening. Remarkably, softening is barely present in training: only 0.74% of the swing's squared displacement. Extensions to a double pendulum and a rocking block show sustained dynamics are learned more faithfully than brief impacts. Yet learned dynamics may fail to appear when state estimation or propagation goes wrong. We turn these tests and findings into strategies to improve video training and generation.

</details>

<a id="paper-106"></a>

### 106. [NIMIS: A Structure-Agnostic, Chemically Informed Slot-Attention Framework for Demixing 1D $^1$H-NMR Mixture Spectra](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25629v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25629

**作者：** Anna Marija Sumrova、Jonathan M. Goodman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25629v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25629v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 NIMIS，将一维 1H-NMR 混合谱中的峰按来源组分分组：把 J-coupling 信息注入多头 Transformer 注意力的峰嵌入，再用 slot attention 分解为组分，并以约 15.8 万条合成混合谱训练。

**相关性简评：** 「把结构先验写入注意力 + slot attention 形成离散组分」的思路，可能为 LVR 中分离重叠视觉证据、把任务必要结构信息注入 latent 槽位提供可核查的方法线索，但其绑定机制能否迁移仍待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NIMIS: A Structure-Agnostic, Chemically Informed Slot-Attention Framework for Demixing 1D $^1$H-NMR Mixture Spectra

**abstract/A1：** The products of chemical reactions are usually complex mixtures that must be separated into their components before interpretable $^1$H-NMR spectra can be obtained. Direct analysis of the spectra of crude mixtures would accelerate reaction analysis and facilitate the exploration of new chemical space. We present NIMIS, a structure-agnostic, attention-based deep learning framework that groups the peaks of a 1D $^1$H-NMR mixture spectrum according to the component from which they originate. J-coupling information is introduced directly into the attention mechanism of a multi-head Transformer encoder, and the resulting contextualised peak embeddings are separated into components by slot attention. NIMIS was trained on 158,005 synthetic mixtures constructed from experimental spectra in the NMRexp database, covering molecules of up to 20 heavy atoms composed of C, H, N, O, S, F, Cl, Br and I. On held-out molecules it reaches a peak-to-component assignment accuracy of 82.28% for noise-free input and 77.34% with 10% integral noise, with a mean NIMIS score of 0.83 for correct assignments. NIMIS outperforms a conventional Transformer and simple baselines, and its performance carries over to a scaffold-disjoint evaluation. By grouping peaks into component-level sets, NIMIS provides a foundation for more robust mixture analysis methods, including methods capable of handling overlapping signals.

</details>

<a id="paper-107"></a>

### 107. [MambaLIFT: Reference-Based Mechanism-Aware Attribution for VMamba](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25644v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25644

**作者：** Jintu Kakati、Niladri B. Puhan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25644v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25644v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MambaLIFT，一个面向 VMamba 的参考基、乘性优先归因框架，把参考相对类 logit 差按空间位置、VSS 块、扫描方向和计算机制分解，并在 ImageNet-1K 上以像素替换下的忠实度等指标评测。

**相关性简评：** 其对选择性扫描、循环状态传播与共享通路的贡献分解，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与贡献分配提供可核查的归因思路；文中亦明确机制分解不等于干预因果效应，可作为因果归因主张的边界参考。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MambaLIFT: Reference-Based Mechanism-Aware Attribution for VMamba

**abstract/A1：** Attributing predictions of visual state-space models remains challenging because input-dependent selective dynamics couple feature selection, recurrent state propagation, and shared computational pathways. We introduce MambaLIFT, a reference-based, multiplier-first attribution framework for VMamba that propagates contributions through its computational graph using interaction-aware rules for selective-scan injection, recurrent memory, LayerNorm, and shared pathways. MambaLIFT decomposes the reference-relative class-logit difference, \\(f\_c(x)-f\_c(x^0)\\), by spatial position, VSS block, scan direction, and computational mechanism. Experiments on ImageNet-1K evaluate VMamba-T and VMamba-B, with hyperparameters selected on a calibration set and faithfulness assessed on a disjoint held-out evaluation set. MambaLIFT achieves numerical conservation to floating-point precision across audited attribution interfaces in both configurations. Under mean-colour pixel flipping, it achieves significantly higher mean faithfulness scores than MambaLRP on both configurations and the highest scores among the evaluated methods, including Integrated Gradients with matched references. Under blurred replacement, MambaLIFT is non-inferior to MambaLRP on VMamba-T and equivalent on VMamba-B, indicating that its advantage depends on the replacement protocol. Ablations on VMamba-T show that interaction allocation, temporal propagation, and pathway composition affect attribution maps. Mechanism-level analyses further show that exact relevance conservation does not imply additive effects under finite interventions: the mechanism decomposition accounts for the reference-relative logit difference but does not estimate intervention effects or establish causal validity.

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [SEAF: Selective Semantic Enrichment and Visual Evidence Fusion for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25672v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25672

**作者：** Faxian Wan、Xiaocui Yang、Yifan Cao、Shi Feng、Daling Wang、Yifei Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25672v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25672v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SEAF 双分支框架做多图多模态讽刺检测：把冻结 MLLM 生成的实体背景用于语义分支（与原文、OCR 文本融合），图像描述只提供跨模态与图间对齐统计，视觉分支融合全局与局部特征，并以 TASA 按图文相似度加权图像后相加两分支得分。

**相关性简评：** 其按图文相似度加权、并刻意保留低字面相似图像贡献的视觉证据筛选方式，可能可供核查 LVR 中多视觉证据的筛选与支持组合；但该工作落点是分类任务而非 latent 推理状态生成，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SEAF: Selective Semantic Enrichment and Visual Evidence Fusion for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal sarcasm detection requires textual, visual, and contextual evidence, particularly when multiple images provide complementary cues. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate auxiliary information from a post's text and images, but their outputs may mix useful background knowledge with redundant visual descriptions. We propose SEAF, a dual-branch framework for selective semantic enrichment and visual evidence fusion. SEAF assigns generated fields to distinct roles: entity background enriches semantic input, while image descriptions supply alignment statistics. The semantic branch combines the original post text, background from a frozen MLLM, and optical character recognition (OCR) text. The aligned visual branch combines global and local visual features with statistics of cross-modal and inter-image similarity. Text-Anchored Smoothed Aggregation (TASA) weights images by their similarity to the original post text while preserving contributions from images with low literal similarity. Summing the branch classification scores integrates contextual semantics with visual evidence. Experiments on the multi-image MMSD3.0 benchmark show that SEAF outperforms all compared methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [Explainability of genotype embedding models through observed cohort paths](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25681v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25681

**作者：** Amashi Niwarthana、Aloysius Tan、Jagath C. Rajapakse

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25681v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25681v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文指出基因型嵌入模型在分数基因型处无定义，使 Integrated Gradients 的直线路径失效，转而沿训练队列观测到的基因型路径在嵌入空间做分段积分，以提升归因的完备性与忠实度。

**相关性简评：** 其「不可插值状态间需沿有效路径归因」的思路可能为 latent 推理轨迹的贡献分配与支持集约束提供核查线索，但对象是基因组嵌入归因，与 VLM 推理尚无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Explainability of genotype embedding models through observed cohort paths

**abstract/A1：** Deep learning models that combine genotypes with neuroimaging predict neurological diseases more accurately than either modality alone. Their clinical use, however, requires identifying which genetic variants a prediction is based on. Path-based attribution methods like Integrated Gradients address this by integrating the model's gradient from a baseline to the input. These methods assume the model accepts any point along that path as a valid input. Genotype models violate this assumption. They map the three discrete allele dosages \\{0,1,2\\} to vectors through a learned embedding defined only at those values, so a straight line passes through fractional genotypes at which no embedding, and therefore no gradient, exists. Attribution must therefore proceed in embedding space where the choice of path can affect the resulting attribution. We show that the commonly used per-variant estimator does not generally satisfy completeness. Additive separability guarantees completeness for this estimator, but interactions between variants generally break that property. Instead, we integrate along piecewise-linear embedding-space paths connecting genotype waypoints observed in the training cohort. Each path satisfies completeness in the continuum although the interiors of its segments are not necessarily observed. Our method improves faithfulness over Integrated Gradients and discretized Integrated Gradients under both perturbation protocols, achieves the strongest results for Parkinson's disease classification under support-preserving perturbations, and is competitive with a support-background Shapley baseline under per-variant perturbations. It also shows higher seed stability and better recovery of planted non-additive effects in a synthetic benchmark.

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [DMT-Attack: Diffusion-Prior Texture Search for Closed-Loop Vision-Language-Action Policies](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25695v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25695

**作者：** Jun Xia、Shangwen Wang、Jun Ma、Ruichao Ren、Zhouyang Jia、Xin Zhao、Huijun Liu、Jie Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25695v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25695v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DMT-Attack：以冻结的文本条件扩散模型界定自然纹理搜索空间，用八个专家组成的 latent 提议网络从教师 latent 初始化、并借闭环 rollout 反馈精化候选，为桌布等普通物体生成自然外观纹理，在四个 LIBERO 套件上攻击 pi\_0.5 与 OpenVLA-OFT，取得高于优化 patch 基线的攻击成功率且更难被检测。

**相关性简评：** 其「latent 提议网络 + 闭环 rollout 反馈筛选候选」的梯度无关搜索流程，可供核查能否迁移到 LVR 的多 latent 假设生成与视觉证据筛选；同时结果显示仅自然纹理即可扰乱 VLA 的视觉反馈与闭环决策，或为视觉证据依赖的诊断线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DMT-Attack: Diffusion-Prior Texture Search for Closed-Loop Vision-Language-Action Policies

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language-action (VLA) policies rely on visual feedback throughout manipulation, making them vulnerable to persistent texture attacks. However, optimized patterns can stand out from their surroundings, making an attack easier to notice and its carrier more likely to be inspected or removed. To address this limitation, our key insight is to search the structured image distribution of a diffusion model for natural-looking textures while using closed-loop feedback to identify task-disruptive candidates. Based on this idea, we propose DMT-Attack, a gradient-free gray-box attack that generates textures for ordinary scene objects such as tablecloths. A frozen text-conditioned diffusion model defines the search space, and an eight-expert latent proposal network is initialized from evaluated teacher latents and refined using rollout feedback. We evaluate DMT-Attack against pi\_0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT across four LIBERO suites. DMT-Attack achieves a macro clean-conditioned attack success rate (cASR) of 57.9%, compared with 44.7% for UPA-RFAS and 19.7% for random natural textures. It also attains higher scene similarity and a lower clean-calibrated detector flag rate (45% versus 97.5-100% for optimized patch baselines). These results demonstrate that natural-looking textures can disrupt VLA visual feedback and closed-loop decision-making, motivating defenses that jointly assess visual appearance and task behavior.

</details>

<a id="paper-111"></a>

### 111. [Slide2PPT: Native PowerPoint Reconstruction with Localization-Guided Refinement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25708v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25708

**作者：** Baoliang Chen、Xinlong Bu、Lingyu Zhu、Xiangjie Sui

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25708v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25708v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练、模型无关的 Slide2PPT 框架：VLM 经统一类型化接口输出对象记录，由固定渲染器生成原生 PPTX，并用定位引导的局部/整页迭代细化，只更新选中记录。

**相关性简评：** 其「定位差异—选择性更新状态记录—渲染反馈验收」闭环可能与 LVR 的视觉证据筛选、状态局部更新与停止决策可供核查地对应，但任务为幻灯片重建，不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Slide2PPT: Native PowerPoint Reconstruction with Localization-Guided Refinement

**abstract/A1：** Image-to-slide reconstruction must recover both visual appearance and native object structure for subsequent editing. Yet visually faithful charts and tables may lack editable series and cells, and direct code generation couples content prediction to rendering implementation. We present Slide2PPT, a model-agnostic, training-free framework that equips both open- and closed-source vision-language models with native PowerPoint reconstruction capabilities. Through a unified typed interface, the model predicts object records while a fixed renderer materializes their native properties, enabling integration with different backbones without task-specific training or architectural changes. Localization-guided refinement then connects visual discrepancies to object records for correction. Regional updates preserve unselected records, while whole-slide updates address cross-region relationships. Record refinement and targeted object editing support structural and attribute-level changes, with rendered feedback governing iterative acceptance. To evaluate this framework across diverse reconstruction demands, we introduce Slide2PPT-Bench, covering controlled elements, real slides, and scientific figures. Evaluation separately measures file production, rendered agreement, and native-object availability. Experiments across open- and closed-source backbones demonstrate substantially more reliable PPTX generation than direct code generation, improved layout and text recovery on scientific figures, and native chart and table interfaces beyond geometric primitives.

</details>

<a id="paper-112"></a>

### 112. [Calibrate in Context: Efficient Data-Free Quantization from Small Synthetic Pools](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25711v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25711

**作者：** Haowen Hua、Sen Lin、Yaokun Huang、Zeyi Shao、Hao Zhang、Wei Sui、John See、Zeyd Boukhers、Cong Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25711v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25711v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究 ViT 的小样本合成池无数据量化：用学生网络当前 block 输入的完整精度响应做 Functional Grid Calibration 选择激活尺度与整数偏移，并用渐进反演加深教师前缀来更新同一批合成图像，在 W4A4/W5A5 上以 256–1000 张图取得较好恢复精度。

**相关性简评：** 其以 block 上下文响应误差而非局部误差来挑选量化参数，可能为低比特压缩下保持 latent 视觉状态的功能保真提供可核查的校准与误差度量线索，但论文任务并非多模态推理，迁移价值需读原文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Calibrate in Context: Efficient Data-Free Quantization from Small Synthetic Pools

**abstract/A1：** We enable accurate low-bit recovery from small synthetic pools by improving both image synthesis and student preparation. A grid with lower local response error can incur greater error in the student's block context. Functional Grid Calibration (FGC) selects activation scales and integer offsets against full-precision responses constructed from the student's current block input. With weights fixed during calibration, FGC evaluates candidates only at their consuming operators, bringing block context to calibration at nearly local-scoring cost. For DeiT and ViT, progressive inversion updates the same images through increasingly deep teacher prefixes. With 1,000 images, the complete pipeline exceeds MimiQ's reported accuracy in all 18 W4A4/W5A5 settings across nine backbones. With 256 images, DeiT-S and ViT-S reach 68.20% and 66.90% ImageNet top-1 accuracy at W4A4 in 26-27 minutes on one RTX 5090. The pipeline outperforms strengthened 1K-image MimiQ by 13.03 and 24.36 percentage points at lower preparation cost and the same recovery-update count. The same low-budget checkpoints retain their advantage on ImageNetV2.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 5 main figures. Supplementary material included

</details>

<a id="paper-113"></a>

### 113. [SteerTrace: Tracing How Target Attacks Steer World Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25719v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25719

**作者：** Yukun Dai、Tianshi Wang、Fengling Li、Jingjing Li、Xin Li、Lei Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25719v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25719v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SteerTrace，一种针对世界动作模型的通用目标攻击：单个共享扰动即可把执行动作推向指定目标，并通过恢复未来 token、当前帧 token、少数 FFN 神经元和残差方向来测量各内部成分对攻击效应的贡献。

**相关性简评：** 其「有符号目标效应 + 逐层恢复内部状态」的因果归因流程，可能可供核查 LVR 中哪些 latent 或视觉证据承载答案相关贡献、贡献如何在 token 与神经元间分配；但研究对象是动作模型的对抗攻击，与多模态推理仅方法层面相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SteerTrace: Tracing How Target Attacks Steer World Action Models

**abstract/A1：** World action models (WAMs) predict future videos and actions with one backbone, and their imagined future is often seen as a safety signal. We introduce SteerTrace, a targeted universal attack on WAMs that also traces its effect inside the model. One $\\ell\_\\infty$-bounded (4/255) perturbation per model, shared across frames, tasks, and camera views, pushes the executed action toward an attacker-chosen target. On eight WAMs from four families (FastWAM, Faster-WAM, OpenWAM and LingBot-VA) on LIBERO and RoboTwin, it drives task success close to zero and moves the attacked action toward the target. Same-budget noise and BadWAM produce little action shift in the target direction. We use the signed targeted effect to measure how much of the effect each intervention removes or reproduces. Under the standard attack, restoring future tokens removes 61-94% of the targeted effect in the five models whose action experts attend to them. When the attack is constrained to keep the predicted future close to the clean prediction, the targeted effect can become more dependent on current-frame tokens. In three of these five models, restoring current-frame tokens removes 87-99% of the effect, compared with 9-15% for future tokens. In six models, restoring just 0.36% of the FFN neurons in layers 10-29 of the video expert removes 72-96% of the targeted effect. Residual directions 2-16 hold only 11-18% of the shift energy, yet removing them cancels most of the targeted effect.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 5 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-114"></a>

### 114. [MemMend: A Selective Revision Framework for Memory Question Answering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25721v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25721

**作者：** Sheng Shi、Geng Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25721v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25721v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** MemMend 提出模块化的选择性修订框架：先用路由器判断长时记忆问答的答案是否可能不完整，只把可疑答案交给编辑器，并以带可解析来源坐标的有界精确文本替换作为接受条件，从而在 LoCoMo 等基准上以更少的修订调用维持或提升答案质量。

**相关性简评：** 其「是否触发修订」的门控与来源约束、编辑成本核算，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/STOP 的收益—成本权衡与证据筛选提供可核查的方法线索，但本文是纯文本记忆问答，未涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MemMend: A Selective Revision Framework for Memory Question Answering

**abstract/A1：** Revising every answer from a long-term memory can consume substantial inference budget and damage facts that were already correct. We introduce MemMend, a modular selective revision framework that routes potentially incomplete answers to an editor, represents corrections as bounded exact-text replacements, and accepts only edits with resolvable source coordinates and valid edit operations. The editor preserves the current answer when no legal repair is proposed; accepted edits require no additional semantic-verification call. On 1,986 LoCoMo development questions, MemMend achieves 67.17 conversation-macro F1 and 88.35 mean Judge score, improving over a verified revision chain by 0.82 and 1.24 points while using 62.37% fewer revision calls. Routing uses 54.15% fewer revision calls than ungated local review, with similar F1 but a 0.67-point lower Judge score. Applied directly to native PropMem, Mem0, and SimpleMem answers, the editing interface raises F1 by 3.79, 6.44, and 0.42 points. It outperforms same-gate rewriting and saves 49.85-83.72% of editing calls relative to ungated editing, which retains higher F1 in this adaptation. A source-by-editing factorial supports local editing for F1 but leaves an independent quality benefit from source obligations unresolved. Transfer to 451 LongMemEval-V2 Small questions also favors revision efficiency, while an external PerLTQA dialogue subset reveals a completeness gap. These results support selective editing as a reusable quality-cost operating point.

</details>

<a id="paper-115"></a>

### 115. [Verifying Before Enhancing: Target Evidence Chain Verification for Infrared Small Target Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25724v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25724

**作者：** Jiayi Zuo、Songwei Pei、Shuhuai Wang、Yifei Wu、Shangguang Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25724v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25724v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VBE-UNet，主张「先验证再增强」：用反事实背景解释建模局部目标证据，并以跨尺度支持与基于扰动的贡献验证共同给出候选可靠性，据此做选择性特征重组，在三个红外小目标数据集上提升精度并降低虚警。

**相关性简评：** 其跨尺度证据聚合与扰动式贡献验证为「响应强度不足以判定证据支持」提供了具体机制，可能可供核查 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与 latent 内容贡献分配；但该工作是单图检测，缺少生成式推理状态与答案使用环节。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verifying Before Enhancing: Target Evidence Chain Verification for Infrared Small Target Detection

**abstract/A1：** Infrared small target detection is essential for early warning, surveillance, remote sensing, and space-based target perception. However, infrared small targets are typically tiny, weakly textured, and boundary-blurred, making them difficult to distinguish from complex backgrounds. Moreover, target-like background structures can produce salient responses across multiple scales, making response strength alone insufficient for reliable target discrimination. To address this challenge, we propose VBE-UNet, a Target Evidence Chain Verification Network that assesses candidate reliability beyond response magnitude through complementary evidence cues. Specifically, counterfactual background explanation models local target evidence, while joint evidence verification integrates cross-scale support and perturbation-based contribution verification to guide feature recomposition. The resulting reliability estimates guide selective feature refinement, promoting target-relevant responses while reducing unsupported background activations. Extensive experiments on IRSTD-1K, NUDT-SIRST, and NUAA-SIRST demonstrate that VBE-UNet achieves superior detection accuracy and lower false-alarm rates compared with state-of-the-art methods. Ablation studies and evidence reliability analysis further validate the effectiveness of the proposed verification framework.

</details>

<a id="paper-116"></a>

### 116. [Towards High-Fidelity Learned Video Compression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25731v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25731

**作者：** Bumyoon Kim、Yongseong Kim、Inhyuk Jeong、Byeungwoo Jeon

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25731v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25731v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究学习式视频压缩在高保真区间的覆盖能力，提出编码器结合块运动搜索与学习块变换搜索（含分区、skip 决策与量化系数率失真选择）的帧间编解码器。

**相关性简评：** 其关于固定 latent 容量限制质量、以及通过搜索选择 latent 分区与量化系数的分析，可能为 LVR 中的 latent 容量分配与证据筛选提供借鉴线索，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards High-Fidelity Learned Video Compression

**abstract/A1：** Professional video workflows operate at qualities that learned video codecs do not reach. We measure how much of a professional quality range a codec covers, from the lowest quality of the standard test conditions, VVC at QP 37, to the highest 4:2:2 grade of the production codec ProRes. To compare inter-frame coding alone, every codec receives original frames as references at the intra positions. On 10-bit YCbCr 4:2:2 test sequences, four recent learned codecs cover 24 to 38% of this range. Their encoders are feed-forward networks with a fixed latent capacity, the number of latent values per sample, of at most 0.25, and removing their quantization raises the quality by at most 1 dB. We propose a learned inter-frame codec whose encoder searches, as the encoders of conventional codecs do. It searches block motion, which a learned network refines, and it searches among learned block transforms whose latent capacity ranges up to 1, selecting the partition, the skip decisions, and the quantized coefficients by a rate-distortion cost. Our codec reaches the upper end of the range on all six sequences. With all residual blocks fixed to 16x16, whose latent capacity is 0.25, the same codec covers 55% of the range, with or without quantization.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-117"></a>

### 117. [PosteriorBench: Evaluating Behavior Posterior Sampling in Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25735v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25735

**作者：** Zhiyang Xun、Daniel D. Johnson、Sarah Schwettmann、Neil Chowdhury

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25735v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25735v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PosteriorBench，在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 上用 21 个行为概率从 1e-5 到 1e-1 的任务，配有十亿级标注响应中的精确后验样本，按计算成本与正反向 KL 距离比较 prompt、后验样本微调等算法的后验采样效果，发现没有方法同时做到高效与忠实。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身针对文本稀有行为而非视觉推理，但其对「稀有事件后验采样」算法与成本-保真度权衡的度量方式，可能为 LVR 中多种 latent 假设的采样筛选和轨迹获取提供方法线索，值得核查这些采样器能否用于 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PosteriorBench: Evaluating Behavior Posterior Sampling in Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Rare language model behaviors can be difficult to study because obtaining them through ordinary sampling can require a very large number of samples. This motivates the behavior posterior sampling problem: given a language model and a behavior of interest, is there an algorithm that efficiently samples from the model's distribution over transcripts that exhibit this behavior? We introduce PosteriorBench, a benchmark for comparing algorithms by their compute cost and by how close their samples are to the true posterior in forward and reverse KL divergence. PosteriorBench contains 21 tasks on Qwen3.5-0.8B with behavior probabilities from $10^{-5}$ to $10^{-1}$, each with exact posterior samples from a corpus of one billion labeled responses. We find that no method we evaluate is both efficient and faithful: simple methods like prompting are cheap but plateau at a high KL divergence from the posterior, fine-tuning on posterior samples is often cost-prohibitive, and several training methods from prior work do not perform better. Building a posterior sampler that can cheaply approximate the true posterior remains an open problem, and we hope PosteriorBench can measure progress towards this objective. Code and data are available at https://github.com/TransluceAI/PosteriorBench.

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [Disentangling Monitor Hardware Artifacts and Smoothing Lag for Shift-Robust Intraoperative EEG and Hemodynamic Risk Calibration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25765v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25765

**作者：** Zeyu Jia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25765v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25765v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向术中 EEG 与血压监护仪的阻抗脉冲、平滑延迟与直流漂移伪迹，提出因果信号调理加漂移鲁棒 conformal 校准框架，并在 5,439 例麻醉手术上比较树集成与深度序列模型。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉 latent 推理无直接关联；其分组加权 conformal 风险控制与延迟缓冲的在线 PID 校准，可能为 VLM 停止决策的风险上界以及「延迟 vs 状态内容」的区分提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Disentangling Monitor Hardware Artifacts and Smoothing Lag for Shift-Robust Intraoperative EEG and Hemodynamic Risk Calibration

**abstract/A1：** Commercial intraoperative electroencephalography (EEG) and blood pressure monitors introduce cyclic impedance pulses, smoothing delays, direct-current (DC) drift, and cuff holds that confound risk forecasting and uncertainty calibration. We developed a causal signal-conditioning and shift-robust conformal calibration framework across 5,439 public anesthesia procedures. Streaming filters blank 10.13-min 64-Hz impedance checks, apply unclipped post-notch conditioning, separate 63-s suppression-ratio lag, and distinguish three-channel cuff inflations from dropouts across propofol-remifentanil total intravenous anesthesia (TIVA; N = 3,214), volatile anesthesia (N = 2,046), European endoscopy sedation (N = 171), and DC-coupled surgery (N = 8). De-impedanced cortical tree ensembles were compared against deep sequence models, paired with patient-cluster Subgroup-Covariate-Weighted Conformal Risk Control (CRC) and lag-buffered online Conformal Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) tracking. Pre-filter blanking removed 97.90% and 97.60% of false flatline conflicts while preserving 99.23% and 98.96% of deep suppression. On unseen TIVA surgeries (N = 525), cortical features achieved area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) 0.3135 (1.94% prevalence) and 0.1627 (0.82% lag-free prevalence), with no detectable difference from deep encoders (p &gt;= 0.36) and no cortical gain for hypotension (0.3432 vs. 0.3437, p = 0.784). Zero-retraining volatile transfer (N = 1,048) showed no detectable difference from an end-tidal gas oracle on lag-free suppression (0.0820 vs. 0.0736, p = 0.472). Subgroup-Covariate-Weighted CRC bounded all 70 TIVA shift cells at &lt;= 4.38% (alpha = 5.0%) in 66.32 ms per 30-s epoch. Causal artifact disentangling and shift-aware conformal calibration support reliable real-time intraoperative risk bounds within explicit physiological observability limits.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [Duopra: From Full-Duplex Conversation to Full-Duplex Agency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25773v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25773

**作者：** Chiyu Chen、Pengzhou Cheng、Yuezhao Ma、Xiaocheng Li、Can Liu、Haonan Hu、Yao Yao、Yifei Yang、Yijie Lu、Taolei Wang、Yansi Li、Muhuan Wu、Chenglu Sun、Gongshen Liu、Zhuosheng Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25773v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25773v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Duopra，一个全双工语音交互模型，在对话控制之外同时学习任务执行：把后端当作工具并接受自包含自然语言请求，通过协议 SFT、奖励及时与完整执行的全双工 RL，以及 thinker 更新后仅修 projector 的三阶段训练，提升 FDB v3 上的工具调用成功率。

**相关性简评：** 其「何时发起请求、请求是否自包含完整」的判定与第三阶段的 projector-only 修复，可能映射到 LVR 中的停止/证据获取决策与思考-读出模块间的贡献分配，但语音全双工场景与视觉推理差异较大，线索需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Duopra: From Full-Duplex Conversation to Full-Duplex Agency

**abstract/A1：** Full-duplex interaction models exhibit strong conversational agency, enabling natural, low-latency turn-taking, backchannels, and interruptions. However, completing tasks further requires task agency: timely tool invocation and argument revision following user corrections. Frontend-backend agents separate initiation from execution: the frontend decides whether and what to request, while the backend determines how. Execution can be offloaded, but unsent requests cannot be executed, and incomplete ones can misdirect the backend. On Full-Duplex-Bench v3 (FDB v3), most failures of Realtime-Venus-Audio + Harness in our streaming pipeline occur before a domain tool executes. We introduce Duopra, a full-duplex interaction model that learns task execution alongside conversational control. Duopra treats the backend as a tool, accepting a self-contained natural-language request. Direct calls and backend delegation share an action interface and requirements for faithful initiation and correct execution. Its three-stage training combines protocol SFT, full-duplex RL rewarding timely and complete execution, and projector-only repair of talker conditioning after thinker updates. With the same backend, Pass@1 rises from 4.7% to 44.7%; Duopra achieves 47.7% calling tools directly (26.0% after SFT) and 49.3% with a DeepSeek backend. It improves three FDB v1.0 takeover rates over the base model. These results show that task agency can coexist with key conversational agency.

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [World-delta: Continually Integrating Observations for Incremental 3D Scene Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25802v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25802

**作者：** Haonan Wang、Hanyu Zhou、Xiaobin Hu、Luxin Yan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25802v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25802v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 World-delta 增量式 3D 场景生成框架，维护区分「观测支持内容」与「临时补全假设」的持久 latent 世界状态，并用 delta 引导的扩散只更新冲突区域、冻结未变区域。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据支持的 latent 状态 vs 临时假设」划分与稀疏 delta 更新，可能为 LVR 中区分视觉证据与生成内容、定位需重算状态提供借鉴线索；但属 3D 场景生成，机制迁移有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** World-delta: Continually Integrating Observations for Incremental 3D Scene Generation

**abstract/A1：** Existing 3D generation methods synthesize visually compelling scenes from fixed inputs, yet struggle to continually expand and revise a 3D world as new observations arrive. Iterative approaches often fuse real observations and generated content into a single representation, treating generated content as established structure rather than revisable predictions. Consequently, early generation errors can persist and accumulate despite new evidence. We propose World-delta, an incremental 3D scene generation framework combining uncertainty-aware persistent world modeling with delta-guided diffusion for continual scene expansion and correction. Our framework comprises three components: 1) Visual Evidence Encoding integrates geometric and semantic cues from incoming images into unified world tokens. 2) Observation-Calibrated World Modeling maintains a persistent latent world state that distinguishes observation-supported content from provisional completion hypotheses. New evidence drives selective world deltas to revise conflicting hypotheses while preserving well-supported content. 3) Incremental World Diffusion Generation uses delta-derived masks to restrict diffusion to regions requiring updates, freezing unchanged regions. Only affected 3D chunks are decoded and integrated into the existing scene. Together, these components enable continual world expansion and revision while reducing redundant generation and preserving scene consistency. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of World-delta in incremental 3D scene generation, observation-driven correction, scene consistency, and update efficiency over successive scene updates.

</details>

<a id="paper-121"></a>

### 121. [Inject Where It Matters: Objective-Dependent Feature Control in Rectified-Flow Image Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25811v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25811

**作者：** Nina Goncharova、Alexandra Ivanova、Aibek Alanov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25811v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25811v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究 FLUX 提示驱动图像编辑中源特征注入的位置与层选择问题：用源/目标条件下匹配反转状态的块输出差异构造自适应空间掩码来门控注入，并用 REINFORCE 在不同保真—可编辑目标下挑选 Top-K 注入块，选定后推理期固定。

**相关性简评：** 按目标选择「注入哪些层、在何处注入视觉特征」的离线搜索加固定推理策略，或可迁移为 LVR 中视觉证据写入位置与层选择的可核查线索；但属图像编辑生成场景，与多模态推理状态无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Inject Where It Matters: Objective-Dependent Feature Control in Rectified-Flow Image Editing

**abstract/A1：** Many prompt-driven FLUX editing methods rely on injecting source features into target-conditioned generation, creating an inherent trade-off between source preservation and the desired edit. We address this problem by controlling where source features are injected and which transformer blocks are used. First, we introduce adaptive masks that spatially gate feature injection using differences between source- and target-conditioned block outputs at matched inversion states. Second, we use REINFORCE to select Top-K injection blocks under different preservation-editability objectives. The selected blocks are fixed at inference and require no online search. On PIE-Bench with FLUX.1-dev, spatial masking controls the alignment-preservation trade-off. On 60 validation examples, a learned nine-block set gives lower whole-image LPIPS than the 18-block RF-Edit set within the same masked pipeline, with slightly lower alignment. Among 50 random nine-block sets, it has the lowest LPIPS but not the highest reward. Separate objective sweeps reveal different block preferences for preservation and alignment.

**comment/C1：** Accepted as a poster at the Principles of Generative Modeling (PriGM) Workshop at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [Anchor and Check: Deterministic Cross-Prompt, Cross-Model Reconciliation for Auditable LLM Decisions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25818v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25818

**作者：** Sivakameswara Reddy Chejarla

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25818v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25818v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出并系统评估一种「锚定—复核」的决策契约：先提交目标模型的贪心答案作为锚，再用不同提示或另一族模型做一到两次检查，由纯函数给出五种结果且仅 Agree 可无人值守执行，并在 6000 题、4.8 万次评估上比较其与池化 semantic entropy 的错误检测与自动化能力。

**相关性简评：** 其用跨提示、跨模型的多路检查与一致性门控来判定答案可否采用，可能为 LVR 中停止决策、证据筛选与「答案是否真正依赖 latent 内容」的干预式核查提供方法线索；但对象是纯文本决策审计，与视觉状态表示无直接关系，需自行核查迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Anchor and Check: Deterministic Cross-Prompt, Cross-Model Reconciliation for Auditable LLM Decisions

**abstract/A1：** Pipelines that let a large language model (LLM) make discrete decisions need to know which decisions they may act on unattended, and a record that explains each one. We study a small, fully specified reconciliation contract: commit the target model's greedy answer (the anchor), draw one or two greedy checks that reach the answer by a different route (an alternate prompt, or a model from another family), and let a pure function return one of five outcomes, of which only Agree is acted on. On 48,000 model-item evaluations over 6,000 questions, with every method given access to verbalized confidence as a tie-breaker, the main finding concerns where calls come from: pooling another family's samples raises semantic entropy's AUROC by 1.7 to 4.0 points at every budget, and a same-model temperature check is the weakest design. As complete pipelines, the point estimates of the three-call contract and pooled semantic entropy over 3 or 5 samples differ by less than one point, with intervals containing zero. As detectors of the anchor's errors, the contract is ahead of pooled entropy with 3 and 5 samples under both tie-breaker conventions; with 10 samples the point estimates differ by less than one point and the sign depends on the convention. Under the strict Agree gate it automates 70% of items and still acts on 16% of errors. What the contract adds is a fixed committed answer, an explicit outcome and a decision replayable from its record; generation itself was not reproducible on the hosted API.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 6 figures, 11 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [Beyond Alignment: Whitening Geometry, Profile Synthesis, and Heterogeneous Ensembles for Cross-Environment WiFi Action Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25831v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25831

**作者：** Lei Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25831v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25831v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究跨环境 WiFi 动作识别的部署退化：上限分析显示问题不在域对齐而在编码器对未见环境的外推，据此提出 Dec-H，组合闭式类内白化、profile 混合增强、交替白化重拟合的少样本适应与异构决策集成，LODO K10 提升至 0.560。

**相关性简评：** 其「对齐无效、真正的杠杆在读出几何与白化预条件」的诊断，可能可供核查 latent 视觉状态去相关、条件数与跨域外推问题，但属远端感知任务，与 LVR 生成机制无直接联系，迁移性需较多判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Alignment: Whitening Geometry, Profile Synthesis, and Heterogeneous Ensembles for Cross-Environment WiFi Action Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Cross-environment deployment is the central obstacle to practical WiFi sensing: a pipeline reaching 0.89+ top-1 in its training environments collapses to 0.408 under Leave-One-Domain-Out (LODO) evaluation with only K-shot labels. A ceiling analysis shows the collapse is not an alignment problem: the action signal is strong across seen environments (about 0.89 K10), environment identity is decodable from the encoder (0.965), and the failure concentrates in the encoder's extrapolation to the novel environment. A pre-registered falsification chain confirms that "align the domains" methods--training-time alignment, readout-level prototype alignment, adversarial confusion, and covariance/subspace alignment (CORAL -3.8pp, subspace -8.3pp)--degrade performance, and that source-domain statistics anti-correlate with cross-environment contribution. The effective levers lie elsewhere; we combine them into Dec-H: (i) closed-form source within-class whitening, provably re-geometrizing the readout's L2 regularization (+8.6pp, zero cost); (ii) profile-mixing augmentation (+1.0pp); (iii) few-shot adaptation with alternating whitening refit (+1.5pp; fixed preconditioners are 20/20 negative); (iv) heterogeneous decision ensembles (+1.7pp); homogeneous scaling and source-weighted stacking are saturated or harmful. Dec-H reaches 0.560 K10 (0.812 K40), +15.2pp over the baseline, consistent across all four environments and seeds; all thirteen configurations were pre-registered with frozen criteria.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables. Preprint of a manuscript submitted to IEEE Transactions on Mobile Computing

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [Rank2Replace: VLM-Ranked Slot Replacement for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25834v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25834

**作者：** Bo-Han Feng、Keng-Te Liao、Shou-De Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25834v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25834v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Rank2Replace：用预训练 VLM 按任务相关性对以对象为中心的 slot 排序，并用另一图像中同秩 slot 嵌入做训练期替换增强，以提升小预测器的 OOD 泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其 VLM 相关性排序与 slot 级替换提供了操控视觉证据内容的训练信号，可能可供核查 LVR 中任务必要视觉信息的筛选与保留，但论文本身不研究 latent 推理或推理期状态更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rank2Replace: VLM-Ranked Slot Replacement for Out-of-Distribution Generalization

**abstract/A1：** Spurious correlations can give image predictors high in-distribution accuracy but poor generalization under distribution shift. We introduce $\\textbf{Rank2Replace}$, which transfers knowledge of task-relevant evidence from pretrained vision--language models (VLMs) to small predictors through training-time augmentation for out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. The VLM ranks object-centric slots by task relevance and guides rank-dependent replacement with same-rank slot embeddings from another image. This augmentation retains the original image's label, encouraging reliance on preserved task evidence as incidental cues change, and supports combination with existing OOD methods. Across four datasets and six splits under shared frozen slot embeddings, the default cross-entropy configuration reaches macro-averaged average-group and worst-group accuracies of $80.43\\%$ and $65.28\\%$ without environment labels or dataset-specific replacement tuning. Slot corruption improves both macro-averaged metrics for all fourteen OOD training methods. Combining it with slot ranking and IRM reaches $81.96\\%$ and $72.68\\%$. Controlled ablations support the ranking and replacement design, with gains across five ranking VLMs and six extractor--predictor combinations. Permutation-invariant predictors further improve both macro-averaged metrics over their unaugmented counterparts while using VLM guidance only during training for efficient inference.

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [ContextRenderer: Dynamic-Context Video Generation for Interactive Game Worlds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25838v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25838

**作者：** Runze Wang、Fei Zha、Bing He、Ke Xu、Zhongyuan Wu、Yuxuan Xie、Wenzheng Wu、Zhongyuan Liu、Youcheng Cai、Ligang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25838v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25838v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向交互式游戏世界的自回归视频扩散渲染器：用分块级动态条件监督和循环历史压缩，在有界 token 预算内跟踪条件更新并保持视觉连续性，并通过跨表示对齐连接代理重建与外观、交互变化，另配套构建 WorldPrism 数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其「有界预算下的循环历史压缩」与分块条件更新可供核查 LVR 中长程 latent 状态的压缩、更新与细节保留，跨表示对齐也可能类比视觉证据在内部表示中的维持；但论文面向游戏视频渲染，不研究 VLM 推理，联系需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ContextRenderer: Dynamic-Context Video Generation for Interactive Game Worlds

**abstract/A1：** Interactive game world models separate executable world evolution from visual generation, using video models to render engine-provided scene representations into detailed observations. During sustained gameplay, however, existing generative renderers struggle to follow changing conditions while preserving visual continuity across diverse proxies and target styles. We therefore introduce ContextRenderer, an autoregressive video diffusion framework designed for both dynamic conditioning and cross-proxy generalization. It couples Dynamic-Context Learning (DCL) for chunk-level interaction supervision with Recurrent History Compression (RHC) to follow condition updates while preserving visual continuity within a bounded token budget. Across proxy representations, Cross-Representation Flow Alignment (CRFA) connects proxy reconstruction with appearance and interaction variation through a shared high-fidelity reconstruction. WorldPrism, our interaction-centric dataset constructed from agent-built worlds, AAA gameplay, and open-source videos, supplies the corresponding renderings and chunk-level annotations for these learning tasks. Experiments on GWM-Bench and Solar-Bench show improved reconstruction and visual quality, while qualitative results demonstrate minute-long interactive rendering under changing prompts and subject references. Project page: https://rainzor.github.io/contextrenderer/

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 7 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [Decoupled Knowledge Retrieval and Compact Memory Enable Reliable Lifelong Model Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25861v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25861

**作者：** Yue Che、Jingze Wang、Chen Guoyi、Yujie Wu、Xuming Ran、Mingkun Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25861v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25861v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BiSpace 框架，将「查询路由与相关编辑检索」放在外部语义空间完成，再以稀疏、低存储的更新把知识写入 LLM 内部表示空间，使基于记忆的终身模型编辑在存储和长编辑序列（16K）下更稳定。

**相关性简评：** 其「外部语义空间检索 + 内部表示注入」的解耦与紧凑记忆，可能为 LVR 中按需检索/写入 latent 状态、控制证据筛选与记忆增长提供方法线索，但需核查是否可迁移到多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decoupled Knowledge Retrieval and Compact Memory Enable Reliable Lifelong Model Editing

**abstract/A1：** Lifelong model editing requires continuously incorporating new facts into deployed large language models (LLMs) without expensive retraining or compromising existing capabilities. Memory-based editors offer an appealing solution by keeping the base parameters untouched, yet their practical use is hindered by linearly growing storage requirements and deteriorating retrieval performance as edits accumulate. To tackle these problems, we introduce BiSpace, a lifelong model editing framework that decouples memory retrieval from knowledge injection across two distinct representation spaces. BiSpace routes queries and identifies relevant edits in an external semantic space, while injecting the corresponding knowledge into the internal representation space of the LLM through sparse and storage-efficient updates. Extensive experiments show that BiSpace shrinks each entry's storage to only about 10.2% of the original and shows strong resilience to long edit sequences of up to 16K edits. On a more challenging benchmark, BiSpace further improves Overall Performance by more than 23% over existing methods. These results advance memory-based editing toward reliable long-term model maintenance at scale.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 9 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [One Schedule Does Not Fit All: Adaptive Inference Schedules for Diffusion Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25895v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25895

**作者：** Rabimba Karanjai、Linsey Pang、Hemanth Hegadehalli Madhavarao、Richard Williamson、Uma Kona、Prakhar Mehrotra

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25895v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25895v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出扩散语言模型的最省质量合格去噪调度随任务、模型与采样器而变，提出 TCDA：基于配对校准数据与单侧 bootstrap 同时置信界，认证满足质量与防退化约束的最便宜候选采样方案，否则回退到完整调度。

**相关性简评：** 其「按任务校准计算量、以统计保证控制误选并保留回退」的框架，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 决策与动态计算分配的成本—收益认证提供线索；但原文限于纯文本扩散模型，未涉及视觉证据筛选或 latent 状态内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** One Schedule Does Not Fit All: Adaptive Inference Schedules for Diffusion Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion language models typically use a fixed denoising schedule across workloads. We show that the cheapest schedule that preserves quality depends on the task, model, and sampler. We propose Task-Calibrated Denoising Allocation (TCDA), a deployment rule with an explicit error guarantee. Given candidate samplers, paired calibration data, a utility tolerance, and guardrails against degeneration, TCDA uses simultaneous one-sided bootstrap bounds to certify which candidates meet both the utility and guardrail criteria. It deploys the cheapest certified candidate or falls back to the full schedule. The probability of deploying an infeasible schedule is asymptotically at most alpha. Candidates include early prefix decoding, state-reusing continuation, reduced time grids, and confidence-threshold decoding. Across seven datasets and two model families, TCDA certifies reductions in denoiser evaluations by factors of 3.4 on XSum and 2.0 on WMT14 for ELF-B; 8.0 on WMT14 and 4.1 on GSM8K for LLaDA-8B; and 1.33 on OpenWebText for both ELF-M and ELF-L. A validation protocol fixed before generation confirmed every selection on fresh seeds. Across 500 calibration splits, false certification remained below 2% in every setting at a nominal level of 5%. Certified schedules vary across tasks and model scales. Trajectory measurements suggest that when decoded tokens commit is more informative for schedule selection than the remaining latent discretization error. We also find that the ELF reference sampler draws its time grid at random, so reproducible schedule comparisons for unconditional generation require fixing the grid.

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [Does It Depend on the Split? Frozen Satellite Features vs. Fine-Tuned CNNs for Tree Species Mapping](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25902v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25902

**作者：** Dharani Deivasihamani、Nandakishore Puttashamachar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25902v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25902v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 TreeSatAI 0.2 m 航拍影像的 20 类树种分类任务上，用线性探针比较冻结的 DINOv3 特征与端到端微调的 ResNet-50，并发现官方划分存在约 28.8% 的空间重叠；改用带缓冲的 2 km 方块空间划分后，两者差异变为不显著（0.648 vs. 0.640），冻结模型仅在稀有类上略优。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结特征＋线性探针 vs. 微调」的对照、对近似类间细粒度差异的失败诊断以及空间划分去泄漏的评价设计，可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节是否保留在内部状态、以及潜在表示诊断结论是否被数据重叠夸大的问题，但任务本身与多模态推理无关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Does It Depend on the Split? Frozen Satellite Features vs. Fine-Tuned CNNs for Tree Species Mapping

**abstract/A1：** Self-supervised foundation models are often said to need fewer labels than supervised training. We test this on a forestry task: classifying 20 tree species classes in 0.2 m aerial images from the TreeSatAI dataset. We compare a frozen DINOv3 model pretrained on 493 million satellite images, with only a linear classifier trained on top, against an ImageNet ResNet-50 that is fine-tuned end to end. On the official split, fine-tuning wins clearly (macro F1 0.764 vs. 0.685). However, 28.8% of the official test patches overlap a training or validation patch, and copying the label of the nearest such patch is correct for 83.6% of them. We therefore build a spatial split that holds out whole 2 km squares with a 200 m buffer. On this split the comparison becomes inconclusive (0.648 vs. 0.640, paired 95% CI of the difference \[-0.054, 0.016\]), and the learning curves of the two models overlap at every label budget from 343 to 33,126 training patches. On the five rarest classes, the frozen model achieves a higher mean F1 (0.566 vs. 0.445), although the confidence interval for this difference includes zero (\[-0.05, 0.23\]). Diagnostics show that the frozen features mainly miss differences between closely related species, such as the two native oaks. Choosing the input size and layer on validation data (224 px, block 16) raises the frozen model to 0.662 on the spatial split. A comparison that looks decisive on the official split is thus inconclusive under geographically separated evaluation. We release the spatial split and our code.

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [From Cases to Criteria: Reusing Evaluation Knowledge for Task-Specific Rubric Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25903v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25903

**作者：** Qiyan Deng、Long-Kai Huang、Kangfei Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25903v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25903v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RubricDuo：将历史 query-rubric 对组织成包含维度、共享概念、局部概念、准则与查询的知识图谱，先利用概念加检索案例起草覆盖较广的评分准则，再逐条回溯概念关联的历史准则与来源查询进行改写精炼，在 RubricBench 与 HealthBench 上取得较优的自动准则生成与下游评判表现。

**相关性简评：** 其「把反复出现的评估要求抽象为概念并关联到准则级证据」的结构，可能为 LVR 轨迹的细粒度验证与贡献分配提供线索，但论文本身不涉及 VLM 内部推理状态，需核查其准则粒度是否可用于逐步评分。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Cases to Criteria: Reusing Evaluation Knowledge for Task-Specific Rubric Generation

**abstract/A1：** Query-specific rubrics make LLM evaluation more fine-grained and transparent, and generating high-quality rubrics requires both identifying the target query's evaluation scope and specifying concrete checks. Historical rubrics provide reusable evaluation experiences, yet existing approaches reuse either complete query-rubric cases or aggregated criteria and dimensions without explicitly connecting recurring evaluation requirements to their criterion-level evidence and source-query context. This can preserve irrelevant task details or lose the broader evaluation structure. In this paper, we introduce RubricDuo, which organizes historical query-rubric pairs into a Rubric Knowledge Graph linking evaluation dimensions, shared concepts, local concepts, criteria, and queries, where reusable evaluation requirements across queries are abstracted by shared concepts. Given a target query, RubricDuo first combines concepts relevant to the target query with retrieved historical cases to draft a rubric with broad coverage. It then revisits each draft criterion, retrieves concept-linked historical criteria and their source queries, and adapts applicable evidence to refine the criterion. Experiments on RubricBench and HealthBench demonstrate that RubricDuo achieves the overall best performance among the evaluated automatic methods in both alignment with human-authored rubrics and downstream LLM judging.

</details>

<a id="paper-130"></a>

### 130. [LIFT: Latent Flow Matching for Probabilistic Cell Fate Dynamics with Single Cell Snapshots](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25917v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25917

**作者：** Bowen Yang、Junda Ying、Yuxuan Wang、Peijie Zhou、Lei Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25917v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25917v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** LIFT 用变分推断把单细胞谱系追踪中的一对多命运结果编码到 latent 空间，配合条件先验实现仅凭初始状态采样，并用 Mean Flow 解码器一次评估直接预测未来细胞状态。论文在合成与真实数据上验证其能同时恢复群体分布与细胞命运概率。

**相关性简评：** 其 latent 空间 + 条件先验 + 单步解码器的 flow matching 构造，可能为 LVR 中「由初始证据状态采样生成 latent 推理状态」及少步/并行状态更新的设计提供可核查线索，但领域为计算生物学，与视觉多模态推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LIFT: Latent Flow Matching for Probabilistic Cell Fate Dynamics with Single Cell Snapshots

**abstract/A1：** Lineage tracing links cell states through clonal ancestry, but does not generally identify unique source-descendant pairings. Learning from such data therefore requires a model of conditional outcomes, rather than only the terminal population distribution. We introduce LIFT, a latent flow framework for probabilistic cell-state generation from lineage-informed couplings. Variational inference encodes alternative outcomes in a latent space, while a conditional prior enables sampling from the initial state alone. A Mean Flow decoder predicts future cell state directly, requiring one decoder evaluation per sampling, which makes it convenient to predict cell fate through large scale Monte-Carlo sampling. This construction combines a representation of one-to-many relationships with efficient sampling and an analysis of conditional prediction error. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate LIFT's ability to recover both population distribution and cell fate probabilities.

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [ROAM-GRPO: Enforcing Reasoning Diversity in GRPO via Routed Off-mode Approach Mining](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25926v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25926

**作者：** Jeff Duan、Matt Thomson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25926v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25926v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ROAM-GRPO，把已掌握题目转入「off-mode」轨道，要求模型给出不同解题策略，经 LLM judge 校验有效性与差异后自蒸馏回策略，以缓解 GRPO 训练后的多样性坍塌。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 RLVR 工作，但其对轨迹多样性检测、独立策略挖掘与自蒸馏监督的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理轨迹坍缩及轨迹级监督提供可核查线索，视觉状态是否保留尚未涉及。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ROAM-GRPO: Enforcing Reasoning Diversity in GRPO via Routed Off-mode Approach Mining

**abstract/A1：** Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) paired with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant recipe for post-training LLMs on complex reasoning tasks, but has been shown to collapse the policy into a low-diversity mode. Repeated LLM outputs are nearly identical, and pass@k accuracy converges or degrades as a result. We propose ROAM-GRPO (Routed Off-mode Approach Mining for GRPO), an augmentation to the GRPO algorithm which incentivizes exploration of distinct reasoning strategies and aims to increase output creativity during evaluation. ROAM-GRPO detects well-solved problems during training and transfers them to an "off-mode" track. The model is prompted to produce distinct solution strategies to such problems, which are verified for logical validity and distinctness by an LLM judge and subsequently self-distilled into the policy. Training Qwen3.5-2b on a 1,000-problem subset of the MATH lvl5 dataset, ROAM-GRPO implementation increases peak pass@16 by 4.7% and correct embedding cosine distance by approximately 1.5-2x on a difficult evaluation set.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the NeurIPS 2026 Transitioning from Pre-Training to Post-Training Workshop

</details>

<a id="paper-132"></a>

### 132. [DTS: Delta-Based Tile Scoring for Efficient Long-Context Sparse Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25938v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25938

**作者：** Emre Uğur、Jiawen Qi、Qinyu Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25938v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25938v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出无需训练的稀疏块注意力打分方法 DTS：按局部向量变化选取 query/key 锚点，在冗余区域用更少代表、异质区域保留更多细节，并以并行 delta packing 加速锚点选取；在 LLaMA-3.1-8B 上保持接近全注意力的长上下文表现并取得加速。

**相关性简评：** 其「依局部变化自适应决定打分粒度」的块筛选思路，可能为长视觉上下文中的证据块或 token 筛选、计算预算分配提供线索；但论文本身是纯文本 prefill 加速，不涉及推理状态或答案对 latent 内容的使用，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DTS: Delta-Based Tile Scoring for Efficient Long-Context Sparse Attention

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) increasingly need to process long documents and codebases, making long-context attention a major prefill bottleneck. Block sparse attention reduces computation by skipping unimportant blocks of the attention matrix, but scoring blocks accurately can itself be expensive. We propose Delta-Based Tile Scoring (DTS), a training-free, content-adaptive tile scoring method for sparse block attention. DTS selects query and key anchors according to local vector changes, using fewer representatives in redundant regions while retaining more detail in heterogeneous regions. To accelerate anchor selection, parallel delta packing partitions the query and key sequences into tile-aligned chunks and performs bounded local scans over these chunks concurrently. The selected tiles are then computed using the original query, key, and value vectors. On LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct, DTS keeps average RULER and LongBench scores within 0.10 percentage points of full attention, with RULER evaluated up to 128K tokens. At 128K tokens, DTS reduces measured scoring-pipeline latency by 38% compared with XAttention. On four NVIDIA H100 GPUs, it achieves a 3.3$\\times$ prefill speedup over a PyTorch full-attention baseline using FlashAttention-2 at 512K tokens.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 4 figures. Accepted at DATE 2027. Emre Uğur and Jiawen Qi contributed equally

</details>

<a id="paper-133"></a>

### 133. [Point Dynamics as Proxy for Robotic World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25946v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25946

**作者：** Dong An、Tianyu Liu、Haoran Ding、Wen Yang、Anqing Duan、Zhengzhong Liu、Eric P. Xing、Guangyi Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25946v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25946v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把3D点动态作为机器人世界模型中的间代理：将首帧提升为点云，用学习到的点动态模型模拟点对机器人动作的响应，再渲染成像素对齐视频供视频模型读取，从而把动态预测与视觉渲染解耦。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式中间状态承载动态、生成模型只负责外观」的分工，可能为 LVR 中把任务必要的变化信息放入可检查的中间表示、避免交给生成模型隐式推断提供线索；但点状态是否承载语义与证据筛选、能否迁移到 VLM 推理仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Point Dynamics as Proxy for Robotic World Models

**abstract/A1：** Robotic world models predict future video frames from the current observation and a sequence of robot actions. While recent models follow the given actions closely, manipulated objects often collapse or move inconsistently with the action. We attribute this to the conditioning: an action, or a rendering of the robot, specifies where the robot goes but not how the scene responds, leaving a single video model to infer the interaction dynamics implicitly while also rendering appearance. We therefore separate dynamics prediction from visual rendering, with 3D point dynamics as the proxy between them. Specifically, we lift the first frame into 3D points, simulate how they respond to the robot's motion with a learned point dynamics model, and render the simulated points back into pixel-aligned videos that any video model can read. In controlled experiments on the DROID dataset with two video renderers of different architectures, this point dynamics proxy reduces object motion error by 8.6% and 6.2% without degrading image quality, and by 8.1% on a real bimanual robot. Moreover, the video follows the simulation: more accurate dynamics yield more accurate object motion, and editing the points steers the video. This suggests that advances in dynamics modeling can translate into more faithful robotic world models.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 7 figures, 11 tables. Project page: https://marsaki.github.io/point-dynamics-proxy/

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [Seeing Beyond the Obvious: Complementary Semantic Guidance for Salient Single-Positive Multi-Label Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25986v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25986

**作者：** Tong Jin、Meng-Long Wang、Xingyu Zhao、Sheng-Jun Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25986v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25986v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对显著单正标签多标签学习（SalSPML）中显著原型覆盖不足的问题，提出 CoST 框架：借助任务学到的类别信息与图像-文本语义对应，从未标注类中筛选语义相关候选并聚合成 Coverage Prototypes，再用基于排序的选择性精修策略只增强语义支持较强的类的软目标。

**相关性简评：** 其用图像-文本语义对应筛选候选证据、并按支持强度选择性加强监督的思路，可能与 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与监督/贡献分配问题存在可借鉴联系，但论文属分类标签补全任务、无推理期状态生成，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Seeing Beyond the Obvious: Complementary Semantic Guidance for Salient Single-Positive Multi-Label Learning

**abstract/A1：** Salient Single-Positive Multi-Label Learning (SalSPML) assigns each image its most salient positive label, leaving other classes unlabeled. Existing methods construct class-level Salient Prototypes from observed positives to guide the recognition of unobserved classes. However, since salient annotations favor visually prominent instances, these prototypes may not fully capture the visual diversity of hidden positives, resulting in insufficient positive supervision. To address this limitation, we propose CoST, a Coverage-guided Complementary Soft-Target Refinement framework that supplements Salient Prototypes with complementary class-semantic information. Specifically, CoST identifies semantically relevant candidates from unknown classes by leveraging task-learned category information and image-text semantic correspondence. The selected candidates are then aggregated into class-wise Coverage Prototypes to complement the semantic representations provided by Salient Prototypes. Furthermore, we introduce a ranking-based selective refinement strategy that identifies classes receiving stronger semantic support from Coverage Prototypes and selectively enhances their soft targets while preserving the base soft targets for the rest. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of CoST, with strong performance gains over existing baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-135"></a>

### 135. [Policy Improvement-Guided Replay for Continual Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25989v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25989

**作者：** Qin-Wen Luo、Haoxuan Gao、Sheng Ren、Xiang Chen、Sheng-Jun Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25989v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25989v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文从策略改进视角重审视觉语言模型的持续强化学习，用性能差引理将历史性能变化刻画为当前策略在历史任务分布上的期望优势，并提出 CoLiR：以正确响应似然上升与错误响应似然下降的对比来打分，选择历史 prompt 进行回放。

**相关性简评：** 其“用当前策略对历史 prompt 做打分筛选、再选择性回放”的机制，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与训练记忆分配提供借鉴线索，但研究目标是持续学习中的遗忘抑制而非 latent 推理状态，需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Policy Improvement-Guided Replay for Continual Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Continual reinforcement learning (CRL) requires models to acquire new capabilities while retaining performance on previous tasks. A stability-oriented perspective common in conventional continual learning emphasizes knowledge preservation and interference mitigation. Complementing this perspective, we revisit CRL for vision-language models through policy improvement, a fundamental principle of RL, to preserve acquired performance gains. Using the performance difference lemma, we characterize historical performance changes through the current policy's expected advantages on historical task distributions, motivating replay of historical prompts with the current policy. To allocate limited replay memory, we propose Contrastive Likelihood Replay (CoLiR), which contrasts likelihood gains on correct responses from the final task policy with likelihood reductions on incorrect responses from the initial policy. Our analysis links correct-response likelihood gains to reference-policy advantages under an idealized KL-regularized optimal update and provides an endogenous-reward interpretation for general policy updates. CoLiR estimates scores from finite training rollouts and cached log-likelihoods, selects prompts at task boundaries, and replays them uniformly during subsequent training, with KL regularization restricted to replayed prompts. Memory construction only requires a small amount of additional cross-policy scoring but no additional response generation. Across three Qwen3-VL model sizes on MRCL, CoLiR achieves the highest mean final accuracy and mean task accuracy among the compared methods while maintaining competitive new-task performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [Reinforcing Few-Step Diffusion Models via Shadow Posterior Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25998v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25998

**作者：** Jingqiao Xiu、Yicong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25998v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25998v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对少步扩散生成器，提出 Shadow Posterior Matching：将生成样本重新加噪为 shadow 输入，由其 shadow posterior 获取奖励信用，并从 KL 正则化奖励目标推导出把奖励值转为奖励方向的回归损失，以较低 reward hacking 获得更高奖励。

**相关性简评：** 其「用后验推断把标量奖励化为方向性信用、并把生成器输出与 shadow 输入对齐」的机制，可供核查 LVR 中轨迹贡献分配与监督信号设计；但对象是图像生成的少步扩散，不涉及视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reinforcing Few-Step Diffusion Models via Shadow Posterior Matching

**abstract/A1：** Few-step diffusion models are widely deployed for their efficiency. However, reinforcing them with black-box rewards, as is routine for multi-step models, faces two structural challenges. First, a few-step generator is a sampler rather than a score model, so its velocity is not the forward velocity of the generated distribution. Second, it must be anchored to the undistilled teacher, which shares no transitions with it. Existing few-step RL methods handle the latter with a marginal KL to the teacher, but each pays for the former in its reward credit. We propose Shadow Posterior Matching (SPM), which renoises generated samples into shadow inputs and obtains the credit from their shadow posterior. From the KL-regularized reward objective, we derive a regression loss that turns reward values into a reward direction while anchoring the generator's outputs to the teacher and aligning the shadow inputs with the deployed inputs. Applied to 4-step generators, SPM achieves higher rewards with less reward hacking than prior few-step RL methods.
