**comment/C1：** Code available at https://github.com/shadow-posterior/code

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [System-One RSI: Building Muscle Memory for Interactive Agents through Guided Practice](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26007v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26007

**作者：** Qingxu Fu、Xuanfa Jin、Ziqian Chen、Zhaoyang Liu、Bolin Ding

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26007v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26007v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 System-One RSI 双循环框架：在线 OODA 循环让决策模型把视觉观测与任务问题映射为结构化判断，再由决策树转成定时动作、不调用推理模型；离线由推理智能体团队改写 prompt 与决策树、从交互记录提取监督并训练更新参数，在 Snake 与两款 3D 游戏中验证。

**相关性简评：** 结构化判断加决策树构成显式中间状态瓶颈，「从交互记录反推监督」与慢推理到快决策的分工，可供核查 LVR 中轨迹级监督、答案是否真正使用中间状态以及 THINK/STOP 成本权衡；论文本身不研究 latent 多模态推理，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** System-One RSI: Building Muscle Memory for Interactive Agents through Guided Practice

**abstract/A1：** Reasoning models provide strong multimodal task understanding but struggle with sub-second control because deliberation increases decision latency. Fast decision models can meet the response window, yet prompting and few-shot examples do not readily provide the fine-grained experience required by a new task. Inspired by coached motor learning, this paper presents System-One Recursive Self-Improvement (System-One RSI), which couples two loops. The high-frequency online inner loop is an Observe, Orient, Decide, Act (OODA) cycle: a decision model maps each visual observation and task question to structured judgments, and a decision tree converts them into timed actions without invoking a reasoning model. The offline outer loop is a recursive improvement cycle carried out by a team of reasoning agents. The team revises the decision-model prompt and decision tree, captures interaction records, derives supervision from those records, and trains or deploys updated model parameters. The improved system then returns to the environment and generates the experience for the next round, consolidating learned distinctions as external muscle memory. The framework operates with a small number of real episodes, without expert demonstrations, environment modification, sandboxing, or parallel simulation. Experiments in Snake, Zenless Zone Zero, and Black Myth: Wukong use a 27B decision model. Mean Snake score increases from 1.30 to 7.35. In an evaluated scenario of Zenless Zone Zero, median completion time decreases by 8.4%. In an evaluated combat scenario of Black Myth: Wukong, the evasive policy achieves 11 times the mean survival time of a stationary reference, while the offensive policy win rate rises from 0% to 66.7% across the evaluated configurations.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 9 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-138"></a>

### 138. [FrontierReplay: Split-Free Exact Prefix Caching for Hybrid Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26020v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26020

**作者：** Zekun Wang、Yizhuo Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26020v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26020v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向混合注意力-循环语言模型提出免切分前缀缓存系统 FrontierReplay：在可预测的结构边界记录循环层输入窗口，用跨层、仅状态的 replay kernel 精确重建检查点，并按前沿优先级保留锚点与分支槽位，从而降低 TTFT 并提升吞吐。

**相关性简评：** 其循环隐状态检查点的低成本重建与树搜索分支保留策略，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态（尤其分支/树搜索轨迹）的缓存、恢复与复用提供方法线索，但该文属推理服务系统、不涉及多模态推理，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FrontierReplay: Split-Free Exact Prefix Caching for Hybrid Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Hybrid language models combine self-attention with recurrent layers whose in-place state updates restrict prefix reuse to exact saved positions. Saving states on a coarse grid wastes reusable prefixes, whereas existing fine-grained exact caches must cut the prefill forward pass at each mid-prompt checkpoint, adding scheduler steps that outweigh the reuse gains under load. We observe that across multi-turn chat, agent loops, document question answering, and tree search, prefix reuse almost always ends at structural boundaries predictable in advance, such as turn ends and known branch points. We present FrontierReplay, a prefix caching system that materializes exact checkpoints at these boundaries without cutting the prefill. While the forward pass runs uncut, FrontierReplay logs a short window of recurrent-layer inputs and invokes a cross-layer, state-only replay kernel to build the target state 6-37x cheaper per token than full recomputation without host synchronization. A frontier-aware retention policy prioritizes active anchors and proven branches while lending dead checkpoint slots to the attention KV cache. Across diverse hybrid architectures, model scales, and workloads, FrontierReplay delivers clear gains over existing exact caches, lowering median time to first token (TTFT) by 16-89% (up to 8.9x), raising 80% service-level objective (SLO) capacity by up to 1.73x, and improving goodput by up to 4.26x (3.67x at larger scales). Compared with approximate replay methods, FrontierReplay delivers up to 1.54x higher goodput while preserving exact task accuracy, avoiding the up to 17% accuracy drop suffered by approximation on long contexts.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages (12 pages main text + references), 12 figures, 4 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-139"></a>

### 139. [Serialization Tax in Shared-Latent Exchangeable Decisions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26029v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26029

**作者：** Siming Zhang、Zhehui Shen、Shijie Chen、Xinle Gu、Yansen Yu、Hang Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26029v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26029v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究共享潜在状态下的「序列化税」：逐项独立边缘化虽保留均值却抹去项间依赖，使组级决策失准；作者用凸序比较给出方差与全变差差距及阈值分离分析，并提出 TaxScore 把决策边界附近后验未解析的隐藏块路由到更强后验头，在 MovieLens 审计中验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「后验分辨率充分性」与「按边界不确定性路由到更丰富后验头」可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态精度、动态计算分配与停止决策的借鉴线索，但论文面向推荐系统组级决策，并非视觉推理本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Serialization Tax in Shared-Latent Exchangeable Decisions

**abstract/A1：** Accurate item probabilities can produce inaccurate group-level decisions. A system may score each item separately, yet act on whether enough hidden items succeed: show a slate, defer a panel decision, or inspect a batch. When items share an uncertain latent state, independent marginalization preserves means but removes dependence from the aggregate posterior. We call this error the serialization tax. For binary items sharing one success rate, classical convex-order comparisons give a more dispersed exact count law than any independent completion with the same total mean. We quantify variance and total-variation gaps, derive threshold separation under margin conditions, and connect posterior approximation to decision regret. Separate results cover non-binary outcomes, heterogeneous items, and sufficient posterior resolution. TaxScore identifies large hidden blocks with unresolved uncertainty near a decision boundary for routing to richer posterior heads. On pre-specified, hidden-target-free MovieLens 1M/10M audits, existing shared-latent heads improve high-score negative log-likelihood (NLL)/utility from 0.673/0.696 to 0.629/0.723 and from 0.670/0.691 to 0.639/0.715. Routing gives 0.006--0.012 full-population utility gains, with matched controls identifying their sources. Our code is available at https://github.com/SiminChang/Serialization-Tax-in-Shared-Latent-Exchangeable-Decisions.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [Iterative Scarcity-Guided Exploration: Bootstrapping Generative Auto-bidding from Narrow Support](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26060v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26060

**作者：** Qingmao Yao、Guangzheng Hu、Yusen Huo、Zhilin Zhang、Chuan Yu、Jian Xu、Xu Chu、Bo Zheng、Xiaotie Deng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26060v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26060v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究冷启动下 auto-bidding 的迭代训练停滞问题，将停滞归因于扩散生成中回报信号被曲率惩罚淹没导致的 SNR 崩溃，并提出 ISGE：以稀缺度评分驱动引导生成高稀缺、高回报轨迹，从狭窄数据支持中自举。

**相关性简评：** 虽非视觉任务，但其对扩散采样中 SNR 崩溃的诊断、「引导切换」式探索信号及自生成轨迹迭代训练，可能为 diffusion 式 latent 推理状态生成、探索与停止信号设计及轨迹自举监督提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Iterative Scarcity-Guided Exploration: Bootstrapping Generative Auto-bidding from Narrow Support

**abstract/A1：** Auto-bidding is a core algorithmic component in online advertising auctions. In practical cold-start settings, iterative training is commonly used to compensate for low-quality, narrowly supported historical data. Unfortunately, in iterative training, representative diffusion-based AI-Generated Bidding (AIGB) methods fail to sustain extrapolation beyond the narrow data support and stagnate at a suboptimal level. In this paper, we theoretically attribute this stagnation to Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) collapse: the weak radial return signal is overwhelmed by a curvature-induced penalty. To break this stagnation, we propose \\textbf{Iterative Scarcity-Guided Exploration (ISGE)}, which introduces a guidance handoff: as the return signal collapses, scarcity subsequently takes over as an exploratory signal to elevate the SNR above a critical threshold. Specifically, ISGE iteratively alternates between a \\emph{Judger} that assigns scarcity scores to trajectories and an \\emph{Explorer} that performs guided generation of high-scarcity and high-return trajectories, thereby bootstrapping from the narrow support with such self-generated trajectories. Extensive experiments on the industrial AuctionNet benchmark demonstrate that ISGE, starting from cold-start datasets, effectively surpasses the performance of the Full-Dataset baseline within three iterations. Code is available at https://github.com/alimama-tech/ISGE

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 (poster)

</details>

<a id="paper-141"></a>

### 141. [Controlling the Humanoid Robot H1-2 With a Simulated Biologically Plausible Network](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26074v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26074

**作者：** Fatima Atwi、Constant Roux、Christian Bergaud、Olivier Stasse

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26074v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26074v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作构建生物启发的脉冲神经网络控制 Unitree H1-2 人形机器人行走，通过蒸馏 RL 专家策略，并比较三种循环权重训练方案（冻结储层加线性读出、奖励调制 STDP 与 e-prop、BPTT）的存活率表现。

**相关性简评：** e-prop 的资格迹式跨时间信用分配，以及「冻结随机循环动力学、仅训练读出」与训练循环权重的对照，可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹的监督信号分配和多步循环状态更新提供可核查的训练线索；但任务是机器人运动控制，缺少视觉证据与推理期评价，相关性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Controlling the Humanoid Robot H1-2 With a Simulated Biologically Plausible Network

**abstract/A1：** Artificial Spiking Neural Networks have gained remarkable attention in the last few years due to their proven ability in simulating biological neural cultures and producing similar activity and trends. Nevertheless, whether they can be trained for humanoid locomotion while retaining biological plausibility and robust performance at once remains an open question. We study this problem by proposing a biologically inspired spiking network, trained by different learning schemes and tested on the H1-2 Unitree Humanoid. These schemes differ in how recurrent weights are trained. Ordered from most to least biologically faithful, they are: a fixed reservoir mimicking an in-vitro neural culture, where recurrent weights are kept frozen and only a linear readout is trained, the same network but with its synapses trained by reward-modulated spike timing dependent plasticity and eligibility propagation, and finally backpropagation through time as a non biological baseline for comparison. All schemes relied on distillation of a reinforcement learning expert policy with dataset aggregation. We show that e-prop reached 100% survival rate matching backpropagation through time, whereas both the fixed reservoir and the reward-modulated STDP, though the closest to biological cultures, plateaued at a much lower performance (~43.7% and ~11% survival). We explore in further detail which trends a real culture could and could not produce until now. Finally we demonstrate brief bipedal stepping on a full size humanoid H1-2.

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [Learning to Compare Neural Representations from Parameterized Computation Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26081v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26081

**作者：** Ya-Wei Eileen Lin、Daniel Herbst、Stefanie Jegelka

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26081v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26081v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究能否从模型参数化计算图直接学习预测独立训练模型间的内部表示相似关系：用 graph metanetwork 把每个内部位置映射为基于其上游计算的嵌入，并处理隐藏坐标置换与内部注意力基变换，给出层检索与 stitching 候选选择的条件性保证，实验显示预测关系可用于层检索和适配连接选择。

**相关性简评：** 不涉及视觉推理，但其在免前向测量下预测表示相似、并显式建模注意力基变换与坐标置换的做法，可能可供核查用于比较不同 VLM 推理模型的 latent 状态对应、层定位与证据筛选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Compare Neural Representations from Parameterized Computation Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Comparing internal representations across trained models is important for identifying corresponding layers and selecting components for model reuse. Yet, direct comparisons at inference require evaluating candidate models on known reference inputs and collecting their internal activations. In this paper, we study whether internal representation similarity measured across independently trained models can instead be learned to predict in unseen models from their parameterized computations. We introduce a framework that applies a graph metanetwork for mapping each internal site to an embedding derived from its upstream computations while accounting for hidden-coordinate permutation and internal attention-basis transformations. We analyze the structural properties of our estimators and derive conditional guarantees for layer retrieval and stitching candidate selection. Empirically, we find that the learned predictors support relation estimation and low-regret layer retrieval. They also select useful stitching connections while not being trained on stitching outcomes. This opens up a new way to study representation similarity directly from trained neural networks: representational relations learned across a collection of trained models can generalize to new computations, and that the predicted relations can guide candidate selection before direct measurement or adapter fitting for functional evaluations.

</details>

<a id="paper-143"></a>

### 143. [RL Can Only Reinforce What It Sees Succeed](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26084v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26084

**作者：** Ruben Sanchez-Corcuera、Unai Zulaika、Aritz Bilbao-Jayo、Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26084v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26084v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究小语言模型在贝叶斯先验与观测冲突的谜题环境中，outcome-reward RL 如何改变二者的权衡；发现「预期成功组数」这一训练前计数可预测学习是否发生，约 35 组成功样本才达到均等概率，且该计数跨问题、组大小与优化器稳定。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 轨迹训练中的成功采样覆盖和贡献分配相关，可能提示稀有正确推理路径能否被 group 采样命中会决定训练成败，可供核查该阈值是否适用于多模态推理轨迹的监督设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RL Can Only Reinforce What It Sees Succeed

**abstract/A1：** When a familiar rule stops working, an agent has to weigh what pre-training taught it against what it now observes. We ask how outcome-reward reinforcement learning (RL) changes this balance, in a puzzle world where both sides are known: small language models trained from scratch on games played by a Bayesian player, so that the prior and the right answer are exact. Base models hedge as their corpus did, and RL turns that hedging into decisions, in whichever direction the reward favours. But RL can only reinforce what it sees succeed. When the right action is rare, each run either learns it everywhere or not at all, and which runs take off is predicted before training by one count: the expected number of groups of attempts in which the action wins. About 35 such groups give even odds. The count holds for new and repeated problems and for groups of 4 to 256 attempts, and across learning rates, optimizers, GRPO variants and model sizes from 1.3M to 85M parameters, which move the threshold but not what is counted.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 6 figures, 1 table

</details>

<a id="paper-144"></a>

### 144. [Approximate Adjoint Models for Memory and Compute-Efficient Online Learning of Hybrid Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26095v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26095

**作者：** Emilio González-Zamora、Pierre Tandeo、Laurent Debreu、Said Ouala

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC、physics.ao-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26095v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26095v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对混合数值模型的在线校准，提出一类梯度近似方法：仅在稀疏锚点状态上用自动微分计算精确 pullback，锚点之间改用恒等映射或冻结复用的 pullback 传播伴随递推，从而在保留全轨迹伴随的同时显著降低峰值显存与运行时间。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但其「保留全轨迹递推、只在稀疏锚点做精确回传」的线索可供核查能否用于长 latent 推理轨迹的低显存信用分配，并规避截断式训练带来的梯度偏差与长程依赖丢失。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Approximate Adjoint Models for Memory and Compute-Efficient Online Learning of Hybrid Models

**abstract/A1：** Differentiable programming enables online calibration of numerical models by minimizing errors accumulated along simulated trajectories. This includes estimating physical parameters and training neural sub-models in hybrid configurations. Gradients are typically computed using adjoint methods, or backpropagation through time (BPTT), whose computational and memory costs grow with the length of the training window. This limits the duration and resolution of hybrid-model training, a major bottleneck for realistic geophysical applications. Approaches like truncated BPTT reduces memory use by discarding sensitivities across truncation boundaries, introducing gradient bias and neglecting long-range dependencies. We propose a class of gradient approximation methods that preserve the adjoint recurrence over the full trajectory while approximating only the pullback operators within it. Exact pullbacks are computed by automatic differentiation at sparse anchor states only. Between anchors, sensitivities propagate either through the identity (Zero-order) or through a reused, frozen pullback (Pullback Reuse). This approximation makes the parameter pullback constant within each chunk, allowing the gradient sum to factorize so that a single evaluation per chunk replaces one per time step. We evaluate both on three systems of increasing complexity, an incomplete Lorenz-63 model, a large-eddy closure for two-dimensional quasi-geostrophic turbulence, and bias correction in a multilayer quasi-geostrophic ocean model. Peak memory falls by up to an order of magnitude and runtime up to fourfold, with Pullback Reuse staying closest to the exact gradient direction. In all three cases, approximate gradients support hybrid-model training at comparable accuracy, showing that effective online learning does not require exact gradients.

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [Frequency-Synchronized Boundary Coupling for Training-Free Multi-Prompt Long Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26098v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26098

**作者：** Yipan Xu、Shuyong Gao、Jiyuan Fu、Keliang Yin、Jiayu Chen、Qianyu Guo、Yang Liu、Wenqiang Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26098v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26098v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练框架 FreqSync，把多提示长视频中的提示切换表述为「频率选择的边界耦合」，用频域同步的源条件注意力与残差裁剪的频谱拼接，使相邻片段共享低中频结构与运动线索、高频细节由目标提示主导。

**相关性简评：** 其跨片段 latent 状态耦合与显式残差约束，可供核查 LVR 中多步推理状态合并或更新时如何权衡保留旧状态与注入新信息（避免信息坍缩或过度保留造成「重影」），但属跨领域迁移线索，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Frequency-Synchronized Boundary Coupling for Training-Free Multi-Prompt Long Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-video generation has made remarkable progress with diffusion models and transformer-based video architectures, yet generating coherent long videos from evolving textual descriptions remains challenging. Training long-video models from scratch requires substantial computation and large-scale multi-event data, while training-free multi-prompt generation must activate new prompt semantics without breaking temporal continuity. We identify a frequency-agnostic boundary coupling problem in existing transition strategies: weak coupling causes abrupt changes, flicker, and background jumps, whereas overly strong or coarse coupling may over-preserve source semantics and produce ghosting or duplicated subjects around prompt boundaries. To address this problem, we propose FreqSync, a training-free framework that formulates prompt transitions as frequency-selective boundary coupling. FreqSync combines Frequency-Synchronized Source-Conditioned Attention (FS-SCA) with Residual-Clipped Spectral Stitching (RCSS), enabling adjacent segments to share stable low- and mid-frequency structure and motion cues while keeping high-frequency prompt-specific details target-dominant under explicit residual constraints. Extensive experiments show that FreqSync achieves state-of-the-art transition smoothness and prompt alignment while maintaining strong perceptual video quality, demonstrating its effectiveness for coherent multi-prompt long video generation.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. Project page: https://yipanxu-22.github.io/FreqSync-project

</details>

<a id="paper-146"></a>

### 146. [HiUni: Harmonizing Understanding and Generation in Shared Visual Space](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26107v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26107

**作者：** Jiahao Hu、Jingdong Du、Yichen Zhang、Yi Song、Zhenyu Zhang、Jiaxin Niu、Ruihang Li、Xiangzhao Hao、Yiqian Zhang、Yong Huang、Zefeng Zhang、Zheyuan Gu、Shuohuan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26107v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26107v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HiUni，让同一个语言对齐 ViT 与稠密 Transformer 主干同时承担视觉理解与生成，任务特定计算只放在输出侧，包括面向像素空间优化的 Pixel Diffusion Head 和补充细粒度空间细节的轻量 HiGate 通路，实验显示理解分数接近预训练 VLM、生成与编辑具有竞争力。

**相关性简评：** 其关键观察「预训练 ViT 的语义抽象会丢弃合成所需的细粒度空间细节」与 LVR 关注的内部状态是否保留视觉细节、以及视觉旁路设计相关；HiGate 在输出阶段注入细节的做法可供核查是否可作 latent 推理中细节保真通路的线索，但该文本身未研究 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HiUni: Harmonizing Understanding and Generation in Shared Visual Space

**abstract/A1：** Unified multimodal models aim to support visual understanding and generation within a single framework. However, prevailing approaches often separate their visual representations or backbone computation to accommodate the different requirements of these two tasks, which limits the degree of unification. In this paper, we present HiUni, which revisits this design choice and explores whether a pretrained vision language model can be directly extended into a unified model with extensive parameter sharing. Specifically, HiUni reuses the same language-aligned ViT and dense Transformer backbone for both capabilities, while confining task-specific computation to the output side. Yet such a design introduces two key challenges. Generation in pixel space is difficult to optimize, and semantic abstraction in pretrained ViT discards fine spatial details required for synthesis. To address these challenges, HiUni introduces a \\emph{Pixel Diffusion Head} tailored to direct pixel-space optimization and a lightweight \\emph{HiGate} pathway that provides fine-grained spatial details near the output stage. Extensive experiments show that HiUni achieves a similar average understanding score to the pretrained VLM while attaining competitive performance on image generation and editing. Controlled comparisons and analyses further demonstrate that a single dense backbone can effectively support both capabilities without task-specific backbone computation, highlighting a simpler path toward unified multimodal modeling.

</details>

<a id="paper-147"></a>

### 147. [SenseAlign: Learning Decision-Aligned Predictive Representations for Active Urban Sensing with UAVs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26109v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26109

**作者：** Weijiang Xiong、Nikolas Geroliminis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26109v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26109v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SenseAlign，用强化学习协调无人机车队在巴塞罗那路网上做主动感知：先训练可利用未来真实交通的特权教师，再让学生在部分观测下借助辅助分支预测未来交通，并将师生在真实与预测未来下的动作分布对齐，从而塑造面向决策的 latent 表征。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测未来状态 + 与决策分布对齐」的 latent 训练方式以及主动选择观测点的设定，可能为 LVR 中预测工作区、视觉证据筛选与「看哪里」决策的监督提供可核查线索；但任务属城市交通感知，迁移性需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SenseAlign: Learning Decision-Aligned Predictive Representations for Active Urban Sensing with UAVs

**abstract/A1：** Active urban sensing orchestrates a mobile sensor fleet to proactively inspect a dynamic road network and gather critical information for system management. This problem simultaneously requires understanding the complex spatio-temporal evolution and making decisions with partial observations. In this work, we focus on active sensing in a large-scale urban road network with utilities grounded in dynamic traffic states, while prior work has rarely considered these factors jointly. We propose \\emph{SenseAlign}, a Reinforcement Learning-based (RL) framework that learns decision-aligned predictive representations for coordinating an urban sensing drone fleet. SenseAlign first trains a privileged teacher agent that exploits future ground-truth traffic for optimal sensing choices. The student agent has an auxiliary branch that learns to predict future traffic with partial observations, in addition to the RL-trained actor and critic branches. During student learning, our framework matches the action distributions of the teacher under ground-truth and student-predicted future traffic, while keeping the teacher's observations unchanged. Such a design shapes the latent space for decision-making instead of prediction alone. At inference time, the student agent has no overhead as all this learning is done in training. We conduct comprehensive experiments with the real road network of central Barcelona containing 1570 road segments, and demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms the baselines on multiple utility formulations. The codebase will be made publicly available upon paper acceptance.

</details>

<a id="paper-148"></a>

### 148. [Learning Reconstruction Errors as Spatiotemporal Evidence for AI-Generated Video Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26116v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26116

**作者：** Jiahua Jiang、Jinglai Li、Jianru Zhang、Jin Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26116v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26116v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将重建误差作为统一的时空证据用于AI生成视频检测：联合优化重建器与下游分类器以获得更具判别性的误差信号，并验证连续重建误差本身已含时序信息，用3D卷积建模，省去额外原始视频特征流。

**相关性简评：** 与LVR主线无直接关系，但「面向下游目标联合训练重建器以产生判别性误差」可供核查：重建误差或可作为内部状态是否保留任务相关视觉细节的度量，可能启发视觉证据筛选与信息坍缩诊断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Reconstruction Errors as Spatiotemporal Evidence for AI-Generated Video Detection

**abstract/A1：** The proliferation of AI-generated videos has raised growing concerns about misinformation, fraud, and potential societal risks, creating an urgent need for detection. Reconstruction-error-based methods have shown strong empirical performance for AI-generated image detection by exploiting discrepancies between an input and its reconstructed version. Their extension to video detection, however, faces two challenges: (i) a fixed reconstructor is not optimized for the detection objective and may yield weak discriminative signals; and (ii) an additional raw-video feature stream is often introduced to model temporal dependencies, increasing architectural overhead. To address these challenges, we develop a detector that learns and exploits reconstruction errors as a unified source of spatiotemporal evidence. First, we jointly optimize a reconstructor and the downstream classifier to produce reconstruction errors that are more informative for detecting AI-generated videos. Second, we show that consecutive reconstruction errors alone carry temporal evidence, and thus a 3D convolution over them captures spatiotemporal evidence without a raw-video stream. Extensive experiments on over 30 existing AI-video generators demonstrate competitive performance against baselines.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-149"></a>

### 149. [Sage-Evolve-Preview:Adaptive Observation for Post-Training Self-Improvement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26132v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26132

**作者：** Yinhao Tang、Youqing Fang、Yanan Sun、Xintian Shen、Hanyu Sun、Wenran Liu、Yue Wu、Weiming Zhang、Bin Liu、Wenwei Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26132v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26132v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 AdaObs：在保持锚点基准与总目标不变的前提下，当训练后自改进循环出现平台期时，由控制器调整诊断粒度、提升难度并补充相关任务，将验证有效的增量融合回训练，从而缓解仅用聚合分数作唯一观测导致的停滞。

**相关性简评：** 其核心主张「平台期可能源于观测耗尽而非能力耗尽」，与 LVR 中监督信号诊断、证据获取和停止决策的设定可供核查地相通；但该工作不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，迁移线索仅限训练循环的观测设计层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sage-Evolve-Preview:Adaptive Observation for Post-Training Self-Improvement

**abstract/A1：** Post-training recursive self-improvement (RSI) iteratively improves a target capability, operationalized by a fixed anchor benchmark, through repeated intervention, training, and evaluation. Yet most loops use the anchor's aggregate score as their sole observation, so early gains give way to stalled progress and repetitive exploration. When this anchor-only feedback ceases to reveal actionable gaps, a plateau may reflect an exhausted observation rather than an exhausted capability. We introduce adaptive observation (AdaObs), an observation-side mechanism of harness evolution that keeps the anchor objective fixed while adapting the active observation. At a plateau, a controller refines diagnostic granularity, escalates difficulty, and adds related tasks; validated increments are fused back into training, while every checkpoint remains evaluated against the anchor. Across eight independently optimized anchors in long-context reading comprehension and instruction following, AdaObs raises the mean score from $66.4$ to $73.7$ under a matched data budget, outperforming FixedObs, the anchor-only baseline. The evolved harness further improves the seven-task average on the independent PostTrainBench across two agent backends. These results identify observation adaptation as a missing mechanism of harness evolution for sustaining post-training RSI.

</details>

<a id="paper-150"></a>

### 150. [Can World Models Predict Rice Growth? Toward Agricultural World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26145v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26145

**作者：** Wenkai Ji、Zeteng Li、Yinkun Guo、Jie Zhou、Liang He、Zhenong Jin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26145v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26145v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「农业世界模型」任务：仅凭单张照片、品种与视角，预测同一水稻或玉米在数天至数月后所处的生长阶段与外观；方法 AgroWM 先用 PhenoClock 推断当前与未来生育期并写出含株高、面积、颜色数值目标的 Growth Script，再由 Rooted Renderer 据此编辑原图，并用植株后续照片按性状与发育时序评估。

**相关性简评：** 其“先推断照片只能间接揭示的隐发育状态、再以显式脚本约束后继生成”的结构，以及指出像素指标偏好不变副本、改用阶段与性状匹配的评估思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中隐状态承载任务必要信息、以及 latent 内容是否真被答案使用的检验线索；但推理为文本数值而非 latent，跨模态长时预测与 VLM 推理距离较远，需进一步判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can World Models Predict Rice Growth? Toward Agricultural World Models

**abstract/A1：** World models can imagine how a physical scene evolves over the next few seconds, but growers need to know how a crop will develop over weeks to months: will this rice plant have headed a month from now, and how will it look at harvest? We introduce agricultural world modeling, the task of predicting how the same plant will look at a requested horizon of days to months, from one photo of it, its cultivar, and its camera view. In preliminary analysis, even state-of-the-art image generators get this wrong: they often grow the plant too little or give it the color and form of the wrong growth stage. We argue that they do not work out the plant's developmental state, which the photo reveals only indirectly. We therefore propose AgroWM, which reasons about development before it draws growth. Its reasoning module, PhenoClock, works out the current growth stage, predicts the future one, and writes a Growth Script with numeric targets for height, plant area, and color; its generation module, the Rooted Renderer, then edits the photo to follow this script, adding new organs and colors to the photographed plant. Because pixel scores can prefer an unchanged copy of the photo, we instead check each forecast's traits and developmental timing against the plant's own later photos. On rice, 54.6% of the forecasts of AgroWM are matched to the target stage (Stage R@1), against 34.8% for GPT Image 2.5, 24.0% for Qwen Image 2.1, and 47.4% for the strongest domain-specific baseline, the same backbone fine-tuned without Growth Scripts; 9 to 20 weeks ahead, it still reaches 46.4%, against 35.7% for that baseline. On maize, a crop with a different body plan and stage scale, the same design again scores best on all four measures.

</details>

<a id="paper-151"></a>

### 151. [RegionAgent: Two-Stage Preference Optimization for Regional Image Restoration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26179v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26179

**作者：** Weiyu Zhou、Jiacong Tang、Tao Hu、Axi Niu、Jinqiu Sun、Peng Wu、Wei Dong、Qingsen Yan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26179v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26179v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RegionAgent，用 VLM 在每轮迭代中为非均匀退化区域选择修复动作，并以步骤级和轨迹级两阶段 DPO 进行偏好学习，另构建区域修复偏好数据集支持训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「按区域逐步选择动作＋轨迹级偏好只监督差异动作」的做法，可能为 LVR 中迭代视觉证据筛选与长程贡献分配提供训练线索，但任务为图像复原，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RegionAgent: Two-Stage Preference Optimization for Regional Image Restoration

**abstract/A1：** Images under non-uniform coupled degradations require diverse restoration capabilities for different degraded regions. Existing methods employ globally uniform operations, potentially overlooking regional differences and causing incomplete restoration or even damage to image content. Moreover, a degraded region may require successive restoration actions, and earlier actions may alter residual degradation characteristics or introduce artifacts, potentially hindering subsequent restoration. To address these challenges, we propose RegionAgent, an adaptive restoration framework that trains a Vision-Language Model (VLM) through two-stage preference learning to generate a region-specific restoration action at each iteration. Specifically, we first train the VLM to select an action suited to the current region through step-level Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), using preferences derived from immediate restoration feedback to supervise only the differing component in each action pair. To account for the effects of current actions on subsequent restoration, we then apply trajectory-level DPO, using preferences derived from complete trajectory scores to supervise the sole differing action in each trajectory pair. Furthermore, as conventional image-pair datasets do not directly provide preferences over regional restoration decisions, we construct a regional restoration preference dataset comprising 10K action pairs and 20K trajectory pairs for two-stage VLM training. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate strong restoration performance under both mixed and single degradations. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of two-stage preference learning in improving restoration quality.

</details>

<a id="paper-152"></a>

### 152. [Invertible Transport Between Sample-Only Distributions via Projected Characteristic Functions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26187v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26187

**作者：** Juan Gutiérrez、Victor Gutiérrez-García、José Luis Blanco-Murillo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26187v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26187v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PCF 框架，用投影经验特征函数对齐两个分布，在无需密度估计、对抗训练和配对样本的情况下学习可逆传输映射，并用位移正则缓解映射非唯一性；在受控任务与高维对齐（含 MNIST–USPS）上验证精度、样本效率与内存开销。

**相关性简评：** 其「仅凭样本、免密度、可逆映射 + 分布对齐」的做法可能可供核查：能否用于把 latent 推理状态或轨迹分布对齐到仅以样本给出的参考分布，从而提供一种不依赖密度估计的 latent 监督或状态对齐手段。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Invertible Transport Between Sample-Only Distributions via Projected Characteristic Functions

**abstract/A1：** Many useful latent distributions can be sampled but do not have a tractable density. Such distributions may be represented by empirical collections of real-world observations or generated by scientific simulators and black-box generative models. These sample-only priors cannot be used directly in likelihood-based normalizing flows, which require evaluating the prior density. We propose Projected Characteristic-Function matching (PCF), a density-free framework for learning an invertible map between a data distribution and a prior specified only through samples. Our method aligns distributions using projected empirical characteristic functions, eliminating the need for density estimation, adversarial training, or paired observations. To address the non-uniqueness of the transport map, we introduce an optional transport regularizer that selects lower-displacement transformations while preserving endpoint alignment. We benchmark PCF on controlled transports, sample-efficiency studies, and high-dimensional alignment tasks. PCF matches the accuracy of kernel MMD on the controlled benchmark and on raw-pixel MNIST--USPS at a cost linear in the batch size, and it is more sample-efficient, achieving lower endpoint error than MMD in 13 of 15 paired runs across library sizes. By avoiding pairwise kernel evaluation, it reduces discrepancy memory by 29.8% and trains at batch sizes where kernel MMD exceeds GPU memory. These results show that PCF provides accurate and efficient invertible transport, particularly in sample- and memory-limited settings.

</details>

<a id="paper-153"></a>

### 153. [Learn, Freeze, and Transfer an Activation Function Across Architectures](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26201v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26201

**作者：** Arun Prakash A

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26201v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26201v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察学习到的激活函数能否脱离权重单独迁移：在 ViT 上联合训练一个五参数、二谐波傅里叶级数形式的逐点激活 FAct，冻结后用于从零训练的多种视觉架构，在图像分类上多数设置优于 GELU 等十一种激活。

**相关性简评：** 其「全局共享、与层维度无关的可学习逐点非线性」可能可供核查为循环或 looped latent 推理块中跨迭代共享的状态更新非线性的参数化方式；但论文本身不涉及视觉推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，与主线仅为方法层面的弱联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learn, Freeze, and Transfer an Activation Function Across Architectures

**abstract/A1：** Transfer learning typically reuses knowledge acquired by a source network through its learned weights. We investigate whether a learned activation function can also be transferred and remain useful in networks trained from scratch. We study this complementary form of transfer through a learn-freeze-transfer protocol, in which the activation is optimized jointly with a source network in a single training run, and is then frozen and reused in target networks trained from scratch. A pointwise activation with globally shared parameters is independent of layer dimensions and can therefore be incorporated into networks of different architectures trained from scratch. We instantiate this protocol with FAct, a five-parameter activation represented by a two-harmonic truncated Fourier series, initialized from a Fourier approximation of GELU and learned in a Vision Transformer (ViT) on ImageNet-1K. When reused in a ViT of the same architecture trained from scratch on ImageNet-1K, the frozen activation (Frozen FAct) improves top-1 accuracy by 1.46 percentage points over GELU. Across four smaller target architectures (standard ViT, Swin Transformer, linear-attention ViT, and MLP-Mixer) and four image-classification datasets, it achieves the highest mean accuracy among eleven activation functions in 15 of 16 settings under a common training configuration, with improvements of up to 1.71 percentage points over the best conventional activation. When the learning rate and weight decay are tuned separately for each activation under the same search budget, Frozen FAct still ranks first in 11 of the 16 settings, second in four, and fourth in one. These results provide empirical evidence that a learned activation function can retain its usefulness when transferred across vision architectures, independently of the weights with which it was learned.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 5 figures, 10 tables. Code: https://github.com/AI4Bharat/LeFT-activation-transfer, Interactive figures: https://arunprakash-a.github.io/papers/learn-freeze-transfer/

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [SpecWM: Turning Verification into Refinement for Streaming World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26211v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26211

**作者：** Jiaming Li、Lingzhen Li、Xiaze Yang、Chenyu Zhu、Kaiqi Liu、Xiaotong Zhao、Songlian Li、Toyota Li、Alan Zhao、Haobin Zhong、Yue Ma、Zhiyuan Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26211v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26211v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SpecWM，一个免训练的流式视频世界模型投机推理框架：用目标模型的 latent velocity field 校验草稿块，并把校验信号复用为一步或三步精修，配合双阈值实现接受、精修或拒绝，以提升质量并加速。

**相关性简评：** 以 latent 动力学信号替代解码帧打分做校验并直接复用为修正，可能为 LVR 中低成本 latent 状态校验、轨迹精修与停止决策提供线索，但属视频世界模型生成，需核查其可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpecWM: Turning Verification into Refinement for Streaming World Models

**abstract/A1：** Recently, video world models have advanced interactive generation, with large models offering higher quality and smaller models enabling faster inference. Existing speculative methods combine their strengths through fast draft generation and selective target regeneration guided by rewards on decoded frames. However, verification covers only the external scorer's evaluation dimensions and adds decoding and scoring costs. Moreover, these scores provide no correction, requiring costly target regeneration for rejected drafts. To address these limitations, we propose Speculative World Model (SpecWM), a unified, training-free inference framework for streaming world models. It verifies draft chunks using the target's latent velocity field. Verification also yields a reusable correction; two thresholds select Accept, Refine, or Reject. Specifically, one-step refinement reuses it to improve perceptual quality, while three-step refinement strengthens camera following and trajectory consistency. These findings motivate transition-aware refinement. On WBench Navigation, SpecWM improves visual quality at 1.63$\\times$ speedup over the target and raises NavScore from the draft's 75.99 to 85.63, achieving the best quality-speed-control trade-off. Additionally, our LongSpecBench shows sustained quality and control over long, multi-turn rollouts, while experiments on Hy-Nomas validate SpecWM across backbones and denoising budgets.

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [MixReact: Physics-Based Reactive Humanoid Control from Multimodal Cues via a Discrete-Continuous Mixture of Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26218v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26218

**作者：** Bin Li、Xing Zhu、Jingya Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26218v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26218v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MixReact，从文本、音频、视频线索出发，在物理仿真中生成可执行的人形反应行为：用离散掩码建模分支与连续自回归扩散分支经单向 mixture attention 组合的 DisCo-MoT 预测下一状态，再由历史感知的动作 Transformer 映射为动作实现闭环控制。

**相关性简评：** 其「离散语义上下文 + 连续状态空间直接预测」的混合设计，以及用离散引导抑制一对多生成中向平均行为坍缩的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态表示与信息坍缩、视觉线索筛选之间的对应关系；但论文面向机器人控制，与 VLM 推理的迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MixReact: Physics-Based Reactive Humanoid Control from Multimodal Cues via a Discrete-Continuous Mixture of Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Humanoid agents in everyday environments are expected to react to diverse cues as humans do, with semantically appropriate and physically executable behaviors. Existing cue-driven reaction generation methods support only a narrow range of cue modalities and are limited to purely kinematic generation, leaving the resulting reactions susceptible to physical artifacts. To this end, we present MixReact, the first unified framework for physics-based reactive humanoid control from text, audio, and video cues, integrating a Discrete--Continuous Mixture of Transformers (DisCo-MoT) for state prediction with a History-aware Action Transformer (HAT) for physical execution. DisCo-MoT couples a discrete masked-modeling branch with a continuous autoregressive diffusion branch via one-way mixture attention within a MoT architecture. Guidance from discrete context mitigates collapse into averaged behaviors in one-to-many reaction generation, while direct prediction in continuous state space bypasses the decoding bottleneck of discrete representations. HAT then maps the predicted next state and observed execution history to executable actions, enabling robust closed-loop control in physics-based simulation. Experiments across all three modalities demonstrate consistent improvements over the baselines in fidelity, diversity, and physical consistency, while ablations validate the complementary contributions of the components. Our code will be released to facilitate future research. Project page: https://binlee26.github.io/MixReact\_Page/

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [Attention Head Specialization: Phase Transitions in Sample Complexity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26228v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26228

**作者：** Léo Catteau、O. Duranthon、Lenka Zdeborová

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.dis-nn、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26228v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26228v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从训练数据量角度研究注意力头功能专门化：在 BERT 中观察到句法关系专用头随数据规模增大而出现，并在可解高维多头注意力模型中证明该专门化是测试误差出现非解析性的样本复杂度相变，可表现为逐级恢复较弱潜在特征的相变级联。

**相关性简评：** 其「弱潜在特征被逐级恢复」的级联与头部专门化随数据、训练阶段涌现的结论，可供核查 LVR 中视觉与任务特征是否分阶段进入特定注意力头，以及注意力结构差异如何影响 latent 相似度与内容-位置信息的读取。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Attention Head Specialization: Phase Transitions in Sample Complexity

**abstract/A1：** Attention heads specialize into distinct functions during training, often emerging in stages together with sharp changes in model behavior. While this phenomenon has mostly been studied as a function of training time, we ask how head specialization depends on the amount of available training data. Empirically, we find that heads specialized to syntactic relations in BERT emerge as the dataset size increases. We then study this phenomenon in a solvable high-dimensional model of multi-head attention and establish that, in this model, head specialization is a genuine phase transition in sample complexity, corresponding to a non-analyticity in the test error and other observables. Depending on data structure and regularization, specialization can follow uninformative head differentiation or occur through a cascade of phase transitions that progressively recover weaker latent features, providing a statistical form of simplicity bias. Finally, we relate these statistical transitions to the stagewise emergence of specialized heads during online learning, disentangling statistical from dynamical origins of head specialization.

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [SKILL.PNG: Learning Agent Skills in Pixel Space](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26296v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26296

**作者：** Maximilian Seeth、Chengzhi Martin Hu、Marie Kempkes、Paul Röttger、Leo Schwinn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26296v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26296v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Skill.png：把 agent skill 表示成图像，像素经由冻结 VLM 的视觉编码器做梯度优化，单张图可针对多模型集成联合优化；在 4 个 VLM、4 个基准的 16 个设置中 9 个优于前沿 LLM 优化的文本 skill，且推理时仅需 64–256 个图像 token，文本 skill 约需 900–2000 个，文本+图像混合 skill 进一步提升表现。

**相关性简评：** 该工作显示视觉通道可承载并压缩指令类信息、且梯度能穿过冻结视觉编码器，可供核查「视觉旁路」与视觉 token 实际被使用程度的问题，但它优化的是技能条件而非 latent 推理状态，迁移价值需进一步确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SKILL.PNG: Learning Agent Skills in Pixel Space

**abstract/A1：** AI agents are commonly specialized for new tasks and domains using skills, i.e. natural language instructions placed in their context. However, the discrete nature of textual skills prevents continuous optimization. Gradient-based approaches such as finetuning, on the other hand, are typically model-specific, preventing skill transfer across different agents. Since many agents today are built on vision-language models (VLMs), we can address these limitations by introducing Skill.png: a method for obtaining skill-like image inputs whose pixels are optimized with gradient descent through the vision encoder of a frozen VLM. Image skills are compatible with any VLM, and a single image can be optimized jointly for an ensemble of models. We show that Skill.png significantly outperforms state-of-the-art textual skills optimized using frontier LLMs in 9 of 16 settings (across 4 VLMs and 4 benchmarks) while requiring only 64 to 256 image tokens instead of roughly 900 to 2000 text tokens at inference time. Hybrid skills, i.e. text and image skills jointly optimized, further improve agent performance. Moreover, an image optimized with an ensemble of VLMs can successfully transfer across the models in the ensemble. Skill.png thus presents the first agent skill that can be optimized with gradient descent while retaining transfer benefits of text skills. Our code is available at: https://github.com/omseeth/skill.png

</details>

<a id="paper-158"></a>

### 158. [Actor-Predictor Architecture for Reinforcement Learning with Formal Specification Objectives](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26313v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26313

**作者：** Josef Brozovich、Qining Zhang、Lei Ying

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26313v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26313v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Actor-Predictor 框架，用预测器学习形式规范进度的替代表示，并将学到的特征作为 actor 的辅助输入，从而避免显式构造自动机。

**相关性简评：** 其通过预测未来是否满足规范来学习紧凑状态表示，可供核查 LVR 中推理状态是否应编码任务进度，以及预测式辅助监督如何影响策略对 latent 的使用；但任务域属 RL 与机器人控制，与 VLM 推理的联系仍需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Actor-Predictor Architecture for Reinforcement Learning with Formal Specification Objectives

**abstract/A1：** One fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning (RL) is enforcing temporal safety constraints, such as satisfying formal specifications. Existing methods often transform logic formulas into automata for state augmentation and reward shaping. However, this approach assumes that automaton construction is both feasible and tractable, which limits its applicability to small specifications and linear temporal logic (LTL). This paper proposes Actor-Predictor, a framework that learns a surrogate representation of logic progress without explicitly constructing an automaton. The predictor continuously estimates whether a partial trajectory will satisfy the formal specification in a later step, and its learned features are provided as auxiliary inputs to the actor, which is then optimized using GRPO and RWR. We show theoretically that, under sufficient predictive accuracy, the learned representation is equivalent to the automaton state. Experiments on grid world and robotic tasks in Isaac Lab demonstrate that Actor-Predictor outperforms automaton-based baselines on LTL-constrained tasks. It also performs well on non-regular formal specifications, such as $(a^n b^n)$, for which finite-automaton-based approaches are not applicable.

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [A Control-Theoretic Approximation to Predictive Coding Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26319v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26319

**作者：** Ryan Fayyazi、Kyle Daruwalla、Mitra Javadzadeh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26319v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26319v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文比较预测编码（PC）与深度反馈控制（DFC）两种局部学习框架，论证 DFC 是 PC 活性动力学在不动点附近的局部近似（Approx PC），浅层线性网络下精确等价，随深度、非线性与远离平衡而系统偏离；并表明通过重线性化反馈控制器、以序列中间 nudge 逼近输出目标可缓解偏离，在 MNIST/FashionMNIST 上达到与 PC 相当的性能且学到的权重可直接迁回 PC 推断。

**相关性简评：** 其把推断写成受反馈控制的不动点迭代，并对收敛步数、nudge 序列与偏离程度给出刻画，可能为 LVR 中循环 latent 状态的更新规则与停止决策提供方法线索，尤其可供核查多步「逼近目标」式更新是否对应 THINK 阶段的计算分配；但论文任务是分类训练而非视觉推理，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Control-Theoretic Approximation to Predictive Coding Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Backpropagation remains the dominant method for training deep neural networks, but its reliance on non-local computations has motivated the search for biologically plausible alternatives. Two prominent frameworks, predictive coding (PC) and deep feedback control (DFC), offer distinct approaches to local learning, yet their relationship remains unclear. Here, we show that DFC arises as a local approximation to predictive coding dynamics near fixed points, with exact equivalence in shallow linear networks and systematic deviations arising with depth, nonlinearity, and distance from equilibrium. We characterize how this approximation, which we call Approx PC, deviates from PC in its activity dynamics, and show that these deviations can be mitigated by relinearizing the feedback controller and approaching the output target through a sequence of intermediate nudges. In networks trained on MNIST and FashionMNIST, Approx PC reaches task performance comparable to PC trained with the same nudges, and weights learned with it transfer directly to PC inference. Our results unify energy-based and control-theoretic perspectives on learning and open the door to applying tools from control theory to the training of predictive coding networks.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-160"></a>

### 160. [OceanCoder: A Verification-Guided Large Language Model for Automated Simulation Modelling in Ocean Engineering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26333v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26333

**作者：** Can Xue、Changhong He、Ziyi Sun、Anliang Ge、Shimin Yu、Bingchen Liang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26333v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26333v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** OceanCoder 基于 Qwen3-8B 构建海洋工程自动仿真建模模型，用可扩展数据流水线生成 1.2 万条经核验的 SFT 样本，并以三层验证框架（输入参数与任务约束、必需分析完成度、输出工程合理性）给出分级奖励驱动 GRPO，同时发布相应基准。

**相关性简评：** 论文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，但其「以多层核验结果构造分级奖励而非仅看代码可执行」的做法，可能为 LVR 中推理轨迹的过程监督、贡献分配与停止/验证信号设计提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OceanCoder: A Verification-Guided Large Language Model for Automated Simulation Modelling in Ocean Engineering

**abstract/A1：** Numerical simulation is important in ocean engineering, but model construction requires expert knowledge. Large language models support automated modelling, but code syntax and successful execution alone do not establish whether generated models and results meet engineering requirements. We present OceanCoder, a Qwen3-8B-based large language model for automated simulation modelling in ocean engineering. To address specialised programming requirements and scarce training data, we develop an extensible data construction pipeline producing 12,000 verified supervised fine-tuning (SFT) examples across 12 engineering task categories. A three-layer verification framework checks model inputs against engineering parameters and task constraints, verifies completion of required simulation analyses, and assesses the engineering plausibility of computed outputs. Its outcomes provide graded rewards for group relative policy optimisation (GRPO), guiding the model towards engineering task completion. We also construct the first benchmark for automated modelling in ocean engineering, covering out-of-distribution tasks and real-world engineering cases. Experiments and ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of the data pipeline, verification framework and reward design. OceanCoder outperforms both base models and the closed-source comparison, with Pass@1 increasing from 0% for the base model to 46.77% after SFT and 51.43% after GRPO. These results support engineering verification as a training signal for automated simulation modelling.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 11 figures, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [Online Temporal Rotations for 2 Bit KV-Cache Quantization in Video Diffusion Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26334v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26334

**作者：** Ryan Lucas、Petros Prastakos、Rahul Mazumder

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26334v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26334v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究长视频扩散 Transformer 的 KV-cache 2-bit 量化，提出 TRQ：对时间相邻的缓存向量分块在线拟合正交变换（参数化为 2-bit 平面旋转树加固定 Hadamard），把逐通道均值压平后再量化以降低误差。

**相关性简评：** 其按视频在线拟合、面向缓存分块的旋转可提供核查线索：缓存与位置相关的 latent 几何如何影响信息保留，以及轻度正交变换能否在不改动权重下稳定内部状态；但论文目标是生成效率而非推理状态，迁移价值需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Online Temporal Rotations for 2 Bit KV-Cache Quantization in Video Diffusion Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Video diffusion transformers reuse key--value (KV) caches across denoising steps, which quickly becomes a bottleneck for long video generation. We find that quantizing temporally distant cache vectors leads to a large range and high quantization error, but that nearby video tokens in the KV-cache share a persistent pattern per channel. These patterns also vary across videos, motivating rotations that are adapted to each video's unique cache. We propose Temporal Rotation Quantization (TRQ), a method that rotates frames in the cache that are adjacent in time before quantization. For each block of temporally adjacent cache vectors, TRQ fits an orthogonal transform that maps the block's per-channel means to a vector that is close to a constant across channels. To keep the quantization format lightweight, we parameterize this rotation as a tree of $2 \\times 2$ planar rotations, each stored with only two bits, followed by a fixed Hadamard transform shared across all blocks. The rotations are fit without gradients or changes to model weights, and we prove that a simple greedy fit is globally optimal for our 'mean-flattening' objective. The transforms are fit online for each new video, and we release fused Triton kernels to make this process efficient at inference. At 2-bit precision and matched compression, TRQ improves VBench subject consistency over the state-of-the-art method QVG from 90.38 to 92.02 on LongCat-Video-13B and from 86.33 to 87.61 on HY-WorldPlay-8B, as well as improving other metrics.

</details>

<a id="paper-162"></a>

### 162. [Sirius-Tiamat: How Small Can a One-Pass Decision Encoder Get Before Its Decisions Change?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26339v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26339

**作者：** Mohammed Nayeem Khan、Vrishank Tiwari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26339v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26339v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察 421M 参数的单次前向选择项决策编码器 Laya 在 bf16、低位分组量化、层剪枝与蒸馏下与原 fp32 决策不一致的比例，并跟踪 ECE 校准，同时检验温度重校准、张量级缩放和 early exit 置信门控的作用。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉主线无直接关系，但其「减少层数或精度会改变决策」的度量，以及「early exit 需先重校准置信门才能省层」的发现，可能可供核查 LVR 中 STOP 门控与动态计算成本权衡，以及深度变化对内部状态可用性的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sirius-Tiamat: How Small Can a One-Pass Decision Encoder Get Before Its Decisions Change?

**abstract/A1：** A one-pass decision encoder reads a text, a question and several named options, and returns a probability for each option in a single forward pass. We ask how far Laya, a 421M-parameter encoder of this kind, can be compressed before its decisions change. We count decisions that differ from Laya's own full-precision (fp32) answer, and track calibration (how well confidence matches accuracy, measured by expected calibration error, ECE), on 1,221 synthetic items with the training schemas, 273 with an unseen schema, and 800 out-of-family questions from ARC, MMLU and HellaSwag. Layer pruning of a related model family is known; we add flips, calibration, held-out data and retraining. bf16 changes 0.2% of in-family decisions but 2.2% out of family; 6-bit grouped rounding 1.3% and 5.9%; deleting 6 layers 0.9% and 9.1%, while ECE rises from 5.9% to 39.2%. One fitted temperature, needing no labels, restores it to 3.6%. One scale per tensor fails below six bits, and CPU dynamic int8 changes 30% of decisions, nearly all through one layer. Total variation tracks flips for rounding but not for deletion, and an early exit saves layers over a static cut only if its confidence gate is first recalibrated. Distilling a 16-layer student cuts its in-family flips from 44% to 9% (45% out of family). At equal stored size, rounding flips fewer decisions than deletion. Data are synthetic and LLM-labelled, for one architecture.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages, 23 figures, 27 tables

</details>

### 163. [Mamba Flow Matching Neural Processes: Linear-Time Inference for Irregularly Observed Spatial Fields](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26355v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26355

**作者：** Cosmo Santoni、Giovanni Charles、Timothy James Hitge、Oliver Watson、Elizaveta Semenova

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26355v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26355v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MambaFlowNP：一种线性计算复杂度的联合预测神经过程，将上下文点与查询点置于同一序列做多方向 Mamba-2 扫描，并用条件 flow matching 训练，从而对稀疏不规则观测的空间场做快速概率插值。

**相关性简评：** 其核心设计——不把上下文压成单一隐状态、让每个查询从自身扫描位置读取状态，并以 flow matching 直接生成整场——可能与 LVR 中视觉上下文压缩导致的信息坍缩、latent 读出的位置与深度效应以及 latent 轨迹生成可供核查，但迁移价值需读原文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mamba Flow Matching Neural Processes: Linear-Time Inference for Irregularly Observed Spatial Fields

**abstract/A1：** Interpolating a spatial field, such as daily maximum temperature, from sparse, irregularly located observations recurs across the environmental and health sciences. Standard Gaussian-process kriging refits hyperparameters and solves an $O(N^3)$ system for every new field. When many related fields are observed, such as daily maps from one sensor network, neural processes (NPs) instead learn the interpolation map from past fields and predict a new field in one forward pass. Joint-predictive variants, which sample whole fields rather than independent per-location values, typically use self-attention over context and query points, costing $O(K^2)$ in the combined point count $K$ and limiting practical use to a few thousand points. State-space models offer a linear-time alternative, but a direct state-space adaptation compresses the context into one hidden state before reading queries, reducing what the decoder sees. We propose MambaFlowNP, a linear-compute joint-predictive NP trained with conditional flow matching. A multi-directional Mamba-2 scan reads context and query tokens in one shared sequence, so each query's velocity is read from its own scan position rather than a compressed context summary, and because queries only read the scan state, predictions are consistent across query sets. MambaFlowNP is competitive with the strongest attention-based joint-predictive baselines on synthetic Matérn Gaussian-process benchmarks, and on regional to continental-scale NOAA temperature interpolation it attains the lowest RMSE and CRPS and near-nominal coverage on every tier, while running 114--784$\\times$ faster than the strongest joint-predictive baseline. A single forward pass scales to $10^6$ points on one NVIDIA L40S GPU in under a second. This makes joint prediction feasible for continental-scale, irregularly sampled sensor networks; predictive quality is evaluated up to $K=5{,}580$.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. XX pages, X figures. Code: https://github.com/Cosmonaught/Mambaflownp

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [Learning from Context: Leave-One-Out Prediction in Jump Diffusions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26359v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26359

**作者：** Christian Wald

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26359v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26359v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究跳扩散离散生成模型中的 leave-one-out（LOO）预测：每个坐标只依据其他坐标的加噪状态进行预测，避免自身腐蚀状态带来的捷径；给出保持规定边缘分布的条件，并提出无需额外网络评估的解析似然补全，在 Text8、LM1B 与 ZINC250k 上评估。

**相关性简评：** 「屏蔽自身状态、强制依赖上下文」的 LOO 训练目标可能可供核查是否有助于缓解 LVR 中依赖局部旁路或视觉捷径而导致的信息坍缩；但研究对象为文本与分子的离散生成，与 VLM 推理的迁移尚未验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning from Context: Leave-One-Out Prediction in Jump Diffusions

**abstract/A1：** Continuous diffusion models generate discrete data through stochastic dynamics in an embedding space. We consider jump diffusions that additionally reset coordinates to embeddings of categorical values. A coordinate's own corrupted state can make prediction easy, potentially reducing the pressure to learn dependencies between coordinates. We study leave-one-out (LOO) prediction, in which each coordinate is predicted from the corrupted states of the other coordinates. We show that, under stated assumptions, jump diffusions with complete coordinate resets and state-independent clocks preserve the prescribed marginals when reset destinations use exact LOO probabilities and the drift uses the full endpoint posterior. We further show that this drift can be constructed from LOO probabilities by analytic likelihood completion without additional network evaluations. We evaluate unrestricted, LOO, and completed predictions on Text8 and LM1B text generation and ZINC250k molecular graph generation. LOO-based variants improve key generation metrics over matched unrestricted controls on all three datasets. Controlled interventions assess reliance on local inputs and context, and we analyze the effect of analytic likelihood completion.

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [How Much Should an Agent Remember? Deployment Lessons from Self-Distilled Memory for LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26361v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26361

**作者：** K. R. Jayaram、Vatche Isahagian、Aswath Vaithinathan Aravindan、Vinod Muthusamy、Punleuk Oum、Gaodan Fang、Michal Ulewicz、Gegi Thomas、Evelyn Duesterwald

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26361v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26361v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建了从 agent 自身轨迹中挖掘 lessons、再整合为带合并计数的 guidelines 的记忆自蒸馏流水线，在 AppWorld 上跨基线 40–92% 的多个模型比较全量与子集记忆，发现合适的记忆量取决于模型本身，并总结部署中的失败教训。

**相关性简评：** 其「按模型容量决定状态保留量并在推理期选择子集」与「从自身轨迹蒸馏经验」的做法，可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态预算、视觉证据筛选与轨迹监督提供可核查线索；但属纯文本 agent 记忆，无视觉实验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Much Should an Agent Remember? Deployment Lessons from Self-Distilled Memory for LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents on long-horizon tasks fail in recurring, procedural ways. They mis-paginate an API, resolve the wrong entity, or answer when no answer was asked. We built a pipeline that lets an agent learn from its own history. It first mines short \\emph{lessons} from the agent's own trajectories, one to three per episode. It then consolidates those lessons into a smaller set of \\emph{guidelines}, where each guideline records how many lessons it merged. At inference the agent receives either all guidelines or a selected subset. The pipeline ships in the open-source altk-evolve library and is deployed in IBM's CUGA agent and watsonx Orchestrate platform. We report what we learned building, measuring, and productizing it. Our primary instrument is the AppWorld benchmark, across models spanning 40--92\\% baseline task completion. The central finding is that the right amount of memory is a property of the model, not the memory. A strong model keeps improving as guidelines are added. DeepSeek-V3.2 gains +9.5 points of task completion and +16.1 points of scenario completion with its full 191-guideline set. A weak model gains less from the same full set than from a small selected subset. gpt-oss-120b starts at 39.9\\% task completion. It reaches 51.2\\% with all 199 of its guidelines but 55.4\\% (mean of two runs) with a selected subset of about 29. Around that result we report what went wrong: a high-support guideline that backfired, provenance silently dropped during consolidation, a swallowed API error that failed every task while producing a plausible log, and a baseline confound that nearly produced a misleading comparison with a prior method. We distill these into recommendations for teams deploying agent memory.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to IAAI 2027 (Oral)

</details>

### 166. [HiSA: Sparsifying the Hidden Dimension of RoPE Attention in LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26363v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26363

**作者：** Ambroise Borbely、Mathieu Salzmann、Olivier Verscheure

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26363v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26363v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HiSA：保留 query/key 投影完整，但依 RoPE 结构把 key/query 拆成若干部分，按 token 位置的静态无参数规则只计算部分点积，使注意力在隐藏特征维度上稀疏；从零训练 LLM 并以 FlashAttention-3 实现。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但其「按位置规则裁剪注意力分数分量」及「稠密注意力会主动压低被裁剪分量」的观察，可能可供核查注意力结构与位置因素如何影响内部状态保留及降低计算成本的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HiSA: Sparsifying the Hidden Dimension of RoPE Attention in LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Attention in large language models (LLMs) scores every query against every key with a full dot product. We investigate whether this dot product must be computed completely for attention to be effective. We propose Hidden Sparse Attention (HiSA), which keeps the query and key projections intact but only computes a partial dot product, following a static, parameter-free rule set by the token positions: attention becomes sparse in the hidden (feature) dimension, an axis much less explored than token sparsity, rather than over tokens as in almost all sparse attention methods. Based on the structure of rotary position embeddings (RoPE), we decompose keys and queries into parts which can be omitted when computing attention. By training LLMs from scratch with HiSA, we find that this partial dot product does not hurt but improves performance compared with the complete product while computing only about a third of the query-key products. During inference HiSA improves decoding speed and shrinks memory footprint. We also extend HiSA to FlashAttention-3 kernels for training and inference. Finally, we motivate our approach by showing that dense attention learns to suppress the attention score parts that HiSA drops. Code is available at https://github.com/ambroise-b/hisa.

</details>

### 167. [EgoCaptioner: Towards Comprehensive and Fine-Grained Egocentric Video Captioning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26380v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26380

**作者：** Yuqi Tang、Yang Shi、Yuran Wang、Tengfei Liu、Rundong Wang、Zongyang Qiu、Qixun Wang、Zhuoran Zhang、Xuanyu Zhu、Yifan Xu、Yiyan Ji、Shujie Li、Shixuan Liu、Haotian Wang、Yuanxing Zhang、Pengfei Wan、Chengwei Qin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26380v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26380v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 EgoCaptioner，用「视频级概览＋带时间戳的分步叙述」格式做第一视角视频描述，构建粗到细标注流程得到 5 万条训练数据，并用事实性、覆盖度、步级时间对齐与冗余惩罚等多重奖励经 GRPO 精修描述。

**相关性简评：** 其奖励设计（事实准确、覆盖度、时间对齐、惩罚碎片化）与「用 pose 派生运动事实补充监督」可作为核查线索，考察能否用于监督 VLM 内部状态对细粒度视觉证据的保留与时间定位，但原文针对描述生成而非 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EgoCaptioner: Towards Comprehensive and Fine-Grained Egocentric Video Captioning

**abstract/A1：** Egocentric video captioning requires fine-grained detail and accurate timing, yet current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often miss brief actions, confuse their order, or misinterpret the wearer's motion. Existing caption formats also offer limited support for combining detailed narration with precise timing. We present EgoCaptioner, which pairs a video-level overview of the setting, participants, and the wearer's overall activity with timed steps whose narration adapts to varying perceptual demands and marks key moments at specific timestamps. We develop a coarse-to-fine annotation pipeline that establishes event structure before expanding step descriptions, yielding 50,000 training examples from eight egocentric video sources. Complementary rewards assess factual accuracy and coverage, measure step-level temporal alignment, and penalize fragmented or redundant narration, guiding caption refinement through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). To improve the reliability of factual supervision, we supplement caption references with pose-derived motion facts when sensor data are available. Across five egocentric benchmarks, EgoCaptioner achieves leading performance among evaluated open-source models in caption quality, caption-conditioned question answering, and temporal alignment. It also surpasses Gemini 3.1 Pro on PhysCapBench and all evaluated proprietary baselines on Minerva-Ego under the caption-to-QA protocol. Ablations support the complementary roles of factual and temporal rewards and show that coarse-to-fine annotation provides more effective supervision than single-pass annotation.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages

</details>

### 168. [Training Vision Models for LLM-Driven Weight-Space Programming](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26408v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26408

**作者：** Timo Ropinski、Sebastian Kranz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26408v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26408v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 LLM 驱动权重空间编程的视觉基准：五个 LLM 在 Waterbirds、CIFAR-100、PACS 等任务上对卷积网络和 ViT 执行重标注与纠错式权重编辑，累计 2600 余次编辑并按成功率、副作用和精度损失评分，同时提出 Edit-Ready Training，通过预演 LLM 的闭式权重编辑使网络更易被改动。

**相关性简评：** 其「为可编辑性而训练」以及编辑成功率／副作用／精度损失的评测协议，或可供核查 latent 状态被定向修改或干预时的可控性与代价，但与推理期 latent 生成和视觉证据使用无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Training Vision Models for LLM-Driven Weight-Space Programming

**abstract/A1：** It has been demonstrated that, in certain settings, large language models can perform weight-space programming to directly modify the weights of neural networks. However, it is unknown how dedicated training of these networks can contribute to the successes of weight-space programming. We present a benchmark of LLM-driven weight-space programming on vision models, in which five LLMs edit convolutional networks and vision transformers on relabeling and error-correction tasks on Waterbirds, CIFAR-100, PACS and Entity-30. We report more than 2,600 such edit processes, whereby each edit is scored on held-out images for success, side effects and accuracy loss. We compare standard training with label smoothing, gradient-based editable training, and our Edit-Ready Training (ERT), a novel method that rehearses the direct, closed-form weight edits LLMs perform. We show that preparing networks for editing raises the success of LLM edits substantially where standard networks are hard to edit. ERT further achieves the highest mean score for GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5 across all evaluated baselines. We will release our LLM testing framework alongside all tasks, editing trajectories and scores. Project page: https://viscom.uni-ulm.de/wsp

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://viscom.uni-ulm.de/wsp

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [Sequential Anytime Valid Tests for Efficient Inference-Time Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26413v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26413

**作者：** Vishesh Kumar Singh、Sayak Ray Chowdhury

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26413v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26413v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 e-RWSC：把 Best-of-N 候选按最终答案聚类，用奖励加权自一致性打分，并以 betting supermartingale（e-process）对每个答案做任意时间有效的序贯检验，在容错 α 下仅在认证某答案占多数时停止，给出 O(log(1/α)/d\_KL) 的样本复杂度及多模型/数据集/奖励模型上的成本与精度实验。

**相关性简评：** 研究对象是纯文本对齐的采样成本，与视觉或 latent 状态无关；但「带统计保证的 anytime-valid 停止规则」可能为 LVR 中 STOP 决策与证据累积何时终止提供可核查的阈值与错误控制思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sequential Anytime Valid Tests for Efficient Inference-Time Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Inference-time alignment methods, such as Best-of-N (BoN), improve language model outputs by generating multiple candidates per prompt and selecting among them using a reward model. Much of this cost is wasted: a fixed budget is applied to every prompt, although the computation required by prompts varies widely. Adaptive methods halt once sampled answers appear settled, but their thresholds are tuned heuristics with no statistical control over early-stopping errors. We instead seek an anytime-valid stopping rule that takes an error tolerance $α$ as input and halts only once an answer is certified to hold a majority ($θ\_a &gt; 1/2$) under the generator's distribution. We aggregate candidates by final answer with a reward-weighted self-consistency (RWSC) score and treat stopping as a sequential test over the resulting clusters. Applying Wald's sequential probability ratio test to these clusters yields SPRT-RWSC, which performs well empirically, but its guarantee fails because the leading answer shifts during sampling, and the run is continuously monitored. Our main contribution, e-RWSC, replaces Wald's test with a betting supermartingale (an e-process) for every answer the policy produces, bounding the probability of ever certifying a non-dominant answer by $α$, uniformly over time and across all answers. The RWSC scores drive the bets, so they affect only how quickly a dominant answer $a^\\star$ is certified, not validity; certification takes $O(\\log(1/α)/d\_{\\mathrm{KL}}(θ\_{a^\\star}\\\|1/2))$ samples. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm the predicted $1/d\_{\\mathrm{KL}}$ scaling of cost and show that accuracy improves over baselines at substantially lower token cost.

</details>

<a id="paper-170"></a>

### 170. [ZeroVLN: Learning to Navigate without Supervised Fine-Tuning of the VLM Brain](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26414v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26414

**作者：** Zixuan Huang、Yang Zhou、Guli Zhang、Sunzhu Li、Hongyan Xie、Zehao Chen、Fuzhen Zhuang、Kaixuan Wang、Xiaozhi Chen、Yikun Ban、Deqing Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26414v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26414v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ZeroVLN 提出 slow-fast 视觉语言导航框架：不对 VLM「大脑」做导航域 SFT，而以自然语言语义工具调用接口把移动决策交给 action expert，再用闭环强化学习联合优化慢决策与快执行。

**相关性简评：** 其「保持基座 VLM 权重大体不变、只训练轻量执行端」的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉能力退化与信息坍缩、以及慢推理与快执行的贡献分配提供可核查线索；任务本身属机器人导航，与 latent 推理状态无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ZeroVLN: Learning to Navigate without Supervised Fine-Tuning of the VLM Brain

**abstract/A1：** Representative vision-language navigation (VLN) methods typically couple a vision-language model (VLM) brain with a learnable action head and fine-tune the VLM brain on navigation-domain data to predict geometric goals.
