However, such adaptation can substantially alter base VLM weights and degrade general visual capabilities. We introduce ZeroVLN, a slow-fast navigation framework that achieves effective navigation with zero navigation-domain SFT of the VLM brain. Rather than teaching the brain to predict geometric goals, we equip it with a semantic tool-calling interface for conveying movement decisions to an action expert in natural language. We first adapt the action expert to carry out these movement requests, then jointly improve the brain's movement decisions and the action expert's continuous execution through closed-loop reinforcement learning (RL). Trained on the same navigation episodes, ZeroVLN substantially outperforms representative SFT-based hierarchical VLN methods on standard VLN-CE (continuous environments) benchmarks, while inducing only minor changes to the brain's weights and largely preserving its general capabilities. To our knowledge, ZeroVLN is the first slow-fast VLN framework with zero navigation-domain SFT of the VLM brain, and the first to jointly optimize its slow and fast policies through closed-loop agentic RL in VLN-CE.

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [FEA solver integration of a composite World Model with a Spiking Neural Network memory Block for computationally efficient modelling of defects and variabilities](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26419v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26419

**作者：** Bassam El Said、Adam Sobey

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26419v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26419v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文为纤维增强复合材料构建多模态脉冲 Transformer「世界模型」代理：材料编码器 + SNN 记忆块跟踪长期材料内部状态、Transformer 块学短期应变，经三阶段训练（含递归微调与 scheduled sampling）抑制累积漂移，并拆解后耦合到非线性 FE 求解器以模拟含缺陷结构。

**相关性简评：** 其递归自驱动 rollout 下的长期内部状态保持与 scheduled sampling 抑制误差漂移训练，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态更新、轨迹训练与贡献分配的稳定性问题提供可核查线索；但属材料代理建模，与视觉推理主线无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FEA solver integration of a composite World Model with a Spiking Neural Network memory Block for computationally efficient modelling of defects and variabilities

**abstract/A1：** Each fibre-reinforced composite structure has a unique material architecture. The manufacturing methods used and the presence of defects lead to local variations which require material-scale models that can resolve the response in real time at every location in the structure. Concurrent multi-scale schemes provide this capability, but at a prohibitive computational cost for large structures as the number of integration points and time increments scales with size. Surrogates have been studied extensively as replacements for material-scale models to mitigate these computational limitations. A surrogate must represent an arbitrary local architecture including defects, track the irreversible material state over an unknown number of increments, remain stable when driven recursively by its own predictions, and fit within the memory available at every integration point. Here, a Composites World Model based on a Multi-Modal Spiking Transformer architecture is designed to meet these requirements. The proposed architecture combines a material encoder, a Spiking Neural Network memory block that tracks the long-term internal state of the material, and transformer blocks that learn the short-term applied strain state. The system is trained in three stages: material architecture encoding, base training on short time windows, and recursive fine-tuning with scheduled sampling to suppress cumulative error drift. The trained model is disassembled at inference and coupled to a non-linear FE solver, enabling structural scale simulation of composites containing embedded defects. Benchmarks against experimental results for IM7/8552 laminates under tension, compression and transverse shear, for pristine and wrinkled composites, show that the SNN memory block successfully tracks the material internal state and results in prediction errors between 0.4% and 4.8%.

</details>

<a id="paper-172"></a>

### 172. [Seer: Guiding Small Deep-Research Agents with Shared Decision Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26423v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26423

**作者：** Yuhao Zhou、Yang Zhou、Yuanxi Li、Jindi Lv、Dan Si、Qing Ye、Jiancheng Lv

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26423v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26423v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Seer 为设备端小型 deep-research agent 提出推理时引导框架，从共享真实轨迹离线训练工具响应模拟器与价值估计器，在仅执行选定动作的条件下评估候选动作。

**相关性简评：** 其将动作提议与后果预测、价值估计解耦，并以想象搜索替代真实工具调用的做法，可能为 LVR 的 latent 假设筛选、证据获取和停止决策提供方法线索，但迁移到多模态推理仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Seer: Guiding Small Deep-Research Agents with Shared Decision Models

**abstract/A1：** Deep-research agents repeatedly decide what to search, read, and verify, often using large cloud-hosted language models for action proposal. However, privacy and local control motivate running agents on users' devices, where limited memory and compute favor smaller models and complicate on-device training. Yet inference-time guidance faces three obstacles: exploring alternatives through real execution adds tool calls, prompted self-evaluation is bounded by the agent's judgment, and model-specific guidance risks repeated adaptation costs. We introduce Seer, an inference-time framework providing local guidance that separates the agent's action proposal from learned consequence prediction and value estimation. From a shared corpus of real research trajectories, we train two models: a tool-response simulator on observed call--response pairs and a value estimator on histories labeled with discounted terminal correctness. The simulator and estimator are trained offline and reused across agents without further training. Moreover, Seer executes only the selected action and adds only real tool responses to the committed history, avoiding tool calls on discarded candidates at additional inference cost. Across multiple deep-research benchmarks, Seer substantially improves agent performance for different language models. For instance, GAIA accuracy rises from 13.6% to 41.7% for Qwen3.5-4B and from 22.3% to 50.5% for Qwen3.5-9B. Furthermore, our analyses suggest that broader candidate coverage can compensate for imperfect simulation, while prediction and scoring errors limit the benefits of wider imagined search. These findings highlight the potential of reusable models for prediction and evaluation to strengthen small research agents through extra local computation without the real execution of the discarded candidates.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [Risk-Controlled Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26438v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26438

**作者：** Zixuan Liu、Hashem Haghbayan、Zizhan Zheng、Siavash Khajavi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26438v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26438v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 R2-VLA：为检索增强的 VLA 训练不确定性预测器，判断检索示范是否被有效利用，并通过任务级风险校准阈值来决定自主执行还是请求专家纠正，同时把纠正写入检索记忆。

**相关性简评：** 其「不确定性触发外部求助、并校准阈值满足失败风险约束」的决策与门控机制，可供核查能否迁移到 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 何时停止推理或补充证据的问题；但该文面向机器人操作，与 latent 推理状态无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Risk-Controlled Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models

**abstract/A1：** Retrieval-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) models enable efficient test-time adaptation by leveraging expert demonstrations as additional context without updating model parameters. However, even when retrieved demonstrations are relevant to the current task, the VLA may fail to effectively utilize the retrieved information, ultimately leading to task failure. To address this challenge, we propose R2-VLA, a risk-controlled retrieval-augmented VLA framework that enables reliable test-time adaptation by satisfying a user-specified task success rate requirement with minimal expert assistance. Specifically, R2-VLA learns an uncertainty predictor that estimates the uncertainty of the VLA's predicted actions given the retrieved demonstrations, guiding when to act autonomously or request expert assistance. We further introduce a task-level risk-control procedure that calibrates the uncertainty threshold to satisfy a user-specified task-failure risk requirement. Moreover, R2-VLA continually incorporates expert corrections into its retrieval memory to improve future adaptation while preserving the prescribed task-level risk guarantee. Experiments across three VLA backbones and four LIBERO benchmark suites demonstrate that R2-VLA improves average task success by 9.2--11.2 percentage points, achieving the desired target with fewer expert requests. These results highlight the effectiveness of connecting the usefulness of retrieved demonstrations with task-level risk control to enable successful robotic manipulation.

</details>

<a id="paper-174"></a>

### 174. [A CPU Inside an LLM: Zero-Gradient Compilation of Exact Circuits into Pretrained Weights](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26450v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26450

**作者：** Tanmay Sah

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26450v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26450v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出零梯度、闭式的「电路移植」原语，把多位数四则运算及布尔、多数、奇偶等确定性电路直接编译进预训练 LLM 权重，借助 Mellin-GLU 求积处理 RMSNorm 尺度、跨 token 尺度抵消与宽度扩展，在不训练、不加运行时钩子的条件下实现精确计算。

**相关性简评：** 该工作纯文本、无视觉与 latent 推理环节，与主线不直接相关；但其对归一化漂移、RoPE 注意力结构与跨 token 尺度抵消的解析处理，可供核查 LVR 中位置、注意力结构与归一化如何影响 latent 状态中任务必要信息的保真与读取。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A CPU Inside an LLM: Zero-Gradient Compilation of Exact Circuits into Pretrained Weights

**abstract/A1：** Pretrained language models (LMs) remain unreliable on exact multi-digit arithmetic without gradient fine-tuning or external tool interpreters. We introduce closed-form, zero-gradient circuit-grafting primitives that compile deterministic computation directly into pretrained LM weights using zero training examples and zero runtime hooks, while bounding per-layer normalization drift and preserving natural-language (NL) competence. Our construction combines three analytical mechanisms: (1) two-layer Mellin-GLU quadrature over bias-free SwiGLU/GeGLU layers, turning unknown per-token RMSNorm scaling into translation along a log-spaced gate grid ($&gt;250\\times$ dynamic range) with IEEE 754 error bounds; (2) cross-token scale cancellation via signed Chebyshev Indefinite-RoPE self-sink attention and memoryless SiLU-antisymmetric GatedDeltaNet shifts; and (3) axis-aligned width extension ($d \\to d+K$) with $\\sqrt{d/(d+K)}$ norm compensation, Base-1000 canonical BPE emission, and differential $(+254,-256)$ tied unembeddings. An automated 5-stage compiler synthesizes 4-8 digit signed $\\{+,-,\\times\\}$ circuits in constant $\\mathsf{TC}^0$ depth alongside 3-literal Boolean (100%, $N=1{,}728$; plus 42-layer Gemma 4 E4B audits) and 5-bit Majority/Parity (100%) circuits. On 6-digit arithmetic ($N=1{,}200$, BF16), IBM Granite 4.2 3B (1.07M-1.33M active non-zeros, $\\le 0.036\\%$) and Alibaba Qwen 3.5 4B (2.25M-2.39M non-zeros, $\\le 0.057\\%$) reach 99.17% and 100.00% ALU-prefix accuracy (100% with an FP32 sidecar) and 98%-100% mid-sentence resumption, outperforming 12.8K-sample LoRA (42.2% / 61.7%, 0% inline continuation). A factorized block-sparse sidecar holds A100 memory at 7.89 / 10.65 GiB (4.60 / 8.14 GiB under INT8/INT4 + FP32 sidecar) while passing all 14/14 pre-registered $&gt;2$K-token Tango splits ($N=4{,}392$; 0/11,134 OOD triggers).

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 3 figures, 24 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-175"></a>

### 175. [Neural Conditional Gaussian Model: Efficient Data Assimilation with Sequential Uncertainty Quantification for Nonlinear Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26463v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26463

**作者：** Chuanqi Chen、Nan Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26463v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26463v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Neural CGM：把未观测物理状态映射到潜空间并让潜状态服从条件高斯动力学，从而在潜空间闭式完成数据同化更新，再由高斯解码器将后验均值与协方差映射回物理空间，实现部分观测下的状态估计、不确定性量化与预测。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理无直接关系，但其「潜后验均值＋协方差随观测序列顺序更新、并直接进入高斯负对数似然训练」的机制，可能为 LVR 中带不确定性的 latent 状态更新与基于置信度的停止决策提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Neural Conditional Gaussian Model: Efficient Data Assimilation with Sequential Uncertainty Quantification for Nonlinear Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Data assimilation (DA) in partially observed nonlinear systems requires repeated estimation of both hidden states and their uncertainty. Conventional nonlinear filtering can be computationally expensive and often relies on an explicit dynamical model. In this paper, we develop the Neural Conditional Gaussian Model (Neural CGM), a data-driven dynamical model that maps unobserved physical states into a learned latent space, where the dynamics of the latent states follow a conditional Gaussian structure. This structure allows closed-form DA updates in the latent space, while nonlinear encoder-decoder mappings retain the flexibility needed to represent complex dynamics in physical space. A key feature of Neural CGM is its treatment of uncertainty in DA. The latent posterior mean and covariance are mapped to the physical variables through a Gaussian decoder that combines local moment propagation with learned nonlinear corrections. The resulting physical-space posterior statistics depend on the sequentially updated latent distribution rather than on a separate post-processing estimate. The same statistics enter a Gaussian negative log-likelihood during training, so both the posterior mean and uncertainty affect the learned model. Because Neural CGM learns the dynamical model itself, it can also be used for state forecasting and simulation without explicit knowledge of the governing equations. Numerical experiments on benchmark nonlinear dynamical systems show accurate state estimation and forecasting while substantially reducing online assimilation cost relative to conventional nonlinear DA methods. Neural CGM is therefore suited to repeated state estimation, uncertainty quantification, and prediction from partial observations.

</details>

<a id="paper-176"></a>

### 176. [A new algorithmic approach for finding flat minima: critically damped Polyak stepsizes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26475v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26475

**作者：** Francesco Caporali、Jason M. Altschuler、Boris Hanin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26475v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26475v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出「临界阻尼 Polyak」（CDP）自适应步长规则：将经典 Polyak 步长乘以 4 并加入阻尼因子，使梯度下降在局部二次型目标上沿极小值流形等幅振荡并收敛，从而在有限学习率下偏向更平坦的极小点，并在非线性 teacher-student 网络与 LLM 微调中观察到类似 SAM 的锐度下降效果。

**相关性简评：** 这是纯优化理论工作，与视觉证据或 latent 状态生成无直接联系，但其向平坦极小的步长调度可能可供核查：在训练 looped/latent 推理模型时能否通过控制锐度改善长序列与深层循环的稳定性及泛化。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A new algorithmic approach for finding flat minima: critically damped Polyak stepsizes

**abstract/A1：** We present a new adaptive stepsize rule that biases gradient descent toward flatter minima in both theory and practice, and establish the first uniform-in-time guarantees for both sharpness and loss reduction for a broad class of locally quadratic objectives. Our critically damped Polyak (CDP) stepsizes multiply the classical Polyak rule by $4$ and a mild damping factor. The factor $4$ is critical in that it produces equioscillations around the manifold of minimizers $\\mathcal{M}$ to leading order. Damping provably drives convergence to $\\mathcal{M}$. CDP thus operates at the edge of stability by adapting its stepsizes to local curvature. Once the loss is small, its projected iterates follow gradient descent on $\\log{λ\_1(\\nabla^2 \\mathcal{L})}$ along $\\mathcal{M}$ to leading order, paralleling the flatness bias of normalized gradient descent (Arora et al., 2022). Unlike normalized gradient descent, CDP step lengths shrink with the distance to $\\mathcal{M}$, enabling convergence to an interpolating solution that locally minimizes sharpness at finite learning rate. We establish our theory for a broad class of non-negative losses with locally quadratic Morse-Bott structure near $\\mathcal{M}$, capturing regular interpolation regimes in smooth overparameterized neural networks. Empirically, CDP stepsizes produce a flattening effect similar to that of Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM, Foret et al., 2021), reducing sharpness even in settings beyond our theory, including nonlinear teacher-student networks and fine-tuning of pre-trained LLMs.

**comment/C1：** 56 pages, 19 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-177"></a>

### 177. [SHUSH: Emission-Aware Reward Design for Proactive Video Assistants](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26478v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26478

**作者：** Shawn Liang、Soamar Homsi、Gourav Datta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26478v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26478v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析 MMDuet2 中 PAUC 奖励不惩罚回复数量而导致过度发言，提出面向区间标注的 emission-aware F1 奖励，用互斥的片段—发言匹配同时惩罚误报与漏报，并在 OmniPro 视觉子集与 ProactiveVideoQA 上检验该奖励的效果。

**相关性简评：** 对「发言次数」定价的奖励设计，以及不受次数影响的指标会高排过度输出策略这一诊断，可供核查是否能迁移到 LVR 中何时停止/输出（STOP）与证据获取成本的训练和评价。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SHUSH: Emission-Aware Reward Design for Proactive Video Assistants

**abstract/A1：** Proactive video assistants must decide not only what to say, but also when to speak. Existing reinforcement-learning objectives can reward timely, correct responses without accounting for response volume, thereby encouraging unnecessary or repeated emissions. We identify this failure mode in MMDuet2, whose proactive area-under-the-curve (PAUC) reward promotes timely responses but does not price the number of emissions. We show analytically that this makes additional responses weakly penalized or effectively free, systematically encouraging over-emission. We then propose an emission-aware F1 reward for interval-valued annotations, using exclusive span--emission matching to price false emissions and missed spans. Under MMDuet2's training recipe, our policy on the visual subset of OmniPro replies 2.5x less often than MMDuet2 while raising timing F1 from 0.259 to 0.396 and content-aware F1 from 0.103 to 0.154; a more conservative variant cuts replies by 82.5% and raises precision from 0.161 to 0.418. On ProactiveVideoQA, the same reward adds replies where the baseline is nearly silent, and PAUC ranks a heavily over-emitting policy second despite near-last F1, because it is insensitive to response count. Our results indicate that proactive assistants should be trained and evaluated with objectives that price emissions.

**comment/C1：** under review at ICASSP

</details>

<a id="paper-178"></a>

### 178. [SGAR: Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction via Sparse Geometry-Guided Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26493v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26493

**作者：** Aoxiang Fan、Maria Pilligua、Corentin Dumery、Oliver Heinimann、Pascal Fua

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26493v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26493v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对 feed-forward 三维重建中稠密跨视图注意力的二次复杂度问题，提出 SGAR：用轻量学习的选择模块预测 token 间跨视图三维邻近性，使每个 patch token 只关注少量其他视图 token，从而以稀疏几何引导注意力替代全局稠密注意力。

**相关性简评：** 其「学习式稀疏 key 选择」可供核查是否可迁移到 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与注意力范围控制，即用轻量预测器决定哪些视觉 token 值得进入推理状态；但该工作本身面向三维重建，与多模态推理无直接联系，迁移性属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SGAR: Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction via Sparse Geometry-Guided Attention

**abstract/A1：** Feed-forward models have been very successful in 3D reconstruction with ad hoc attention designs that relate all view patches densely. However, the cost of this dense global attention grows quadratically with the number of views, preventing the model from applying to larger-scale reconstruction settings. In this paper, we argue that most dense attention computations are unnecessary from a classical structure-from-motion perspective: 3D structures stem from geometrically matching regions in 2D. We bring this principle to learning-based feed-forward reconstruction with our Sparse Geometric Attention Reconstruction (SGAR) method. In SGAR, each patch token attends to only a small set of patch tokens from other views, chosen by a lightweight learned key selection module trained to predict cross-view 3D proximity of tokens. Thus, dense global attention is replaced by much more efficient geometric-similarity-guided sparse attention. SGAR is a general method that can be applied to different feed-forward models by fine-tuning. In our experiments, we show that SGAR is the fastest among both state-of-the-art dense and sparse methods and the most accurate sparse one. It runs up to 12-times faster than dense attention at 1,000 views with stable pose accuracy where existing sparse methods fail, and shows the best performance in reconstructing more complete geometries on the same time budget.

</details>

<a id="paper-179"></a>

### 179. [AURA: AUdio-dRiven streaming Avatar](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26540v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26540

**作者：** Nathaniel Cohen、Nicolas Dufour、Amélie Royer、Alasdair Newson、Patrick Pérez

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26540v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26540v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AURA，一种音频驱动的流式说话头像视频扩散模型：用滚动 KV cache 逐 chunk 因果生成，只依赖当前音频块而非多秒前瞻，并通过两阶段蒸馏（教师轨迹对齐、加反向模拟的 DMD）压缩扩散步数，使 1.3B 模型单卡实时输出 576p、5B 输出 720p。

**相关性简评：** 其滚动 KV cache 与 chunk 级滑动窗口因果注意力，以及「轨迹匹配 + 少步蒸馏」压缩计算的训练方案，可能为 LVR 中流式推理状态的缓存管理、受限计算下 latent 更新步数压缩提供可供核查的方法线索；但论文本身面向视频生成，与视觉证据筛选和推理决策无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AURA: AUdio-dRiven streaming Avatar

**abstract/A1：** Current audio-driven video diffusion models can synthesize realistic talking-head videos but generally require dozens of diffusion denoising steps over multi-second video chunks, making them unsuitable for streaming applications such as voice assistants and virtual avatars, where low-latency and online causal generation are required properties. AURA generates video chunk by chunk with a rolling KV cache, requiring only the audio of the current chunk to start generating, rather than a multi-second audio look-ahead like bidirectional models. It comes in two sizes: AURA 1.3B streams 576p video in real time on a single GPU (20.7 fps for a 16 Hz playback rate), making it suited to interactive avatar applications, and AURA 5B streams 720p video at 9.7 fps. Our two-stage distillation pipeline first aligns the causal student with the teacher, by training it to match the teacher's trajectories with fewer diffusion steps and a chunk-wise sliding window causal attention mask, then applies Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) with backward simulation to close the remaining quality gap. On standard talking-head benchmarks, AURA 1.3B runs 23x faster than its own bidirectional teacher and reaches a quality comparable to TalkVerse at a lower resolution, and AURA 5B matches its TalkVerse teacher at 720p while running 17x faster. To support reproducibility and further research on streaming avatar generation, we release the weights of both models along with a dataset of 30K synthetic videos and their precomputed teacher trajectories.

**comment/C1：** BMVC 2026. Project page: https://kyutai.org/aura

</details>

<a id="paper-180"></a>

### 180. [The Geometry of Lies: Knowledge and Persona Directions for Detecting Deception in Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26545v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26545

**作者：** Md. Abdullah Al Zami、Suraiya Shanaz、Mahdi Hasan、Shoaib Ahmed Dipu、Farig Yousuf Sadeque、Md. Sabbir Ahmed

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26545v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26545v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 70B 规模上检验由诚实／欺骗角色扮演得到的无标注 persona 方向以及「知识」方向能否区分 LLM 的谎言，并发现这些方向往往只在措辞层面起作用，多数增益落在随机方向波动范围内。

**相关性简评：** 其「用对照刺激构造内部方向、并以随机方向带和措辞混淆作为基线」的解读流程，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态究竟编码视觉内容还是表层措辞；但研究对象是文本欺骗而非多模态推理，迁移仅为线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Geometry of Lies: Knowledge and Persona Directions for Detecting Deception in Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Linear probes are the cheapest LLM lie detectors but often fail to transfer across kinds of lie. Label-free directions from honest and deceptive role-play are untested at 70B scale and on lies about knowledge, where a lie and an honest denial can be identical. We widen a published persona bank with roles that deny knowledge they have, plus honest counterparts. At each model's middle layer the resulting persona direction ranks lies across Liars' Bench better than single-dataset probes on three of its four models; it fails on Qwen, even when Qwen stays in role far more often. On Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct it beats directions from a random persona bank, but its gain over the published direction is within the range of random widenings. On lies about knowledge, persona directions mainly track a reply's wording: where lies and honest denials share their words, our three main contrasts stay inside the random-direction band on Llama and Qwen and reach only its edge on Gemma, though on Mistral one clears it. A knowledge direction contrasts questions the model can answer with ones nobody can, each followed by the same scripted denial. It separates lies from identical honest denials on two models and narrowly on a third, but not on the fourth; its other results partly depend on the benchmark's wording. On the Gender-Secret fine-tune it clears the band by a few hundredths on Llama and fails on Mistral. The sum of the two directions ranks lies better than a supervised probe at the same depth on all four models (on one, both near chance) but is level with the persona direction on Llama's average and behind that probe on held-out lies. More varied role-play questions do not help the persona direction on Liars' Bench.

</details>

<a id="paper-181"></a>

### 181. [ProtoDiffusion: Token-per-Layer Generative Modeling of Multi-Layer Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26586v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26586

**作者：** Adrià Ciurana Lanau、Harleen Hanspal、Lusine Abrahamyan、Emilio Tylson Baixauli、Yu Cao、Danai Triantafyllidou、Stefano Zorzi、Gustavo Zanoni Felipe、Peter Griger、Stefan Sietzen、Petru-Daniel Tudosiu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26586v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26586v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ProtoDiffusion：以多层 VAE（ProtoVAE）把一个分层设计编码为一份共享空间 prototype 加每层一个候选向量，再对该紧凑表示做 latent diffusion 联合去噪，支持图像或文本生成分层图像并自行决定层数与布局。

**相关性简评：** 「共享 prototype + 每层候选槽」的紧凑 latent 表示与空槽/变槽数机制，可能为 LVR 中压缩视觉细节、按需分配多个候选槽（证据或假设）提供表示与容量分配线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ProtoDiffusion: Token-per-Layer Generative Modeling of Multi-Layer Images

**abstract/A1：** Graphic designs are ordered compositions of RGBA layers whose separation enables selection, movement, and reuse. Existing layered models represent each layer with a full-canvas or region-sized spatial latent grid, so representation size grows with the cumulative spatial extent of the layers. We present ProtoDiffusion, a latent-diffusion framework built on ProtoVAE, a multi-layer variational autoencoder that encodes a design into one shared spatial prototype and one candidate vector per layer. Each candidate conditions a shared decoder to reconstruct its corresponding RGBA layer. For $N$ layers, the representation contains $HW+N$ tokens before backbone-specific packing, compared with $NHW$ for full-grid-per-layer representations. ProtoDiffusion jointly denoises the prototype and a fixed set of candidate slots, conditioned on an image or text, with bounding boxes as optional layout guidance. The model leaves unneeded slots empty and determines the number of layers and, in absence of boxes, their layout, requiring neither as input. The same architecture supports both image-to-design and text-to-design. ProtoVAE achieves high-fidelity reconstruction from its compact shared latent representation, reaching 40.43 dB PSNR on layer crops. On the LayerD evaluation split, image-to-design achieves RGB L1 scores of 0.040 with bounding boxes and 0.057 without, with box-guided alpha soft IoU (0.850) comparable to the strongest fine-tuned baseline (0.871). On merged images, text-to-design reaches 66.41 FID versus 86.84 for FLUX.2 \[klein\], achieves a 0.860 text-accuracy score, and runs $19.1\\times$ faster than the same-backbone generation-then-decomposition cascade.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 16 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-182"></a>

### 182. [Active Feature Acquisition by Distilling an Oracle Teacher](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26598v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26598

**作者：** Yang Meng、Zixin Ding、Robert L. Grossman、Yuxin Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26598v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26598v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构造一个闭式 oracle 教师：训练时利用标签与未观测特征值，按各候选特征对下游预测误差的影响排序，再用软目标模仿把该排序蒸馏成预算约束下逐实例决策「获取哪些特征」的可部署策略，并在多模态医学诊断与 AFABench 上评测。

**相关性简评：** 「按信息价值排序并蒸馏成获取策略」的监督方式可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与 LOOK/STOP 决策提供训练线索，且其显式教师便于度量可恢复性；但对象是可数表格特征而非视觉 token，可供核查迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Active Feature Acquisition by Distilling an Oracle Teacher

**abstract/A1：** Acquiring every feature of an instance is often costly: a measurement can carry a monetary price, a time delay, or a privacy cost. Active Feature Acquisition (AFA) addresses this by learning a policy that decides, per instance, which features to acquire under a budget. Existing AFA methods train this policy by optimizing a self-constructed utility target rather than distilling an explicit teacher. We construct this teacher explicitly. At training time the label and the unobserved feature values are available, and together they determine the teacher in closed form: an oracle that ranks every candidate by its effect on the downstream predictor's error. OracleImitator distills this ranking into a deployable policy by soft-target imitation. Across multimodal medical diagnosis and the AFABench benchmark, OracleImitator matches or outperforms existing AFA methods across the budget range, recovering 90% of the attainable accuracy gain at under a quarter of the acquisition cost. Because the teacher is an explicit algorithmic object rather than an implicit optimization target, we can also measure directly how much of its ranking is recoverable by a policy restricted to what is visible at deployment.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-183"></a>

### 183. [Depth Pro 2: Sharp and Temporally Consistent Metric Monocular Depth](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26603v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26603

**作者：** Peiyun Hu、Marko Mihajlovic、Lyndon Duong、Lahav Lipson、Wei Dong、Amaël Delaunoy、Stephan R. Richter、Vladlen Koltun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26603v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26603v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Depth Pro 2：用单一可扩展网络统一处理图像与视频，在原生分辨率下输出稠密 metric depth，使深度图同时满足度量准确、边界锐利与跨帧时序一致，并发布一组在精度、锐利度、一致性与运行时间上构成新 Pareto 前沿的模型。

**相关性简评：** 原文以单网络联合训练来权衡「跨帧时序一致」与「单帧边界锐利」，可能可供核查 LVR 中复用先前 latent 状态与重新提取视觉证据之间的取舍，以及高分辨率视觉细节在内部状态中的保留程度；但该工作本身属稠密几何预测，非推理研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Depth Pro 2: Sharp and Temporally Consistent Metric Monocular Depth

**abstract/A1：** We present Depth Pro 2, an approach to estimating dense metric depth for images and video at full resolution. By analyzing prior methods for monocular depth estimation, we find that they commonly trade off temporal consistency and boundary sharpness: multi-frame methods favor consistency, single-frame methods favor sharpness. To overcome this trade-off, we train a single, scalable network that processes both images and video and predicts depth maps at native resolution. Depth Pro 2 produces depth maps that are metrically accurate, sharp, and temporally consistent all at once, outperforming prior work in all of these dimensions. We release a family of models that define a new Pareto frontier in terms of metric accuracy, sharpness, temporal consistency, and runtime. We release code and weights at https://apple-aiml-research.github.io/ml-depth-pro-2/

**comment/C1：** Code and weights available at https://apple-aiml-research.github.io/ml-depth-pro-2/

</details>

<a id="paper-184"></a>

### 184. [Bio-PM-Guided Synthetic IMU Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26607v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26607

**作者：** Deepali Singh、Deepesh Parpani、Aviral Gupta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26607v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26607v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在预训练生物信号编码器 Bio-PM 的冻结表示空间中合成 IMU 数据：分别训练自回归 transformer 解码器与扩散解码器逐段生成 token 序列，用分布指标和嵌入可视化评估真实性，并以留一被试 HAR 分类、TSTR 及 PatchDecoder 波形重建检验下游可用性。

**相关性简评：** 「冻结编码器潜空间 + 自回归/扩散两种生成器」的对照及其对生成 token 真实性与可解码性的评价方式，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 推理状态如何生成、如何判定保真度，但任务域为传感器信号，迁移线索需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bio-PM-Guided Synthetic IMU Generation

**abstract/A1：** Collecting and labeling inertial measurement unit (IMU) data for human activity recognition (HAR) is expensive, and models trained on one group of subjects often fail to generalize to unseen individuals. Even with balanced data, classifiers struggle to distinguish dynamically similar activity classes and benefit from targeted augmentation. We explore whether synthetic IMU data generated in the representation space of a pretrained biosignal encoder, Bio-PM \\cite{b1}, can serve as a practical augmentation source. Rather than synthesizing raw signals, we leverage Bio-PM's frozen encoder to convert 10-second IMU windows into token embedding sequences and train class-conditional decoders in this space: (1) an autoregressive transformer decoder that predicts tokens sequentially, and (2) a diffusion decoder that learns the token distribution through a denoising objective. We analyze generation realism through distributional metrics and embedding-space visualizations, evaluate downstream utility through leave-one-subject-out HAR classification, and present a PatchDecoder for approximate raw waveform reconstruction. Our diffusion decoder achieves a Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) macro-F1 of 0.68, demonstrating that generated tokens are realistic enough to train a classifier that transfers to real data. Combining synthetic-token augmentation with a 320-dimensional gravity-aware feature representation raises LOSO macro-F1 from 0.729 to 0.809 over a real-only baseline across 51 subjects on the WISDM benchmark, with statistically significant gains concentrated in the most challenging activity classes. These results demonstrate that high-fidelity generation in a pretrained encoder's representation space is achievable and practically useful for cross-subject HAR augmentation.

</details>

<a id="paper-185"></a>

### 185. [One Prior, Two Dynamics: Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Self-Supervised Latent Action Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26617v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26617

**作者：** Yuxin Jiang、Wenqiang Sun、Jiaqi Li、Yuchao Gu、Lvmin Zhang、Ivor Tsang、Mike Zheng Shou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26617v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26617v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 O²LaF：用同一个预训练视频 DiT 分别初始化逆动态编码器（可学习 action query 的前馈转移编码器）与因果、动作条件的前向视频生成器，经紧凑瓶颈端到端联合优化，从无标注视频学到可迁移的 latent action，并验证可换用 V-JEPA2 等预测表示先验。

**相关性简评：** 其「控制信息访问的紧凑瓶颈＋前向/逆动态联合训练＋预测表示先验」可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态如何避免信息坍缩、如何分配训练贡献；但研究对象是视频动作学习与具身迁移，与 VLM 推理无直接关联，线索需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** One Prior, Two Dynamics: Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Self-Supervised Latent Action Learning

**abstract/A1：** Videos reveal how the world changes, but rarely the actions that cause those changes. Latent action learning recovers such supervision from visual transitions, yet often learns the underlying dynamics largely from scratch. We ask whether a pretrained video generator can instead provide the prior for both inverse and forward dynamics. We introduce O$^{2}$LaF, built on a simple principle: one prior, two dynamics. We initialize both models from the same pretrained video DiT and adapt them to their distinct roles: a feed-forward transition encoder with learnable action queries for inverse dynamics, and a causal, action-conditioned video generator for forward dynamics. By controlling information access and optimizing both dynamics end-to-end through a compact bottleneck, O$^{2}$LaF learns transferable actions from unlabeled videos. Across navigation and robotic manipulation, O$^{2}$LaF sets a new state of the art in few-shot action probing and enables stronger zero-shot action transfer across visual contexts and embodiments. Beyond generative video priors, we further extend our framework to predictive representation priors such as V-JEPA2, achieving competitive performance. Our findings suggest a broader paradigm for latent-action learning: repurposing pretrained video models to discover transferable actions and predict their consequences.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://showlab.github.io/o2laf Code: https://github.com/showlab/O2LaF

</details>

<a id="paper-186"></a>

### 186. [Posterior Matching Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26629v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26629

**作者：** Hidir Yesiltepe、Julius Berner、Arash Vahdat、Pinar Yanardag

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26629v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26629v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Posterior Matching Distillation（PMD），以指数重加权的后验均值约束在多个噪声层级上匹配教师完整后验，使学生可任意步数采样；在 SDXL 与 FLUX.2 上取得 4 步方法中最好的 FID。

**相关性简评：** 其用一族均值约束监督整条条件分布、避免 mode-seeking 塌缩，并让训练后可支持任意步数预算，可能为 LVR 中多假设 latent 状态的分布级训练与动态计算预算提供借鉴线索，需核查能否迁至序列式推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Posterior Matching Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion distillation accelerates image and video generation by compressing a pretrained teacher's iterative sampling process into a few network evaluations. Distribution-based methods achieve this by matching the student's output distribution to the teacher's. This matching is often driven by reverse-KL objectives, whose mode-seeking bias favors dominant teacher modes at the expense of diversity across samples. We introduce Posterior Matching Distillation (PMD), a new member of the distribution-based distillation family that combines mode-covering posterior sampling with any-step generation. At zero population loss, PMD recovers the teacher's full posterior of clean images at each trained noise level. Its stochastic student supports any-step sampling budgets within the trained timestep grid without further training. To learn these posteriors, PMD matches exponentially reweighted averages of student samples conditioned on a fixed noisy state to corresponding teacher posterior means. Under Gaussian corruption, exponentially tilted posterior mean is a single teacher evaluation at a shifted input. We prove that, matching tilted means over an open neighborhood uniquely identifies the full posterior. PMD thus supervises the entire conditional distribution through a family of mean constraints, without any auxiliary student-score network. Experiments on SDXL and FLUX.2 \[klein\] 4B demonstrate that PMD achieves the best FID among 4-step methods on both models, while maintaining competitive or leading performance across prompt-alignment and perceptual-quality metrics.

**comment/C1：** Project Page: https://pmd-distillation.github.io/

</details>

<a id="paper-187"></a>

### 187. [Density Feedback Control for Transferability of Generative Sparse Adversarial Attacks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25001v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25001

**作者：** Dongsu Song、Jay Hoon Jung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25001v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25001v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Density Feedback Control：每轮结束时按观测到的硬掩码密度与目标密度之差收紧或放松稀疏系数，使生成器式稀疏对抗攻击在单次训练内逼近指定密度并改善迁移攻击成功率，并分析了在特征重要性区域限制下该方法失效的原因。

**相关性简评：** 其「观测实际密度—回调软系数」的闭环反馈机制，可能为 LVR 中按证据量或不确定性动态分配计算与停止预算提供类比线索，显著性区域掩码与视觉证据筛选的关系也值得核查，但任务目标与视觉推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Density Feedback Control for Transferability of Generative Sparse Adversarial Attacks

**abstract/A1：** Generator-based sparse adversarial attacks train encoder--decoder networks to produce transferable sparse perturbations for black-box robustness evaluation. Their perturbation density, however, is set only indirectly by a fixed sparsity coefficient whose mapping to density is unknown and shifts during training, so reaching a target density requires re-tuning; this fixed pressure also yields little transferability gain once density reduction begins. We propose Density Feedback Control (DFC), which tightens or relaxes this coefficient at the end of each epoch according to the gap between the observed hard-mask density and the target, without changing the generator architecture or loss. At a 10\\% target density, DFC brings the mean density of three base attacks within one percentage point of the target in a single run and improves the transfer attack success rate (ASR) of two of them on CNN and Transformer targets, including under per-image $\\ell\_0$ budgets, while perturbing fewer pixels. Tracing GPG's training, we observe that as DFC relaxes the coefficient, the transfer ASR recovers with the density and overtakes the fixed-coefficient baseline with fewer pixels. For EGS-TSSA, which restricts perturbations to source-model feature-importance regions, DFC yields no consistent gain; we analyze how this restriction interacts with density feedback.

**comment/C1：** preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-188"></a>

### 188. [A Model of Diverse Sampling from Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25003v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25003

**作者：** Manuel Prada-Corral、Yahya Emara、Timothy J. O'Donnell、Ryan Cotterell、Tim Vieira

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.FL、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25003v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25003v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PoolDPP：用行列式点过程刻画语言模型输出的质量与多样性，并通过从可处理提议分布抽取候选池、重要性加权及贪心 MAP 变体近似实现 K-DPP 采样，给出池大小 O(1/N) 的 KL 收敛界，并在开放生成、歧义 text-to-SQL 和 GSM8K 上验证多样性与质量权衡。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但其基于嵌入几何刻画差异、并用候选集筛选近似难解目标分布的思路，可能为 latent 假设的多样化采样与视觉证据候选筛选提供参考，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Model of Diverse Sampling from Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Language models often produce high-quality individual samples but poor sets, with repeated samples clustering around the same semantic modes. We formalize diverse generation as sampling a size-$K$ subset of complete strings from a determinantal point process (DPP), where the likelihood encodes item quality, and the embedding geometry encodes repulsion between similar outputs. Exact inference over all strings is intractable, so we propose PoolDPP: draw a finite candidate pool from a tractable proposal, importance-weight the candidates toward the target quality, and run a $K$-DPP sampler on the induced pool kernel; a greedy MAP variant provides deterministic selection. We prove a finite-sample bound with an explicit constant: the KL divergence between the sampler's output and the target $K$-DPP decays as O(1/N) in the pool size. Empirically, PoolDPP extends the quality--diversity frontier on open-ended generation and the precision--recall frontier on ambiguous text-to-SQL (Ambrosia), and on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), it raises diversity at about the same pass@$K$.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 paper

</details>

<a id="paper-189"></a>

### 189. [Closing the Analyze-to-Design Gap: An Authorization-Bounded Agent for Self-Driving Laboratories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25043v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25043

**作者：** Yimeng Liu、Eric Isbrandt、Felix Katzenburg、Kevin P. Greenman、Han Hao、Yang Cao、Varinia Bernales、Alán Aspuru-Guzik

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25043v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25043v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个受授权约束、可审计的 agent 架构，接入自驱动实验室的「分析—设计」环节，在 LC-MS 染料筛选实验中自主提交测量任务、解读质谱与 DAD 数据并排序氧化剂条件。

**相关性简评：** 其在有限注入次数下选择测哪些样品、以及何时交还人类，可能与 LVR 的证据筛选与停止决策（LOOK/STOP）问题形成类比；但工作侧重化学流程授权与谱图解读，不含视觉或 latent 状态，可供核查的线索有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Closing the Analyze-to-Design Gap: An Authorization-Bounded Agent for Self-Driving Laboratories

**abstract/A1：** Artificial intelligence (AI) agents are increasingly integrated into self-driving laboratories (SDLs) to plan experiments and interpret results. However, analytical reasoning alone does not make a laboratory autonomous: autonomy in an SDL is achievable only when the agent can be trusted to act on physical hardware unsupervised and knows when to defer to a human. Here, we present an architecture that integrates an agent at the Analyze-Design interface of the Design-Make-Test-Analyze (DMTA) cycle, enabling agent-driven measurement selection and data interpretation while ensuring that state-changing operations are authorized, validated, and auditable. We demonstrate this architecture on a liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry (LC-MS) instrument in an oxidant-screening campaign for synthesizing an organic dye. Within human-defined campaign limits, the agent submitted measurement jobs, interpreted mass spectrometry (MS) and diode-array detection (DAD) data, flagged failed experiments, and produced an oxidant ranking. This ranking matched that obtained from scripted peak integration, and organic chemists confirmed the agent's spectral interpretations and failure assignments. In a simulated environment that replays the archived LC-MS data, where the agent chose which wells to inject under varying injection limits, it measured the highest-yielding condition at about its seventh injection, against 14.5 on average for a random order, and identified it in every campaign with a limit of eight or more injections. An agent can therefore close the analysis-to-design gap while its actions stay bounded and verifiable, which is a step toward greater autonomy for SDLs.

</details>

<a id="paper-190"></a>

### 190. [BuggyMLE: Scaling Machine Learning Engineering Difficulty with Bug Injection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25088v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25088

**作者：** John Wu、Rushi Qiang、Yuchen Zhuang、Guowang Li、Girish Narayanswamy、Max Xu、Sam Schmidgall、Mark Malhotra、Shwetak Patel、Jimeng Sun、Daniel McDuff、Bo Dai、Xin Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.MA、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25088v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25088v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出基于十项结构特征的代码复杂度指标 CCI，用以预测基准解题率，并借助受控 bug 注入构建仓库级、难度分级的 MLE 评测集与三级难度训练课程。

**相关性简评：** 用结构特征提前预测解题率、按难度分层评测并据此设计训练课程的做法，可能为 LVR 中构造难度梯度、预测推理失效区间提供可核查的评测与课程设计线索；但论文本身不涉及视觉证据或 latent 推理状态，与主线内容联系薄弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BuggyMLE: Scaling Machine Learning Engineering Difficulty with Bug Injection

**abstract/A1：** Enabling AI systems to autonomously improve AI models requires agents capable of performing machine learning engineering (MLE). In practice, designing, training, and evaluating complex models demands modifying multi-file repositories to reproduce results, debug training runs, integrate datasets, and port frameworks. However, existing MLE benchmarks limit agents to single-file edits without full repository or shell access, and common repository-level software engineering benchmarks have rapidly saturated. To address this, we introduce the Code Complexity Index (CCI), a difficulty metric based on ten structural features spanning repository scale, dependency depth, and patch size. CCI strongly predicts average solve rates across eight public coding benchmarks ($R^2 = 0.951$), identifying patch size and complexity as top predictors. Guided by CCI, we develop BuggyMLE, a scalable framework where agents inject controlled mutations into working ML repositories, synthesizing over 6,600 bug-injected MLE repository variants and nearly 260K lines of injected code based on 222 source repositories. Using BuggyMLE, we make two contributions. First, we construct BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite, the first difficulty-graded, repository-level variant of MLE-Bench Lite; across 14 evaluated models, the top solve rate drops from 95% to 9% as difficulty is scaled from the easiest to the hardest tier. Second, we synthesize a training curriculum across three difficulty levels over 200 additional ML repositories, fine-tuning a Qwen3.5-9B model to rival frontier models on BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite. Our trained model transfers beyond ML, raising its SWE-bench Lite resolve rate from 24% to 33% and its LiveCodeBench v6 pass@1 from 69% to 71%.

</details>

<a id="paper-191"></a>

### 191. [Auditing Learned Dynamics in Weather Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25119v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25119

**作者：** Skylar Gu、Christina Last

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25119v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25119v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 利用 GraphCast 的稀疏自编码器特征，检验因果相关性是否必须有物理语义，并在自回归 rollout 中干预内部激活以考察极端天气预测能否改善。

**相关性简评：** 「可解释的物理关联 ≠ 因果效用」以及按特征缩放定向干预 latent 方向的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 内容的因果作用判定、贡献分配与证据筛选提供可借鉴的概念与评价线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Auditing Learned Dynamics in Weather Models

**abstract/A1：** Learned weather models are experimentally accessible computational systems whose internal representations can be inspected and manipulated. Using sparse autoencoder features from GraphCast, DeepMind's autoregressive weather forecasting model, we test whether causal relevance requires physical semantics, a question that matters for auditing whether AI forecasters used in high-assurance settings rely on reliable mechanisms. We also look at whether physically associated latent directions can be steered to improve extreme-event forecasts, where interventions are applied to the model's internal activations during the rollout. We find that ablating a feature locked to GraphCast's computational mesh increases global geopotential height error despite lacking conventional physical semantics. Second, suppressing convection- and low-level-vorticity-associated features weakens predicted tropical-cyclone deepening (the fall in central pressure during intensification), while amplifying them reduces intensity error, with amplification of convection features through feature scaling achieving a median of 51\\% error reduction over seven storms. Together, these results separate interpretable, physical association from causal utility and establish sparse features as experimental coordinates for auditing and intervening on learned weather dynamics.

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [EverydayBench: Evaluating Satisfactory Completion of Live User-Facing Digital Work](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25146v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25146

**作者：** Fangrui Huang、Bingyang Wang、Chuhan Li、Yijiang Li、Arpandeep Khatua、Austin Shen、Riki R Z Choi、Cuiyuanxiu Chen、Jin Huang、Zachary Robertson、Kate Lin、Akshay Paruchuri、Sijun Tan、Xin Liu、Zhengmin Ni、Sanmi Koyejo、Ehsan Adeli

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25146v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25146v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 EverydayBench：涵盖八个日常生活与工作领域的 77 个人工撰写任务，预先设定成功标准但不规定答案或流程，并用约束筛查、对 agent 引用网页的实时证据核验以及评分量表三级协议评估交付物质量，报告了 23 个 agent-模型配置的表现。

**相关性简评：** 其「引用证据实时核验＋受控 grounding 失败注入」的评测协议，可能可供核查 latent 推理是否真正使用任务相关的视觉或检索证据，但该工作面向网页文本交付任务，无多模态与内部状态内容，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EverydayBench: Evaluating Satisfactory Completion of Live User-Facing Digital Work

**abstract/A1：** Everyday digital tasks, from selecting products and planning trips to conducting research and creating artifacts, require AI agents to deliver useful outcomes rather than merely retrieve web information. Yet evaluating these outcomes on the live web is challenging, as a single request may admit multiple valid results, and changing web information can alter which results remain valid over time. Although existing benchmarks address aspects of this setting, they do not fully integrate predefined success criteria, current evidence verification, and deliverable-quality assessment for agent-selected outcomes. To address this gap, we introduce EverydayBench, a benchmark of 77 human-authored tasks spanning eight domains of everyday life and work. Each task specifies success criteria before execution without prescribing an answer or workflow, allowing agents to select their own outcomes. To evaluate submissions under fixed success criteria, EverydayBench combines (1) deterministic checks of explicit constraints, (2) verification of claims against agent-cited webpages retrieved at evaluation time, and (3) rubric-based assessment of final deliverable quality. We further validate the protocol through human judgments, repeated scoring under fixed evidence, and controlled grounding failures. Across 23 reported agent-model configurations, Claude Opus 5.5 and Claude Fable 5.1 tie for the highest strict pass rate at 54.55%; a controlled GPT-6 Sol effort sweep ranges from 29.87% at low effort to 45.45% at max effort. Reliability varies substantially by task type: GPT-5.6 Sol leads lookup, Fable 5.1 leads planning, Opus 5.5 and Fable 5.1 tie on comparison, and GPT-6 Astra and Sonnet 5.5 tie on customized deliverables. Together, these findings highlight the difficulty of delivering complete, well-supported, and usable outcomes for everyday user requests.

</details>

<a id="paper-193"></a>

### 193. [Rate-distortion Assessment for Hierarchical Content Summarization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25151v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25151

**作者：** Yunye Gong、Indranil Sur、Jared Claypoole、Pritish Sahu、Ajay Divakaran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25151v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25151v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将分层内容摘要形式化为信息编码问题，基于率失真理论构建语义层级框架，量化压缩效率与信息损失的权衡，并在长上下文问答与检索增强生成中评估大语言模型。

**相关性简评：** 其率失真与语义层级的评估视角可能启发 LVR 对 latent 状态保留任务必要视觉信息、压缩成本与证据筛选的度量，但研究对象是文本摘要，需核查能否迁移到多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rate-distortion Assessment for Hierarchical Content Summarization

**abstract/A1：** Powerful generative models including large language and vision models have demonstrated success across a wide range of content generation, editing and manipulation tasks via end-to-end prompt-based generation. However, current assessment of the quality of generative content manipulation remains subjective, limiting scientific understanding of model capabilities. Inspired by rate-distortion theory from Shannon information compression theory, in this work we formalize hierarchical content summarization as an information encoding problem. We design a principled framework to extract the semantic hierarchy of the source content to support quantitative characterization of the rate-distortion trade-off between compression efficiency (rate) against information loss (distortion) during generative content manipulation, which allows us to benchmark various models with respect to performance conditional on rate budget of interest. We demonstrate the use of the framework with empirical experiments on content summarization for long-context question-answering with state-of-the-art large language models, offering new insights into their efficiency and comprehension capabilities. We further demonstrate the use of the semantic hierarchy as guidance for performing effective retrieval augmented generation, showing improved rate-distortion characteristics against current baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-194"></a>

### 194. [Bayesian $κ$-Explorer for Minimax Model Estimation in MDPs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25160v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25160

**作者：** Xihe Gu、Urbashi Mitra、Tara Javidi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25160v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25160v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Bayesian κ-Explorer：在 MDP 转移分布上放置 Dirichlet 先验，以事后均值为估计器，使后验期望平方误差等于总后验方差，从而得到闭式探索信号，规划时无需置信集联合优化；在给定假设下保留原方法的收敛率与后验方差界，并在 Pendulum、Mountain Car、Taxi 上降低最坏情形转移估计误差。

**相关性简评：** 其用后验方差构成闭式信息获取目标，可能为 LVR 中「何时继续 LOOK、如何筛选视觉证据」的信息价值度量与主动取证策略提供可核查的类比线索，但属纯 MDP 探索理论，向多模态推理的迁移尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bayesian $κ$-Explorer for Minimax Model Estimation in MDPs

**abstract/A1：** We study active exploration for minimax transition estimation in unknown Markov decision processes (MDPs). Frequentist $κ$-Explorer~\\citep{gu2026kappa} addresses this problem through a parameterized family of exploration objectives, but requires confidence-bound constructions and joint optimization over plausible transition kernels and occupancy measures, introducing a computational burden that limits its practical applicability. We develop Bayesian $κ$-Explorer, which simplifies this procedure by placing Dirichlet priors on the transition distributions. Under the posterior mean estimator, the posterior expected squared $\\ell\_2$ error equals the total posterior variance, providing a closed-form exploration signal. The algorithm plans under the posterior mean model, eliminating confidence-set optimization. Under the stated assumptions, this simpler planning procedure retains the $\\widetilde{O}(T^{-1/3})$ surrogate convergence rate of frequentist $κ$-Explorer. We further bound the maximum posterior variance relative to the optimal asymptotic minimax allocation benchmark. Experiments on Pendulum, Mountain Car, and Taxi demonstrate lower worst-case transition-estimation errors than the tested baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-195"></a>

### 195. [MV-JEPA: Post-Training Video Encoders for Cross-View Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25172v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25172

**作者：** Yangchen Zhao、Ali Payani、Caiwen Ding

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25172v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25172v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MV-JEPA，对 JEPA 视频编码器做多视角预测式后训练：共享编码器独立处理遮挡的上下文视角，训练时的预测器联合预测被遮挡区域的 latent token 与完全留出的视角，并与单视角掩码预测交替，以在保留单视角推理接口的前提下增强跨视角实例与粗粒度时刻对应。

**相关性简评：** 「在 latent 空间预测另一视角」可作为构建视角一致内部状态、筛选任务必要视觉证据的训练信号线索，可供核查其与 LVR 视觉信息保留问题的联系；但该工作面向表征与检索，不涉及 VLM 推理或推理期状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MV-JEPA: Post-Training Video Encoders for Cross-View Understanding

**abstract/A1：** JEPA-based video encoders learn strong representations for video and image understanding through prediction in latent space. However, their standard pretraining predicts features within individual images or video clips, without explicitly exploiting paired camera views of the same object or moment. Consequently, the learned representations may not reliably support matching across different camera viewpoints. To solve this problem, we introduce multi-view predictive post-training to strengthen cross-view instance and temporal correspondence while retaining independent single-view inference. A shared encoder processes masked context views independently, and a training-time predictor jointly predicts latent tokens for masked context regions and a fully held-out view. We alternate cross-view prediction with single-view masked prediction on general image and video data to help retain the encoder's pretrained capabilities. After only 9,000 post-training updates on a single GPU, MV-JEPA improves cross-view instance retrieval and coarse cross-camera moment retrieval on various datasets, with further gains in zero-shot action-phase retrieval and frozen-feature classification on AE2. Frozen-probe evaluations further indicate that post-training largely preserves motion recognition, image recognition, and dense semantic segmentation performance on the evaluated benchmarks. Together, these results demonstrate that multi-view predictive post-training can strengthen instance and coarse temporal correspondence in JEPA video encoders without modifying the encoder architecture or single-view inference interface.

</details>

<a id="paper-196"></a>

### 196. [Phantom Success, Silent Failure: Auditing the Success Checkers of Simulated Manipulation Benchmarks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25179v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25179

**作者：** Zirui Song、Jiadong Zhang、Zheng Lu、Xiaosong Ma、Xiaojun Chang、Xiuying Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25179v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25179v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SimAudit，用 LLM 分析任务指令与 success checker 代码，找出二者可能判断不一致的情形，在仿真中复现并用多视角渲染核验行为是否真正满足指令，审计 RoboTwin 2.0 等三个平台的判定器，共报告 61 处缺陷，区分出 phantom success 与 silent failure 两类错误。

**相关性简评：** 「判定标准与实际行为不符」的诊断形式及多视角视觉核验，可能可供核查 LVR 中成功判定是否真正依据视觉证据、以及如何构造模糊情形来检验验证协议；但该文不涉及 latent 推理状态，联系较为间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Phantom Success, Silent Failure: Auditing the Success Checkers of Simulated Manipulation Benchmarks

**abstract/A1：** Simulated manipulation benchmarks measure policy performance using hand-written success checkers that label each episode as a success or failure. Passing an expert demonstration shows that a checker accepts one way of completing the task. Learned policies, however, may stop after partial progress or complete the task in a different way. A checker may then accept an incomplete execution (phantom success) or reject an execution that satisfies the instruction (silent failure). In this paper, we introduce SimAudit, an LLM-based pipeline that audits success checkers against task instructions. SimAudit analyzes the instruction and checker code to identify scenarios where the two may disagree, recreates those scenarios in simulation, and runs the original checker. Multi-view renderings are then used to verify whether the observed behavior satisfies the instruction. Across RoboTwin 2.0, RoboCasa365, and RoboDojo, 183 simulated test cases produced the predicted checker verdict. Visual verification and deduplication yielded 61 distinct defects across 49 tasks, including 40 phantom successes and 21 silent failures. Most defects in RoboCasa365 accepted incomplete executions, most in RoboDojo rejected valid executions, and both error types were common in RoboTwin 2.0. These findings suggest that validating success checkers requires testing both incomplete executions and valid alternatives to expert demonstrations, and motivate more rigorous evaluation protocols for future benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-197"></a>

### 197. [φTD: Distributional Reinforcement Learning using Characteristic Functions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25208v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25208

**作者：** Thomas Mousseau、Tyler Kastner、Davide Baldelli、Amir-massoud Farahmand

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25208v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25208v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 φTD，用特征函数在频域计算损失做分布式强化学习，统一了 quantile 与 categorical 方法，并能表示混合连续分布等以往难以刻画的回报分布。

**相关性简评：** 用特征函数承载整条回报分布的做法，可能可供核查为 LVR 中的信念状态或不确定性表示提供线索（如为 THINK/STOP 决策估计收益分布），但与视觉或多模态推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** φTD: Distributional Reinforcement Learning using Characteristic Functions

**abstract/A1：** Distributional Reinforcement Learning methods aim to learn the entire distribution of returns, yet current algorithmic approaches are limited to a narrow class of distributions. We propose $\\varphi$TD, a method to overcome this limitation by computing the loss in the frequency domain. We demonstrate that this further unifies quantile and categorical approaches to distributional RL, and enables the learning of distributions that were previously impossible to capture, such as mixtures of continuous distributions. We empirically study $\\varphi$TD, across synthetic benchmarks and large-scale deep RL environments and demonstrate that the flexibility obtained from wider parametric families can lead to improved performance.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. First two authors contributed equally

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [Steering Automated Scientific Discovery via Audited Epistemic Exploration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25214v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25214

**作者：** Fangxu Yu、Tao Feng、Xuying Ning、Haolin Yang、Jiaxuan You、Tianyi Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25214v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25214v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EpiCompass 即插即用框架，用链接假设、证据、信念与修订的「认知树」显式维护 epistemic state，按预期认知增益选择候选假设并对照累积证据核验最终结论，在三个科学发现基准与三个 LLM 骨干上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其显式信念状态、冲突驱动的假设重访与「预期认知增益」候选选择，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 决策、视觉证据筛选与状态更新提供概念参照，需核查这些机制能否对应到 latent 推理状态的维护与读取。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Steering Automated Scientific Discovery via Audited Epistemic Exploration

**abstract/A1：** Scientific discovery requires revisiting the hypotheses in light of new evidence and identifying promising directions beyond those already explored. However, research agents often make claims that are not supported by their observations, explore a narrow hypothesis space, and retain beliefs despite recognizing conflicting evidence. These failures motivate the search for an explicit epistemic state that captures both beliefs about existing hypotheses and directions for further exploration. To this end, we propose Epistemic Compass (EpiCompass), a plug-and-play scaffold that maintains this state through an epistemic tree linking hypotheses, evidence, beliefs, and revisions. As an investigation progresses, EpiCompass reassesses the hypotheses explored against accumulated evidence and uses unresolved conflicts and unexplored directions to guide hypothesis generation. It selects candidates by anticipated epistemic gain, accounting for both refinement of existing hypotheses and expansion of the hypothesis space, and verifies the final conclusion against the accumulated evidence. Experimental results across three scientific discovery benchmarks and three LLM backbones demonstrate that EpiCompass consistently outperforms baselines, with further analyses showing reductions in errors of unsupported claims and failures to revise beliefs.

**comment/C1：** Technical Report

</details>

<a id="paper-199"></a>

### 199. [Moving Generality from Model to Modeler: LLM Agents for PHM on a Deployment-Style Benchmark](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25252v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25252

**作者：** Zhenling Chen、Zhiguo Zeng、Anne Barros

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25252v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25252v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出部署式基准 NextUnit-Bench（24 个公开数据集、58 个折，留出设备或工况），让 LLM 编码 agent 在限时内为未见设备交付可执行预测器，并据此论证「泛化性正从模型转移到建模者」。

**相关性简评：** 其中「几百字纯文本计划即可承载两个前沿 agent 的大部分差距」「必须先接触数据才形成有效设计」可供核查 latent 工作区中表征压缩度与任务证据获取的关系；但论文属时序 PHM 基准，与视觉细节保留无直接联系，仅具概念启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Moving Generality from Model to Modeler: LLM Agents for PHM on a Deployment-Style Benchmark

**abstract/A1：** Coding agents built on large language models (LLMs) now help develop many models published as general solutions. Should generality instead reside in the agent that builds a model for each new task? We examine this question in prognostics and health management (PHM), where every model must work on unseen equipment. Our deployment-style benchmark, NextUnit-Bench, covers 24 public datasets split into 58 folds that hold out units or operating conditions; each agent has two hours to deliver an executable predictor, then run on the hidden units. First, frontier agents outperform AutoML, which reaches less than half the best agent's score, and faithful implementations of published methods on four datasets, of which only 6 of 30 beat the strongest agent's own design; published accuracies, where reproducible, rest on the papers' own conventions, and a single convention, capping the remaining life of test engines, can halve the reported error. Second, the agents' advantage is a design made on each machine's data; once made, a plain-text plan of a few hundred words carries most of the gap between two frontier agents, and given a weaker agent's plan, the strongest agent falls to that agent's level, often to identical predictions. Before touching the data, however, it matters little whose plan an agent carries out, and domain toolkits appear to help neither: what sets the strongest apart is how much it learns from the data. Third, such designs reach only implementers that can execute them: given the strongest agent's designs, a locally deployable open-weight model delivers half as often, even when they are lightened, and scores no higher than without them. Generality, we argue, is not disappearing but moving, from the model to the modeler.

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [Scone: Symmetry-aware Neural Combinatorial Optimization for Graph Coloring](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25273v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25273

**作者：** Paolo Pellizzoni、Melanie Weber

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25273v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25273v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对图着色这一组合优化问题，提出 Scone 模型：利用颜色置换对称性压缩策略需要学习的有效状态空间，并用颜色等变的图神经网络逐步构建着色，使先前赋值的信息得以传播并打破节点对称，在多个着色任务上取得高质量解与较低推理时间。

**相关性简评：** 其「利用对称性压缩有效状态空间」与等变表示思路，可能为 latent 推理状态的对称性归一化与状态抽象提供概念参照，可供核查；但任务本身与视觉多模态推理相距较远，迁移性未经验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scone: Symmetry-aware Neural Combinatorial Optimization for Graph Coloring

**abstract/A1：** Graph coloring, which entails assigning colors to nodes such that no adjacent nodes have the same color, is a fundamental combinatorial optimization problem. In general, however, finding a node coloring with no monochromatic edges given a fixed palette is NP-hard. This motivates learning-based methods that can learn to exploit recurring structure across instances to efficiently produce high-quality colorings. Crucially, the validity of a coloring is invariant to permutations of the colors, and we show that exploiting this symmetry reduces the size of the effective state space over which the model has to learn its policy. At the same time, the coloring algorithm must be able to break some symmetries, for example to assign different colors to two adjacent nodes belonging to the same automorphism orbit. In this paper, we introduce Scone, a model that uses a color-equivariant graph neural network to construct colorings sequentially, allowing information from previous assignments to propagate and break node symmetries. Scone achieves perfect solution quality with low inference times across several graph coloring tasks, including on sudoku\_extreme and register allocations, and showcases generalization to graph families not encountered during training.

</details>

<a id="paper-201"></a>

### 201. [Accord: Universal Online Metric Localization and Dense Mapping from one Camera and an IMU](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25279v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25279

**作者：** Jaafar Mahmoud、Arthur Movsesyan、Cuong Huynh、Mikhail Iumanov、Sergey Zagoruyko、Sergey Kolyubin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25279v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25279v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Accord 面向单目相机加 IMU 的在线稠密 metric 建图：用 VIO 位姿锚定关键帧窗口并三角化 metric 三维点，借助共视图像间的双向稠密深度一致性估计和校正每图尺度误差，再以因子图融合子图。

**相关性简评：** 其「多视图一致性用于校正单视图尺度误差」可能为 LVR 中跨视角视觉证据筛选、latent 状态的几何自一致性校正提供类比线索，但任务目标与表示形式差异较大，需核查其一致性信号是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Accord: Universal Online Metric Localization and Dense Mapping from one Camera and an IMU

**abstract/A1：** Dense metric mapping from a single camera and IMU remains challenging: visual-inertial odometry (VIO) provides metric motion but sparse geometry, whereas feed-forward depth models predict dense structure without ensuring metric scale. We present Accord, which constructs an accurate online dense metric map from any camera with an IMU. A VIO filter publishes metric poses, a depth foundation model conditioned on them predicts depth for short keyframe windows, and a factor graph fuses the resulting submaps. Recent work has explored several approaches to this problem. What is essential is the multi-view agreement (Accord), which is considered in SLAM as a source of geometric self-consistency. Accord anchors each keyframe window to metric 3D points triangulated from the filter poses, then exploits bidirectional dense depth agreement between co-visible images to estimate and correct per-image scale errors while preserving the metric scale established by the anchors. Context keyframes further improve depth prediction, and the resulting submaps are fused in a factor graph. We evaluate a single mapping configuration across five public camera-IMU setups and a new Phone-rig/GoPro dataset. With nothing tuned per dataset/setup and every map aligned only by Accord's own trajectory, Accord achieves consistently best/second-best mean F-score at 5 cm on every platform. Accord is open-source along with the phone-rig dataset for the benefit of the research community

</details>

<a id="paper-202"></a>

### 202. [CalibCache: Cache-to-Full Feature Dynamics Calibration for Diffusion Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25296v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25296

**作者：** Mingyu Liang、Dingkun Xu、Jingwei Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25296v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25296v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CalibCache：通过离线中心最小二乘拟合标量加速度校正图，修正缓存特征与完整计算特征在采样步间的二阶差分偏差，在 20 步采样下提升 FLUX.1-dev、Qwen-Image 与 DiT-XL/2 的生成质量并保持约 2.86 倍去噪加速。

**相关性简评：** 其「缓存近似与完整计算之间的系统性偏差可离线标定」这一思路，可能供核查 LVR 中多步或循环 latent 状态更新时何时复用近似状态、如何补偿累积偏差；但论文本身针对图像生成加速，与视觉推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CalibCache: Cache-to-Full Feature Dynamics Calibration for Diffusion Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion Transformers generate high-quality images, but iterative sampling remains computationally expensive. Feature caching reduces the number of full network evaluations, yet its approximation error can become more severe under short sampling schedules, where fewer full-compute steps and a coarser sampling grid can make history-based extrapolation less reliable. In this paper, we propose CalibCache, a lightweight offline calibration method that corrects systematic discrepancies between cached and full-compute feature dynamics. Specifically, CalibCache calibrates feature acceleration, defined as the second-order finite difference across sampling steps. Using only a single pair of full-compute and naive-cache trajectories at each setting, CalibCache fits scalar acceleration maps offline by centered least squares. These maps are then applied during sampling without additional full-network evaluations or online optimization. Across FLUX.1-dev and Qwen-Image on DrawBench and DiT-XL/2 on ImageNet-256, CalibCache consistently improves fidelity or generation quality over TaylorSeer under 20-step sampling at cache intervals $N=3,4,5$. At $N=5$, it reduces FLUX.1-dev LPIPS from $0.563$ to $0.429$ while achieving a $2.86\\times$ denoising speedup over full-compute sampling, and lowers DiT-XL/2 FID from $16.39$ to $10.37$ relative to TaylorSeer.

</details>

<a id="paper-203"></a>

### 203. [Prescribed Bayesian Efficiency under Lifetime Adjustment Costs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25311v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25311

**作者：** Viet Bac Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25311v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25311v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在贝叶斯学习框架下研究学习者在持续更新后验的同时保留已发出动作、并在调整成本约束下的效率问题：刻画累积超额损失 O(G(J)) 与有限期望终身成本能否同时达到，给出与时间尺度无关的 checkpoint 策略，并证明超额为 O((log T)^r) 时所需修订次数的下界。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留旧动作还是付出成本更新」的权衡、修订次数下界与 checkpoint 调度，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态更新频率、计算–收益折中及何时继续更新或停止提供形式化类比，可供核查迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Prescribed Bayesian Efficiency under Lifetime Adjustment Costs

**abstract/A1：** A Bayesian learner may retain an issued action while updating its posterior, trading predictive loss against adjustment charges. We assume population curvature, boundary control, an order-$1/n$ lower bound on expected posterior uncertainty about the optimal action, and an observable unbiased gradient witness whose squared norms are uniformly integrable. Sample losses may be unbounded or nonconvex. Cumulative expected excess measures the loss increase relative to current predictive Bayes actions. For bounded nonnegative nondecreasing charges $c$ with $c(0)=0$ and nondecreasing profiles $G: \\mathbb{N} \\to \[1,\\infty)$ with $G(J)=o(J)$, we characterize joint attainability of cumulative expected excess $O(G(J))$ at horizons $T=2^J$ and finite expected lifetime charge. Here $J$ indexes logarithmic time. The criterion is finiteness of a prefix-constrained allocation of tracking-error allowances across action-distance scales, equivalently its decreasing-weight dual. Adaptive timing and private randomization do not enlarge the attainable region. For each feasible pair, a horizon-independent deterministic checkpoint policy attains the profile with lifetime-cost moments of every positive order. For excess $O((\\log T)^r)$, $0&lt;r&lt;1$, every policy needs $Ω((\\log T)^{2-r})$ revisions in expectation, and a charge-independent schedule achieves a matching pathwise upper bound for every feasible charge. We verify the assumptions for logistic decision models with nondegenerate covariates, finite second moments and a locally positive smooth prior density, including fixed-ridge models with unbounded parameter priors.

**comment/C1：** 47 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-204"></a>

### 204. [Improving Imitation Policies for Multi-Phase Tasks through Residual Learning Allocation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25320v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25320

**作者：** Clinton Enwerem、Saleh Kemal、John S. Baras、Calin Belta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.RO、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25320v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25320v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究多阶段模仿策略中残差强化学习的预算分配，提出「完成增益」指标，把一次修正对整任务成功概率的影响分解为「到达该阶段的概率」与「条件改善」，并用配对轨迹估计，据此决定修正哪些动作分量、各阶段如何分配训练回合。

**相关性简评：** 其「失败最多的阶段未必是最值得修正的阶段」与按增益分配回合预算的思路，可能启发 LVR 中多步 latent 推理的贡献分配与计算预算/停止决策评价，可供核查如何将增益估计迁移为对推理步骤或状态更新的信用度量。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Improving Imitation Policies for Multi-Phase Tasks through Residual Learning Allocation
