**abstract/A1：** Residual reinforcement learning corrects an imitation-trained base policy using a limited budget of environment interaction. In a task with several phases, this budget must be divided among the phases, and the phase in which the base policy fails is not necessarily the phase in which a correction most improves task completion. We formalize this distinction through the completion gain: the change in full-task completion probability when, during a single phase, a state-feedback controller replaces selected components of the base policy's action. We decompose the completion gain into the probability of reaching the phase and the conditional improvement in completion from the start of the phase, and we estimate it from paired episodes. The positive estimates determine which action components the residual policy corrects and how training episodes are distributed across phases. In stochastic point-mass docking experiments, most failures occurred in the final docking phase, whereas the largest completion gain occurred in the preceding channel phase; gain-based allocation improved completion over allocation proportional to failure counts and, in a variant with a longer approach, over uniform allocation at half of the budget. In simulated bimanual robotic assembly, the completion-gain estimates were positive only for insertion and release, although few failures occurred during release; in a second assembly configuration, a residual policy trained in the release phase improved completion from held-out states at the start of release by 16.9%.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 5 figures, 6 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-205"></a>

### 205. [Pandora's Toolbox Or Why Agent Safety Cannot Be Verified](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25325v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25325

**作者：** Mario Franco、Carlos Gershenson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25325v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25325v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从可计算性角度论证：语言模型与通用计算设备耦合而成的智能体，其安全性判定不可判定；在上下文、取值与运行时间受限时可判定但仍为 PSPACE 完全，且安全性不具组合性，并讨论了实践中可用的宽松安全定义。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及多模态表示或 latent 生成，但其关于行为验证的可判定性与组合性边界，可能为「latent 推理是否真正使用视觉证据」这类过程验证目标提供理论类比，具体可迁移程度需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pandora's Toolbox Or Why Agent Safety Cannot Be Verified

**abstract/A1：** Present artificial intelligence agents are constructed by means of a coupling between a fixed language model with a general computing device. We show that this coupling makes safety undecidable. In particular, we show that given some dangerous program, no algorithm can predict whether an agent will run such a program that is semantically equivalent. In general, guardrails, blacklists, whitelists and certifications can only lead to imperfect safety solutions. Under finite constraints (bounded context, values and running time) safety is decidable but still intractable. Specifically, the problem is $\\mathsf{PSPACE}$-complete even when the description of the agent is known. Finally, safety is not a compositional property, i.e., agents that can be proven safe on their own can still produce unsafe behavior when grouped together. We also discuss relaxed notions of safety that could be used in practice.

</details>

<a id="paper-206"></a>

### 206. [Pro$^2$ Agent: Scientific Problem Probing for Autonomous AI R&amp;D](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25357v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25357

**作者：** Yixiong Chen、Liqian Peng、Tian Tan、Ziqian Lin、Blake Jianhang Chen、Chao Zhao、Yaojie Liu、Alec Go、Alan Yuille

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25357v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25357v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Pro² Agent，将自主 AI 研发的主要状态从代码库状态转为显式的「问题状态」，即记录阻碍成功的问题及其候选解的结构，并通过受控假设检验更新问题重要性与候选解收益，在 MLE-Bench 等基准上取得较高奖牌率。

**相关性简评：** 其显式问题状态与假设检验式状态更新，可能与 LVR 中如何显式维护多个 latent 假设、按证据更新其权重并据此停止推理的问题同构，可供核查；但任务与视觉模态无直接关联，迁移仅属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pro$^2$ Agent: Scientific Problem Probing for Autonomous AI R&amp;D

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents can now autonomously conduct AI research by writing code and running experiments. They typically do so by iteratively optimizing a codebase, while ignoring the problem structure behind the task. This formulation is effective on simpler tasks, but on difficult problems it often gets stuck in the wrong solution region because the agent never explicitly represents its understanding of the problem. We introduce Pro$^2$ Agent, which shifts the primary state of autonomous AI research from a codebase state to a Problem State: an explicit structure of what problems prevent success and which solutions are likely to address them. Pro$^2$ Agent initializes the Problem State through comprehensive analysis of available resources and constraints. It then performs targeted research to relate the identified problems to candidate solutions. Through controlled hypothesis testing, the agent updates the importance of a suspected problem or the benefit brought by a candidate solution. On the complete MLE-Bench, Pro$^2$ Agent matches the best reported medal rate (70.7%) under the standard 24-hour budget and reaches 76.0% with a 48-hour budget, the highest medal rate reported on the complete benchmark. It also achieves competitive performance on PostTrainBench. In addition, it produces research notes for each task that make the research process transparent and reusable by human researchers.

</details>

<a id="paper-207"></a>

### 207. [Drifting, Discriminating, and Directioning for Action Coordination in Higher-dimensional Action Space](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25385v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25385

**作者：** Taisuke Kobayashi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25385v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25385v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出用 drifting model 作为轻量策略，单次前向生成高维动作，并以源分布共优化、漂移前后判别器的对抗正则以 best-of-N 采样三步训练来促发动作协调，在三项高维运动控制任务上优于对角分布建模。

**相关性简评：** 单次前向隐式建模复杂分布、漂移前后分布的判别约束与 best-of-N 候选筛选，可能为 LVR 中一步/并行生成 latent 推理状态、状态分布对齐及候选验证提供借鉴线索，需核查其与视觉推理的契合度。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Drifting, Discriminating, and Directioning for Action Coordination in Higher-dimensional Action Space

**abstract/A1：** This paper proposes a lightweight policy model to mitigate the difficulty of handling higher-dimensional action spaces in reinforcement learning (RL), alongside an algorithm to effectively train it. To address this, we focus on drifting models, which can implicitly represent complex distributions and infer actions in a single forward pass. However, while drifting models can be straightforwardly trained via backpropagation of the Q-gradient, this alone cannot be expected to appropriately capture the desired action coordination. Therefore, this paper constructs a three-step learning algorithm to encourage the action coordination. First, the source distribution of the drifting model is co-optimized as a diagonal policy model. This conditions the drifting model to attach the coordination, which cannot be expressed by a diagonal distribution, as residuals. Second, a discriminator model before and after drifting is introduced with adversarial training for a regularization term. This actively promotes the extraction of the coordination that fundamentally separates the two distributions. Third, best-of-N sampling is applied for action selection during rollouts in order to give directionality toward/beyond the regions where the coordination is insufficiently extracted/sufficiently discovered. The proposed method consistently improves the sample efficiency and the final control performance over the conventional diagonal distribution modeling across three locomotion tasks with high-dimensional action spaces. Furthermore, the analysis indicates that this improvement is achieved by reducing variance in certain action dimensions, effectively coupling them with other actions as action coordination.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-208"></a>

### 208. [Interference Bounds and Merge-Aware Training Dynamics for Composable Fine-Tuned Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25430v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25430

**作者：** Alireza Moayedikia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25430v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25430v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将微调模型的组合性形式化为任务向量集合的性质，证明合并模型的超额损失由跨任务曲率重叠与更新符号对齐构成的干涉泛函控制，并提出曲率引导正则项在训练中压低该泛函，用二次、非二次模型及 Transformer 实验检验界与排序关系。

**相关性简评：** 其“曲率度量下的夹角与干涉泛函”属于表示几何视角，可能与多个 latent 推理模块或推理行为的叠加干扰问题相关；但对象是参数更新而非推理期状态，能否迁移到 LVR 组合与贡献分配尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interference Bounds and Merge-Aware Training Dynamics for Composable Fine-Tuned Models

**abstract/A1：** Combining fine-tuned models by composing their parameter updates has become a standard alternative to multi-task training. We formalize composability as a property of a collection of task vectors and prove that the excess loss of the merged model is controlled by an interference functional of the cross-task curvature overlap and the signed alignment of the updates. In the quadratic regime this functional equals the excess loss, giving a criterion that is necessary as well as sufficient and that measures the angle between task vectors in the curvature metric rather than the Euclidean one. Fine-tuning under a curvature-guided regularizer -- constructed from curvature statistics of the kind memory-efficient optimizers maintain -- keeps displacement in the base curvature metric below a ceiling set by the penalty coefficient at every iterate, at the standard $O(1/\\sqrt{T})$ non-convex rate. Under a single Loewner comparison between the base statistic and the task curvatures, this control converts into a ceiling on the interference functional, inversely proportional to the penalty coefficient, so that the curvature term of the bound is controlled during training. A first-order term, present because regularized solutions are not stationary for the task loss, is not reduced in the same way. Experiments on an exact non-quadratic model confirm the budget and the ceiling, show the first-order term dominating for strong penalties, and find the curvature-guided penalty slightly better than an isotropic one. In a three-seed transformer study the functional is rank-correlated with merging degradation, but no more strongly than the Euclidean displacement norm.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to a journal. The journal info will be confirmed once the paper is accepted

</details>

<a id="paper-209"></a>

### 209. [Raptor: Rejection-aware Adaptive Tree based on Speculative Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25436v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25436

**作者：** Qishu Hu、Haozhao Wang、Zicong Hong、Yufeng Zhan、Yuanqing Xia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25436v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25436v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Raptor 是一个免训练框架，用拒绝反馈提取「覆盖不足」与「过度扩张」两类互补信号，联合调节树状推测解码的深度、分支宽度与草稿预算，以加速长视觉上下文下的 VLM 解码。

**相关性简评：** 其用拒绝信号区分「候选覆盖不足」与「冗余扩张」并据此自适应分配计算、限制无效扩展的做法，可能与 LVR 中动态计算与停止/扩展决策的判据设计有概念联系，可供核查；但论文针对解码加速，不涉及推理期 latent 状态内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Raptor: Rejection-aware Adaptive Tree based on Speculative Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Tree-structured speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models (VLMs) by verifying multiple candidate continuations in parallel. However, long visual contexts increase the cost of speculative computation, making candidate coverage and redundant expansion important considerations beyond tree topology adaptation. We propose Raptor, a training-free framework that uses rejection feedback to jointly regulate candidate coverage and drafting effort. Our key insight is that acceptance length alone cannot distinguish insufficient candidate coverage from excessive deep expansion, and these conditions require different allocation decisions. Raptor therefore extracts complementary rejection-aware signals to characterize speculative progress, frontier coverage, and redundant expansion. These signals jointly guide bounded, momentum-smoothed updates of tree depth, branching width, and drafting budget, together with execution control that limits unnecessary drafting and progressively restores expansion when appropriate. Extensive experiments with two VLMs across seven multimodal benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of Raptor, achieving up to 2.39x average speedup over autoregressive decoding.

**comment/C1：** 18pages, 7figures, 3tables

</details>

<a id="paper-210"></a>

### 210. [Wearable Logo, Silent Hijack: Stealthy Cross-Modal Semantic Attacks on Visible--Infrared Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25437v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25437

**作者：** Qike Zhang、Jiaju Han、Xuelian Shi、Xiang Chen、Dehe Zhao、Banglong Nong、Wenhao Zheng、Lei Wang、Jiahuan Long、Jiujiang Guo、Chengyin Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25437v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25437v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LoSA：在保持 logo 轮廓不变的前提下，用黑盒打分优化其内部配色布局与摆放位置，对可见光-红外 VLM 实施跨模态协同的定向语义攻击。

**相关性简评：** 该工作可作为视觉证据可靠性的压力测试手段，供核查 VLM 答案究竟依赖局部视觉细节还是语义先验、以及跨模态扰动下证据筛选是否稳定，但与 latent 推理状态的生成与使用无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Wearable Logo, Silent Hijack: Stealthy Cross-Modal Semantic Attacks on Visible--Infrared Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Logos appear naturally on clothing and daily necessities, where their recognizable shapes and semantic associations make them familiar visual cues. This familiarity helps them blend into real-world environments and remain visually inconspicuous. We ask whether an existing logo can serve as a carrier for coordinated targeted semantic attacks on visible-infrared (VIS-IR) vision-language models (VLMs) without changing its original outline. This setting requires expressive adversarial variation within a fixed shape while accounting for different appearance characteristics and model responses across modalities. We propose Logo-guided Semantic Attack (LoSA), a score-based black-box framework that optimizes a logo's interior appearance and placement while preserving its silhouette. LoSA uses a contour field and low-frequency graph spectral modes to structure the interior color layout, a frozen appearance adapter to couple VIS and IR appearances, and particle swarm optimization to jointly optimize shared design parameters. Experiments across four datasets demonstrate that LoSA consistently outperforms the evaluated baselines in simultaneous Top-1 targeted success. Cross-architecture experiments, visual naturalness ratings, and captioning/VQA transfer analyses further characterize its effectiveness and limitations. These findings reveal that familiar visual symbols from real-world environments can serve as shape-preserving carriers for coordinated targeted semantic attacks across imaging modalities.

</details>

<a id="paper-211"></a>

### 211. [FamilySpace: From Emergent Chemical Semantics to Controllable Crystal Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25452v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25452

**作者：** Haowei Hua、Irwin King、Wanyu Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.mtrl-sci

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25452v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25452v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文发现普通晶体 VAE 的原子级 latent 空间会自发按元素族分区（称为 emergent chemical semantics），并据此提出 FamilySpace，用按族匹配的多源 flow 模型实现生成晶体元素族组成的可控。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但「latent 空间自发语义分区并按分区选择源分布以控制生成」这一机制，可能为 LVR 中按推理类型或证据分区引导 latent 状态提供类比线索，可供核查其是否只是晶体领域的特定现象。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FamilySpace: From Emergent Chemical Semantics to Controllable Crystal Generation

**abstract/A1：** Latent crystal generative models typically combine a variational autoencoder (VAE) for learning atom-wise representations with a flow model that transports a simple source distribution to the learned latent distribution. However, previous studies have mainly focused on the quality of generated crystals, leaving the structure and generative utility of the atom-wise latent space largely unexplored. In this work, we show that even a vanilla crystal VAE implicitly organizes atom-wise latent representations according to elemental families, with different families occupying largely separated regions of the latent space. We term this phenomenon emergent chemical semantics. This structure suggests that sampling from a family-specific latent region can bias the decoder toward atoms from the corresponding elemental family. Building on this insight, we propose FamilySpace, a framework for controlling the elemental-family composition of generated crystals. FamilySpace assigns each elemental family a distinct source component, forming a family-indexed multi-source distribution, and trains a flow model using source-target pairs matched by family. In inference, a user-specified family template selects the source component for each atom-wise representation, enabling direct control over the family composition of generated crystals. Experiments on MP-20 and MPTS-52 show that FamilySpace achieves competitive crystal-generation performance while matching prescribed elemental-family compositions. Moreover, selected family templates increase the fraction of generated crystals predicted to be stable or metastable, demonstrating that latent-space composition control can support targeted exploration of promising chemical regions without additional optimization techniques such as reinforcement learning. Code is available at https://github.com/hhw11/FamilySpace

</details>

<a id="paper-212"></a>

### 212. [Dimension-Free Buffer Bounds for Compressed-Momentum Error Feedback](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25463v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25463

**作者：** Alireza Moayedikia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25463v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25463v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析压缩动量误差反馈的缓冲界，证明把压缩动量重新注入压缩前累积量后，误差缓冲满足不含维度 m、n 与秩 r 的递推，结论覆盖 top-k 与截断 SVD，并在 BERT 微调上实测反馈衰减的代价。

**相关性简评：** 误差反馈以残余缓冲持续把压缩丢弃的信息注入状态，可能为 latent 推理中压缩视觉细节后的信息保留、状态更新与容量需求提供可核查的抽象类比，但本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dimension-Free Buffer Bounds for Compressed-Momentum Error Feedback

**abstract/A1：** Analyses of error feedback bound the memory buffer through a compressor contraction constant -- $r/p$ for the best rank-$r$ approximation of a $p$-dimensional spectrum -- which survives into the rate. We show it need not appear at all. When the compressed momentum is re-injected into the pre-compression accumulator, the previous compressed momentum $β\_1 M\_{t-1}$ is an admissible competitor in the projection, and the buffer obeys $\\\|E\_t\\\|\_F \\le (1-β\_1)\\\|G\_t\\\|\_F + γ\\\|E\_{t-1}\\\|\_F$, free of the dimensions $m,n$ and of the rank $r$. The argument uses only that the compressor is a metric projection onto a closed cone, so it covers top-$k$ selection as well as truncated SVD, holds for every selection from the projection set with no condition on singular-value crossings, and in its trajectory-tracking form assumes nothing about the gradients. In the two-stage template of the standard analyses it replaces a Stage-2 constant of order $p^2/r^2$ by one of order $1$, against $Θ(p/r)$ for the sharpest contraction-based bounds. Transferring the full rate costs a leakage term, which we bound by the step-weighted gradient mass $\\sum\_{u\\le t}η\_u\\\|G\_u\\\|\_F$ and show tight up to a factor $\\sqrt{2}$; the price is an additive $O(\\log^2 T)$ floor, not a slower rate. On BERT fine-tuning at rank ratio $32/768$ the feedback decay costs under $0.008$ validation $F\_1$.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to a journal and details will be confirmed once paper accepted

</details>

<a id="paper-213"></a>

### 213. [WeightCode: Installing New Capabilities into Neural Networks via Weight-Space Programming](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25476v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25476

**作者：** Bo Li、Taoyong Cui、Bob Zhang、Mengran Li、Chengyang Zhang、Yang Zhang、Pheng Ann Heng、Wanli Ouyang、Zhenfei Yin、Yingcheng Wu、Ling Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25476v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25476v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Weight-Space Programming（WSP）框架 WeightCode：由冻结的语言模型编写可执行的「权重程序」，直接读取或改写已训练网络的权重，在不做梯度训练的情况下把新能力（指令组合、新动作、ImageNet 鲁棒性）装入网络，并考察跨网络复用与累积编辑。

**相关性简评：** 其通过写入选择性门控并对权重做因果干预来定位增益来源的做法，可能为 LVR 中「latent/内部状态是否真正被使用、贡献如何分配」的诊断与条件化计算提供可核查的间接线索；但论文本身不研究视觉推理状态生成，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WeightCode: Installing New Capabilities into Neural Networks via Weight-Space Programming

**abstract/A1：** Neural networks encode learned capabilities in their weights, yet extending those capabilities with explicit task knowledge remains a fundamental challenge. Here we show that Weight-Space Programming (WSP), in which executable programs read, construct or modify a network's weights directly, can install new capabilities within trained neural networks. WeightCode, a WSP framework, uses a frozen language model to write executable weight programs that install new computations into trained neural networks using task knowledge and their existing weights, without gradient-based training. Once installed, the new capabilities run independently of the language model. Given a grammar or eight demonstrations, weight programs enable previously unseen command compositions (0% to 99.8% exact match) and a new pickup action (0% to 98.4% success), while preserving the original command and navigation capabilities. On ImageNet, four weight programs achieve net gains of 8.84-17.62 points while preserving clean accuracy, outperforming the best of eight fine-tuning methods under matched examples, checks, and retention constraints. In a Vision Transformer, causal interventions show that installed noise-correction gates drive robustness gains, while their selective activation preserves clean accuracy. Rerun on five independently trained Vision Transformers without further language model calls, ten weight programs retain 83-110% of their source-network gains on average, whereas copying their fixed weight edits yields negligible gains. Six programs written for an already edited network add 5.23-14.50 points of contrast robustness while retaining the previously installed noise gain. Together, these results establish WSP as a new route to acquiring and accumulating capabilities in trained neural networks through reusable weight programs.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 11 figures, including appendix. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/WeightCode

</details>

<a id="paper-214"></a>

### 214. [TAP: Tapping the Discriminative Potential of Frozen Representations for Class-Incremental Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25478v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25478

**作者：** Zihao Wang、Yu-Wei Zhan、Xiao-Qian Liu、Zhen-Duo Chen、Weitong Liu、Xin Luo、Xin-Shun Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25478v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25478v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TAP 框架，在冻结的 CLIP 嵌入空间中用类别条件视觉分布建模、类自适应语义校准和视觉-语义后验融合累积类信息，仅做轻量优化以实现类增量学习。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线的推理机制距离较远，但其在冻结表征上建模类条件协方差并以专家乘积融合视觉与语义后验的做法，可能为「视觉细节在内部状态中保留多少、如何与语言先验结合」提供可核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TAP: Tapping the Discriminative Potential of Frozen Representations for Class-Incremental Learning

**abstract/A1：** Pre-trained models provide transferable representations for class-incremental learning (CIL). However, sequentially adapting these representations introduces optimization costs and may disrupt old-class feature structure. This motivates us to explore whether the discriminative potential of these representations can be further exploited by continually accumulating class information while keeping the encoders frozen. To this end, we propose TAP, a new and novel framework for tapping the discriminative potential of frozen representations in class-incremental learning. It accumulates visual and semantic class information in the frozen CLIP embedding space with only lightweight optimization. TAP comprises three modules: class-conditional visual distribution modeling, class-adaptive semantic calibration, and visual-semantic posterior fusion. The first module retains class means and within-class scatters, combining class-specific and shared covariances to stabilize distribution estimation. The second module learns class-specific weights over text templates from current-task images, adapting text representations to downstream classes. The third module combines the visual and semantic posteriors through a weighted product of experts for prediction over all seen classes without retaining images from previous tasks. Across nine datasets under two incremental protocols, TAP achieves state-of-the-art performance, attaining the highest average incremental and final-stage accuracies among the compared methods. The code is provided as an ancillary file.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 3 figures. Code is included as an ancillary file

</details>

<a id="paper-215"></a>

### 215. [When Should a Voice Agent Speak? End-of-Turn Detection in Turkish](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25489v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25489

**作者：** Ahmet Tuğrul Bayrak、Fatma Nur Korkmaz、Mustafa Sertaç Türkel、Nur Yağmur Tuncer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25489v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25489v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究土耳其语语音助手在静音起始时应否开口的问题，从真实双人视频通话语料中区分轮次结束与轮内停顿，比较微调 TurnGPT、零样本语言模型与多语种检测器，并结合发声时序与面部特征，在录音/说话人分离设定下评估，还做了延迟模拟。

**相关性简评：** 其核心是「何时继续等待、何时终止」的停止决策与停止信号校准，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 判据（如从部分状态判断是否已获足够证据）提供概念类比，但模态与任务距离较远，需核查其校准与延迟—错误权衡的度量方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Should a Voice Agent Speak? End-of-Turn Detection in Turkish

**abstract/A1：** A voice agent that waits for a fixed silence before replying either interrupts users who pause mid-utterance or responds late to users who have finished. This study examines the decision at the onset of silence, using turn ends and turn-internal pauses extracted from the Real-TurnTurk corpus of Turkish dyadic video calls. A fine-tuned Turkish TurnGPT model, a zero-shot Turkish language model and a deployed multilingual detector are compared, together with voice-activity timing and facial features, under recording-disjoint and speaker-disjoint evaluation with paired cluster bootstrap tests. On held-out recordings, the fine-tuned model separates turn ends from pauses better than both text baselines and is the best calibrated of the three. Timing features of the current speaker are similarly informative, and their combination with the fine-tuned text score gives the best timing-based results; with logistic regression, this combination also outperforms the same combination with either text baseline. In a latency simulation, adding the text score to a silence timeout shortens the mean response delay at the same interruption rate. On automatic Whisper transcripts, the fine-tuned model retains its advantage over the zero-shot model.

</details>

<a id="paper-216"></a>

### 216. [PRIO: Pre-trained Representation for Interactive Objects in 4D Worlds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25500v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25500

**作者：** Shixun Huang、Lifu Wei、Yue Chang、Chen Geng、Heng Zhang、Jinhyung Ahn、Caoliwen Wang、Siyuan Chen、Donglai Xiang、Jiajun Wu、Peter Yichen Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25500v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25500v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PRIO，从静态几何出发一次前向预测物体特有的配置空间，为拉格朗日力学提供广义坐标，使物体在用户动作下按物理律实时运动、无需再跑网络推理，并支持网格、点云与 3D Gaussians。

**相关性简评：** 其「一次前向预测紧凑状态空间、随后在该空间内低成本迭代更新」的结构与 LVR 关心的 latent 状态表示和推理期计算成本解耦有形式上的相似，可供核查状态空间设计与动态更新分工的借鉴，但任务场景（物理仿真、机器人操作）与视觉语言推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PRIO: Pre-trained Representation for Interactive Objects in 4D Worlds

**abstract/A1：** 3D worlds can now be captured, reconstructed, and generated at scale, yet the objects in them stay still: a pressed pillow does not compress, and a pulled door does not swing. Bringing these objects into interactive 4D world models requires a representation of how they can move, deform, and respond physically to user actions in real time. We present PRIO, a pre-trained representation for interactive objects. Given static geometry, PRIO predicts an object-specific configuration space in a single forward pass. This space provides generalized coordinates for Lagrangian mechanics, allowing physical laws and user actions to govern motion. Configurations are linear in these coordinates, allowing the quantities needed by the dynamics to be precomputed. Dynamics then run at interactive rates without further network inference. Pre-trained on diverse 4D trajectories, PRIO learns priors over object configurations that generalize to unseen shapes. It supports meshes, point clouds, and 3D Gaussians, bringing objects in reconstructed and generated scenes into interaction. We demonstrate its use in real-to-sim workflows and robot manipulation. In addition, compared with existing 4D generation methods, PRIO generates dynamics faster and follows user actions more faithfully.

</details>

<a id="paper-217"></a>

### 217. [Coordinated Use of Large Language Models in Academic Research](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25502v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25502

**作者：** Vladislav Marinov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25502v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25502v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出多 LLM 协同开展学术研究的方法论框架，以程序独立、功能分配、分歧与收敛的诊断价值、外部验证、核查比例性等七项原则组织一个非线性协议，并用实际研究案例加以说明。

**相关性简评：** 「分歧/收敛的诊断价值」与「核查比例性」可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、何时停止追加检查及多路径贡献分配的后果表述，但领域较远，仅属概念层面的启发线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coordinated Use of Large Language Models in Academic Research

**abstract/A1：** This study proposes a methodological framework for the coordinated use of multiple large language models in academic research. Its central argument is that involving more models does not in itself guarantee more reliable results; what matters is how cognitive and verification operations are organised. The framework is based on seven principles (procedural independence, functional allocation, the diagnostic value of divergence, the diagnostic value of convergence, external verification, proportionality of checking, and human epistemic responsibility) and sets out a non-linear multi-LLM protocol in which independently obtained results are compared, critically examined and, where necessary, verified against external sources. The approach is illustrated through real-world cases drawn from research practice. The study concludes that moving from a single language model to a coordinated multi-LLM process represents a change in the organisation of research, in which the researcher makes the final decisions and bears scholarly responsibility.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 1 figure, 1 table. English translation of an article originally published in Bulgarian in Media and Language (2026). The original Bulgarian publication remains the version of record

</details>

<a id="paper-218"></a>

### 218. [Mesh-Free Keypoint Learning for Category-Level 6D Object Pose Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25513v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25513

**作者：** Junghoon Seo、Hakjin Lee、Jaehoon Sim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25513v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25513v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MORSEPose：由位姿与尺寸标注构造带容差的膨胀包络来筛选观测点作为重建支撑，结合多级 RGB-D 特征、自适应关键点和可学习类别上下文做类别级 6D 位姿估计，并在 REAL275、HouseCat6D 上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其自适应关键点与归一化物体坐标可作为物体中心式的几何表示线索，包络式支撑筛选也可能供核查部分观测下 LVR 监督目标的构造；但论文本身不涉及 latent 推理，联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mesh-Free Keypoint Learning for Category-Level 6D Object Pose Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Category-level 6D pose estimation relates partial RGB-D observations of unseen instances to a canonical frame shared across a category. Object-aware reconstruction helps adaptive keypoints retain the geometry of the visible surface. Common reconstruction targets select observed points by their distance to a registered instance surface. We present MORSEPose, which derives this support directly from standard pose and metric-size annotations. We map observed 3D points to normalized object coordinates and retain those inside a tolerancedilated pose-and-size envelope. This construction is consistent across camera frames and metric scales. When the annotated extent bounds the object surface, the envelope conservatively contains the support of a conventional surface-distance selector. We integrate this target with multi-level RGB-D features, adaptive keypoints, and learnable category context. Experiments on REAL275 and HouseCat6D show that the envelope closely matches surface-based supervision at both point and task levels, remains stable under boundary perturbations and viewpoint changes, and supports competitive category-level pose accuracy across benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-219"></a>

### 219. [Multi-View Relighting with Mergeable Radiance Fields](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25523v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25523

**作者：** Yinan Wang、Rohan Kadkol、Hyunjin Yoo、Jean-François Lalonde、James J. Clark

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25523v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25523v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MergeableRF，用共享几何加每种基光源一个外观网络来表示场景，通过共享初始化与 merge-aware 训练使网络参数的加权组合对应图像的加权组合，从而在给定环境贴图时直接合并参数完成重光照，兼容 NeRF 与 3DGS。

**相关性简评：** 其「共享主干 + 分条件分支、并训练参数组合复现输出组合」的合并式表示，可能为 LVR 中多假设 latent 状态或分条件推理状态的组合与可加性提供可核查的类比线索，但任务本身是渲染重光照，与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multi-View Relighting with Mergeable Radiance Fields

**abstract/A1：** We propose MergeableRF, a method for radiance field relighting through model parameter merging. Given multi-view images of a scene under known basis lights, our goal is to relight the scene by directly combining the parameters of radiance fields trained under these lights. This is motivated by the linearity of light transport: the image under a mixture of lights is the same weighted combination of the images under each light. However, combining the parameters of independently trained radiance fields does not produce the corresponding combination of images. To address this, we represent a scene with shared geometry and a separate appearance network for each basis light. We start these networks from the same initialization and introduce merge-aware training, which teaches their parameter combinations to reproduce the corresponding combinations of images. Given a target HDR environment map, we compute its basis-light coefficients and use them to merge the appearance networks into a single relit model. Our method supports both Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), without material estimation, additional relighting networks, or target-specific optimization. Unlike image-space blending, it requires only one rendering pass per viewpoint. Experiments on synthetic Neural Gaffer objects and real OLATverse captures demonstrate relighting quality competitive with methods trained on the same multi-light observations.

</details>

<a id="paper-220"></a>

### 220. [How Feedback-Free Self-Evolution Reshapes Agent Skills: A Systematic Study](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25534v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25534

**作者：** Litao Guo、Man Chen、Xinli Xu、Jinsong Zhou、Zixin Zhang、Ying-Cong Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25534v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25534v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统研究无反馈自我进化如何改变 agent 技能文档：模型在没有任何任务、运行或评判的情况下逐轮重写专家技能，结果各基准性能均下降（SkillsBench 三十轮后平均低 15 分）；分析显示重写把技能拉向模型自身先验并最先丢弃先验之外的知识，据此提出的 SkillDiff 原位标记这部分知识，不改文本、不用反馈即平均提升 4.8 分。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但「无反馈重写使外部知识向模型先验坍缩、先失去先验外信息」的观察，可能可供核查 latent 状态中视觉细节被语言先验吞没的同类失效，以及保留证据类信息的标记式做法。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Feedback-Free Self-Evolution Reshapes Agent Skills: A Systematic Study

**abstract/A1：** Agent skills are documents in which experts write down procedural knowledge, and models increasingly maintain them by rewriting them without feedback. Such rewriting could adapt a skill to the model that uses it or erode the expert knowledge that makes it useful, yet how skills evolve under this feedback-free self-evolution has not been studied. We let models rewrite expert skills round after round without any task, run or judgment and run agents built on the same models with the rewritten skills. Our experiments show that the skills lose value rather than improve, as every model we test performs worse with them on every benchmark, by 15 points on average on SkillsBench after thirty rounds. Sampling each model's prior with skills it writes from the topic alone, we find that rewriting pulls skills toward this prior and gives up first the knowledge outside it. Putting this knowledge back recovers much of the lost pass rate, so an expert's skill is valuable largely for what the model does not write on its own. Building on this insight, SkillDiff marks this knowledge in place and raises the pass rate on SkillsBench by 4.8 points on average without feedback or any change to the skill's text. Project repository: https://github.com/LitaoGuo/How-Self-Evolution-Reshapes-Skills.

</details>

<a id="paper-221"></a>

### 221. [Efficient Multimodal Agents for Auditing 3D Worlds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25543v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25543

**作者：** Jingbo Yang、Ziyan Jiang、Li An、Jiabao Ji、Yujian Liu、Tommi Jaakkola、Yang Zhang、Shiyu Chang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25543v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25543v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 3D 世界自动审计系统：用在前沿教师轨迹上端到端微调的 4B VLM 群体担任探索者，负责导航与触发隐藏故障，再由前沿模型复核其记录并判定问题，在 WorldAuditBench 上以更低成本超过已有审计器。

**相关性简评：** 「小模型主动获取视觉证据、前沿模型做判定」的分工与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中的证据获取、停止决策和成本分配有具体联系，可供核查哪些子能力可下沉到小模型、以及识别环节为何明显落后。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient Multimodal Agents for Auditing 3D Worlds

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal agents can now build interactive 3D worlds and games, yet these worlds, like those built by people, often contain glitches such as floating objects, walls that can be walked through, or objects that change when no one is looking. An automatic auditor could check such worlds at scale and provide rewards for agents that build them, but only if it is both accurate and cheap. Auditing requires three abilities, namely navigating to the objects that may contain a glitch, triggering glitches that appear only after a specific behavior, and recognizing what is wrong. Current auditors are either accurate but slow and expensive or cheap but unreliable. We study how much of the audit small models can take on. A 4B vision language model fine-tuned end to end on verified trajectories of a frontier teacher guided by Bayesian search learns to navigate and trigger, and six such explorers together match a frontier model on both, but recognition stays far behind. We therefore build an auditor in which a swarm of small explorers navigates and triggers, and a frontier model reviews what they record and report. On WorldAuditBench, our auditor succeeds on 55.9% of 213 tasks in Unreal Engine 5 and Three.js worlds, 13.6 points above the strongest auditor reported, at less than a quarter of its cost. It matches the frontier reviewer exploring on its own at 4.6 times lower cost and about half the latency. Without further training, it also answers embodied questions about real-world scans, matching the frontier reviewer exploring on its own and surpassing four other frontier models by 6.4 to 16.6 points.

</details>

<a id="paper-222"></a>

### 222. [Design and Evaluation of Epira: An Automated Interview System for Case Investigation and Contact Tracing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25577v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25577

**作者：** Ishita Asnani、Shaghayegh Fazliani、Zachary Ryan、Kriyoshig Prasai、Chirag Kumar、Indra Gonzalez Ojeda、Marc Lipsitch、Madeleine Udell

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25577v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25577v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出 Epira，一种由规则控制器约束 LLM 对话的自动化访谈系统，用于多病种病例调查与接触者追踪，强制提问必填项、结构化记录病例，并通过模拟病例和志愿者研究评估字段与接触者回收率、可用性与信任度。

**相关性简评：** 其「控制器维护缺失信息状态并决定下一问」的机制，可能与 LVR 中证据获取顺序与停止决策的显式控制及信息覆盖度评价形成类比，可供核查；但该研究不涉及视觉或多模态内部表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Design and Evaluation of Epira: An Automated Interview System for Case Investigation and Contact Tracing

**abstract/A1：** Objective: Case investigation and contact tracing are labor-intensive. Web forms struggle when respondents need clarification before disclosing contacts, and open-ended chatbots may deviate from protocols. We developed and evaluated Epira, a protocol-controlled large language model (LLM) interviewer for multi-disease case investigation and contact tracing. Materials and Methods: Epira pairs LLM dialogue with a rule-based controller enforcing required questions, safety handoffs, and a structured case record, and adapts to new diseases by questionnaire upload. Local (Qwen2.5) and cloud (GPT-5.4) backends were tested. Study 1 used 108 simulated cases with ground-truth fields and contacts for measles and gonorrhea under Epira and open-ended conditions (864 sessions). Study 2 enrolled 72 volunteers in a 2$\\times$2 disease-by-model interview assessing completion, usability, acceptability, and trust. Results: Across Study 1, Epira with a local LLM captured 88.9% of required fields and 60.1% of ground-truth contacts. Open-ended interviewers recovered 31.1-41.9 percentage points fewer required fields than the same models in Epira. In Study 2, completion was 100%; mean adapted System Usability Scale (SUS) was 82.95 $\\pm$ 16.17 on a 0-100 scale and mean trust was 3.85 $\\pm$ 0.77 on a 1-5 scale. Discussion: Protocol control drove completeness more than model choice. Local and cloud deployments produced similar records, supporting privacy-preserving local use with human review of routed interviews and missed contacts. Conclusion: Epira recovers required fields and contacts under diverse disclosure conditions, works with local and cloud LLMs, and received favorable usability and trust ratings.

</details>

<a id="paper-223"></a>

### 223. [Learning Networked Dynamical Systems via Neural ODE-based Graph Structure Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25586v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25586

**作者：** Peiyan Sun、Jinming Yang、Gujia Gao、Yaohui Jin、Yanyan Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25586v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25586v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 NOGI，用确定性图学习器耦合 MPNN 驱动的 Neural ODE，从多变量时间序列中以连续时间向量场推断潜在交互图，并以伴随法端到端训练，在多个模拟动力系统和 fMRI 基准上评估结构恢复与预测。

**相关性简评：** 其将推理与固定观测时刻解耦、在观测间连续积分潜状态与梯度的做法，可能为 LVR 中面向不规则或稀疏视觉证据的 latent 状态更新与计算分配提供可供核查的类比线索；但该文不涉及多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Networked Dynamical Systems via Neural ODE-based Graph Structure Inference

**abstract/A1：** Inferring the latent interaction graph from multivariate time series is fundamental to understanding complex dynamical systems in neuroscience, biology, and physics. Despite recent progress in neural relational inference, most methods rely on discrete-time modeling, which degrades under sparse and irregular observations and tends to entangle multi-hop dependencies into spurious direct edges. We show that a continuous-time formulation naturally decouples inference from any fixed observation schedule, allowing gradient signals to be integrated continuously between observations for both dynamics and structure learning. We propose NOGI (Neural ODE-based Graph Inference), a framework that couples a deterministic graph learner with an MPNN-based Neural ODE. The two modules are trained end-to-end via the adjoint method. Across four simulated dynamical systems at two scales, NOGI achieves the best structure recovery in 30 out of 32 evaluated cases with highly competitive forecasting performance, remaining robust under observation degradation. On the realistic Netsim fMRI benchmark, it attains an average AUPRC of 0.90 (AUROC 0.96). These results suggest that explicitly decoupling relational interactions from self-dynamics within a continuous-time vector field provides a powerful mechanism for graph structure learning under realistic observation conditions.

</details>

<a id="paper-224"></a>

### 224. [Characterizing Age-Conditioned Affective Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25593v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25593

**作者：** Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy、Azmine Toushik Wasi、Lin Gu、Ruogu Fang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25593v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25593v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作在900张OASIS图像上用年轻、中年、老年提示评估17个VLM的情绪效价评分，并与822名被试的分年龄评分对比，考察提示引发的评分变化是否在单张图像层面吻合人群的年龄差异。

**相关性简评：** 其「整体偏移与图像级一致性分离」并给出相关性上限的分析框架，可能可供核查 latent 推理状态的变化究竟携带条目特异信息，还是仅施加全局偏置。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Characterizing Age-Conditioned Affective Reasoning in Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** An age prompt can change how a vision-language model (VLM) rates the emotion evoked by an image. Does the change predict how people of different ages rate that same image? We evaluate 17 VLMs on 900 OASIS images using young-, middle-, and older-adult prompts and estimate image-specific age differences from ratings by 822 participants. Without an age cue, models closely track average human valence (median Pearson $r=0.891$). Under an older-adult prompt, 16 of 17 models assign lower valence on average than under a young-adult prompt. Yet after adjusting for image pleasantness, correlations between model and human age effects range from $-0.030$ to $0.093$, against an estimated ceiling of $0.595$. The discrepancy is clearest on 54 sensitive images rated as pleasant. Across models, the older prompt reduces valence by $0.845$ points on average, while older participants rate these images $0.443$ points higher than younger participants. Providing human ratings from other images improves agreement on the direction of age differences, although image-specific fidelity remains modest on 600 held-out images ($r\_{\\mathrm{part}}=0.105$). These results show why evaluations of demographic personalization in foundation models need human comparisons at the level of individual stimuli. Project page available at https://shahriyar-zaman.github.io/age-conditioned-emotion-reasoning/

**comment/C1：** Preprint. Under review

</details>

<a id="paper-225"></a>

### 225. [Cyber-Physical RSI for AI-RAN: A Case Study](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25619v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25619

**作者：** Binghan Wu、Kai Zeng、Zhiqi Li、Zhigang Wang、Yang Liu、Ya-Qin Zhang、Joseph Sifakis、Ye Ouyang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25619v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25619v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出面向 AI-RAN 的信息—物理递归自我改进框架，把任务学生模型、实验规划教练、可校准虚拟环境与物理执行代理连成闭环，并以乒乓切换为案例，用 12 次物理执行结果更新模型并在保留配置上评估泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其「物理测量作为证据同时驱动模型更新与下一轮实验规划」的闭环，可能与 LVR 中何时获取额外视觉证据、如何用证据修正内部状态并选择后续支撑配置的问题同构，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cyber-Physical RSI for AI-RAN: A Case Study

**abstract/A1：** Learned models for radio access networks (RANs) are often developed using simulation, yet their predictions can shift when exposed to physical propagation conditions, protocol behavior, and equipment implementations. This paper presents a cyber-physical recursive self-improvement (RSI) framework for AI-RAN that connects a task-oriented student model, an experiment-planning coach agent, a calibratable virtual environment, and a physical host agent. Physical measurements are organized as evidence for both model adaptation and subsequent experiment planning. We examine an implementation of the physical-feedback pathway using ping-pong handover as a case study. The system completes 12 physical executions organized into six baseline--candidate pairs, updates the student from measured outcomes, and evaluates the revised model on reserved physical configurations. In the observed short-window tests, increasing the time-to-trigger from 320 to 1280\\,ms changes the ping-pong count from three to zero. The updated student achieves a grouped-validation mean absolute error (MAE) of 2.673 round trips/min, while a common three-configuration physical evaluation yields MAEs of 1.240 and 1.601 round trips/min for the pre-feedback and post-feedback models, respectively. These results characterize both the operational feedback loop and the parameter-dependent generalization behavior revealed by new physical experiments.

</details>

<a id="paper-226"></a>

### 226. [CaT-Movie: Balancing Global Planning and Local Rendering for Long Multi-Shot Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25620v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25620

**作者：** Jinpeng Lu、Haoyuan Shi、Yuanshen Guan、Jiyuan Xia、Bin Wu、Yinda Chen、Zhiwei Xiong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25620v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25620v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CaT-Movie 长时段多镜头视频生成系统，由 LLM 全局故事规划器与局部窗口渲染器组成，并用确定性代码在镜头之间维护故事板、生成镜头提示并索引参考帧，另构建带语义状态转移标注的数据集与评测基准。

**相关性简评：** 其显式持久的故事板状态与「语义状态转移」标注，可能与长程推理中的外部状态维护、身份信息漂移与表示坍缩问题形成类比，可供核查；但任务属视频生成，迁移到 VLM latent 推理仍属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CaT-Movie: Balancing Global Planning and Local Rendering for Long Multi-Shot Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Current multi-shot video generators render only limited durations per pass, requiring long movies to be stitched from local segments across which narrative progression and visual identity drift. To synthesize long-horizon movies with Coherence across Time, we present CaT-Movie, a multi-shot generation system consisting of a global story planner and a local window renderer. The planner expands user requests via a large language model to preserve, infer, and complete story details, while deterministic code maintains the resulting storyboard across shots to compile self-contained shot prompts and index reference frames from generated history. To execute this guidance, the renderer applies reference-routed sparse self-attention and shot-wise cross-attention to route identities from the indexed frames while isolating per-shot textual conditions. Given the scarcity of high-quality open-source multi-shot data, we construct a dataset of over 350K film shots annotated with semantic state transitions, entity grounding, and multi-granularity requests. We further build CaT-MBench, a benchmark on held-out films with the same annotations. It gives every baseline its native format and counts cross-shot consistency only when a cut opens a new camera setup, preventing repeated framing from passing as consistency. On CaT-MBench, CaT-Movie attains the highest average score and the most consistent characters among all methods. The project page is available at https://jinplu.github.io/CaT-Movie/.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-227"></a>

### 227. [Deviations from the NTK Regime in CNNs under Joint Depth-Width Scaling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25624v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25624

**作者：** Maria Matveev、Pascal Esser、Gitta Kutyniok、Mariia Seleznova

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25624v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25624v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用数值实验考察 CNN 在联合深度-宽度缩放下的 NTK 行为，发现初始化时 NTK 离散度随 L/C 增大（ordered regime 除外），训练中核旋转也随 L/C 增强，且二者缩放规律不同。

**相关性简评：** 其「离散度/核旋转随有效深度比变化」的度量思路，或可作为核查深层 latent 推理中表示是否随层数漂移、推理状态是否稳定的分析视角，但与原论文的视觉推理任务无直接联系，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Deviations from the NTK Regime in CNNs under Joint Depth-Width Scaling

**abstract/A1：** The concentration and training-time constancy of the neural tangent kernel (NTK) depend on the interplay between network width, depth, and initialization. While joint depth-width scaling has been studied in fully-connected, residual, and recurrent networks, its effects in convolutional neural networks (CNNs) remain less understood. We investigate these effects numerically in CNNs, examining NTK dispersion at initialization and kernel rotation during training. Let depth $L$ be the number of convolutional layers, and width $C$ the number of channels per layer. We find that NTK dispersion increases with $L/C$ in the critical and chaotic initialization regimes, but remains small in the ordered regime. At critical initialization, dispersion curves approximately collapse as a function of $L/C$, suggesting a nontrivial joint scaling regime. During training, kernel rotation generally increases with $L/C$, even in the ordered regime where initial dispersion is small. Together, these results suggest that $L/C$ may govern NTK deviations from the infinite-width limit in certain regimes, and that NTK concentration at initialization and stability during training exhibit different scaling behaviors.

</details>

<a id="paper-228"></a>

### 228. [QUBO-based feature selection for MRI undersampling pattern design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25625v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25625

**作者：** Xiaoxin Huang、Masayuki Ohzeki

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25625v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25625v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把笛卡尔 MRI 欠采样掩膜设计表述为二值特征子集选择问题：用候选掩膜的互信息与条件互信息估计，加上采样数惩罚构造 QUBO 模型，以模拟退火和量子退火求解，并在 fastMRI 膝部数据上通过零填充重建与四种常规采样方法比较误差与结构相似度。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但「信息量估计＋采样预算惩罚」这种把证据获取写成组合优化目标的表述，可能为「视觉证据筛选」和动态取证的代价权衡提供可核查的建模思路，需较多迁移验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** QUBO-based feature selection for MRI undersampling pattern design

**abstract/A1：** We formulate undersampling mask design for Cartesian magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) as a feature subset selection problem. Each sampling line is represented by a binary feature indicating whether it is included in each candidate mask. Using candidate masks and their training-set evaluations, we estimate mutual information (MI) and conditional mutual information (CMI). These estimates and a sampling-count penalty define a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) model. The model is solved using simulated annealing and quantum annealing to determine a feature subset and its corresponding sampling mask. We assess the selected subsets on the test set through zero-filled reconstruction in Fourier undersampling simulations using fastMRI knee magnitude reference images, with patient-disjoint training and test sets. At all six tested target sampling rates from 15% to 40%, the resulting reconstructions have lower mean root mean square error than those obtained with four conventional sampling methods. They also have higher mean structural similarity than those obtained using univariate mutual-information ranking at all tested rates. These results support information-theoretic feature selection as an approach to MRI undersampling mask design under the tested zero-filled simulation settings.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-229"></a>

### 229. [TRACE: Attribute Execution for Sequential Image Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25648v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25648

**作者：** Xiyang Zhang、Yuanhe Tian、Hongzhi Wang、Yan Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25648v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25648v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TRACE 用于顺序图像编辑：把持续生效的属性与其视觉载体分离，用 active-goal binding 判定哪些要求仍需保留、为同一区域选择合适像素载体并做色调校准，在冻结编辑器与顺序编辑基准上验证颜色—材质联合恢复。

**相关性简评：** 其“跨轮保持仍生效约束＋完整请求检查＋证据（载体）选择”机制，可能与 LVR 中任务必要信息的延续性状态与视觉证据筛选问题形成类比，但方法停留在像素编辑层面，可迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRACE: Attribute Execution for Sequential Image Editing

**abstract/A1：** Given an image and successive user instructions, sequential image editing should return an updated image that satisfies each new request and preserves earlier requirements that remain active. This lets users develop a result without repeatedly restating or repairing their decisions. The difficulty is that a retrieved image can contain a valid color on an obsolete surface, while the current image may lack the newly requested material. Neither reference selection nor a precise object mask alone specifies how to reconcile attributes occupying the same region. We present TRACE, which separates the continuing attribute from its visual carrier and prioritizes local appearance operations under complete-request checks. Active-goal binding identifies what should persist, carrier selection supplies suitable current or newly generated pixels, and tonal calibration reduces tinting near highlights and shadows. Experiments across frozen editors, independent visual readers, new source images, and complete sequential benchmarks support joint color-material recovery and show how local execution preserves the surrounding scene.

</details>

<a id="paper-230"></a>

### 230. [Extending Rademacher Complexity-Based Generalization Theory to Domain Generalization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25650v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25650

**作者：** Xi Wang、Liang Bai、Xian Yang、Richard Yi Da Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25650v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25650v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将域泛化的域空间形式化为表示空间上的 Wasserstein 空间，基于 CD(K,∞) 曲率理论导出依赖几何的泛化误差界，分离不变性与曲率两类效应，并用实验考察曲率对域泛化的影响。

**相关性简评：** 其用表示空间曲率与不变性刻画跨分布泛化的思路，可能为分析 LVR 中 latent 状态空间几何结构如何影响推理与长度、任务泛化提供概念工具，但与视觉推理机制的具体联系尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Extending Rademacher Complexity-Based Generalization Theory to Domain Generalization

**abstract/A1：** Domain generalization (DG) aims to train models on multiple source domains with distinct distributions to ensure robust performance on unseen target domains. In doing so, it elevates the concept of generalization from the traditional sample level to the distribution level. Existing theoretical frameworks introduce a domain space endowed with a meta-distribution to extend classical generalization analysis to this new setting. However, these approaches typically treat the domain space as devoid of geometry. In reality, the domain space is an inherently infinite-dimensional, nonlinear abstract space composed of probability distributions, and its complex geometric properties fundamentally determine generalization behavior. Consequently, existing geometry-free theories of domain generalization have a fundamental limitation. They lack a formal characterization of what distinguishes distribution-level generalization from sample-level generalization, leaving the inherent difficulty of generalizing across domains largely unexplained. To bridge this gap, we formalize the domain space as the Wasserstein space over the representation space. Building on the geometric properties of this space (specifically the $\\mathrm{CD}(K,\\infty)$ curvature theory) we derive a novel, geometry-dependent DG error bound. By introducing an additional curvature-dependent confidence term, this bound formally disentangles the two levels of generalization effects and reveals the essential nature that domain generalization is affected by both the invariance and the curvature of the representation space. Finally, we experimentally validate the effect of curvature on domain generalization.

</details>

<a id="paper-231"></a>

### 231. [CompAct-Bench: A Benchmark for Working-Context Compaction in Long-Horizon Agent Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25673v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25673

**作者：** Jiayi Chen、Lizhe Chen、Yujia Chen、Shihan Dou、Ming Zhang、Chendi Ge、Shiguang Ni、Tao Gui、Qi Zhang、Xuanjing Huang、Maxm Pan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25673v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25673v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 CompAct-Bench，在长程 agent 任务中对工作上下文做压缩，让模型在受控保留比例下继续原任务，以检验压缩对下游任务完成的影响，并发现压缩不仅丢失信息，还会扭曲任务状态。

**相关性简评：** 其「状态压缩导致任务状态失真而非仅信息缺失」的观察与受控保留比例评测协议，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态容量、信息坍缩与保留内容的评价提供可核查的类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CompAct-Bench: A Benchmark for Working-Context Compaction in Long-Horizon Agent Tasks

**abstract/A1：** As language-model agents are applied to increasingly extended tasks, their working context continuously accumulates interaction history, tool observations, intermediate decisions, and task progress. Context compaction offers a practical way to reduce this growing history while allowing execution to continue, yet the compaction ability of modern LLMs remains insufficiently understood. We introduce CompAct-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating working-context compaction through its downstream effect on agent task completion. Starting from successful agent trajectories, we insert compaction boundaries at different stages of execution, compress the preceding working context with the model under evaluation, and let the same executor continue the original task. To enable fair comparison across compactors, we evaluate them under controlled retention ratios using a lightweight iterative ratio-adjustment procedure. CompAct-Bench covers information-seeking, software-engineering, and workspace-oriented tasks through BrowseComp, SWE-bench, and an internal CompanyBench evaluation. Our experiments show that working-context compaction remains highly lossy even for frontier models: Claude- and GPT-family models perform strongest overall, yet substantial degradation in downstream task accuracy persists under aggressive compaction. Further analysis reveals that failures are often caused not only by omitted information, but also by distorted task state, such as turning unresolved issues into apparent conclusions or forgetting previously established results, which can lead to incorrect decisions or redundant execution. These findings highlight working-context compaction as a distinct and still challenging ability for LLMs operating in extended agent tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-232"></a>

### 232. [Minimal Memory for Correctable Visual Continuation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25675v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25675

**作者：** Xiyang Zhang、Yuanhe Tian、Hongzhi Wang、Yan Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25675v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25675v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究可修正的视觉延续中，维持可修订场景状态所需的最小记忆，给出稀疏与稠密两种信息论界，并提出按历史存在约束选取单一状态的 Coherent Archive Memory 及相应几何更新与测试。

**相关性简评：** 其「后续修正所需比特下界」与保留候选状态的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的信息充分性、纠正后信息坍缩与视觉证据保留提供概念参照；但对象是几何续帧而非语言推理，可供核查迁移边界。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Minimal Memory for Correctable Visual Continuation

**abstract/A1：** Given past images and known actions, visual continuation generates future frames from one evolving scene state. Keeping this state revisable matters when an object reappears or a later view changes an earlier interpretation. Once past images cannot be replayed, a summary sufficient for current rendering can lose candidates needed after correction. We establish matching sparse and dense bounds on the bits needed for later correction under stable observations and bounded disagreement. For static streams at the stated smoothness and margin scales, correction incurs at least logarithmic overhead in one dimension and polynomial overhead in higher dimensions. Coherent Archive Memory (CAM) stores geometric summaries with exact updates under clipped expansion and coordinate assignments, then uses historical existence constraints to select one state for all requested frames. Exhaustive photographic tests show strict worst-truth image-risk gains from joint witnesses, while controlled neural continuations recover positions hidden in the current view.

</details>

<a id="paper-233"></a>

### 233. [D3-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Models with Dynamic Mode Decomposition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25689v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25689

**作者：** Yanbo Li、An Xiang、Xiaoxin Hu、Feng Wang、Cheng-zhong Xu、Kejiang Ye、Xitong Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25689v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25689v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 D3-Cache：用 Dynamic Mode Decomposition 拟合近期精确 velocity 的短历史，仅预测 scheduler 所需的下一个 velocity；并以「horizon-aware gate」按预测误差决定何时重新调用网络、最多连续跳过多少步，实现免训练、免逐模型标定的扩散采样加速。

**相关性简评：** 虽属图像/视频扩散加速而非 VLM 推理，但其「用历史状态动力学外推下一步、再以误差门控决定重算时机」的机制，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态更新与 STOP/计算分配的停止决策提供可核查的类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** D3-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Models with Dynamic Mode Decomposition

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion Transformers generate high-quality images and videos, but every sampling step requires a full network evaluation. Training-free caching reduces this cost by reusing or forecasting network outputs across steps, but common approaches use per-model calibrated skip criteria or maintain per-layer prediction histories. We present D3-Cache, which applies Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD) to Diffusion sampling: it fits DMD to a short history of exact velocities and forecasts only the velocity that the scheduler consumes. A horizon-aware gate decides when to skip: whenever the network is evaluated, its output is compared with the forecast for that step, and the larger the error, the sooner the network is evaluated again, up to a cap on consecutive skipped steps. D3-Cache requires no training or per-model offline calibration curve and adds negligible memory. It shares one history length and risk tolerance across the four evaluated models. On four state-of-the-art image and video models, D3-Cache achieves speedups of 2.6-3.8x with high fidelity to the base model output.

</details>

<a id="paper-234"></a>

### 234. [Measurable Does Not Imply Transportable: A Prospective Audit of Model Explanations in Multi-Year Field Trials](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25692v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25692

**作者：** Rayhaneh Shabani Nia、Ali Karkehabadi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25692v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25692v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在多年 Genomes-to-Fields 玉米田间试验上形式化「前瞻性解释可迁移性」，考察 16 个环境概念的解释能否在真实未来年份中被复现，并与测量可靠性、上下文内可重复性作对比。

**相关性简评：** 其核心区分「可靠测量与上下文内可重复并不保证解释可迁移」可能为 LVR 中视觉证据归因、latent 相似度等解释量的跨上下文稳定性评价提供概念参照，具体指标能否借用可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Measurable Does Not Imply Transportable: A Prospective Audit of Model Explanations in Multi-Year Field Trials

**abstract/A1：** Model explanations are increasingly used as scientific evidence, yet it remains unclear what they license scientists to conclude beyond the data on which they were constructed. When a fine-grained explanation fails to replicate, the failure may be attributed to noisy measurement of its target, raising a fundamental question: does reliable measurement imply a reproducible explanation? We test this directly. We formalize prospective explanation transportability: the extent to which an explanation learned from past data is recovered in a genuinely future context. This property is distinct from feature importance and measurement reliability. Using multi-year Genomes-to-Fields maize trials, where each new year is a real future context rather than a random resample, we evaluate 16 agronomically interpretable environmental concepts across five forward splits and one prospectively locked test year. Coarse environment-level explanations transport reliably (median cosine 0.83); their locked 2024 value, 0.811, fell within a prespecified 80% bootstrap percentile interval. For grain yield, genotype-conditioned explanations have a historical median cosine of 0.30 and range from 0.006 to 0.822 across years. Reliability does not order these outcomes: the year with the highest split-half reliability had the lowest explanation transport. Within-context resampling showed that GxE explanations were reproducible within a fixed context (0.77-0.95) while exhibiting lower prospective alignment across the 2020-2023 transitions (0.01-0.36). A second phenotype transports strongly at both resolutions, and non-temporal validation inflates apparent interaction-level transport by +0.22. These results show that target reliability and within-context explanation reproducibility do not by themselves establish when a model explanation can support a reproducible scientific claim.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at AISciK @ NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-235"></a>

### 235. [LIFT: Lifting Language-Controlled Image Fusion to Free-Viewpoint 3D Scenes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25696v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25696

**作者：** Jingning Tian、Hao Zhang、Yumin Huang、Zizhuo Li、Xingyu Jiang、Erdun Zhao、Jiayi Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25696v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25696v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 LIFT 框架与「语言控制的 3D 图像融合」任务，用语言指令定位语义目标及其模态偏好并绑定到共享 Gaussian 场景，再以语言与视角为条件做自适应多模态外观混合，并构建多视角语言监督数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其「语言指令→持久语义-空间对应→按需选择模态与区域」的机制，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的定位、筛选与跨视角一致保留提供一种表示层面的参照；但工作本身不做推理与答案生成，可供核查的具体线索有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LIFT: Lifting Language-Controlled Image Fusion to Free-Viewpoint 3D Scenes

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal image fusion traditionally generates a single fused image from paired observations at a specific viewpoint, limiting comprehensive scene perception and adaptability to diverse perceptual objectives. To overcome these limitations, we present LIFT, a novel framework for our proposed language-controlled 3D image fusion (L3D-IF) paradigm that extends multimodal image fusion from 2D images to free-viewpoint 3D scenes, enabling language-guided fusion tailored to diverse perceptual objectives. First, LIFT formulates Semantic Modality Binding (SMB), which interprets language instructions to identify semantic targets and their associated modality preferences and grounds these targets in corresponding regions of a shared Gaussian scene, establishing persistent semantic-to-spatial correspondences for consistent fusion control across viewpoints. Second, Language-Conditioned Gaussian Appearance (LCGA) is developed to achieve scene-wide multimodal appearance modeling by combining adaptive multimodal blending with language- and viewpoint-conditioned appearance refinement, enabling spatially adaptive realization of diverse fusion appearances while preserving source fidelity at pure-modality endpoints. Finally, we construct a Multi-View Language-Supervision (MVLS) dataset that associates language instructions and calibrated viewpoints with modulated fused images, providing effective supervision for learning instruction-dependent fusion from finite multi-view observations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LIFT achieves coherent multimodal fusion across continuous viewpoints, accurate semantic target localization, and consistent regional fusion control under diverse language instructions.

</details>

<a id="paper-236"></a>

### 236. [SkillLens: Action-Guided Dense Visual Dynamics for Proficiency Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25712v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25712

**作者：** Deokhyun Ahn、Hyukjin Kim、Jae-Ho Han、Bumsub Ham、Heeseung Choi、Ig-Jae Kim、Haksub Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25712v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25712v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SkillLens 做人类熟练度估计：不直接把动作语义作为标签监督，而是用原子动作描述作时间引导，在 ego-exo 视频中定位与熟练度相关的窗口，提取密集视觉动态特征，并以同类动作高/低熟练度原型间隔打分，再经 CORAL 序数校准与全局 ego-exo-gaze 基线融合。

**相关性简评：** 用文本动作描述引导视觉窗口选择、再压缩为动态特征的思路，可能为 LVR 中「视觉证据筛选」与语言指导 LOOK 的设计提供参照，但其面向熟练度回归而非 latent 推理状态生成，线索需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SkillLens: Action-Guided Dense Visual Dynamics for Proficiency Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Human proficiency estimation is challenging because skill is a high-level and often subjective property that is not captured by action identity alone. Existing approaches leverage multimodal observations such as ego-exo video, gaze, pose, and inertial signals, but the gap between sensor-level evidence and abstract proficiency labels remains difficult to bridge. We present SkillLens, a proficiency estimation framework that uses atomic action descriptions as temporal guidance, rather than direct semantic supervision, to localize proficiency-relevant windows for dense visual-dynamics extraction. Each window is summarized into an action-dynamics feature and scored by a high-low prototype margin against higher- and lower-proficiency executions from the same activity-action category. Fusing these ego/exo PDM cues with a global ego-exo-gaze baseline through CORAL ordinal calibration, our method achieves 0.542 accuracy on Ego-Exo4D. Our project page is available at https://skill-lens.net/

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables, Project page: https://skill-lens.net/

</details>

<a id="paper-237"></a>

### 237. [What's in the box? Revealing Object Layouts Inside Opaque Boxes via Contactless Vibration Excitation and Sensing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25728v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25728

**作者：** Ethan Turok、Matan Kichler、Shai Bagon、Mark Sheinin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25728v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25728v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 WhisperBox：用扬声器激发不透明箱体振动，以激光散斑网格和相机非接触测量可见壁面振动，学习将其编码为 latent 再解码为箱内物体的 2D 占据布局，并分析哪些频段携带信息、latent 是否保留位置信息及支持算术操作。

**相关性简评：** 从间接外部测量推断隐藏空间状态、并以解码探测 latent 是否保留位置信息，可能与 LVR 中「内部状态是否保留视觉细节、latent 内容可否被读出」的评估思路形成概念类比，可供核查，但领域相距较远、无直接方法迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What's in the box? Revealing Object Layouts Inside Opaque Boxes via Contactless Vibration Excitation and Sensing

**abstract/A1：** Can we reveal what is inside an opaque box by observing its surface vibrations? Objects inside a box alter its mechanical response, providing cues about their hidden locations. We introduce a method that uses these cues to predict a box's 2D occupancy layout entirely remotely. A nearby speaker excites vibrations, which we measure on one visible wall using a grid of laser points and a camera measuring each point's speckle-pattern shifts. Our model, WhisperBox, learns to encode these vibrations into a latent representation and decode it into an occupancy map of the box's contents. The method requires no physical contact to excite or sense vibrations. We demonstrate layout recovery across various boxes and single- and multiple-object scenes, with generalization to unseen object instances, positions, orientations, and weights, as well as changes in laser placement and excitation source. Our analysis reveals that informative frequency bands need not coincide with the box's resonances and that the learned latent space captures object placement and supports arithmetic operations. These results demonstrate the potential of remote vibration sensing to infer spatial layouts hidden from view.

</details>

<a id="paper-238"></a>

### 238. [CGM: Chaos-Guided Graph Attention and Expert Routing for Autism Stereotypy Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25730v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25730

**作者：** Hafsa Aslam、Yue Li、Saba Aslam、Abdur Rasool、Qiao Pan、Muhammad Rizwan、Ovidiu Bagdasar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25730v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25730v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CGM，用逐关节、逐帧的熵型「不规则度」张量驱动骨架图注意力的边权重与四选二专家路由，用于从视频检测自闭症刻板动作。

**相关性简评：** 其按帧不规则度信号调制注意力与专家选择，可能为 LVR 中依据不确定或异常证据分配视觉计算、决定路由与停止提供线索；但该工作无数 latent 推理状态，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CGM: Chaos-Guided Graph Attention and Expert Routing for Autism Stereotypy Detection

**abstract/A1：** Skeleton graph attention for autism video analysis weights joints by content alone, giving an erratic wrist the same standing as a still one. Stereotypy, however, is defined by how unpredictably movement unfolds rather than how much of it occurs: in our cohort, every amplitude-based kinematic descriptor falls in stereotyped clips, while windowed Sample Entropy of angular velocity alone rises (Hedges' g = +1.05). We compute this irregularity as a per-joint, per-frame tensor that structurally drives computation. The proposed chaos-guided graph attention and mixture-of-experts model (CGM) couples two mechanisms. First, a learned pairwise term added to the graph-attention logit concentrates attention on irregular joints and re-ranks the remaining edge weight toward jointly irregular pairs; its unconstrained coefficient neither vanishes nor inverts, and zeroing the tensor realigns attention in 94% of clips. Second, a gate conditioned on the same signal routes each frame to two of four experts, whose weights track frame-level irregularity. Under subject-grouped five-fold cross-validation on 116 clips, CGM reaches 0.930 balanced accuracy, exceeding the graph-attention baseline by +0.187; the component ordering replicates on the public MMASD dataset, and on held-out Panoptic Studio sessions, CGM keeps 0.86 specificity without retraining, against at most 0.35 for chaos-free baselines. In ablation, routing without irregularity yields no reliable gain, whereas chaos modulation alone contributes +0.132. A severity index from the same tensor reaches 0.87 split-half reliability, separates the cohorts with Hedges' g = +1.08, and stays consistent within children across therapy recordings, supporting video-only monitoring of stereotypy during intervention.

</details>

<a id="paper-239"></a>

### 239. [ADAPT: Adaptive Decision and Abstention with Pool-Size-Aware Thresholds for Service Routing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25754v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25754

**作者：** Manile Srun、Muntasir Adnan、Arslan Shaukat、Carlos C. N. Kuhn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25754v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25754v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ADAPT，一种带显式弃权（fallback）选项的轻量服务路由架构：以最高相关度分数与候选分数分布的差距作为路由置信度，并用随候选池大小 K 自适应、无需重训分类器的阈值来纠正固定阈值随 K 增大而失准的问题。

**相关性简评：** 其「分数分布差距作为置信度 + 依候选规模自适应阈值」的弃权机制，可能为 LVR 中的 STOP 决策与停止阈值标定（何时证据已足够、何时弃权）提供可供核查的概念线索，但该置信度定义能否迁移到 latent 推理状态尚不清楚。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ADAPT: Adaptive Decision and Abstention with Pool-Size-Aware Thresholds for Service Routing
