**abstract/A1：** Retrieval-based classifiers efficiently route queries to specialised services, but they typically select a candidate even when no suitable service exists. We introduce Adaptive Decision and Abstention with Pool-Size-Aware Thresholds for Service Routing (ADAPT), a lightweight routing architecture with an explicit fallback option that enables queries to abstain from specialist selection. ADAPT uses the separation between the highest relevance score and the candidate score distribution as routing confidence. Experiments across retrieval models and increasing numbers of services reveal a consistent empirical behaviour: the confidence distribution changes systematically with candidate-pool size K, making fixed abstention thresholds increasingly miscalibrated. ADAPT addresses this effect with a K-aware threshold that adapts as services are added without classifier retraining. Evaluation across two specialist datasets shows that this simple mechanism provides an effective accuracy-cost trade-off compared with generative LLM routing, supporting lightweight and dynamically extensible service-routing architectures.

</details>

<a id="paper-240"></a>

### 240. [Driver Gaze Target Identification via Gaze Estimation and Structured Driving Scene Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25771v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25771

**作者：** Xinze Cao、Ke Wang、Deyong Guan、Yuanquan Li、Ke Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25771v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25771v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出模块化驾驶员注视目标识别框架：以 MAE 预训练 ViT 加 FiLM 头姿条件化估计注视方向，借全景场景理解构建结构化候选集，经个体化多项式岭回归映射到场景坐标，再由熵驱动融合边界几何与类级语义先验定位目标。

**相关性简评：** 其结构化候选集与熵驱动几何/语义融合，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选和「该看哪里」的获取决策提供线索，但整体属驾驶感知应用，未涉及推理期 latent 状态，联系需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Driver Gaze Target Identification via Gaze Estimation and Structured Driving Scene Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Identifying a driver's gaze target provides object-level information for visual attention monitoring in intelligent vehicles. Gaze-zone classification and continuous attention localization provide limited information about the identity of the attended scene target. This paper proposes a modular framework that identifies object- or region-level gaze targets from synchronized in-cabin driver images and forward-view road images. The framework comprises four components: MFGaze, which combines a Vision Transformer pretrained with a masked autoencoder (MAE) and head-pose conditioning through feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM); panoptic scene understanding, which constructs a structured candidate set; polynomial ridge regression, which maps gaze features to 2D scene-image coordinates through individualized calibration; and entropy-driven target inference, which adaptively fuses boundary-distance geometry with class-level semantic priors. Experiments evaluate the framework using three public gaze datasets, a laboratory test using Cityscapes road scenes and self-collected gaze data, and the Look Both Ways (LBW) dataset. The complete framework achieves an overall Top-1 gaze target identification accuracy of 96.27% in the laboratory experiment. After sequence-specific calibration, it achieves Top-1 accuracies of 79.92% for task-relevant objects and 85.58% for the road class on LBW. MFGaze achieves the lowest mean angular errors among the compared methods, with 4.06$^\\circ$, 4.23$^\\circ$, and 10.52$^\\circ$ on MPIIFaceGaze, ETH-XGaze, and Gaze360, respectively, and demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization. Sensitivity analyses show stable performance under hyperparameter perturbations. These results support the framework as a source of object-level gaze information for driver monitoring.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-241"></a>

### 241. [How Reliable Are Explanations of Language Models That Detect Insider Threats?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25796v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25796

**作者：** Suramya R. Angdembay

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25796v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25796v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文审计基于语言模型的内部威胁检测器的解释可靠性：在模型内部移除被指认的神经元特征，并与「尽量相似但未真正移除」的对照比较，提出 tipping radius 来量化这一差异对相似性度量选择有多脆弱，并在 CERT 模拟办公日志上的七个模型、34 次运行中检验；还发现告警分数部分反映「是谁」而非「做了什么」。

**相关性简评：** 其「移除 vs 匹配对照 + 度量脆弱半径」的审计思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容对答案的因果贡献是否只是归因度量的人为产物，与贡献分配和因果作用评估有概念联系；但对象为日志异常检测而非视觉推理，仅作启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Reliable Are Explanations of Language Models That Detect Insider Threats?

**abstract/A1：** Organizations monitor their workers' activity for insider threats, in which workers misuse their legitimate access, by copying confidential files before leaving. Detectors score how unusual each day of activity is, flagging the most unusual for an analyst to review. Prominent current approaches use opaque neural networks, so explanations of a flag point to internal features: patterns of neuron activity most active on days of misuse. We audit such explanations for language-model detectors trained on CERT's simulated workplace logs. To test an explanation, we see what happens to the detector's score when we remove its features inside the model. We compare this to a control: a change that is as similar as possible to the removal, but without actually removing the features. Because defining "similar" requires choosing a metric, our central contribution is the tipping radius: a first-order prediction of how far that metric must move before the difference between removal and control disappears, a measure of how fragile the result is to that choice. In 33 of 34 runs of seven language models, the tipping radius is at most 18% of the distance between two standard choices of metric. Moving that far removes at least 58% of the difference or reverses it, while random moves of the same size leave it largely intact. The result replicates in thirteen of fourteen pre-declared primary runs on openly released models, though on our trained detectors the difference is often too small to measure. We also find that a flag's score partly reflects who the worker is, not what they did, and that the features named depend on whom they are compared against. Audits should therefore fix the control's metric in advance, report its tipping radius, and disclose that comparison.

**comment/C1：** 34 pages, 9 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-242"></a>

### 242. [What Places an Action Boundary? Structure-Aware Attribution for Temporal Action Localization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25815v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25815

**作者：** Ruoshan Lan、Zongru Shao、Chieh Tsai、Mihai Surdeanu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25815v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25815v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出结构感知归因框架，按 Shapley-Shubik 原则在随机证据恢复顺序上平均贡献，并以感受野与软梯度路径降低计算量，用于分析时序动作定位中边界由哪些连续证据决定。

**相关性简评：** 其对连续时序证据的归因与忠实性评价（含边界位移方向）可能为 latent 推理中的贡献分配、视觉证据筛选及停止/边界判断提供可核查的度量线索，但领域与 VLM 推理相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Places an Action Boundary? Structure-Aware Attribution for Temporal Action Localization

**abstract/A1：** Temporal action localization (TAL) detectors must identify actions and estimate their temporal boundaries, but localization remains a major source of detection errors. While understanding what determines boundary placement requires faithful attribution of temporal evidence, existing attribution methods have not sufficiently addressed the temporal structure of TAL or the need to tailor attribution to boundary localization. We introduce a structure-aware attribution framework that averages contributions across random evidence-restoration orders following the Shapley-Shubik principle. We make it tractable by using the detector's receptive field to limit which time steps are ordered individually, and by approximating each order with a soft gradient path. For boundary predictions, we further introduce cell-structured restoration orders and boundary-anchored references that selectively remove adjacent evidence. We also adapt faithfulness evaluation to assess the joint effects of contiguous temporal evidence and the direction of boundary displacement. Experiments on THUMOS14 and EPIC-KITCHENS-100 with TriDet and ActionFormer demonstrate improved attribution faithfulness over existing methods. Attribution analysis and controlled interventions further reveal that class-score changes do not reliably track boundary displacement, providing input-level evidence that classification relies on content less informative about boundary placement. Code and evaluation artifacts will be publicly released.

</details>

<a id="paper-243"></a>

### 243. [Limits of local curvature for constructing search subspaces in segmentation models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25822v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25822

**作者：** Maxim Bolshim、Alexander Kugaevskikh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25822v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25822v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在语义分割模型上检验由局部曲率（广义 Gauss-Newton 方向）构造的低维搜索子空间能否代表有限步后梯度更新场的变化，用 50 张图像、两个模型比较等秩子空间对局部响应与场变化的表示误差。

**相关性简评：** 其「低维子空间能否忠实表示状态变化」的度量思路，可能为核查 LVR 中低秩 latent 状态或缓存是否保留了任务必要信息提供评价参照，迁移可行性尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Limits of local curvature for constructing search subspaces in segmentation models

**abstract/A1：** We examine how well subspaces constructed from local curvature represent the change in the gradient update field after a finite step in semantic segmentation models. The local response is the action of the curvature operator on the actual input displacement. In a study of 50 images and two models, we measure the ratio of the loss increase attained in a fixed subspace to that attained in the full space. This ratio decreases as the search radius increases. On ten images from the same sample, we compare the one-dimensional subspace spanned by the initial gradient with two rank-16 subspaces for both models. The two equal-rank subspaces are assessed at the same initial states, using the same actual steps and measured vectors. Adding generalised Gauss-Newton directions to the initial gradient reduces the mean relative error in representing the field change from 0.922 to 0.874 for the first model and from 0.819 to 0.763 for the second. At the same rank, including the response direction for the initial gradient nearly eliminates the error in representing the local response. The mean error in representing the field change does not decrease. A decomposition with fixed pixel weights shows that the finite-step remainder is comparable in norm to the field change and makes the largest diagonal contribution to the squared projection error of the field after the step. Evaluation of a search subspace should measure how accurately it represents the field change and the local response.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables. Includes an appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-244"></a>

### 244. [TrajCal: Trajectory-Calibrated Time Schedules for Few-Step Flow Sampling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25847v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25847

**作者：** Junchi Zhang、Zihan Gu、Jiangcan Jia、Lulu Zhao、Wenbo Su、Hua Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25847v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25847v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** TrajCal 提出免训练的 few-step flow sampling 校准框架：以 scatter search 从多样种子调度出发变形、组合候选，再按最终输出与多步参考的保真度选出一个固定时间调度复用于新输入，在图像与视频生成上以较低评估预算取得更高参考保真度。

**相关性简评：** 其以「与多步参考的保真度」搜索步预算与时间调度的做法，可能为 latent 推理中离散步数分配和以全计算参考评价轨迹提供类比线索；但调度固定、面向图像生成，是否可迁移到 THINK/STOP 或动态计算仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TrajCal: Trajectory-Calibrated Time Schedules for Few-Step Flow Sampling

**abstract/A1：** Flow matching enables high-quality image and video generation, but its iterative sampling process requires repeated evaluations of large neural networks. Existing few-step methods can distort the output and reduce its agreement with a many-step reference. We propose TrajCal, a training-free calibration framework for few-step sampling. Based on scatter search, a well-established metaheuristic for difficult optimization problems, TrajCal starts with diverse seed schedules, deforms and combines these candidates, and selects one fixed schedule by final output fidelity for reuse on new inputs. For both image and video generation, TrajCal achieves higher reference fidelity than all compared baselines at low evaluation budgets while maintaining competitive visual quality. The search is effective even with randomly generated seeds, while trajectory-based seeds further improve fidelity. For cross-style generalization, photography-calibrated schedules also improve fidelity on unseen image styles.

</details>

<a id="paper-245"></a>

### 245. [Interpretable Prototype-Conditioned Adaptation for Weather Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25848v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25848

**作者：** Zhixuan Li、Hao Jiang、Chen Hui、Kai Geng、Weisi Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25848v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25848v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向数据驱动天气预报，提出 ProtoCast：用分组原型库结合历史特征与时间变化做演化感知匹配，并以稀疏低秩算子和门控注意力进行局部修正，同时通过 slot tracking 与原型删除分析预测误差来源。

**相关性简评：** 其「原型作为显式模式参照 + 原型删除归因」可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容对答案的贡献分配与因果作用诊断，但领域与推理任务相距较远，需较多迁移判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpretable Prototype-Conditioned Adaptation for Weather Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Data-driven weather forecasting requires distinguishing local states and their temporal evolution. However, learned patterns remain implicit in neural forecasters, making their influence difficult to interpret. Failing to distinguish local evolution can lead to similar forecasts for locations that develop differently, increasing prediction error. This motivates explicit references for distinguishing and interpreting local patterns. Prototypes provide explicit pattern references, but sample- or task-level conditioning leaves open how to adapt to distinct locations and variable groups. To deal with this problem, we design ProtoCast, which uses group-specific prototype banks to adapt local features while accounting for cross-variable dependencies. Specifically, (1) we combine each group's historical features and temporal change with forecast context from cross-variable attention for evolution-aware prototype matching. (2) Sparse mixtures of prototype-specific low-rank operators tailor corrections to each group and location. A gated attention block then exchanges the corrected features across groups before prediction. Each group's correction decomposes into explicit prototype contributions before this fusion. (3) We jointly train the forecasting network and operators from scratch and refit each group's prototypes before every training epoch. Slot tracking preserves prototype-operator associations, making each prototype's contribution to local corrections more interpretable. Experiments on GlobalForecast and TyphoonTracking show improved forecasting accuracy, while prototype deletion links local feature contributions to changes in prediction error. This work advances the development of more accurate and interpretable data-driven weather forecasting models. Project page: https://zhixuanli.github.io/projects/li2026protocast

**comment/C1：** 38 pages, including supplementary material. Project page: https://zhixuanli.github.io/projects/li2026protocast

</details>

<a id="paper-246"></a>

### 246. [Meta-RTL: Evolving Agent Harnesses for RTL Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25891v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25891

**作者：** Xinzhuang Xiong、Yonghao Wang、Shengyao Sun、Chengxi Li、Evangeline Young

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.AR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25891v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25891v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Meta-RTL，在优化模型、EDA 流程与单设计预算固定下，从最小循环出发用 EDA 反馈自动演化 RTL 优化 agent 的执行搜索流程，并报告迁移效果与搜索机制消融。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉和 latent 表示无关，但其「演化推理时控制循环＋固定预算」及对搜索机制的消融归因，可能为 LVR 中动态计算、停止决策与轨迹贡献分配提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Meta-RTL: Evolving Agent Harnesses for RTL Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Large language models enable agents to optimize RTL using EDA feedback, yet their executable search procedures remain largely hand- engineered. We present Meta-RTL, an EDA-grounded framework that evolves RTL optimization harnesses from a minimal loop while keeping the optimization model, EDA flow, and per-design budgets fixed. Compared with Dr. RTL, Meta-RTL\* reduces aggregate WNS/TNS violations by 7.8%/11.0% across 20 development designs, with area reductions on 70% of them. Without further evolution, Meta-RTL\* transfers to 10 unseen IC-RTL designs, reducing aggregate WNS/TNS violations by 20.5%/14.0% and total synthesized area by 12.6%. We further characterize three search mechanisms in the evolved harness and assess their contributions through ablations and a case study.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-247"></a>

### 247. [TwinD: Generalizing Deepfake Detection from Blending to Entire Face Synthesis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25935v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25935

**作者：** Andrii Yermakov、Jan Cech、Mario Fritz、Jiri Matas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25935v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25935v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出现有面部 deepfake 检测器在整脸合成（EFS）数据上泛化严重不足，提出 TwinD：仅用 FaceForensics++ 假样本与两类「孪生」样本（自编码器重建后回填真实像素、以及真实帧与其篡改副本混合）训练，以捕捉解码器痕迹与混合痕迹，在 19 个数据集上取得明显提升。

**相关性简评：** 该工作不涉及 latent 推理，但「用重建残差与合成痕迹判断视觉内容真伪」可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与可靠性判定提供线索，可供核查其线索是否可作为推理前的证据过滤信号。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TwinD: Generalizing Deepfake Detection from Blending to Entire Face Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** Facial deepfake detectors are usually trained on blending fakes, where a generated face is pasted back into a real frame. Recent generators synthesize the entire face and leave no traces of blending. On a benchmark of 19 datasets released from 2019 to 2026, the best prior facial deepfake detector reaches a mean AUROC of 96.2% on ten blending datasets, but none exceeds 78.1% on five datasets of entire face synthesis (EFS). AI-generated content (AIGC) detectors do not close this gap. We propose TwinD, a detector trained on FaceForensics++ fakes and two kinds of twins: fakes made from a real frame. A reconstruction twin is a real frame reconstructed by an autoencoder, with the real pixels put back in a random region. It targets the neural traces of a decoder. A blob twin blends an altered copy of a real frame with the frame itself, targeting blending artifacts. It needs no generator and no face-shaped mask. Together, these twins provide two complementary forensic cues that generalize to unseen manipulations. TwinDreaches a mean AUROC of 96.3%, 8.9 points above the prior state-of-the-art, with 98.1% on the blending datasets and 95.6% on the EFS datasets. Trained on twins alone, without a deepfake dataset, single models still reach 94.3% on average over three seeds. For reproducibility, the code, trained model, and training data are at: https://github.com/yermandy/TwinD

</details>

<a id="paper-248"></a>

### 248. [Gradient Boosting with Kolmogorov-Arnold Units for Interpretable Regression beyond Pairwise Interactions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25942v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25942

**作者：** Minjong Cheon、Hanseon Joo、Eunji Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25942v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25942v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MJBoost：以单个 Kolmogorov-Arnold 单元 g(Σφ\_p(x\_p)) 为弱学习器的梯度提升方法，用带惩罚样条与交替最小二乘闭式拟合，并给出可精确计算的函数 ANOVA 分解（主效应、成对效应与高阶余项），在合成长阶交互目标与 OpenML-CTR23 表格回归上优于 EBM。

**相关性简评：** 表格回归与视觉/多模态推理无关，但其按阶数把模型输出方差拆成主效应、成对与高阶余项的做法，可能可供核查作为量化 latent 推理状态中各来源贡献及高阶交互份额的分解工具。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Gradient Boosting with Kolmogorov-Arnold Units for Interpretable Regression beyond Pairwise Interactions

**abstract/A1：** Interpretable models for tabular regression, such as the explainable boosting machine (EBM), restrict a prediction to main effects and pairwise interactions so that every part of it can be plotted. When the target depends on three or more inputs jointly, this restriction costs accuracy, and a black-box model with post hoc explanations becomes the usual fallback. Here we present MJBoost, a gradient boosting method whose weak learner is a single Kolmogorov-Arnold unit $g(\\sum\_p φ\_p(x\_p))$ with penalized spline inner and outer functions. Each unit is fitted in closed form by alternating penalized least squares, starting from principal Hessian directions of the residual, and categorical and missing inputs enter the units directly. Because a unit depends on each input only through one inner function, the fitted model has an exact functional ANOVA decomposition into main effects, pair effects and a higher-order remainder, which one-dimensional convolutions compute. On synthetic targets of interaction order three to six, the test error of MJBoost is up to 13 times lower than that of EBM, and its decomposition recovers the true variance share of every order to within 0.04. On the 35 regression tasks of the OpenML-CTR23 benchmark, it has a lower test RMSE than EBM on 21 tasks and a higher one on 2 (geometric-mean RMSE -29.1%). On these tasks the main and pair effects of the fitted model explain a median 0.992 of its variance, so the model reads as plots where the data allow, and the decomposition measures the higher-order part where they do not. With Newton steps the same algorithm classifies: on 32 binary tasks of OpenML-CC18 its test log-loss is on par with that of EBM, and on 11 multiclass tasks its mean log-loss is lower than that of EBM on 8 and than that of tuned LightGBM on 7.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-249"></a>

### 249. [Sample-efficient end-to-end Optical Music Recognition through Active Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25975v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25975

**作者：** Eric Ayllon、Francisco J. Castellanos、Antonio Rios-Vila

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25975v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25975v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 Active Learning 引入端到端光学乐谱识别，在低资源标注预算下比较基于不确定性与多样性的采样策略（staff-level 与 full-page 两种语料），发现基于置信度的采样效果接近依据真实错误进行的 oracle 选择，且不同场景适合不同策略。

**相关性简评：** 其按不确定性/多样性挑选信息量样本的思路，可能为 LVR 与 VLM 推理训练中「哪些视觉证据或样本值得标注与监督」以及 LOOK 式证据筛选提供可核查的线索，但它不涉及推理期 latent 状态生成或停止决策，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sample-efficient end-to-end Optical Music Recognition through Active Learning

**abstract/A1：** End-to-end approaches to Optical Music Recognition (OMR) have recently shown promising results, but their performance often degrades on unseen data. A common solution is to pretrain and fine-tune models with annotated samples from the target domain, improving adaptation to new distributions. However, this process typically requires extensive manual annotation to yield competitive models, making it costly and time-consuming. In this work, we explore Active Learning to reduce this burden by selecting informative samples for labeling in low-resource scenarios, where the amount of manual annotation that can be performed is limited. We evaluate both uncertainty- and diversity-based sampling strategies on staff-level and full-page corpora. Results show that confidence-based sampling performs comparably to oracle selection (based on true error), and that each scenario benefits from a tailored strategy.

</details>

<a id="paper-250"></a>

### 250. [AI agents can redesign engineering parts with more than a thousand CAD parameters and nonlinear simulations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26003v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26003

**作者：** Alexandra Starostina、Alexander Hermann、Mahish Guru、Kartik Bali、Roland Aydin、Christian C. Cyron

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26003v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26003v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作让通用 LLM 智能体在约 1650 个 CAD 参数的空间中、仅 15 次非线性有限元仿真的预算内反复提出并修正零件设计，并将每位点的完整仿真结果反馈给模型。

**相关性简评：** 「昂贵验证下的少量迭代＋全局重设计而非局部微调＋预算内停止」或可启发 LVR 中推理状态更新、证据反馈与 STOP 决策的表述，但属工程设计远领域，需核查其状态如何随反馈改写。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AI agents can redesign engineering parts with more than a thousand CAD parameters and nonlinear simulations

**abstract/A1：** Simulation-driven design of real engineering components has to meet three conditions at once. It runs on few solves, since a nonlinear finite-element simulation of a 3D part takes hours; its design space has many values, the hundreds to thousands of values of the part's CAD model; and a better design often needs a redesign, coordinated changes of the part's proportions rather than local polishing of its surface. Automated optimisers meet at most two of these conditions. Classical optimisers, Bayesian or derivative-free, need a person to cut the design down to a few parameters, and on all the values they need far more solves; industrial shape optimisers move every surface node of the mesh in one solve per cycle, but only in small steps that polish rather than redesign. We ask whether a general-purpose large language model (LLM) can meet all three by joining what it knows with what the simulation shows. GPT-6 Astra edits a magnesium stent through a CAD geometry parametrisation of about 1,650 values, with a budget of 15 simulations; every proposal is built in CAD, meshed and simulated with large-deformation plasticity and contact, and the LLM reads the full results of every solve. Within that budget it lowered the objective, a high percentile of the strains in the stent's bends, by 32%; on the same budget, the industrial shape optimiser Tosca improved the original design by a tenth and then stopped improving. An earlier GPT-6 Astra design, 25% lower in the same objective, was checked in an independent, higher-fidelity model of the full deployment with crimping and damage: it was 14% less strained than the original after the full chain, and its most damaged spot began to fail later and carried 63% less damage at full expansion. LLMs can thus join their learned knowledge with simulation data to adjust more than a thousand design values and reach valid designs with real improvements.

</details>

<a id="paper-251"></a>

### 251. [When Agents Play Differently: Localizing Human--Agent Behavioral Divergence with Conditional Distributional Testing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26012v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26012

**作者：** Oleg Smirnov、John Pertoft、Sara Karimi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26012v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26012v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出分层条件分布检验框架，在上下文单元内计算非参数差异统计并挖掘子群规则，用于定位智能体与人类行为分歧的位置；在三消游戏中发现会话级输赢指标几乎无额外信号，而过程级指标能揭示特定模式下的真实差异。

**相关性简评：** 其「按情境分层、定位差异子群」的检验思路可能可供核查，用于判断 latent 推理步骤（THINK/LOOK/STOP）在哪些情境下带来真实收益、哪些只体现在均值层面；但该工作不涉及视觉推理或内部状态，指标需自行改造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Agents Play Differently: Localizing Human--Agent Behavioral Divergence with Conditional Distributional Testing

**abstract/A1：** Evaluating whether artificial agents behave like humans requires comparing not just average outcomes but entire behavioral distributions across diverse contexts. We propose a stratified framework that computes nonparametric discrepancy statistics within contextual cells, and discovers subgroup rules identifying where agent behavior departs from human play. We show that session-level metrics dominated by win/loss outcomes offer little signal beyond mean comparisons, whereas process-level metrics such as move efficiency reveal genuine behavioral differences. Applied to a commercial match-3 game, it uncovers patterns invisible to aggregate metrics: a playtesting bot is diverging substantially from humans in certain game modes, while showing no detectable divergence in others. We release our implementation as open source to support reproducibility and adoption.

</details>

<a id="paper-252"></a>

### 252. [The Delayed Societal Cost of Unfairness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26030v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26030

**作者：** Giovani Valdrighi、Isabel Valera、Marcos Medeiros Raimundo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26030v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26030v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究二分类决策下人群资格分数随决策及其结果演化时，静态数据上 Pareto 最优的策略为何可能在部署后诱导的人群上被更公平策略反超，并分析 Rawlsian 与 egalitarian 公平约束下决策者损失与群体成本的延迟效应。

**相关性简评：** 其「在被策略自身诱导的分布上重新评估、静态权衡可能反转」的论点，可能可供核查 LVR 中自生成 latent 状态与停止决策造成评估分布偏移时的长期成本核算问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Delayed Societal Cost of Unfairness

**abstract/A1：** Fair machine learning typically evaluates the societal costs of a decision policy -- the loss incurred by the decision-maker (DM) and the unfair distribution of costs among demographic groups of decision-subjects (DS) -- on static data, where reducing one often comes at the expense of the other. Yet decisions change the populations on which they act, and prior work has shown that fairness interventions designed on static data may fail to benefit, or even harm, the disadvantaged group over time. We investigate a complementary question: When can a policy that is Pareto-optimal on pre-deployment data be outperformed by a fairer policy once each is evaluated on the population it induces, reducing costs for one stakeholder without increasing them for the other? In a setting where qualification scores evolve in response to binary decisions and their outcomes, we show that under Rawlsian fairness, which never lowers any group's acceptance rate, placing sufficiently high weight on fairness reduces societal costs whenever accepting disadvantaged DS at the DM's break-even point leaves them in a state less costly to the DM than rejecting them. Under egalitarian fairness, which may lower the advantaged group's acceptance rate, this additionally requires the reduction in DM loss on the disadvantaged group to outweigh the increase on the advantaged group. Under both fairness notions, however, when DM loss is accuracy-based, improvements in the post-deployment population may arise from declining DS qualification, revealing that gains for the DM do not necessarily translate into better outcomes for DS. Our results show that policy-induced population dynamics can overturn apparent performance-fairness trade-offs, allowing fairer policies to reduce the delayed societal costs of decision-making.

**comment/C1：** Preprint, under review

</details>

<a id="paper-253"></a>

### 253. [SEOYUN: Refusal-Inducing Exam Documents for AI-Glasses Pipelines](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26033v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26033

**作者：** Dohyeon Kwon

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26033v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26033v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SEOYUN 框架，探讨能否通过设计「诱导拒答」的考试文档，降低 AI 眼镜拍摄后 VLM 作答的可用性，给出威胁模型、分析性界限，以及区分数字、模拟与物理输入的评测协议，本版本尚未报告任何模型效果测量。

**相关性简评：** 其 encoder 级视觉干预与「拒答不等于答案泄漏减少」的论证，可能可供核查 VLM 输出是否真依赖视觉 latent 内容及如何做因果性评测；但仅有框架、无实证，联系尚属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SEOYUN: Refusal-Inducing Exam Documents for AI-Glasses Pipelines

**abstract/A1：** Camera-equipped glasses can convert an examination page into a query to a vision-language model (VLM). Device detection addresses possession; a complementary question is whether an examination document can reduce the usefulness of an unauthorized capture. We present SEOYUN, an application-specific framework for refusal-inducing exam documents, with optical and encoder-level interventions as extensions. We use reverse jailbreaking to describe the direction of the objective: inducing abstention rather than bypassing refusal. Refusal induction and defensive visual prompt injection already have precedents; our contribution is a threat model, analytical limits, and an evaluation protocol for examination use. We distinguish refusal from correct-answer leakage, show why removing an added dummy option is not itself a reduction in the original answer space, and derive a sampling comparison under explicitly stated camera assumptions. The protocol separates digital, simulated, and physical inputs and requires held-out evaluation after subject-specific selection. A local CSAT candidate corpus and rendering harness have been prepared, but this version reports no model-performance measurements, physical validation, or human-subject results. Accordingly, SEOYUN is a research framework, not a validated defense or a basis for allegations against individuals.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages; research framework and analytical limits; no empirical efficacy results. Includes an author reflection in Korean in Appendix C

</details>

<a id="paper-254"></a>

### 254. [Streaming-AAA: Streaming AAA Game Video Generation at High Resolution, High Frame Rate, and Low Latency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26040v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26040

**作者：** Zhihao Hu、Jianqi Ma、Jun Zhang、Jiayang Bai、Haifeng Shi、Yanxiang Zhuang、Zhongqian Sun、Weidong Zhang、Wei Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26040v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26040v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Streaming-AAA，通过重设计高分辨率 VAE 解码、以 diffusion forcing 延长时序训练窗口，并结合后训练蒸馏与记忆压缩，实现 1080p、43 FPS、约 198 ms 延迟的可控 AAA 游戏视频流式长时生成。

**相关性简评：** 其长时程「持续记忆」与记忆压缩、流式状态维护机制，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态的长期保持与缓存压缩问题；但研究对象是像素级视频生成而非推理状态，联系需较多推断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Streaming-AAA: Streaming AAA Game Video Generation at High Resolution, High Frame Rate, and Low Latency

**abstract/A1：** Controllable streaming video generation offers a promising route toward interactive game simulation, yet existing methods remain far from practical gameplay. They often fail to jointly achieve high visual fidelity, high frame rates, and low interaction latency, resulting in poor responsiveness and degraded user experience. We present Streaming-AAA, a new paradigm for controllable infinite generation of AAA-quality game videos. Our system generates 1080p gameplay videos at 43 FPS with 198 ms end-to-end latency interaction on merely NVIDIA A800 GPUs, while supporting long-horizon play without severe temporal degradation or collapse. Streaming-AAA is built upon three key designs. First, we redesign the VAE architecture for efficient high-resolution decoding, enabling real-time synthesis at 1080p. Second, we pretrain the diffusion model using diffusion forcing and significantly extend the temporal training horizon, allowing the model to capture long-term dependencies and maintain persistent memory. Third, we perform post-training distillation and memory compression to improve inference efficiency while retaining visual quality and temporal consistency. Experiments show that Streaming-AAA substantially improves visual fidelity, frame rate, latency, and long-term stability over prior controllable video generation approaches, enabling a significantly more playable experience for interactive AAA game video generation.

</details>

<a id="paper-255"></a>

### 255. [Less Than Meets the Eye: Action-Set Identifiability and Principled Abstention for 3D Physical Geometry](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26056v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26056

**作者：** Reshad Ul Karim、Abrar Samin、Sammam Mahdi、Syeda Maliha Tabassum、Md Rashad Al Hasan Rony

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26056v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26056v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Intervene3D：给定图像与初始几何估计，预测各假设（走廊、镜面、显示图像）在候选相机运动下的视图，按 maximin 可分性选择运动，仅在剩余假设可被区分时报告机制与首个物理表面，否则弃权，并附加拒绝无假设可解释观测的充分性检验；在合成与真实显示基准上评估。

**相关性简评：** 「选动作取证据—检验证据是否足够—不可判定则弃权」的回路，可为 LVR 中何时继续获取视觉证据（LOOK）与何时停止作答（STOP）的问题表述和评价提供参照，但对象是相机运动与 3D 表面假设，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Less Than Meets the Eye: Action-Set Identifiability and Principled Abstention for 3D Physical Geometry

**abstract/A1：** A corridor, its mirror reflection, and its image on a display can look identical, yet their physical surfaces lie at different depths. We test whether bounded camera motions distinguish these explanations. Intervene3D takes an image and an initial geometry estimate, predicts the view under each hypothesis and candidate motion, and selects a motion by maximin separability. It reports the mechanism and first physical surface only if the available motions separate the hypotheses still in contention. Otherwise it abstains. An adequacy test also rejects observations that no hypothesis explains. On a matched synthetic benchmark with exact landmarks, disabling abstention gives confident answers on 76.4% of unresolvable scenes. With abstention, none receives a confident answer under exact or noisy landmarks. At 100% accuracy on answered scenes, maximin selection answers 61.0% of scenes and summed selection 43.1%. The adequacy test lowers wrong answers on strongly curved mirror scenes from 56.8% to 12.4%. Real display experiments compare two hypotheses using one frozen depth-network estimate. The second image supplies photometric evidence and is never passed through the depth network. One executed motion reduces the pooled median surface error of twelve networks by 31.5%. These networks have larger error inside the annotated panel than outside on 33.4% of illusion-benchmark images. They place the first physical surface behind the glass on 36.9% of Mirror3D frames.

</details>

<a id="paper-256"></a>

### 256. [Domain-Aware Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy Spacecraft Pose Estimation under Sim-to-Real Shift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26065v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26065

**作者：** Nidhal Eddine Chenni、Arunkumar Rathinam、Djamila Aouada

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26065v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26065v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 SPEED+ 航天器位姿估计上考察 conformal prediction 在 sim-to-real 分布偏移下的失效，观察到合成图校准得到的位姿集合在真实图像上失去覆盖率保证且大量失败是「静默」的，并提出先用外观转换（保持几何）后的合成图做校准，从而在无需真实标注的前提下恢复覆盖率、显著降低静默失败比例。

**相关性简评：** 视觉估计在分布偏移下的覆盖率保证与「静默失败」判定，可能为 LVR 中依据视觉证据决定何时信任状态或停止推理（STOP）提供评价与校准思路，但该工作面向位姿回归、不涉及 latent 推理，迁移可行性需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Domain-Aware Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy Spacecraft Pose Estimation under Sim-to-Real Shift

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations require not only an accurate estimate of the pose of the target spacecraft, but also a reliable indication of when this estimate can be trusted. Conformal prediction provides such an indication with a formal guarantee, by producing pose uncertainty sets that contain the true pose with a specified probability. Such a guarantee, however, assumes that calibration and test data follow the same distribution, an assumption that is violated in orbital perception, where labelled calibration images are available only from synthetic renderings while operational images are real. In this work, we show on the SPEED+ dataset that conformal pose estimation calibrated on synthetic images loses its coverage guarantee on real images, and that many of the resulting failures are silent: the predicted pose set appears valid but excludes the true pose. To address this, we calibrate on synthetic images translated to the appearance of real images. Since translation alters appearance while preserving geometry, the synthetic pose labels remain valid and no labelled real images are required. Experiments with two translation models show that this calibration restores coverage above the specified level for miscoverage levels of 0.1 and above, and reduces silent failures from up to 43.5% to at most 3.3% of the test images, bringing conformal pose estimation a step closer to trustworthy deployment in space.

</details>

<a id="paper-257"></a>

### 257. [How Network Topology and Data Heterogeneity Shape Decentralized Learning: A Spectral Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26104v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26104

**作者：** Luigi Palmieri、Lorenzo Valerio、Chiara Boldrini、Andrea Passarella、Marco Conti、János Kertész

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.SI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26104v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26104v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建谱分析框架研究去中心化联邦学习：将聚合算子分解为主导特征模态（决定共识轨迹与节点影响力，均匀平均下稳态影响正比于「节点度+1」）与非主导模态（刻画分歧的传播与衰减），并结合数据异构性分析拓扑规模与连通度如何改变局部信息的相对贡献。

**相关性简评：** 其按特征模态区分「共识收敛」与「分歧衰减」的谱刻画，可能为 latent 推理中跨迭代或跨层的状态混合、信息坍缩与证据权重分配提供可核查的分析工具，但研究对象是去中心化 FL 聚合，与 VLM 推理仅有间接概念联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Network Topology and Data Heterogeneity Shape Decentralized Learning: A Spectral Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Network topology influences decentralized federated learning through information mixing and the relative contribution of individual nodes. While existing analyses primarily focus on spectral mixing properties, they often assume doubly stochastic aggregation, overlooking topology-dependent influence under irregular communication networks. We develop a spectral framework that separates two complementary mechanisms: the dominant eigenmode determines the consensus trajectory and node influence, while non-dominant eigenmodes govern the propagation and attenuation of disagreement. Under uniform neighborhood averaging, we show that the stationary influence is proportional to node degree plus one and derive a bound relating model disagreement to spectral mixing and gradient heterogeneity. We then investigate how these mechanisms interact with the data distribution across nodes. Experiments across regular and heterogeneous network topologies reveal nearly identical learning dynamics under IID data, while network size and connectivity affect how unevenly distributed information is learned under non-IID data. For non-IID settings, we characterize how the distribution of data across nodes shapes the relative contribution of local information to consensus dynamics.

</details>

<a id="paper-258"></a>

### 258. [Loss-Guided Tsetlin Machines: Making the Learning Objective Explicit](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26114v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26114

**作者：** Mayur Shende、Runar Helin、Ole-Christoffer Granmo、Vladimir I. Zadorozhny

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26114v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26114v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Loss-Guided Tsetlin Machine，用可替换的显式损失函数（CE、SCE、ASL）替代固定的 margin 更新规则来驱动子句反馈与权重更新，在多类、多标签与二分类数据集上验证精度、F1 与校准改善，并保持子句级可解释性。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线无直接关系，但其「用显式、可替换目标驱动离散子句级反馈」的思路，可供核查 LVR 中针对结构化推理组件的监督与贡献分配设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Loss-Guided Tsetlin Machines: Making the Learning Objective Explicit

**abstract/A1：** The Tsetlin Machine (TM) has no explicit notion of a loss function. Its learning signal is fixed to an implicit, margin-based update rule (MBUR), with no way to choose an objective function suited to a specific task. This paper introduces Loss-Guided TM, which replaces MBUR with an explicit and swappable loss function. In our proposed method, clause feedback and weight updates are driven by the loss, while keeping the Tsetlin Automaton and clause logic unchanged. The approach incorporates cross entropy (CE), symmetric cross entropy (SCE), and asymmetric loss (ASL). We evaluate Loss-Guided TM across six datasets that span multiclass, multilabel, and binary classification tasks. With a suitable loss, Loss-Guided TM matches or improves accuracy and F1 over MBUR on five of the six datasets. The gains are greatest for the imbalanced multilabel datasets, where ASL improves macro F1 by up to 9 percentage points. With CE, Loss-Guided TM is also significantly better calibrated than MBUR on all six datasets, reducing calibration error by a factor of 3 to 11. A qualitative inspection shows that the clause-based global interpretability, examined at the per-class level on image and text data, is preserved when using the Loss-Guided mechanism. These results show that our explicit TM learning objective allows direct targeting of uncertainty calibration, data imbalance, and task structure, opening the TM to the broader literature on loss functions.

</details>

<a id="paper-259"></a>

### 259. [CryoGaussian: Heterogeneous Cryo-EM Reconstruction Made Explicit with Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26128v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26128

**作者：** Ziwen Wang、Jiajun Fan、Chaoran Cheng、Ruihan Guo、Yanru Qu、Juexiao Zhou、Ge Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26128v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26128v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CryoGaussian，用显式高斯泼溅表示直接拟合冷冻电镜粒子图像，通过学习 particle-image latent 与 per-Gaussian latent 来刻画组成与构象异质性，并优化光栅器以提升训练速度。

**相关性简评：** 从高噪声观测中推断连续隐状态、并用 per-primitive latent 调制基础表示的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态解耦以及内容与状态的分离提供可供核查的表示线索，但领域与推理任务相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CryoGaussian: Heterogeneous Cryo-EM Reconstruction Made Explicit with Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** Single-particle cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) determines macromolecular structures from noisy particle images. However, rare, transient compositional states and subtle, continuous conformational variation are difficult to resolve. We present CryoGaussian, a Gaussian splatting method for heterogeneous cryo-EM reconstruction that fits an explicit Gaussian density representation directly to the particle images through a differentiable rasterizer rendering orthographic line integrals modulated by the contrast transfer function. The Gaussians are initialized randomly, with their number derived from the estimated spatial support of the molecular density and their minimum size bounded by the Nyquist frequency of the particle images. To capture the compositional and conformational variation across particle images, we learn particle-image and per-Gaussian latents that jointly modulate the base position, amplitude, and scale of each Gaussian. Each structural state is then reconstructed by fitting the Gaussians conditioned on its inferred latent. On CryoBench, CryoGaussian achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction accuracy for both compositional and conformational heterogeneity. On experimental data, it resolves a rare compositional state of the 50S ribosomal subunit (class C4, 1.3% of particles) with rRNA helix 68 docked in the absence of the central protuberance, and continuous conformational motion of the SF3b and helicase subcomplexes of the pre-catalytic spliceosome. We further optimize the rasterizer for additive cryo-EM projection, reducing training time by 12.8-28.0$\\times$.

**comment/C1：** 59 pages, 25 figures, 15 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-260"></a>

### 260. [NariQ: Sub-3-Bit Quantization for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26157v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26157

**作者：** Doyeob Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26157v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26157v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 NariQ，一种基于 trellis 编码的训练后量化方法，用 BF16 模型在 agent 会话中生成的 token 做校准，使 Qwen3.8-27B 在 2.5–3.0 bit/weight 下于 DeepSWE、Terminal Bench 等长程 agent 任务上与全精度持平。

**相关性简评：** 其核心观察是短文本校准与 perplexity 无法预测长程多步任务表现，且低位宽会侵蚀内部状态中任务必需的信息，可供核查低精度或压缩对 latent 状态保真度与长程评价指标的影响；但未涉及视觉证据或 latent 推理生成，关联仅为间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NariQ: Sub-3-Bit Quantization for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks

**abstract/A1：** Long-horizon agentic tasks require a model to call tools over many steps at contexts of 100K tokens or more, yet many low-bit quantization methods are calibrated on short web text and judged on perplexity. This mismatch is costly: existing quantizations of Qwen3.8-27B at or below 3 bits per weight match BF16 on shorter tasks like AIME, yet in published results solve at most 72% as many tasks as BF16 on DeepSWE, a long-horizon agentic benchmark. We propose NariQ, a trellis-coded post-training quantization method that calibrates on tokens generated by the BF16 model in agent sessions. On Qwen3.8-27B, NariQ at 3.0 and 2.5 bits per weight stays level with BF16 on two long-horizon agentic benchmarks, DeepSWE and Terminal Bench, as well as on AIME and RealWorldQA. Our checkpoint at 2.0 bits per weight trails this performance. To our knowledge, these are the first quantizations at or below 3 bits per weight shown to match their full-precision version on long-horizon agentic tasks. We release the files as Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-GGUF (https://huggingface.co/nari-labs/Qwen3.8-27B-NariQ-GGUF), with inference available through our llama.cpp fork at https://github.com/nari-labs/nari-llama.cpp.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-261"></a>

### 261. [When Foundation Models Disagree: Representational Heterogeneity for Calibrated Uncertainty in Whole-Slide Image Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26159v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26159

**作者：** Yekai Shen、Yuqian Wang、Zishun Liao、Rongsheng Wang、Wenting Chen、S. Kevin Zhou、Qingsong Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26159v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26159v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CALIFUSE，将多个病理基础模型的表示差异当作显式置信线索，融合切片级决策共识与区块级证据一致性（用 Jensen-Shannon 散度度量）来做不确定性估计，编码器冻结、各模型另训 nnMIL 分类器，在七个 WSI 分类任务上改善校准、误分类检测与选择性预测。

**相关性简评：** 其「跨编码器表示异质性 + 细粒度证据一致性」可作为多 latent 假设间一致性检验或视觉证据筛选的可核查线索，选择性预测也与 STOP 决策相关，但场景远离 VLM 推理，仅为概念启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Foundation Models Disagree: Representational Heterogeneity for Calibrated Uncertainty in Whole-Slide Image Classification

**abstract/A1：** Pathology foundation models supply strong representations for whole-slide image classification, yet the confidence attached to their predictions remains hard to trust. Prevailing uncertainty estimators act within a single feature space and thereby overlook the uncertainty that the choice of foundation-model representation itself induces. This work introduces CALIFUSE, a calibration-oriented framework that fuses several pathology foundation models for the specific purpose of uncertainty estimation. Departing from accuracy-driven fusion, CALIFUSE reads representational heterogeneity across encoders as an explicit confidence cue. Slide-level decision consensus is coupled with chunk-level evidence agreement quantified through Jensen-Shannon divergence, so that both prediction stability and fine-grained evidence consistency inform the estimate. Foundation-model encoders stay frozen. A task-specific nnMIL classifier is still trained for each FM; CALIFUSE then estimates confidence at inference without training an extra fusion module. Evaluated on seven WSI classification tasks under several foundation-model combinations, the framework sharpens calibration, misclassification detection, and selective prediction relative to both the strongest single-encoder baseline and naive ensemble averaging, all while preserving competitive balanced accuracy.

**comment/C1：** accepted by IEEE BIBM 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-262"></a>

### 262. [Time Series Structure Learning with Local Causal Parent Discovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26232v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26232

**作者：** Jacqueline Maasch、Shu Wan、Hank Zhu、Huan Liu、Zhiguang Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26232v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26232v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 ITPD，一种面向因果非平稳多变量时间序列的约束式因果结构学习算法，以「先局部、后全局」的方式逐个变量确定父集，并借助时间结构先验、图划分与局部 Markov 条件，用低阶条件独立检验替代高阶检验，在仿真中取得 F1 与耗时的 Pareto 优势。

**相关性简评：** 该文与 LVR 主线不直接相关，但其「局部确定因果父集、以低阶检验替代高阶检验」的思路，可能为「哪些历史或视觉证据应进入推理状态、如何低成本筛选因果相关成分」提供问题表述层面的启发，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Time Series Structure Learning with Local Causal Parent Discovery

**abstract/A1：** Learning and inference on time series can pose significant statistical challenges, including autocorrelated observations, distribution shift over time, and the curse of dimensionality. These challenges complicate the practical application of causal discovery algorithms, which already suffer from high computational and sample complexity in the general case. To mitigate these issues, this work introduces Iterative Temporal Parent Discovery (ITPD): a sound and complete constraint-based procedure for causal structure learning on multivariate time series with causal nonstationarity. ITPD takes a local-to-global learning approach that discovers the global graph by sequentially discovering the parent set for each variable. ITPD exploits temporal structural priors, graph partitioning, and the local Markov condition to swap higher-order conditional independence tests for lower-order alternatives. With an exact oracle, ITPD performed the least number of tests and used the smallest average conditioning set sizes relative to baselines. In benchmarking simulations, ITPD sat on the Pareto frontier with respect to F1 and wall-clock time and was faster than baselines on large graphs ($\\geq$ 1000 nodes). For graphs with 2000 nodes, ITPD ran $2.3\\times$, $9.1\\times$, and $40.9\\times$ faster than iterative IAMB, DF-PC, and PCMCI, respectively.

</details>

<a id="paper-263"></a>

### 263. [Attention Drift Regularization with Adaptive Initialization for Domain-Robust Visual Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26234v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26234

**作者：** Shivangi Rai、Vinod K Kurmi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26234v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26234v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对无源域自适应中的两个失效模式——分布偏移下注意力图漂向虚假或背景特征、以及对所有目标域统一使用同一源模型初始化——提出 DGA-CAFC：用反事实注意力扰动与学习注意力下表示的一致性约束稳定特征，并按目标预测熵估计域差距以选择源模型变体。

**相关性简评：** 其「注意力漂移」诊断与反事实注意力扰动下的表示一致性约束，可能为核查注意力结构如何影响 latent 中视觉证据的保留与虚假特征混入提供可借用的扰动式探针思路，但原文面向域自适应图像分类，与 LVR 的推理状态生成无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Attention Drift Regularization with Adaptive Initialization for Domain-Robust Visual Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Source-free domain adaptation (SFDA) adapts a pretrained source model to unlabeled target domains without source data access, yet two critical failure modes remain unaddressed. First, attention drift: source-learned attention maps shift toward spurious or background features under distribution shift, destabilizing feature representations. Second, domain-gap-agnostic initialization: applying a single fixed source model uniformly across all target domains ignores domain gap severity, causing premature overfitting to noisy pseudo-labels under large shifts. We propose DGA-CAFC to address both failures. The Counterfactual Attention-based Feature Consistency (CAFC) module enforces feature stability by minimizing the discrepancy between representations computed under learned attention and those under counterfactual-inspired normalized perturbations, thereby promoting domain-robust features independent of spurious attention shifts. The Domain-Gap Aware Source Model Selection (DGASS) module estimates domain gap severity via target prediction entropy and selects the source model variant whose confidence level best matches the estimated gap, reducing negative transfer and stabilizing pseudo-label quality. Crucially, CAFC transfers beyond SFDA: applied as a lightweight regularizer to standard domain generalization objectives without target data. Extensive experiments across SFDA and DG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently improves over recent baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness and generality of the proposed framework. Project page is available at https://visdomlab.github.io/DGA\_CAFC

**comment/C1：** Accepted in WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-264"></a>

### 264. [TwinLab-Bench: Evaluating AI Scientists in Agentic Twins of Real Labs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26248v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26248

**作者：** Yang Wan、Linchao Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26248v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26248v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TwinLab-Bench，用真实实验室的数字孪生环境评测 AI scientist 在 52 项物理、生命科学与工程任务中自主设计实验的能力，由 TwinLab agent 执行被测 agent 设计的实验，并用真实实验记录中的隐藏答案评分。

**相关性简评：** 其「从给定设计空间选择下一步测量、自主设计实验并以原始记录校验」的评测形式，可能与 VLM 推理中的证据获取与停止决策问题表述有类比价值，可供核查；但与视觉表征和 latent 推理状态无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TwinLab-Bench: Evaluating AI Scientists in Agentic Twins of Real Labs

**abstract/A1：** AI agents are increasingly expected to act as AI scientists, which requires three increasingly open abilities: carrying out a given computational workflow correctly, choosing the next measurements from a given design space, and designing their own experiments. Existing benchmarks mainly evaluate the first two abilities, in a terminal or in a closed loop that selects from predefined candidates. Evaluating the third ability requires reliably executing an experiment that an agent designs, with readings grounded in a real laboratory. We introduce TwinLab-Bench, which evaluates AI scientists on self-designed experiments in 52 tasks from laboratories in the physical sciences, the life sciences, and engineering. The benchmark supports more than 100 experimental action types, and the agent's report is scored by comparison with a hidden answer taken from real laboratory records. Each task has its own TwinLab agent, an agent that serves as the environment and executes the experiments that the tested agent designs, reading the raw materials of the laboratory and calling calculators that are built from its records. Compared with a language model that sees only the task description, the TwinLab agent almost eliminates readings that deviate from the real records and inconsistent answers to a repeated request, and closed-book probes control for answers recalled from memory. We find that these tasks remain hard for frontier models: the strongest, Claude Fable 5.2, averages a score of only 45.0, and we conduct an empirical study of where it fails.

**comment/C1：** https://twinlab-bench.github.io/ contains tasks and code. 33 pages, 6 figures, 14 tables. LLM-agent benchmark for autonomous experimental design in digital twins of real labs (physics, chemistry, wet-lab biology, materials, engineering). 13 agents evaluated: Claude Fable 5.2 45.0, Opus 5.5 40.2, GPT-6.1-sol 31.7, plus DeepSeek, Qwen, Kimi, GLM and others

</details>

<a id="paper-265"></a>

### 265. [The Stability Gap in Continual Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26252v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26252

**作者：** Sebastian Pusch、Matthia Sabatelli、Gido M. van de Ven

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26252v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26252v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个保持环境动力学与奖励不变、仅改变观察表示的持续强化学习基准，并据此展示「稳定性差距」：引入新任务后旧任务性能显著下降、随后部分或完全恢复，即使智能体可访问全部过去任务。

**相关性简评：** 其「只换观察表示、不换动力学」的基准设计可能可供核查视觉表征变化如何影响 latent 推理状态的保持与恢复；聚合指标掩盖适应期性能回退的现象，也与推理过程中间状态和停止决策的评价方式有间接类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Stability Gap in Continual Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Continual reinforcement learning (CRL) aims to enable agents to adapt to changing tasks while preserving previously acquired capabilities. Evaluation often relies on final aggregate metrics that can hide temporary performance degradation during adaptation. This work introduces a simple CRL benchmark that varies observation representations while preserving environment dynamics and rewards, supporting proof-of-concept demonstrations, inexpensive experimentation, and easily reproducible sanity checks. Using this benchmark, we demonstrate the stability gap in CRL: a substantial drop in prior-task performance after the introduction of new tasks, followed by partial or complete recovery. This pattern occurs despite the agent having complete access to past tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-266"></a>

### 266. [Tracking Objects Through Assembly and Decomposition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26269v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26269

**作者：** Cheryl Lam、Yihong Sun、Bharath Hariharan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26269v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26269v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究视频中跨越分解与组装变换的目标跟踪，提出无需初始提示的任务设定、零样本系统 Atlas（联合推理正向与逆向时空划分以恢复目标轨迹及变换事件），并构建密集标注基准 OmniST。

**相关性简评：** 「组装即分解的逆过程」与正逆向划分联合推理的思路，可能为 latent 状态的逆向动力学、双向状态更新与变换事件检测提供概念线索，但论文不涉及语言/VLM 推理，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Tracking Objects Through Assembly and Decomposition

**abstract/A1：** Tracking objects through transformations is essential for understanding real-world dynamics. Recent work has extended traditional object tracking to detect state changes. However, existing methods require an initial object prompt and detect only its decompositions, missing assembly events, which begin with multiple objects rather than one. We therefore introduce a prompt-free formulation of this task, where the goal is to recover all object tracks and detect both decomposition and assembly events given only a video. Our key insight is that assembly is decomposition in reverse. Building on this insight, we present Atlas, a zero-shot system that constructs and jointly reasons over forward and reverse spatiotemporal partitions to recover object tracks and their transformations. We further introduce OmniST, a benchmark with dense annotations of objects and both transformation types. On OmniST, Atlas recovers more than twice as many state changes as prior methods while remaining competitive on existing benchmarks that only evaluate decomposition.

</details>

<a id="paper-267"></a>

### 267. [FirstCross: Convex Single-Photon 3D Reconstruction from Sparse Views](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26321v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26321

**作者：** Chi-Wei Lee、Cheng-Han Chiang、Yi-Chun Chang、An-Kai Chen、Po-Chih Kuo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26321v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26321v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FirstCross 面向稀疏视角单光子 lidar 的 3D 重建，放弃神经网络，提出按「光线首次进入物体」的 first-crossing 泊松似然分解，将问题化为体素网格上一小段凸优化，并用 running-maximum 透射率替代 NeRF 的乘积透射率，从而在少光子条件下快速得到低深度误差。

**相关性简评：** 其「首次交点 + 遮挡感知透射率」的似然分解思路，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的可见性/遮挡筛选与「哪些像素证据可进入内部状态」提供一种非学习式的形式化参考，但与该主线距离较远，需核查是否真有迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FirstCross: Convex Single-Photon 3D Reconstruction from Sparse Views

**abstract/A1：** Single-photon lidar records the arrival time of each returning photon, enabling 3D reconstruction from few views and scarce photons. However, leading neural methods such as Transientangelo require over an hour of non-convex per-scene optimization, and their accuracy degrades as photon flux decreases. We propose FirstCross, a network-free approach that instead solves a short sequence of convex problems on a voxel grid. For a solid object, the Poisson likelihood of a ray with given signal and background rates depends on the scene solely through its first crossing, the point where the ray first enters the object. Decomposing this likelihood yields two tractable terms. The first penalizes surfaces in front of detected photons and admits a tight convex closure, namely volume rendering with a running-maximum transmittance in place of NeRF's product transmittance. The second accounts for photons that no surface explains and admits a convex upper bound once the object is assumed to be thick behind each detected surface point. Compared with Transientangelo under its own real-data protocol, FirstCross achieves lower depth error across every test scene, view count, and flux level. With three views and 100 times fewer photons than at the protocol's lowest flux, our mean object depth error stays under half of Transientangelo's with all photons. Given our detected surface points, the best classical fusion has 1.3 to 1.8 times our three-view error across flux levels. On real room-scale captures with 1% of their photons, TransientNeRF has 2.8 to 7.7 times our error. FirstCross lets few-view single-photon scanners cut exposure to 1% and reconstruct an object in about a minute on one GPU.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://arthur031221.github.io/firstcross/

</details>

<a id="paper-268"></a>

### 268. [Predicting Surface Displacement Fields of Laser Powder Bed Fusion Parts Using STL-Derived Graphs and Part-Level Descriptors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26323v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26323

**作者：** Saima Ahmed Suhi、Lang Yuan、Md Habibor Rahman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26323v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26323v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用双分支图注意力网络从 STL 派生的网格图直接预测激光粉末床熔融零件的三维表面位移场：一支学习网格顶点间的局部几何关系，另一支融合 12 个质量分布、基板接触、悬垂等零件级描述符，并以 ANSYS 仿真标签做按零件划分的五折交叉验证。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但其「局部图连通性 + 全局几何描述符」的双通道编码与逐顶点连续场预测，可供核查几何细节如何绑定到内部状态及如何做空间化评价，需注意其输出是仿真代理回归而非推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Predicting Surface Displacement Fields of Laser Powder Bed Fusion Parts Using STL-Derived Graphs and Part-Level Descriptors

**abstract/A1：** Predicting part distortion before fabrication remains challenging in laser powder bed fusion (LPBF), while thermomechanical simulation is computationally expensive for repeated design evaluations. This study presents a two-branch graph attention network (GAT) to predict three-dimensional surface displacement directly from STL-derived graphs. The first branch learns geometric relationships among connected vertices of a densified STL surface mesh, while the second incorporates 12 part-level descriptors of mass distribution, build-plate contact, overhang, size, and shape. Displacement labels from ANSYS Additive Print simulations are mapped onto STL vertices using Gaussian-kernel weighted averaging. Evaluated on 117 parts using five-fold cross-validation split by part, the model achieves an R^2 of 0.946 and a mean absolute error of 0.0178 mm for displacement magnitude. Relative to a node-wise multilayer perceptron, GAT improves R^2 from 0.913 to 0.946; for build-direction displacement, R^2 increases from 0.903 to 0.939. GAT also outperforms GraphSAGE (R^2 = 0.936) and a graph convolutional network (R^2 = 0.934) across all five folds. Ablation experiments show that removing mass-distribution descriptors reduces magnitude R^2 to 0.900, while removing build-plate contact and overhang descriptors reduces it to 0.920. These results demonstrate the value of combining local mesh connectivity with global geometric descriptors to predict LPBF distortion, enabling faster design iteration and earlier identification of distortion-prone geometries.

</details>

<a id="paper-269"></a>

### 269. [Rigel-Ladon: An Option-Count Law for a One-Pass Decision Encoder, Tested on Held-Out Option Counts and Held-Out Schemas](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26332v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26332

**作者：** Mohammed Nayeem Khan、Vrishank Tiwari、Navin Kumar R

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26332v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26332v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究小型单遍编码器 Laya 在选项数 K 从 2 增至 35 时准确率如何下降，拟合 logit 准确率对 log(K-1) 的线性规律，并在未见选项数和跨领域问题上检验外推，同时报告顺序平均、预测集等方法的迁移失败。

**相关性简评：** 候选集扩大时单遍决策的衰减律，可能为 LVR 中候选打分、计算分配与「何时停止单遍判断」提供参照；但仅涉文本、合成标注和单一模型族，能否迁移到视觉 latent 推理尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rigel-Ladon: An Option-Count Law for a One-Pass Decision Encoder, Tested on Held-Out Option Counts and Held-Out Schemas

**abstract/A1：** Laya is a small neural network that reads a text, a question and K written options, and returns one probability per option in a single pass. We ask how its accuracy falls as K grows from 2 to 35 and whether that can be predicted. We score nested option sets (every K-option question contains the smaller ones) on four question pools, for the released model (Base) and our fine-tune (FT), and score every item against each single rival. FT trained on one 12-option topic question (in-family); a 12-option HR (department-routing) question is out-of-family. Logit accuracy (the log of correct over incorrect answers) is linear in log(K-1) with slope 0.68 to 0.88. Fitted on K up to 6, the law predicts K=7 to 12 within 0.6 points and K up to 24 within 1.2 (a log-linear rule: 1.6 and 6.7). Taking the slope from the topic question and measuring only two-option accuracy on HR predicts its other K within 0.3 points (FT) and 0.7 (Base). On a 35-label pool of five schemas the law fails (5 to 11 points). Independence of irrelevant alternatives is rejected: the gold-versus-rival log-odds shifts by +0.24 to +2.07 nats per doubling of K, yet pairwise answers reproduce K-way accuracy within 4.7 points. Order averaging (averaging over several orderings of the options), shortlists, a temperature law and calibrated prediction sets did not transfer (FT's 90% sets cover 84% on HR). 2 of 10 predictions made in advance held. Caveats: one model family and LLM-labelled synthetic text.

**comment/C1：** 43 pages, 29 figures, 35 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-270"></a>

### 270. [ClosureBench: A Benchmark for Reasoning under Closure Assumptions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26335v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26335

**作者：** Francesco Belardinelli、Flavio Corradini、Matteo Leonesi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26335v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26335v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 ClosureBench，在 OWA、CWA、LCWA 三种缺失信息处理约定下对同一基础场景做平行测试，并以「所有变体均正确」为核心判据，考察 LLM agent 是否遵循给定规则处理缺失信息。960 个实例显示总体准确率会掩盖明显的约定切换错误，部分 agent 在 LCWA 下仍输出 unknown。

**相关性简评：** 缺失信息应视为未知还是为假，与 LVR 中「证据是否充分、何时继续获取视觉证据或停止作答」的判断相关，其按约定变体分离度量的设计可供核查如何评价模型对信息缺失与不确定性的利用，但材料未涉及视觉或多模态状态，联系需进一步确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ClosureBench: A Benchmark for Reasoning under Closure Assumptions

**abstract/A1：** AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) often answer questions from incomplete records. Missing information might be interpreted as unknown or false, depending on the completeness of the source. Current evaluations fail to distinguish these two aspects, as they are usually interwoven with other reasoning demands. We present ClosureBench, a benchmark that tests whether LLMs follow the stated rules for dealing with missing information. Every base scenario is tested under OWA, CWA, and LCWA, and only the treatment of missing information changes between variants. ClosureBench provides a verified base benchmark as well as agent extensions for action choice, source scope, and updates. A model is considered correct on a base scenario only if it provides correct answers in all of its closure variants. Across 960 instances, overall accuracy can mask substantial closure mistakes. Models achieve overall accuracy from 71.18% to 99.86% across replicated runs, but their core switch accuracy, measured on the scenarios whose gold label actually changes with the contract, varies between 18.23% and 99.31%. Large discrepancies emerge under LCWA. For example, some agents choose unknown despite the prompt indicating that missing information should be treated as false. Our findings demonstrate that evaluations of AI agents should not only measure whether agents can reason over existing facts but also whether they use the appropriate assumption when information is absent.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 20th International Conference on Neurosymbolic Learning and Reasoning (NeSy 2026). Conference version: https://openreview.net/forum?id=TEaW3pdZOZ

</details>

<a id="paper-271"></a>

### 271. [DeepQuest: From Knowledge Gaps to Scientific Discovery in Alzheimer's Disease](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26343v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26343

**作者：** Chunbo Jiang、Yifei Chen、Beining Wu、Fuyou Mao、Weihong Chen、Enyu Bao、Mingxuan Liu、Feiwei Qin、Qiyuan Tian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26343v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26343v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** DeepQuest 是一个面向阿尔茨海默病研究的知识缺口驱动 AI scientist 框架，将领域综述中的未解问题转为可追溯、可检验的假设，经数据可行性筛选与分组 Elo 排序，并配合迭代实验和两级审计（实验可靠性与知识贡献）来评估发现。

**相关性简评：** 其「数据可行性筛选 + 可靠性/知识贡献两级审计」的候选假设排序与证据评估流程，可能与 LVR 中多 latent 假设的取舍、视觉证据筛选及贡献分配的评价设计存在概念类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DeepQuest: From Knowledge Gaps to Scientific Discovery in Alzheimer's Disease

**abstract/A1：** Automating experiments does not necessarily advance scientific understanding. Research agents must identify meaningful knowledge gaps, determine what available data can resolve, and assess whether their findings advance existing knowledge. We introduce DeepQuest, a knowledge-gap-driven AI scientist framework for Alzheimer's disease research. DeepQuest transforms unresolved questions from domain reviews into traceable, testable hypotheses and prioritizes them through data feasibility screening and groupwise Elo ranking. It couples iterative experimentation with two-level auditing that separates experimental reliability from knowledge contribution relative to two frozen literature corpora. On OASIS-3, DeepQuest produces 42 feasible hypotheses and outperforms the strongest baselines in 14 of 15 evaluator-dimension comparisons across three language-model reviewers. The full framework achieves an audit-assessed scientific finding rate of 95.24%, outperforming both general-purpose executors and domain-specific research agents. This scientific finding rate remains stable across literature corpora and evaluator model families. Representative findings concern neuropsychiatric symptoms and dementia risk, longitudinal amyloid spatial stability, and neocortical tau-associated clinical impairment, with key associations supported in independent ADNI and AIBL cohorts. These results show how aligning knowledge gaps, data feasibility, and evidence auditing can turn automated research into traceable, evidence-supported scientific discovery. Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DeepQuest-3066/

</details>

<a id="paper-272"></a>

### 272. [Context as a Resource](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26346v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26346

**作者：** Xufei Tian、Han Hu、Wenli Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26346v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26346v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ClonAct：面向多会话常驻个人智能体的无路由上下文虚拟化架构，用 Context MMU、Context Cell、带隔离试验合并门的 Synaptic Bus 与波刷新共享主干来管理多个上下文，并在多流基准上评估合并正确率与等待成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「上下文作为资源」的显式隔离与试验式合并门，可能启发 LVR 中多个推理状态的隔离、合并与冲突验证，以及视觉证据筛选后的状态整合问题，但论文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，需核查其合并判据是否可移植。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Context as a Resource

**abstract/A1：** Managing concurrent tasks across separate agent sessions quickly exhausts human attention, motivating always-on personal agents behind a single continuous interface. Yet when every context shares identical frozen backbone weights, funneling interleaved dialogues and external events into one linear session induces cross-topic constraint bleed and head-of-line stalling. Conversely, router-ticket hierarchies incur lossy prose handoffs, heavy routing overhead, and blind merges - collapsing true-conflict repository merges to a 33.3% three-run mean despite high isolated branch passes. Rather than scheduling anthropomorphic roles, we introduce ClonAct, a routerless architecture that virtualizes context blocks directly through a pluggable single-pass non-generative Context MMU, in-place Context Cells, a footprint-aware Synaptic Bus with an isolated trial merge gate, and a wave-flushed Shared Trunk. Across three programmatic multi-stream benchmarks and three model families, our portable trial merge gate lifts true-conflict merges to 63.3%, while ClonAct's routerless virtualization attains this ceiling with 45.9% fewer prompt tokens and prevents compact-model router collapse. Under continuous surge streams, ClonAct matches top completion (95.0%) with the lowest median wait and zero timed-out status checks.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, including appendices. Code: https://github.com/Viacol0916/ClonAct

</details>

<a id="paper-273"></a>

### 273. [OffBeat: Multicoset Sampling for 4D Capture Beyond the Camera Frame Rate](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26347v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26347

**作者：** Chi-Wei Lee、An-Kai Chen、Cheng-Han Chiang、Yi-Chun Chang、Po-Chih Kuo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26347v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26347v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将错峰触发（staggered）的多相机阵列建模为空间分布式 multicoset 采样器，仅凭标定推导出同步发射的相机分组及其发射顺序，并证明基元不透明度须覆盖至少一个相机帧间隔才能使各分组被等权加权，据此在 Spacetime Gaussians 上用最短生命周期与平滑 box 降低 flicker，在 N3DV、PanopticSports 上提升动态区域 PSNR。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 或 latent 推理无直接联系，但「采样时刻表决定哪些状态量可辨识、并需满足最短时间跨度」这一思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据采集调度与推理状态时间粒度的设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OffBeat: Multicoset Sampling for 4D Capture Beyond the Camera Frame Rate

**abstract/A1：** For 4D reconstruction, a synchronized multi-camera rig records all views at the same timestamps and misses the motion between them. Staggered triggers spread the same frames over more timestamps. However, when the cameras along each row fire one after another, as in 4DSloMo's schedule on the N3DV rig, the row acts as one moving camera that confounds depth with motion. We introduce OffBeat, which models a staggered rig as a spatially distributed multicoset sampler: shifted copies of one sampling grid. From the calibration alone, OffBeat derives camera groups that fire together and their firing order. Since one group records each timestamp, a short-lived primitive can fit an error its cameras share, such as a color offset, and flicker in novel views. We show that a primitive's opacity over time weights all groups equally, for every number of groups, only if it lasts at least one camera frame interval. On Spacetime Gaussians, a minimum lifetime approximates this bound, and a closed-form smoothed box of that length satisfies it exactly, with less flicker than a Gaussian in its place at nearly equal PSNR. In simulated staggered capture on N3DV, OffBeat outperforms the full 4DSloMo pipeline with video diffusion on every test frame, by 3.04 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR. Between the first and last synchronized frame, it outperforms the best synchronized method of each scene, with or without frame interpolation, by 1.98 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR, while synchronized capture is better at its own frames. On PanopticSports, in this frame range, OffBeat is comparable in PSNR to synchronized capture with interpolation.
