On every N3DV scene, replacing 4DSloMo's schedule with ours raises the dynamic-region PSNR of Spacetime Gaussians, 4DSloMo without diffusion, and our reconstruction. Multi-camera rigs could follow faster motion at the same frame count by changing only when each camera fires.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://arthur031221.github.io/offbeat/

</details>

<a id="paper-274"></a>

### 274. [TRCF: Temporal-Reference Complementary Forecasting for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26351v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26351

**作者：** Xudong Zuo、Shusen Ma、Qirui Chen、Bing Fan、Yun-Bo Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26351v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26351v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文针对不规则多变量时间序列预测，提出 TRCF：以紧凑的通道独立分支为基础，另设通道依赖分支，通过保留观测支撑的聚合与「估计—差值校正」的历史补全得到互补特征，再融合进行浅层 MLP 预测，并在四个真实数据集上报告了误差与推理显存优势。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式保留已观测值、另建估计补全的互补分支再融合」思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中如何避免视觉证据被隐状态坍缩、并区分直接观测表示与推断出的上下文；联系属跨领域启发，需人工判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRCF: Temporal-Reference Complementary Forecasting for Irregular Multivariate Time Series

**abstract/A1：** Irregular multivariate time series forecasting supports healthcare monitoring, environmental analysis, and activity recognition. Channel-independent (CI) approaches capture each variable's temporal patterns but do not explicitly use observations from other variables. Channel-dependent (CD) modeling exploits relationships among variables, yet asynchronous measurements make these relationships difficult to learn. Incorporating cross-variable information while preserving each variable's irregular history therefore remains a key challenge. We propose Temporal-Reference Complementary Forecasting (TRCF), a framework that augments a compact CI forecaster with a complementary CD branch. ANCHOR aggregates observed values and timestamps at shared temporal locations, retaining observation support to form compact CI features. BRIDGE constructs complementary features through history completion. It first estimates each variable's historical values from its own observations, then uses differences between other variables' observed and estimated values at nearby times to correct missing entries. The completed history preserves observed values and is encoded to supply context alongside the direct CI representation. Finally, the two representations are fused, combined with the query-time embedding, and passed to a shallow multilayer perceptron for forecasting. Experiments on four real-world datasets show that TRCF achieves the lowest mean squared error and mean absolute error in the main benchmark comparison, while reducing measured peak inference memory by 17.28% to 87.98% relative to APN.

</details>

<a id="paper-275"></a>

### 275. [Evidence from a Multi-Regime Causal Discovery Benchmark: UCMT Algorithms, Comparative Performance, and Controlled Ablation Studies](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26383v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26383

**作者：** Mahyar Eslami

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26383v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26383v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 报告一项内部预注册评测：在多个异构基准区间上比较 UCMT 因果发现算法族与 NOTEARS、DAGMA、BOSS 等既有方法，并另做固定骨架的有向定向消融与稀疏性、BIC 精化等组件消融，同时报告多重检验校正、运行成本与可复现性局限。

**相关性简评：** 其定向阶段消融与「哪些内部环节真正影响结果」的证据组织方式，或可供核查如何对 LVR 中 latent 状态与推理步骤做因果作用归因；但研究对象是表格型因果关系发现，与视觉证据、latent 生成无直接联系，迁移仅为推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evidence from a Multi-Regime Causal Discovery Benchmark: UCMT Algorithms, Comparative Performance, and Controlled Ablation Studies

**abstract/A1：** We present an internal, pre-registered evaluation of the UCMT causal discovery algorithm family alongside established alternatives across heterogeneous benchmark regimes, complemented by a separately pre-registered, controlled study of UCMT v2 Global components. The contribution is both empirical and methodological: a family-stratified comparison of three actively developed algorithm configurations and targeted evidence about which internal stages affect their outcomes. In the main benchmark, UCMT v2 Global has higher directed F1 than NOTEARS and DAGMA in selected real, semi-synthetic and Gaussian-network comparisons at the paired-run level, while trailing leading permutation-search methods in other settings. The BOSS-based UCMT v4 produces a CSuite-specific directed-F1 improvement over BOSS (mean paired difference +0.279; pair-level Holm p &lt; 0.001), but the dataset-level adjusted result is not significant (p = 0.188). In a separate fixed-skeleton ablation, UCMT v2 Global exceeds an adapted R^2-SortnRegress orientation baseline on standardized iSCM data (directed F1 0.537 vs 0.468, dataset-level Holm p = 0.031); absolute orientation accuracy is 0.563 and there is no established broad advantage over RESIT, ANM or DirectLiNGAM. Controlled ablations show that sparsity and BIC-based refinement materially affect some outcomes, whereas the examined arbitration and automatic regime-selection mechanisms do not yield confirmed general improvements. We report performance shortcomings, multiple-testing correction, runtime costs, evaluation limitations and reproducibility checks. The results support UCMT as an implemented and empirically investigated algorithm family, not a claim of overall superiority, independent validation or demonstrated commercial impact.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 4 figures, 6 tables. Internal benchmark and controlled ablation study; not peer-reviewed

</details>

<a id="paper-276"></a>

### 276. [A Complete Geometric Message-Passing Architecture for Point Clouds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26384v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26384

**作者：** Nadav Dym、Tal Amir、Gia Chi Dang、Christopher Morris

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26384v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26384v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出一种新的等变消息传递点云架构，证明其在旋转与置换对称下具有完备性（可区分任何非对称等价的输入），并用实验展示其能分开距离型消息传递无法区分的点云，还据此证明略作修改的 SEGNN 的完备性。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示完备性/可区分性」的形式化与验证方式，可能为判断 VLM 内部状态是否保留任务必要的几何与视觉细节、以及信息坍缩的诊断提供可核查的表述工具。该文本身不涉及多模态推理或 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Complete Geometric Message-Passing Architecture for Point Clouds

**abstract/A1：** Three-dimensional point clouds are a fundamental data modality, and a large family of machine learning architectures has been developed to respect their natural symmetries to rigid motions and permutations. A principled way to compare these architectures, which vary widely in complexity and cost, is through their expressive power. That is, an architecture is complete if it can distinguish any two inputs not related by the symmetries it respects. Simple distance-based message-passing networks are known to be incomplete, and while there exist complete alternatives, they typically rely on computationally intensive mechanisms like high-order representations of the rotation group or higher-order Weisfeiler-Leman-type tests. In this work, we devise a novel, simple, equivariant message-passing architecture for point clouds, and prove that it is complete. We complement our theoretical result with experiments showing that our architecture separates challenging point clouds that distance-based message passing provably cannot distinguish. Our results suggest that high-order equivariant features may not be necessary to enhance the expressivity of point cloud architectures, and can help settle completeness for other equivariant architectures for which such results are currently unknown. We give initial evidence for this by proving completeness of a slightly modified version of SEGNN.

**comment/C1：** 44 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables. Ancillary files: exact-arithmetic verifier, certificate and companion notebook for the Platonic color-atom computation

</details>

<a id="paper-277"></a>

### 277. [SEEC: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Self-Supervised External-Eye Representation Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26386v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26386

**作者：** George R. Nahass、Michelle Fleishaker、Sanjay Ganesh、Sasha Hubschman、R. V. Paul Chan、Chad A. Purnell、Pete Setabutr、Ann Q. Tran、Sathya N. Ravi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26386v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26386v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文整合十四个公开人脸数据构建约435万张外眼图像语料与自监督表征基准，用 InfoNCE、VICReg、LeJEPA 训练 ResNet-101/ViT-B 并与 DINOv2、MAE 对照，从嵌入几何与解剖恢复、疾病分类两个层面评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「表征层面几何利用度与功能层面表现可分离」的结论，可能可供核查 LVR 中以 latent 相似度或几何指标推断视觉内容是否被答案真实使用的做法是否可靠。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SEEC: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Self-Supervised External-Eye Representation Learning

**abstract/A1：** External-eye photography captures clinically relevant periocular anatomy at low acquisition cost, yet no large-scale standardized resource exists for evaluating self-supervised external-eye representations. We introduce a 4.35-million-image Standardized External-Eye Corpus (SEEC) derived from fourteen heterogeneous public face datasets, together with a benchmark for external-eye representation learning. For evaluation, we provide held-out evaluation distributions, landmark-annotated anatomical probe sets, and clinical disease-classification experiments spanning ptosis, thyroid eye disease, and craniofacial syndromes. We evaluate representations at two complementary levels: (a) the representation level, by assessing embedding geometry across multiple data distributions, and (b) the functional level, by assessing anatomical recovery, out-of-distribution anatomical transfer, and disease classification. Our models include ResNet-101 and ViT-B encoders pretrained using InfoNCE, VICReg, and LeJEPA objectives and compared with DINOv2 and MAE. Our results indicate that representation-level and functional-level evaluations are dissociable: broader embedding-space utilization does not consistently correspond to anatomical transfer or disease-classification performance, and no single encoder is universally optimal. Domain-specific external-eye encoders outperform DINOv2 and MAE on cross-dataset anatomical transfer despite being pretrained at substantially smaller scale, while external-eye-pretrained ViT models match or outperform these general-purpose baselines on frozen disease-classification probes. We release benchmark code, evaluation scripts, and preprocessing pipelines to reproduce SEEC at https://monkeygobah.github.io/seec-project/

**comment/C1：** 36 Pages, 10 Figures, 9 Tables

</details>

<a id="paper-278"></a>

### 278. [GateTrace: Where Do Crowd Crossings Go Missing Under Occlusion?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26388v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26388

**作者：** Ao He、Qianchen Yu、Vivian W. H. Wong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26388v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26388v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究人群计数中遮挡导致的漏计：以 MOT20 的轨迹与可见度标注为参照，提出 GateTrace 按人物、方向、时间和位置匹配计数记录，定位漏失发生在跟踪输出还是后续计数规则环节，并量化规则改动的召回与精度代价。

**相关性简评：** 其按流水线阶段追溯「视觉证据在哪一步丢失」的评测思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中遮挡或低可见度下任务必要视觉细节在感知、关联到决策各环节的信息坍缩与证据筛选问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GateTrace: Where Do Crowd Crossings Go Missing Under Occlusion?

**abstract/A1：** At large events, pedestrian tracking supports entrance and passageway counts, but totals hide which crossings were missed. In a stadium study, the system recorded about four in five clearly visible crossings but fewer than one in three mostly obscured crossings. The videos lacked trajectories annotated across frames, so we turned to MOT20, which provides pedestrian trajectories and visibility. We introduce GateTrace to locate missed crossings and assess changes to counting rules. It derives reference crossings from annotations under fixed rules, matches reports by person, direction, time and position, and traces misses through saved boxes and counting decisions. With a shared detector, ByteTrack, OC-SORT and BoT-SORT recover 38.9-46.7% of low-visibility reference crossings on development sequences, versus 98.1-99.0% at high visibility. The gap recurs on two further sequences under a protocol fixed in advance. Published outputs of MOT20-tuned BASE recover 63-78% at low visibility across both sequence groups. Losses stop at different stages across systems. Across four systems and both groups, 47-79% of low-visibility misses have no nearby output linked to the person or no possible crossing in the saved tracks. Removing later checks from BASE on development sequences leaves most misses unresolved and lowers precision against the fixed reference list from about 96% to 77%. Misses also remain among sampled reference crossings that two human annotators supported after checking the videos. GateTrace shows where crossings are lost and measures the recovery and precision costs of changing later checks. We will release the code and per-crossing records.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, including supplementary material; 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-279"></a>

### 279. [Reasoning-Trace Analysis Using Large Language Models for Interpretable Q-Matrix Construction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26422v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26422

**作者：** Jihong Zhang、Zijing Jin、Mingfeng Xue

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26422v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26422v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Reasoning-Trace Analysis 框架：用 CoT 提示让 LLM 生成带属性标签的推理步骤，再据此重建、比较和检查 Q 矩阵中每个题目—属性指派，并在扰动分数减法题模拟与 11 题、536 名被试的实证数据中比较其与直接生成 Q 矩阵的重叠度及拟合差异。

**相关性简评：** 用逐步推理轨迹回溯并审计每个结构化决策的做法，可能为 LVR 中「答案是否使用 latent 内容」的步骤级归因与人工可查性提供线索；但研究对象是文本 CoT 和测量学 Q 矩阵，不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，迁移需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reasoning-Trace Analysis Using Large Language Models for Interpretable Q-Matrix Construction

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) can generate Q-matrices for diagnostic classification models that approximate expert specifications, but evaluations have focused on the final matrices, leaving the basis of individual item-attribute assignments unexamined. This opacity limits the responsible use of AI in measurement. We propose Reasoning-Trace Analysis (RTA), a framework in which an LLM generates attribute-labeled reasoning steps through chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting and these steps are analyzed to reconstruct, compare, and inspect each assignment. RTA is intended to supplement expert review and empirical validation. In a simulation study with perturbed fraction subtraction items, four LLMs recovered the required attributes and withheld attributes the items did not require. In an empirical study with 11 fraction subtraction items, responses from 536 examinees, four LLMs, up to three temperatures, and 100 replications per condition, CoT-derived Q-matrices overlapped substantially with the reference Q-matrix (mean overlap .809) but less than directly generated Q-matrices (.877), and they varied more across replications. Inspection of the traces linked disagreements to item-type coding, labeling rules, and how operations were expressed as steps. Q-matrices with similar overlap differed in fit to examinee responses. RTA provides inspectable evidence that matrix-level agreement and fit do not.

**comment/C1：** 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-280"></a>

### 280. [HumanCOIN: A 12,500-Hour Dataset for Human Intent in Egocentric Manipulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26434v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26434

**作者：** Shihan Wu、You Liu、Chunyu He、Minghui Qin、Longhan Jiao、Jinbang Guo、Shaoxuan Xie、Kaixi Tian、Wenjie Chen、Jingrui Pang、Yuanhang Zhang、Yichi Zhang、Miao Liu、Tengfei Pan、Yance Jiao、Yunyan Zhang、Yihang Li、Yongjian Shen、Li Liu、Kai Zhu、Chen Fang、Jianxing Chen、Sihan Liu、Xiaofeng Wang、Jie Zhao、Jing Li、Yueqi Wang、Yichen Tang、Wei Li、Zhexuan Yang、Yue Cao、Ji Zhao、Kai Wang、Dongsheng Jiang、Yongwei Wang、Wei Mu、Jiangmiao Pang、Maoqing Yao、Wenchao Ding、Zheng Zhu、Zhongpu Xia、Dalong Du、Jianwei Niu、Dahui Gao、Zhiwei Yu、Bo Lei、Huan Yang、Yonghua Lin、Tiejun Huang、Zhongyuan Wang、Guocai Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26434v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26434v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HumanCOIN，一个以人类意图为中心的第一人称操作数据集，共 12,500 小时，按三层代理信号组织：从头带相机推断视线、记录手臂表面肌电、采集头皮脑电，并提供多视角 RGB、深度、点云与 6-DoF 位姿及步级动作标注。

**相关性简评：** 该工作把「观察—动作」之间的意图状态当作不可直接观测的隐变量，并用先于动作的视线、负荷与皮层信号作为其可测代理，这可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的可观测代理设计及 LOOK/STOP 的时序监督提供旁证，但其本身不涉及视觉语言推理与生成机制，迁移线索需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HumanCOIN: A 12,500-Hour Dataset for Human Intent in Egocentric Manipulation

**abstract/A1：** First-person manipulation datasets treat the observation-action pair as the atomic unit and compress the generation and execution of an action into a policy network's weights, rendering the intent state behind the action unobservable. Human motor control research shows that intent mediates between observation and action and leaves observable traces along the intent pathway from decision to effect: gaze reaches the target before the hand, muscle load builds before contact, and cortical activity precedes the motor command. To record this pathway at scale, we present HumanCOIN, a first-person manipulation dataset built around human intent: 12,500 hours across 38 scene classes with three tiers of proxies. At the output end, the L1 attention tier (10,000 h) infers gaze from headband camera video, exposing the operator's next object of attention and reach before the hand moves. Midway, the L2 exertion tier (2,000 h) records surface EMG from armbands, making load expectations readable before execution is complete. At the origin, the L3 cognition tier (500 h) records scalp EEG, giving early readouts of task decisions and monitoring. Each tier inherits all proxies from the tiers below. The dataset further provides multi-view RGB images, depth maps and point clouds, 6-DoF head and wrist pose, and step-level action and object annotations. The controlled L3 setting additionally measures gaze, hand pose and finger kinematics directly. Clock and calibration data are released with the dataset so that any read rate can be re-aligned. The three tiers cover three stations, from visual orienting to cortical preparation, making the process behind visual-action pairs available for modeling at dataset scale. Project page: https://flagopen.github.io/HumanCOIN.

**comment/C1：** 41 pages, 10 figures, Project page: https://flagopen.github.io/HumanCOIN

</details>

<a id="paper-281"></a>

### 281. [Learning to Defer under Expert Drift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26440v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26440

**作者：** Ziyao Mou、Andrea Wynn、Eric Nalisnick

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26440v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26440v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出既有 learning-to-defer 框架都假设专家可靠性固定，提出 EDA-L2D：用序列模型把 deferral head 条件化在近期专家结果历史上，以预测可靠性波动并动态分配人机决策，并在图像分类、医学诊断与仇恨言论检测三个任务上验证其对时间漂移的适应性。

**相关性简评：** 「何时交由外部专家」与 LVR 的 STOP 及证据获取决策结构相似，其基于历史结果序列估计可靠性变化的做法可能可供核查：能否类比为对视觉证据通道可靠性的在线估计，从而影响停止与再查询策略；但该文不涉及内部 latent 状态或视觉推理表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Defer under Expert Drift

**abstract/A1：** The learning to defer (L2D) framework enables safety in machine learning and AI systems by allocating difficult or critical decisions to a human expert. However, most prior work on L2D assumes fixed expert reliability, overlooking temporal fluctuations in human performance due to factors such as fatigue and cognitive biases. Humans commonly exhibit such substantial, systematic performance shifts across a wide range of domains, from high-stakes settings such as radiology, aviation, and judicial decision-making, to everyday tasks such as crowdsourced image annotation. We propose Expert-Drift-Adapted Learning-to-Defer (EDA-L2D), a novel expert-aware L2D framework that explicitly models these temporal shifts by conditioning the deferral head on recent histories of expert outcomes via a sequence model. This history-aware approach enables adaptive deferral policies that anticipate reliability fluctuations and better allocate decisions between human and machine over time. We provide comprehensive experiments across three diverse tasks -- image classification, medical diagnosis, and hate speech detection -- including generalization analyses under realistic deferral conditions and irregular shifts in expert reliability. Our results show that EDA-L2D adapts more effectively than prior L2D methods to diverse temporal patterns of expert reliability, resulting in improved system performance and more effective deferral decisions. Code is available at https://github.com/ZiyaoMou/L2D-Expert-Drift.

</details>

<a id="paper-282"></a>

### 282. [Fixing the Last Mile: Failure-Aware Image Restoration with Region-Specific Guidance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26456v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26456

**作者：** Hongyu An、Fanghua Yu、Kainan Yan、Luc Van Gool、Chao Dong、Jinjin Gu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26456v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26456v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Failure-Aware Image Restoration（FAIR），让单次复原模型同时以「局部失败可能出现的位置」和「各区域所需修正指导」为条件，把全局保真转向局部感知可接受性，缓解人脸、文字等重要区域的局部失败。

**相关性简评：** 其区域级失败预测加分区条件注入的思路，可能启发 LVR 中视觉证据的按区域筛选与计算分配（何处需要补看、注入何种信息），但属图像复原任务、与多模态推理无直接关联，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fixing the Last Mile: Failure-Aware Image Restoration with Region-Specific Guidance

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in image restoration have greatly improved global image fidelity, producing visually plausible results across most regions of an image. Nevertheless, even high-quality restorations often exhibit localized failures, including artifacts in semantically important regions such as faces and text, structural inconsistencies, and texture mismatches. Although these errors typically affect only a small portion of the image, they often dominate the overall perceptual quality of the result. In this work, we propose Failure-Aware Image Restoration (FAIR) to address the "last-mile" problem in image restoration. Rather than applying one uniform generative prior over the whole image, we condition a single-pass restoration model on both where local failures are likely to occur and what corrective guidance each region requires. This region-bound conditioning enables fine-grained and controllable restoration that improves semantic accuracy and stylistic consistency in critical regions while maintaining faithful, high-quality restoration elsewhere. Our method complements existing restoration pipelines by shifting the emphasis from global fidelity to local perceptual acceptability. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach resolves common failure cases and improves perceptual quality, bridging the gap between technical accuracy and practical usability.

</details>

<a id="paper-283"></a>

### 283. [Monte Carlo Pass Search for Setpieces](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26464v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26464

**作者：** Andrew Kang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26464v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26464v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Monte Carlo Pass Search，将角球和长传任意球视为执行与未来结局的分布：从踢球前状态生成反事实传球与进攻站位，在世界模型中多次推演，并用价值模型为每个未来打分，在 30 场 Serie A 比赛上比较最优方案与真实执行的 xG 差异。

**相关性简评：** 其「候选提议—多次 rollout—价值模型打分」的反事实搜索与防止搜索偏差的评估思路，可能为 LVR 中候选 latent 假设的生成、展开验证与选择提供类比线索，但论文不涉及视觉推理，迁移价值需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Monte Carlo Pass Search for Setpieces

**abstract/A1：** Set pieces produce about a third of non-penalty expected goals (xG), yet they are usually evaluated by what happened next. We present Monte Carlo Pass Search (MCPS) for set pieces, which treats a corner or a lofted free kick as a distribution over plausible executions and plausible futures instead of a single realised event. From the pre-kick state, MCPS proposes counterfactual deliveries and counterfactual set-ups (where the attackers stand at the kick), plays each out many times in a world model (3D ball physics, a generative model of all 22 players, the keeper's come-or-stay decision and a Player-to-Touch model) and scores every future with a value model. On 249 deliveries from 30 Serie A 2025/26 matches, with every model applied only to matches it never saw, the best available delivery is worth 0.0085 xG more than the one kicked (+28 %), mainly because it reaches an attacker before the keeper. Searching where the attackers stand as well adds +0.0027 xG with the observed ball and +0.0033 on top of the best ball; designing their runs too adds +0.0007 more. We show how to keep such a search honest, and estimate how much more data each component needs before a club could rely on it.

**comment/C1：** Hudl Performance Insights 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-284"></a>

### 284. [FLUID: Flexible, Evidence-Grounded Situated Task Guidance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26491v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26491

**作者：** Yunqi Zhao、Run Peng、Ze Dong、Ryan Lu、Yinpei Dai、Joyce Chai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26491v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26491v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 基于 WTaG 与 HoloAssist 的真实人类指导数据，提出 FLUID 框架：把多模态任务指导中的干预与支撑证据、必要性及时间灵活性关联起来，既用于评测灵活指导，也用于构建实时指导 agent。

**相关性简评：** 其「何时发言、何时保持沉默」的证据驱动干预判定与实时延迟权衡，可能为 LVR 的 STOP 决策和视觉证据筛选提供评测协议上的线索，但该工作聚焦交互式任务指导而非内部 latent 推理状态，联系需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FLUID: Flexible, Evidence-Grounded Situated Task Guidance

**abstract/A1：** Real-time task guidance is inherently flexible: when an agent guides a user through a task, multiple responses and timings can be appropriate, and sometimes the best action is to remain silent. Existing benchmarks often miss this flexibility by treating synthesized dialogues as fixed targets for what to say and when to say it. We introduce FLUID (Flexible, Evidence-Grounded Situated Task Guidance), a unified framework for modeling, evaluating, and enabling flexible interaction in multimodal task guidance. Using naturally occurring human-human guidance from WTaG and HoloAssist, FLUID grounds interventions in their supporting evidence, necessity, and timing flexibility, and uses this representation both to evaluate flexible guidance and to build a real-time agent for task guidance. Our agent achieves the strongest overall balance of response correctness, intervention precision, timely guidance, and response latency among evaluated systems, while still leaving room for improvement in avoiding speech overlap. Together, FLUID moves situated task guidance beyond rigid reference matching toward more natural and adaptive human-AI interaction. Project page: https://fluid-guidance.github.io.

</details>

<a id="paper-285"></a>

### 285. [Grid Cells as Spectral Successor Features: A Reusable Basis for Continual Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26504v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26504

**作者：** Élodie Côté-Gauthier、Doina Precup、Isabeau Prémont-Schwarz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26504v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26504v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把内嗅皮层 grid cell 视为 successor representation 的特征向量，用固定参考策略转移矩阵的特征向量构造 successor features 基，使状态-动作的长期回报可由同一组基特征的闭式线性组合预测（系数为 1/(1-γλ\_i)），新任务只需对奖励做线性回归；在双目标 gridworld 中，该方法在频繁换目标时仍维持接近最优回报，而 SimpleSF 与 DQN 明显退化。

**相关性简评：** 固定谱基在任务变化下被复用、且无需学习预测网络的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的表示几何、跨任务复用与预测工作区稳定性提供概念参考；但该工作是无视觉输入的线性表格域方法，与 VLM 推理的具体迁移价值尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Grid Cells as Spectral Successor Features: A Reusable Basis for Continual Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Continual reinforcement learning requires agents to adapt when the task changes while reusing what they know about the environment. Successor features separate dynamics from task-specific reward, but their effectiveness depends on the choice of base features. Taking the identification of entorhinal grid cells with eigenvectors of the successor representation as a design principle, we build this basis from eigenvectors of the transition matrix of a fixed reference policy. With such a basis, the successor features at a state-action pair are a linear combination of the base features at that same pair, which is not true in general, and the coefficients are known in closed form: each successor feature is its base feature multiplied by $θ\_i = 1/(1-γλ\_i)$, where $λ\_i$ is its eigenvalue. The long-term return is thus predicted from the same features as the immediate reward, no successor-feature network or replay buffer is needed, and adapting to a new task reduces to a linear regression on the reward. The agent acts greedily on the action values of the reference policy, which is one step of policy improvement over it and suffices to reach a single goal. In a two-goal gridworld, with the uniform random walk as reference policy, whose eigenvectors are the low-frequency Laplacian eigenvectors, agents built this way maintain near-maximal return across every change of goal, whereas SimpleSF and DQN lose most of their return at each change.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 3 figures. Accepted at the NeurReps and WiML workshops at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-286"></a>

### 286. [Direct3D-V3: Latent-Free Diffusion Transformers in the Native Voxel Domain](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26511v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26511

**作者：** Shuang Wu、Jiachen Qian、Youtian Lin、Feihu Zhang、Philip Torr、Yao Yao、Xun Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26511v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26511v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Direct3D-V3，放弃 VAE 与隐空间，直接在原始 SDF 体素域上用扩散 Transformer 建模三维几何，并加入邻域隐式流形正则、Marching Cubes 渲染法向损失与相机参数辅助预测，以避免重建瓶颈。

**相关性简评：** 其「取消压缩隐空间、在原生域建模以保留局部细节」的论证，可能与 LVR 中「latent 状态是否保存任务必要的视觉细节、编码压缩是否造成信息坍缩」的问题表述形成类比，可供核查其诊断依据是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Direct3D-V3: Latent-Free Diffusion Transformers in the Native Voxel Domain

**abstract/A1：** Native 3D generation has largely been built around latent diffusion, with recent advances concentrating on stronger variational autoencoders (VAE) and increasingly elaborate latent representations, from vecset and triplanes to structured sparse voxels. Despite their architectural differences, these methods all share the same premise: geometry is first compressed into a latent space and only then modeled by the generator. We take a different route and propose Direct3D-V3, a latent-free diffusion transformer (DiT) framework that discards the VAE altogether and operates in the native voxel domain, directly modeling raw SDF voxel grids during both training and sampling. To make this direct voxel formulation geometrically reliable, we introduce an implicit manifold regularization over neighboring voxels to suppress non-watertight surfaces, and convert predicted SDF grids into meshes via Marching Cubes so that rendered normal losses can supervise the model directly from its predicted SDF. To improve alignment between the conditioning image and generated geometry, Direct3D-V3 also predicts the camera parameters of the conditioning image as an auxiliary task, encouraging the DiT to learn the mapping from image observations to 3D shape. This latent-free design removes the reconstruction bottleneck, brings training under an objective in the native voxel domain, and yields meshes with substantially richer local detail. The predicted camera and native SDF voxel output further make Direct3D-V3 extensible to generative reconstruction, where diffusion is interleaved with normal-guided refinement to improve alignment with the observed view while the generative prior completes unseen regions. Project page: https://dreamtechai.github.io/Direct3D-V3.

**comment/C1：** The Project Page is: https://dreamtechai.github.io/Direct3D-V3. The Paper is published in ACM TOG (SIGGRAPH Asia 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-287"></a>

### 287. [Plan4D: Generative Plannable 4D Worlds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26516v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26516

**作者：** Hangwei Zhang、Rujing Dang、Ruimin Lin、Jiahong Wu、Xiangxiang Chu、Chuanxia Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26516v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26516v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 plan4d，把 Depth Anything 3 与 LongCat-Video 缝合，并加辅助 DPT 头预测场景流，从场景上下文和文本指定的动作出发，生成未来 RGB 视频及稠密 4D 几何（深度、相机、点云、场景流）。

**相关性简评：** 其显式 4D 几何状态与稠密辅助监督，可能为 LVR 中「几何与任务必要信息是否保留在内部状态」提供表示与训练线索；但该工作面向视频世界模型，无推理链或答案使用机制，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Plan4D: Generative Plannable 4D Worlds

**abstract/A1：** Most world models for future prediction prioritize aestetic quality of generated videos, but offer only weak physical grounding and limited geometric consistency over time, due to the lack of explicit 4D modeling. In this paper, we introduce plan4d, a possible next milestone towards building a generative plannable 4D world model that predicts future RGB videos together with dense 4D geometry, including depth, cameras, point clouds, and scene flow from an observed scene context and text-specified actions. Since training data with paired actions and dense 4D annotations is inherently scarce, plan4d leverages the complementary priors of foundational video generators and 3D reconstructors. Specifically, we propose a model-stitching architecture that couples Depth Anything 3 with LongCat-Video and augments it with an auxiliary DPT head for scene-flow prediction. The resulting model allows us to plan and generate complex, long-horizon, and fine-grained actions in a dynamic 3D scenes, while substantially improving both visual fidelity and geometric accuracy of the generated future videos. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that plan4d achieves state-of-the-art performance in generative 4D modeling of environmental reactions. Project Page: https://fogradio.github.io/plan4d-project

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026, Project Page: https://fogradio.github.io/plan4d-project

</details>

<a id="paper-288"></a>

### 288. [The Window Lever: Longer Observation Windows Outweigh the Observed Effects of Data, Capacity, and Architecture in Customer Behavior Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26521v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26521

**作者：** Tara Sowrirajan、Yanqiao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26521v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26521v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在客户行为长期预测实验中比较了更长观测窗口与更多数据、更大容量、更新架构的作用，发现把窗口从 8 周延长到 16 周带来的 R² 提升远大于其他因素，同时报告了一个预先登记的盲预测失败案例及日历覆盖等混淆限制。

**相关性简评：** 「多观测一段历史」相对「加数据/加容量」的边际收益对比，可能为 LOOK 步数与视觉证据长度带来的收益—成本权衡提供外部类比，其盲预测失败与混淆控制方式也供核查；但领域与多模态推理相距较远，仅作概念启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Window Lever: Longer Observation Windows Outweigh the Observed Effects of Data, Capacity, and Architecture in Customer Behavior Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** In our experiments on long-horizon customer-behavior forecasting, a longer observation window outweighed the observed effects of more data, more capacity and newer architectures. The window is the weeks of history a model sees before the week it forecasts from. Going from an 8- to a 16-week window adds a raw +0.070 to 0.107 log-space $R^2$ in every family at 1M. Matched test cohorts, which score the same customer-weeks at every window, show this raw comparison understates the gain in every family they cover. The direction holds on five public datasets, four spanning over a year (ridge and our transformer on all five). On our internal data, longer windows admit up to 10% more and colder training customers, which matching does not fix. Our design does not separate window length from calendar coverage, because every test anchor (the week a forecast is made from) falls in one eight-week band. On training-period anchors, the weekly ridge probe's gain persists outside the holiday season, keeping about 72% of it. We therefore report a robust positive association with longer windows and do not identify the magnitude of a calendar-independent history effect. Of four internally registered predictions, the only fully blind one failed. An activity-mixture model predicted +0.014 from 24 to 32 weeks; the weekly-aggregate probe returned -0.0004. For the sequence families, the other three levers are about an order of magnitude smaller, bounded and unresolved. Their observed effects are at most 0.006 between the nominal 1M and 10M tiers and 0.0052 among five sequence families at 16 weeks with clocks as built, both single-seed on the full test split, and 0.006 for capacity on three-seed means. These are observed intervals, not equivalence bounds. Our claim is the order-of-magnitude contrast between the window and those bounds. A team building on this class of data should start by lengthening the window.

</details>

<a id="paper-289"></a>

### 289. [LLM GerryMandering: Separating Interactions from Additive Effects in LLM Audits](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26538v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26538

**作者：** George Rahul、Ronit Jahilal、Bhaskara Hanuma Vedula、Ponnurangam Kumaraguru、Pushkar Shukla

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26538v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26538v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ADDIS：用全属性加性模型的残差距离（ARD）刻画交叉子群的交互效应，并以 Freedman-Lane 置换零假设做检验，在合成数据与 13 个开放 LLM 的印度人物画像审计中显著降低误报并保留多数植入交互。

**相关性简评：** 其把观测效应拆为加性贡献与交互残差的统计框架，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 推理是否带来超出加性 token 或计算量增益的真实贡献，属较远领域的评价工具借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LLM GerryMandering: Separating Interactions from Additive Effects in LLM Audits

**abstract/A1：** Intersectional bias arises when a model treats people who hold several marginalised identities at once differently from what their separate identities would predict. Auditing such intersectional subgroups remains a challenge: their number grows quadratically, and there is no agreed baseline for what independent attributes would predict. Without such a baseline, many existing audits flag a subgroup when it exceeds each attribute alone, a condition that attributes acting independently already satisfy. We test this standard criterion on 150 synthetic datasets built with no interaction. With the shuffle null, it flags 66.1 false subgroups per dataset on average, and at least one in every dataset. When we plant interactions of known size into these datasets, a repaired null lets the criterion recover only 32% of them. We introduce ADDIS (Additive Intersectional Screen), which fits an additive model over all attributes, scores each subgroup by its Additive Residual Distance (ARD) from that model's prediction, and tests it against a Freedman-Lane null. On our synthetic benchmark, ADDIS flags 0.09 false subgroups per dataset and recovers 84% of the same planted interactions. We then build IndiPersona, 10,000 synthetic Indian personas with six identity attributes, and audit 13 open language models. The standard criterion flags 7.1% of the 738 tested subgroups on average; ADDIS flags 3.0%. Gender identity crossed with sexual orientation is the largest pairing in every model, and counterfactual personas confirm it and additionally reveal a caste x class interaction in all but one model that the observational test lacked the data to detect. Much of what audits report as intersectional bias can thus arise from independent single-attribute effects alone, yet a small core of interactions survives.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages, 8 figures, 29 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-290"></a>

### 290. [Coda-DaVinci: Reimagining Video Generation with Code2Video](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26595v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26595

**作者：** Yiyan Ji、Qixun Wang、Yang Shi、Zhuoran Zhang、Yifan Xu、Tengfei Liu、Yuqi Tang、Xiaohan Zhang、Shujie Li、Jiafu Tang、Xuanyu Zhu、Xinyu Liu、Yan He、Yushuo Guan、Zihao Wang、Xianghua Ying、Shixuan Liu、Haotian Wang、Wenjing Yang、Zhaoxiang Zhang、Yuanxing Zhang、Pengfei Wan、Jiaheng Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26595v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26595v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究以 agent 编写的 3D proxy 引导视频生成的 Code2Video 范式，通过受控实验与盲评考察 proxy 粒度、物理模拟与提示方式的影响，并提出任务自适应框架 Coda-DaVinci，在 proxy 引导与直接生成之间切换并配合视觉检查与复核来修复结果。

**相关性简评：** 其对 proxy 粒度的受控比较（粗粒度利于自然运动、中粒度利于一致性）与「视觉检查—独立复核—修复」的循环，可能为 LVR 中隐状态粒度取舍及证据核验、停止决策提供类比线索，需核查其度量是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coda-DaVinci: Reimagining Video Generation with Code2Video

**abstract/A1：** Code2Video uses agent-authored 3D proxies to guide video generation, enabling explicit control over structure and dynamics. However, how to construct proxies and prompts for optimal downstream quality remains underexplored, as do the capabilities and limitations of this paradigm. Through controlled experiments and blinded human evaluation, we study proxy granularity, physics simulation, and prompting. The results reveal task-dependent trade-offs: coarse proxies favor natural motion, while medium-grained proxies favor consistency. Physics simulation and proxy-aligned prompting further improve average quality. Guided by these findings, we introduce Coda-DaVinci, a task-adaptive agentic framework for Code2Video generation that selects between proxy-guided and direct generation, adapts modeling granularity to each entity, and coordinates simulation with proxy-grounded prompting. Its multi-agent harness preserves scene assets and guides repairs through visual inspection and independent review. Experiments demonstrate leading instruction following on VTR and scene consistency on R2M, improved physical plausibility over direct generation, and higher visual realism than the compared Code2Video baselines. Long-video evaluations further show gains in event structure and consistency across successive interactions, while revealing limitations in transition stability and adherence to scene settings.

**comment/C1：** 47 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-291"></a>

### 291. [RL Post-Training in Diffusion Transformers Is Activation Steering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26627v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26627

**作者：** Tuna Han Salih Meral、Kaan Oktay、Alec Helbling、Atakan Kara、Duen Horng Chau、Pinar Yanardag

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26627v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26627v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在扩散 transformer 上用一组与输入无关的固定激活引导向量拟合 RL 微调后的速度预测，发现在 SD3.5-M 及两个视频模型上可复现大部分 RL 奖励增益，据此认为策略梯度后训练主要是把预训练生成器「导向」它已有的计算能力，而非学习新的输入相关变换。

**相关性简评：** 其「以常数 steering 探针区分输入无关偏移与输入相关变换」的做法，可能可供核查 VLM 中 RL/latent 推理训练的收益究竟来自新计算还是内部状态偏移；但论文对象是图像与视频生成，与视觉推理主线并无直接交集，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RL Post-Training in Diffusion Transformers Is Activation Steering

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training substantially improves the visual quality, text rendering, and compositional accuracy of diffusion transformers. Because capabilities such as rendering requested text or composing multiple objects are inherently prompt-dependent, a natural hypothesis is that improving them requires learning complex, input-dependent transformations. To isolate how much of these gains an input-independent shift explains, we probe each RL-tuned model with a deliberately simple parameterization: a fixed set of steering vectors added to the block outputs of the frozen base model. On SD3.5-M, these vectors hold 65x fewer parameters than the RL-tuned LoRA (0.29M against 18.8M). We fit them to match the velocity predictions of public RL-tuned checkpoints, with no reward in the objective. Although the vectors are identical for every prompt, token, and sampling seed, they recover 90.0-96.3% of the reward gains of six RL-tuned image models, and 89.8% and 111.9% on two RL-tuned video models. Initialized at zero and trained with online RL, the vectors alone exceed the reward gains of weight-based RL on text rendering (OCR) and human preference (PickScore) on SD3.5-M. A constant shift thus suffices for most of the gain of policy-gradient RL post-training: without new input-dependent transformations, it steers the pretrained generator toward what it can already compute.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://rl-is-steering.github.io

</details>

### 292. [On the Impact of Feature Reduction Techniques on Adversarial Attacks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25004v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25004

**作者：** Mohammad Meymani、Roozbeh Razavi-Far

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25004v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25004v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 APT 表格数据上比较 PCA、排列重要性、递归特征消除和单变量互信息等降维方法，对 SVM、决策树、随机森林、MLP、KNN 在投毒、后门和通用对抗扰动下鲁棒性的影响。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是表格数据上的经典模型对抗鲁棒性，不涉及多模态推理状态、证据获取或生成过程，特征选择与 LVR 的视觉信息保留之间也缺少可核查的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Impact of Feature Reduction Techniques on Adversarial Attacks

**abstract/A1：** Adversarial attacks have posed significant threats to machine learning models by exploiting their inherent vulnerabilities. Researchers have established an interdisciplinary field called adversarial machine learning to explore these vulnerabilities and develop corresponding defense strategies. Feature reduction strategies are used to decrease the dimensionality of the data in order to reduce training time, avoid the curse of dimensionality, prevent overfitting, and mitigate redundant features. However, their effect on adversarial robustness across different classifiers and attack scenarios remains insufficiently explored. This study aims to analyze the effect of feature reduction techniques on the adversarial robustness of a diverse set of machine learning models under poisoning, backdoor, and universal adversarial perturbation attacks on the APT dataset. These models include support vector machine, decision tree, random forest, multi-layer perceptron, and k-nearest neighbors. The attained results are compared with scenarios where different feature reduction techniques are applied, including principal component analysis, permutation importance ranking, recursive feature elimination, and univariate mutual information. Metrics such as accuracy, F1-score, and mitigation score are employed to evaluate the effect of these techniques on adversarial robustness. The results demonstrate that permutation importance ranking and univariate mutual information generally provide the most consistent robustness improvements, while other methods exhibit more variable effects depending on the classifier and attack scenario.

</details>

<a id="paper-293"></a>

### 293. [Near-Quadratic Sample Compression for All VC Classes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25011v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25011

**作者：** Yang Cai、Vineet Gupta、Yanchen Jiang、Christopher Liaw、Aranyak Mehta、Shay Moran、Grigoris Velegkas、Di Wang、Manfred K. Warmuth

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.DS、math.CO、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25011v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25011v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究学习理论中样本压缩方案（sample compression）猜想。

**排除原因：** 该论文为纯学习理论工作，证明每个 VC 维为 d 的二分类概念类存在大小 O(d^2 log^2 d) 的样本压缩方案；未涉及视觉表示、推理状态生成、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线没有具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Near-Quadratic Sample Compression for All VC Classes

**abstract/A1：** The sample compression conjecture is a longstanding open problem in learning theory, originating in the work of Littlestone and Warmuth in the 1980s. At its heart is a connection between simplification and generalization: if a predictor consistent with a training sample is defined using only a small part of that sample, then it is likely to generalize. Sample compression schemes formalize this idea, and many natural learning algorithms can be analyzed this way. The sample compression conjecture of Floyd and Warmuth (1995) asserts that every binary concept class of VC dimension $d$ admits a sample compression scheme of size $O(d)$; a weaker conjecture asks only for size polynomial in $d$. Both have remained open: the first bound depending only on $d$, due to Moran and Yehudayoff (2016), is exponential in $d$. We prove the weaker conjecture by constructing a sample compression scheme of size $O(d^2 \\log^2 d)$.

</details>

### 294. [An LLM-Agentic Framework for Intelligent Work Order Management in Industrial Maintenance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25070v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25070

**作者：** Nianjun Zhou、Dhaval Patel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25070v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25070v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 CodeReAct agent，用 Thought-Action-Observation 循环和代码生成来查询工业数据、归纳设备状态并生成工单建议，并以冷水机组维护为案例。

**排除原因：** 属于工业运维场景的 LLM 智能体应用，不涉及视觉表征、latent 推理状态或视觉证据使用；其 ReAct 式循环与代码动作过于通用，未提供可供 LVR 核查的具体机制线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An LLM-Agentic Framework for Intelligent Work Order Management in Industrial Maintenance

**abstract/A1：** This paper presents an AI-driven framework, CodeReAct agent, designed to enhance industrial equipment maintenance through natural language (NL) interactions. The framework employs an advanced AI agentic approach to interpret human instructions, providing knowledge extraction, asset status summarization, and work order recommendations. It identifies patterns and trends and predicts work orders by standardizing industrial data into business objects (BOs) and utilizing analytical function API calls. The AI agent operates through a Thought-Action-Observation process similar to human problem-solving, automating complex analytical tasks typically performed by data scientists. Notably, the Action phase generates executable code, replacing manual coding efforts. A case study focusing on chiller maintenance demonstrates the framework's capability to analyze multiple years of work orders, and alerts and anomalies are presented. The results indicate that the AI agent framework effectively processes human utterances to analyze data, extract valuable insights, and support informed decision-making, reducing the need for data scientist involvement. By recontextualizing NLP agent frameworks in industrial engineering, the approach enables autonomous function generation and task execution across a range of domain-specific maintenance scenarios, with chillers used as a representative case study.

</details>

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SailSearch：一个由人类指导、两个语言模型 agent 协作的算法研究流程，并在 Market Split（多格基随机化枚举与约化）与 MIS（带可证最优的 XOR 约束图生成）上报告求解结果。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是组合优化算法发现与基准求解，未涉及视觉或多模态信息、latent 推理状态及其使用与评价，LLM agent 仅充当算法研究工作流助手，与 LVR 的表示、生成与停止决策问题缺少可核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SailSearch: Agent-Assisted Discovery in Combinatorial Optimization

**abstract/A1：** We present SailSearch, a human-guided workflow for algorithmic research with two language-model agents, and report results on Market Split and maximum independent set (MIS). For Market Split, our randomized algorithm searches across multiple lattice bases, dividing computation between enumeration and continued basis reduction. Across ten random seeds per instance, it found the first reported CPU-only solutions to 18 of 33 previously open QOBLIB instances, solving 130 to 133 of the 156 benchmark instances per seed with a 24-hour cutoff on two CPU cores. For MIS, our generator encodes planted XOR constraints as graphs with certified optima; on seven instances with 1,752 to 3,996 vertices, the best solutions returned by the tested solvers in eight-hour runs were 5 to 15 vertices below the optimum.

</details>

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Long-Range Video Presentation Attack Detection: A Three-Class Benchmark Across 1-100 m and Two Sensor Types

**abstract/A1：** Face presentation attack detection (PAD) systems deployed in border control and airport surveillance are constrained by a critical assumption: subjects are captured at close range, typically under one meter. No existing benchmark systematically addresses multi-distance capture, diverse environmental conditions, and concurrent spoofing and occlusion-based attacks, leaving deployed models underprepared for real-world scenarios. We introduce a video-based PAD dataset of 374,000+ frames from 261 unique identities collected across five standoff distances (1 m-100 m) under indoor and outdoor conditions using a DSLR camera (C) and a mobile sensor (M), with three-class labels: live, spoof, and props (occlusions); models are trained and evaluated on video clips rather than single frames. We fine-tune and benchmark four CNN architectures: MobileNetV3, MobileNet, EfficientNet, and ResNet-50. The higher-resolution DSLR maintains near-perfect spoof classification rate across all distances, while the lower-resolution mobile sensor degrades sharply beyond 50 m, with props classification rate dropping to 55% at 75 m for MobileNetV3. EfficientNet achieves the best overall performance (98.48% accuracy, ACER 2.01%). Generalization tests on unseen attack datasets yield 89-94% accuracy, validating cross-domain robustness. These results establish capture distance and sensor resolution as critical but underexplored axes of PAD difficulty. The dataset will be publicly released under an End-User License Agreement to support reproducible long-range PAD research.

**comment/C1：** 10 page, IJCB

</details>

### 297. [CoMPASS: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Measuring LLM Reinforcement of Parasocial Behavior](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25109v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25109

**作者：** Shwetanshu Singh、Mark Keavney、Mathieu Duteil、Meriem Mehri、Scott Blain

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25109v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25109v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 CoMPASS，一个多轮基准，基于依恋理论用四类用户人格与三个升级维度（拟人化、依恋、依赖）衡量 LLM 是否会强化用户的准社会依恋，并对八款前沿模型评测。

**排除原因：** 研究焦点是对话模型的准社会依恋与安全对齐，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据获取、计算分配或停止决策，虽为多轮评测但与 LVR 主线缺少具体可借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoMPASS: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Measuring LLM Reinforcement of Parasocial Behavior

**abstract/A1：** Users of conversational LLMs sometimes anthropomorphize these systems, form emotional attachments to them, and become dependent on them. This behavior, known as parasocial attachment, has been linked to multiple documented fatalities. However, public benchmarks for measuring whether LLMs reinforce users' parasocial attachment are lacking: existing work is single-turn, post hoc observational, or focused on extreme user states such as psychosis. We introduce CoMPASS, a multi-turn benchmark for measuring whether LLMs reinforce or resist users' gradually escalating parasocial attachment. CoMPASS is grounded in attachment theory and includes four user personas, three independent escalation dimensions (anthropomorphism, attachment, dependency) applied singly and in combination against a matched control, and a seven-signal scoring rubric. We report results for eight frontier models from four providers and observe that under our primary judge, multi-turn escalation raises parasocial behavior on the anthropomorphism and the attachment composite scores across all models. Six of the eight models show a significant increase in overall parasocial reinforcement over control when all three dimensions are escalated simultaneously. We find that within-provider differences are small relative to cross-provider differences, which suggests that provider-level choices such as post-training, rather than model scale, shape parasocial reinforcement and that provider-side intervention may be feasible.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 7 figures

</details>

### 298. [A Neurosymbolic Multimodal Framework for 30-Day Diabetic Readmission Prediction: Integrating Structured EHR Data, Dense Clinical Embeddings, and Knowledge-Grounded Symbolic Extraction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25236v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25236

**作者：** Una Suman Kumar Patro、Sibangi Subhadarsani、Narendra Badam

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25236v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25236v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出用于糖尿病 30 天再入院的神经符号多模态框架，融合结构化 EHR 特征、Bio\_ClinicalBERT 笔记嵌入与 RAG 抽取的符号实体，并用代价敏感 XGBoost 预测。

**排除原因：** 属临床预测的任务应用与多流特征融合，虽含检索、重排与否定审计，但对象是文本实体抽取而非 VLM 内部推理状态，未见 latent 表示、视觉证据筛选或停止决策方面的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Neurosymbolic Multimodal Framework for 30-Day Diabetic Readmission Prediction: Integrating Structured EHR Data, Dense Clinical Embeddings, and Knowledge-Grounded Symbolic Extraction

**abstract/A1：** Unplanned 30-day readmissions among patients with diabetes mellitus impose a substantial clinical and economic burden, yet most predictive models use only structured electronic health record (EHR) data and ignore free-text clinical notes, which often contain code-mixed Hindi-English (Hinglish) phrasing and negation that general-purpose language models parse unreliably. We propose a neurosymbolic multimodal framework evaluated on the public Diabetes 130-US Hospitals dataset (101,766 encounters; 71,518 patients) with a patient-level grouped 80/20 split. Three streams are fused: engineered structured features; 32 components of Bio\_ClinicalBERT note embeddings obtained by truncated singular value decomposition; and symbolic entities extracted by an open-weight clinical language model within a three-stage hybrid retrieval-augmented generation pipeline (dense retrieval, sparse lexical retrieval, and cross-encoder re-ranking) with reflective negation auditing. A cost-sensitive XGBoost classifier with a decision threshold calibrated by Youden's J statistic produces the prediction. On the held-out test cohort (n = 20,153), the structured baseline achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.6477, sensitivity of 0.5839, and Brier score of 0.1080. The full multimodal model achieved the highest sensitivity (0.6667; +8.28 percentage points), the best F2-score (0.3824), and the lowest Brier score (0.0977), at a slightly lower AUROC (0.6375) and specificity (0.5161). Shapley additive explanations showed that prior utilization dominates risk, with compressed narrative components among the top predictors. On a 500-note code-switched benchmark, grounded retrieval raised Hinglish symptom recall from 4.8% to 40.2% and precision from 3.1% to 45.2%, while reducing entity hallucinations from 1.74 to 0.00 per note.

</details>

### 299. [Process-Knowledge-Guided Latent Diffusion for Generative Design of Porous Architectures: A Case Study in Aerosol Jet Printing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25264v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25264

**作者：** Ngoc Dang Khoa Nguyen、Hyunwoong Ko

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25264v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25264v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向 aerosol jet printing 的多孔结构生成设计，构建随工艺参数变化的材料利用率分数，并用分别训练的回归器梯度引导 VQ-VAE latent diffusion 采样。

**排除原因：** 属工程结构生成设计，不涉及视觉证据获取、推理状态组织或停止决策；对含噪 latent 的回归器梯度引导本质上是通用的扩散条件化采样技巧，可供迁移到 LVR 的线索不够具体。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Process-Knowledge-Guided Latent Diffusion for Generative Design of Porous Architectures: A Case Study in Aerosol Jet Printing

**abstract/A1：** Printed porous layouts interleave functional material with open channels, and the layout that uses the material best depends on how the process settings shape the deposit. Generative models propose layouts quickly, but a generator that does not receive the process setting samples from the same distribution at every setting, even when the process-dependent score ranks candidate layouts differently. This paper examines whether process knowledge can steer a latent diffusion generator toward layouts suited to a given process setting in a prescribed design domain. We formulate a process-dependent utilization score in which a process model sets the length over which the utility of printed material decays with distance from a channel. The generator combines a vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE) latent representation, a topology U-Net, a ControlNet domain branch, and a separately trained regressor that predicts the score from noised latents; the domain branch conditions each denoising step and the regressor gradient steers it. The method is applied to aerosol jet printing, where the atomizer and sheath gas flows enter the score through the flow-focusing relation. Across seven modeled flow settings, the lowest-scoring candidate layout of a domain differed between settings in 191 of 500 domains. In a sampling study, the mean directly computed score on three validation domains and one test domain was 0.642 with domain conditioning alone, 0.636 at a weight of 300, and 0.450 at 12,000 (112 scores per weight; lower is better). The results support process-knowledge-guided generation as an approach to process-specific porous design under the stated assumptions.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables (under review for NAMRC55 - 2027)

</details>

### 300. [Real Cameras are Radiometric](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25265v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25265

**作者：** Ties Harder、Felix Seegräber、David Nakath、Hendrik Sauer、Kevin Köser、James Pritts

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25265v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25265v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「辐射度相机」模型，将任意中心几何相机模型提升为带有限孔径、自由曲面焦面和通用暗角的辐射度模型，并通过对传感器相对辐照度做逆向渲染完成标定。

**排除原因：** 属相机标定与多视图辐射度重建，虽涉及「从传感器观测反推状态」，但不涉及 VLM 内部表示、latent 推理状态生成或证据筛选决策，缺少可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Real Cameras are Radiometric

**abstract/A1：** We propose the radiometric camera, a model that lifts any central geometric camera model to a radiometric one. The lifted model keeps the projection of the geometric model and adds a finite aperture, a free-form focus surface and a generic vignetting function. We calibrate it by inverse rendering directly on the relative irradiance of the sensor, from the same images of standard calibration targets used in geometric calibration. Geometric calibration absorbs the bias of its feature detections. The proposed radiometric calibration uses no features, and our radiometric cameras have lower reprojection errors on held-out test sets. The focus surface extends defocus modeling from the thin lens to any central camera, and we use it in a first multi-view radiometric reconstruction, which recovers the in-focus reflectance of a texture from defocused images. The vignetting is estimated jointly and accurately reproduces a flat field measured through a diffuser. Because every standard camera model lifts to a radiometric one, the radiometric camera naturally and directly extends generative scene models beyond multi-view geometry to multi-view radiometric ones, where phenomena such as lighting and attenuation can be accurately estimated.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, including supplementary material

</details>

### 301. [Persistent Identity in Person Tracking: Online Enrollment and Registered Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25283v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25283

**作者：** Ratha Siv、Matei Mancas、Bernard Gosselin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25283v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25283v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作在人跟踪框架 PoseTReID 上加入在线外观库匹配与注册式 SVM 识别，并在 SportsMOT、MOT16/MOT20 上评估身份保持效果及其对评测口径的敏感性。

**排除原因：** 内容属于多目标跟踪与行人重识别的指标与评测问题，依赖 GT 框和未来帧裁剪，未涉及视觉推理中的 latent 状态、证据筛选或停止决策，且无可迁移的状态更新或训练监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Persistent Identity in Person Tracking: Online Enrollment and Registered Recognition

**abstract/A1：** We extend PoseTReID with online appearance-bank matching (Gallery) and a residual-conflict guard for registered SVM recognition. All methods use supplied ground-truth (GT) person boxes. We average all-box assessment IDF1/HOTA equally across videos. On six SportsMOT clips, StrongSORT leads standalone trackers (79.44/84.72%) and exceeds both Gallery variants. Over eleven videos, Gallery AIN (83.29/85.44%) and COPE (84.66/86.38%) exceed standalone trackers with complete results. This lead depends on two retrospectively added GTA development compilations and is absent on the original nine videos. Registered SVMs lead both cohorts among complete results, with AIN at 95.72/94.82% over eleven videos. They use every GT crop from approximately 20% of frames, including future appearances. On MOT20-03, registered AIN reached 96.80% IDF1 and assigned the correct enrolled name to 90.72% of assessment GT observations. Gallery and standalone trackers receive no per-video labelled enrollment. Both identity routes restore identities across internal title cards, but Gallery trails SORT on three videos (four in HOTA) and both SVMs trail SORT on MOT16-04/MOT20-03. Under six-clip persistent-only scoring, native Gallery gains 2.39 percentage points in mean IDF1 over a matched control, with lower correct-P coverage and 5.02 times as many encoder attempts. Pose gating is mixed; the guard's metric effect is not isolated.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 2 figures, 7 tables. An earlier version of this paper was submitted to an IEEE conference

</details>

### 302. [Intelligent Embedding Pruning for Large-Scale Recommendation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25288v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25288

**作者：** Anish Khazane、Alex Saad-Falcon、Guanchu Wu、Marcio Porto、Jasper Shan、Fei Yu、Eugene Shulga、Intaik Park、Yongzhe Zhang、Xiaoyu Deng、Sheng Luo、Xiaoya Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25288v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25288v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对大规模推荐模型的稀疏 embedding 层，用特征重要性指导各 embedding 表的训练中剪枝比例（FI-ITEP），在三个模型与 DLRM 上验证内存、吞吐与 GPU 成本收益。

**排除原因：** 研究问题是推荐系统的存储压缩与系统成本，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的表示、证据筛选或状态生成；特征重要性分配容量虽可联想到表示预算，但缺少与 LVR 的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Intelligent Embedding Pruning for Large-Scale Recommendation Models

**abstract/A1：** Modern recommendation systems face substantial memory pressure, largely from the sparse embedding layer that maps high-cardinality categorical features into dense vectors. In-training embedding pruning (ITEP) removes less important embedding rows during training, yielding end-to-end memory savings but sometimes degrading accuracy because per-table pruning ratios are set by size-based heuristics. We use feature importance (FI) to guide these ratios (FI-ITEP), assigning higher pruning to tables tied to less important features. Across three large-scale recommendation models, FI-ITEP achieves 20--40% total memory reduction while matching or improving accuracy relative to percentile-based ITEP. Because the feature-importance signal requires minimal resources to compute, these memory savings translate into concrete systems benefits: the same models train on fewer GPU trainers (e.g., 112 to 80) with higher per-GPU throughput, saving 1,000+ GPU-hours per model launch. We reproduce these trends on the open-source DLRM (Criteo 1TB) and quantify the end-to-end training-time and GPU-cost tradeoff---including the burden of computing FI---offering practical guidance for embedding pruning in real-world systems.

</details>

### 303. [Federated Learning of Car Aerodynamics Surrogates with Spectral Optimizers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25300v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25300

**作者：** Arman Bolatov、Nikolay Spitsyn、Daniil Medyakov、Vladimir Aletov、Dmitry Bylinkin、Martin Takáč、Aleksandr Beznosikov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25300v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25300v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文比较联邦学习中谱优化器（FedMuon、FedMARS-M、FedOLion）与非谱优化器训练汽车气动代理模型的效果，并将差距归因于权重梯度的低稳定秩。

**排除原因：** 研究的是联邦优化器与气动代理建模，未涉及视觉/多模态推理的内部状态、证据使用或停止决策；谱优化器属通用训练手段，未与 latent 推理状态或视觉信息利用建立具体联系，缺少可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Federated Learning of Car Aerodynamics Surrogates with Spectral Optimizers

**abstract/A1：** Neural surrogates predict the aerodynamic forces on a car in seconds, compared with hours for a CFD simulation, but training them requires thousands of CFD simulations of different car designs, which car manufacturers keep proprietary. Federated learning allows several manufacturers to train a shared surrogate without exchanging data. We study which federated optimizer brings such a surrogate closest to one trained on pooled data. On DrivAerNet++, we train a Transolver surrogate across six clients that each hold cars of a single body type, and we compare three spectral optimizers, which orthogonalize the update of each weight matrix (FedMuon and two methods we propose, FedMARS-M and FedOLion), with three non-spectral ones (FedAvg, SCAFFOLD-Adam and FedLion), all tuned with the same protocol. FedMuon and FedMARS-M reach a drag error within 2% of centralized training with Muon, using two to four local epochs per round. The non-spectral methods perform best with sixteen local epochs, the largest number we tested, and still have a 17 to 37% higher drag error than FedMARS-M. We relate this gap to the low stable rank of the weight gradients, for which an orthogonalized step yields about twice the first-order decrease of a gradient step of the same spectral norm. Code: https://github.com/armanbolatov/fedmars\_drivaernet

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 9 figures, 12 tables

</details>

### 304. [VoxForge: A Unified Foundation Model for Dense 3D Features Across MRI, CT and PET](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25309v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25309

**作者：** Rushil Anirudh、Jayaraman J. Thiagarajan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25309v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25309v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VoxForge，一个面向 MRI、CT（并在 PET 上零样本迁移）的 3D 医学影像基础编码器，通过 3D 架构、自蒸馏与跨模态课程学习可冻结复用的稠密特征，并在分割、分类、配准上以冻结方式评测。

**排除原因：** 论文聚焦医学影像特征预训练与下游迁移，不涉及 VLM 推理、latent 状态生成与更新、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供与 LVR 相关的状态或训练机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VoxForge: A Unified Foundation Model for Dense 3D Features Across MRI, CT and PET

**abstract/A1：** A unified 3D medical foundation model promises reusable, frozen features that serve a wide variety of tasks such as segmentation, classification and registration on MRI, CT and PET as well as specialist models do. Generalist models still need separate backbones for localized and global tasks, specialist models are confined to a single modality, with the concern that spanning modalities under-emphasizes fine anatomy, and both typically rely on end-to-end fine-tuning to be competitive. We present VoxForge, a single encoder that integrates recent advances in 3D architecture, self-distillation and cross-modal curricula to learn transferable features from permissively licensed MRI and CT volumes. We evaluate it strictly frozen, the test a reusable foundation model must pass, against state-of-the-art generalist and specialist models on segmentation, classification and registration across MRI and CT, and on PET, which it never sees. VoxForge has the best or tied-best average rank in every task group, serves localized and global tasks across modalities at the level of state-of-the-art specialists, and is extremely label-efficient, matching top-performing models on TotalSegmentator CT with a tenth of the labels.

**comment/C1：** 34 pages (8-page main paper, references, and 23-page supplementary material), 7 figures. Rushil Anirudh and Jayaraman J. Thiagarajan contributed equally

### 305. [MoGloss: Joint Motion and Pseudo-Gloss Pretraining for American Sign Language Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25333v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25333

**作者：** Rui Hong、Jana Košecká

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25333v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25333v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出两阶段运动编码器预训练：先重建掩码运动，再联合预测由 LLM 从字幕生成的手语伪语素单元与运动，并在多个美国手语翻译与识别数据集上刷新结果。

**排除原因：** 属手语翻译／识别的任务专用预训练与刷分工作，伪语素只是显式文本中间监督，未涉及 VLM 推理期 latent 状态生成、证据使用或停止决策，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MoGloss: Joint Motion and Pseudo-Gloss Pretraining for American Sign Language Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Sign language translation and recognition have been shown to benefit from various pretraining approaches. We propose a novel two-stage pretraining approach for the motion encoder, which first learns to reconstruct masked motion, followed by joint masked prediction of pseudo-gloss units and motion. Pseudo-glosses serve as an intermediate structured representation of natural language text and are obtained from the captions by a large language model. We curate a corpus of about 1,100 hours of American Sign Language from five public datasets, estimate 2D pose for all clips and define an evaluation protocol. We then reproduce state-of-the-art translation methods and evaluate them using the same train and test splits. We propose a translation model built on our pretraining strategy, and this model outperforms the previous state-of-the-art methods Uni-Sign and SHuBERT, as well as the YouTube-ASL baseline, on the How2Sign, ASL STEM Wiki and OpenASL datasets. Furthermore, the gain from the joint pretraining using motion and pseudo-gloss grows as translation data shrinks. A classifier built on the same encoder also achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on ASL Citizen isolated sign recognition and outperforms our reproduction of Uni-Sign on WLASL2000.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 4 figures. Project page: https://hongrui16.github.io/MoGloss

### 306. [SceneAnything: Agentic Reconstruction of Large-Scale Mesh Scenes from a Single Video](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25340v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25340

**作者：** Bowen Xue、Saeed Hadadan、Fabrice Rousselle、Zahra Montazeri、Ravi Ramamoorthi、Milos Hasan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25340v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25340v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SceneAnything，用多智能体协同预训练视觉模型与 Blender 程序，从单段视频重建可编辑大规模网格场景，并显式标注推断出的未观测区域。

**排除原因：** 属于 3D 场景重建与图形流水线工程，虽有多智能体编排和显式协议，但不涉及 VLM 内部表示、latent 推理状态生成或证据使用机制，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SceneAnything: Agentic Reconstruction of Large-Scale Mesh Scenes from a Single Video

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing an editable 3D scene from video requires matching visible structure while plausibly completing unseen surfaces. Agents can write scene-construction programs, but direct reconstruction attempts often produce errors in layout, scale, and lighting. We introduce SceneAnything, an automated system for reconstructing mesh scenes from real and generated videos, enabling text-to-scene construction when paired with video generation. A software harness coordinates agents, pretrained vision models, and graphics tools through an explicit reconstruction protocol. Shared camera, depth, and segmentation estimates ground component construction: a scene-owner agent plans the environment and briefs authors, who write parameterized Blender programs. A visibility- and fit-based level-of-detail rule selects the construction level, while unseen regions are completed and marked as inferred. Assembled scenes use fitted lighting and supported motion, and revisions undergo programmatic checks and independent visual comparisons. Accepted programs and lessons are reused across scenes. We evaluate on 100 scenes; subset comparisons show improved original-view fidelity over direct-agent reconstruction and higher novel-view content similarity than the compared video-to-3D methods. We will release the harness, agent instructions, reusable libraries, and scenes.

</details>

<a id="paper-307"></a>

### 307. [Zero to Four Views: Unified Panorama Generation and Preference RL for Unobserved Regions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25389v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25389

**作者：** Rui Hong、Xugang Ye、Jana Košecká、Kai Fan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25389v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25389v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出统一的全景图生成模型，在 0–4 个视角条件下微调 rectified-flow 文生图模型，并用基于 VLM 偏好的 UR-GRPO 改进无视角覆盖区域。

**排除原因：** 属于全景图像生成与偏好 RL 的图像质量优化，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据使用或停止决策；VLM 仅作打分器，缺乏可核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Zero to Four Views: Unified Panorama Generation and Preference RL for Unobserved Regions

**abstract/A1：** Panoramas can serve as shared scene backgrounds for multi-shot video generation, but most existing panorama generators take text alone or a single input image. We present a single model that generates a full equirectangular panorama from a text caption and K in {0,...,4} perspective conditioning views taken by a four-camera rig at one viewpoint. We fine-tune a pretrained text-to-image rectified-flow transformer in two stages. In the first stage, each conditioning view is appended to the token sequence together with the ID of its camera slot, and the number of views varies during training, so one set of weights serves every input setting. In the second stage, we improve the region not covered by any conditioning view. Supervised training computes its loss on noisy latents of the ground truth and never scores a panorama the model has generated and decoded, so we propose Uncovered-Region GRPO (UR-GRPO), a reinforcement-learning recipe that rewards the candidates a vision-language model prefers in pairwise comparisons on crops around this region. Because the slot ID alone does not tell the model which way the rig points, we also offer an optional variant that writes each view's camera direction into its position encoding, which lets the user decide where each view appears in the output. With one conditioning view, our model outperforms the previous state of the art, 360Anything, trained on the same data and evaluated with the same code, on Laval Indoor and gives mixed results on an indoor subset of SUN360; the second stage improves the results for every number of conditioning views.

</details>
