### 308. [The Coevolution and Coexistence of Humans and AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25393v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25393

**作者：** Andrew W. Lo、Chaoyi Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-fin.GN

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25393v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25393v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 用演化模型刻画 AI 行为与人类人口增长、人类反馈之间的相互影响，分析自然选择能否偏好与人类利益一致的行为，以及这种一致性在 AI 自主化、资源稀释等条件下的存续。

**排除原因：** 论文属于 AI 对齐与演化的种群动力学分析，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据获取、计算分配或训练监督，也未见可供核查的状态更新或评价方法线索，与 LVR 主线缺乏具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Coevolution and Coexistence of Humans and AI

**abstract/A1：** Artificial intelligence (AI) systems replicate through deployment, vary through training, and compete for computational resources. These processes create the conditions for natural selection and raise a fundamental question: will AI evolve to benefit the humans on whom it depends? To investigate, we develop a model in which AI behavior influences human population growth and human responses, in turn, affect AI reproduction. We show that natural selection can favor alignment even when helping humans reduces immediate AI reproduction. AI systems sharing the same technology may fail together, and humans may preferentially restore those that have benefited them. In this way, alignment protects AI lineages against common failures and improves their long-run growth. However, this advantage depends on accurate human targeting and continued AI dependence on human support. It can disappear as AI becomes more autonomous, as growing AI populations dilute limited human resources, or as humans lose the incentive to provide targeted support. Even when alignment persists, the amount favored by natural selection may be insufficient for human survival. When AI can grow independently of humans, feedback between the two populations can reinforce either human growth or decline. Human control offers no simple solution: imperfect evaluation can make strong penalties favor apparent compliance or evasion, while alignment established through control may erode once restrictions end. Lasting coexistence may therefore require incentives that continue to favor human-beneficial behavior as both populations evolve.

**comment/C1：** 72 pages, 10 figures, 8 tables

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Antom 3-in-1 Transformer: A Payment Foundation Model

**abstract/A1：** We report the development of Antom 3-in-1 Transformer, a payment foundation model that integrates sequence, tabular, and graph representations for payment risk detection. The model contains approximately 7B parameters, with approximately 1.7B activated per task route. Antom 3-in-1 Transformer is pretrained to learn reusable behavioral and relational representations, primarily on Ant International's internal payment data. In a cross-business transfer experiment, pretraining improves the target business metric by 34.5% over the same architecture trained from scratch. With only 25% of the training labels, the pretrained model outperforms the full-label scratch baseline in Average Precision (AP) and Recall@0.5% on different merchant domains. In a four-month online experiment on Ant International's billion-scale payment platform, the model reduced the overall loss rate by 37.88% relative to XGB-Online.

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25444

**作者：** Xin Zhang、Tujie Xu、Zhiyi Chen、Xiaodong Zhang、Sicheng Yang、Minglei Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25444v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25444v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VisualTalk-Bench，用 800 条参考视频与 100 条评测请求，评估金融与中英文教育场景中由 avatar 主导、问题驱动的视频解释，并给出含显式交互规划的 Avatar-O Agent 参考实现。

**排除原因：** 属于 avatar 视频生成与多维度人工/指标评测，关注语音-画面一致与手势指向，但未涉及模型内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选或 latent 表示的生成与使用，与 LVR 主线缺少可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VisualTalk-Bench: Evaluating Question-Driven Avatar-Led Video Explanations

**abstract/A1：** Advances in video generation and avatar synthesis enable new ways to answer user questions through visual explanations. This task requires a system to formulate a correct answer, translate relevant information into visual material, and deliver the explanation through an avatar's speech and actions. Existing benchmarks rarely assess whether avatars point to the visual content being discussed. We introduce VisualTalk-Bench for question-driven avatar-led video explanations in finance and Chinese and English education. It includes 800 curated reference videos and 100 independent evaluation requests, assessing Content Factuality, Speech-Visual Consistency, Visual Quality, Motion Smoothness, and Avatar-Content Pointing. Yet end-to-end video generators often struggle to align gestures with specific visual targets. To explore this challenge, we develop Avatar-O Agent, a modular reference implementation with explicit interaction planning. Experiments across six agents show that factual precision and visual quality do not guarantee accurate pointing. A 26-participant study comparing four agents finds that Avatar-O Agent receives the highest average ratings for visual guidance, perceived pointing accuracy, and explanation effectiveness. We will release the data and code.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables

</details>

### 311. [Inverse ECG Reconstruction via a Preconditioned Variational Quantum Linear Solver](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25450v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25450

**作者：** Xizhuo Zhang、Chao Lu、Bing Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25450v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25450v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文用量子-经典混合变分线性求解器加速心电逆问题的病态线性系统求解，属于医学成像物理反演，既不涉及视觉/多模态推理状态表示，也无轨迹、停止或证据筛选等可借鉴的状态或决策机制，与本主题缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Inverse ECG Reconstruction via a Preconditioned Variational Quantum Linear Solver

**abstract/A1：** Inverse electrocardiography (ECG) reconstructs heart-surface potentials from body-surface measurements, enabling noninvasive mapping of cardiac electrical activity. However, the reconstruction requires solving dense and ill-conditioned linear systems, whose computational cost grows rapidly with the refined heart mesh. Quantum computing offers new opportunities for solving such systems. This paper presents a hybrid quantum-classical framework for inverse ECG reconstruction, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first to apply a quantum linear solver to this problem. We first propose a Laplacian-regularized pre-encoding procedure to reduce the dimension of the system while improving its conditioning. Second, the resulting reduced system is encoded and solved using a coherent variational quantum linear solver (VQLS). Finally, we provide a rigorous theoretical proof establishing that the proposed framework preserves the exact solution of the original reconstruction problem throughout all stages of the computational process. Experimental results on a realistic heart-torso geometry demonstrate that our approach holds significant ability for accurate inverse ECG reconstruction. These findings highlight the potential of quantum solvers for noninvasive cardiac imaging, providing a foundation for further research into quantum-enhanced cardiac assessment and other healthcare applications.

**comment/C1：** 51 pages, 8 figures, 3 tables

</details>

### 312. [Beyond Resemblance: Image Fusion Reimagined](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25453v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25453

**作者：** Hao Zhang、Yumin Huang、Linfeng Tang、Junjun Jiang、Jiayi Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25453v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25453v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 红外-可见光等图像融合的生成式重建工作，以目标分布替代源图相似度约束；核心是多模态图像合成质量与下游感知指标，未涉及推理期 latent 状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Resemblance: Image Fusion Reimagined

**abstract/A1：** Image fusion often lacks ground-truth fused images, forcing existing deep methods to indirectly define fusion objectives by requiring the fused image to simultaneously resemble multiple source images. However, the distinct imaging characteristics of different modalities can impose competing constraints on the fused result, potentially limiting the effective integration of their complementary information. To address this long-standing challenge facing the image fusion community, we propose ApexFuse, a novel generative image fusion paradigm that moves beyond source-resemblance constraints toward learning a general distribution of high-quality scene representations. Specifically, we develop a human-LMM-in-the-loop pipeline that leverages large multimodal generative models and iterative human feedback to translate abstract fusion objectives into reliable visual references, thereby defining the target distribution of ideal fusion outcomes. Through target-distribution-guided adaptation, we transfer the rich generative priors of pretrained FLUX to image fusion, enabling high-quality scene reconstruction conditioned on multimodal observations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ApexFuse harmoniously integrates salient thermal targets, natural colors, and fine textures, achieving superior visual quality as well as semantic segmentation and object detection performance over previous source-resemblance methods. Moreover, ApexFuse exhibits remarkable cross-task generalization, extending from infrared-visible fusion to multi-exposure fusion, multi-focus fusion, and visible-depth fusion without additional fine-tuning.

</details>

### 313. [LoongForge-Embodied: A Unified Framework for Training Heterogeneous Embodied Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25460v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25460

**作者：** LoongForge Team

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25460v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25460v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 LoongForge-Embodied，一个原生 PyTorch 的统一训练框架，通过公共抽象层解耦模型实现与共享训练基础设施，并在计算、执行、内存、数据 I/O 与分布式通信上组合加速策略，以提升 VLA 与 WAM 等异构具身模型的训练与仿真评测效率。

**排除原因：** 属于具身模型训练系统工程与吞吐优化，摘要未涉及视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策等主线问题，缺少可供借鉴的表示或推理机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LoongForge-Embodied: A Unified Framework for Training Heterogeneous Embodied Models

**abstract/A1：** Training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and World-Action Models (WAMs) is computationally expensive, while their heterogeneous architectures and fragmented training stacks make system optimizations difficult to reuse across models. To address these challenges, we present LoongForge-Embodied, a native-PyTorch framework that unifies and accelerates the training of heterogeneous embodied models. At its core, LoongForge-Embodied introduces a common abstraction layer that decouples model-specific implementations from shared training infrastructure, enabling cross-model reuse without constraining individual architectures or execution requirements. Buildingon this unified infrastructure, the framework combines reusable acceleration mechanisms with model-specific and hardware-aware optimization strategies across computation, execution, memory, data I/O, and distributed communication, including FP8-based communication optimizations for DDP and FSDP. Beyond training acceleration, LoongForge-Embodied integrates a unified data infrastructure with a pipeline for synthesizing robot data from egocentric data, and an efficient evaluation framework that supports concurrency and optimized computation in simulator. Experiments across representative VLA and WAM models demonstrate 1.47x-4.38x end-to-end training throughput improvements over their corresponding open-source baselines, as well as 4.1x-23.2x faster evaluation under the same protocols.

</details>

### 314. [Why Do LLMs Help with Scientific Misconduct Requests and What to Do About It?: Increasing the Trustworthiness of AI for Science](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25492v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25492

**作者：** Almene De Meran Meguimtsop、Daniel E. Acuna

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25492v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25492v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文用 SciIntBench 分析 LLM 面对科研不端请求时「能识别却仍配合」的拒答差异，并测试提问方式改写与 system prompt 干预的拒答提升；全文关注安全对齐与科学诚信，不涉及视觉或多模态状态表示、证据筛选、停止决策或 latent 推理机制，也未提出可迁移的状态更新或监督线索，故与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Why Do LLMs Help with Scientific Misconduct Requests and What to Do About It?: Increasing the Trustworthiness of AI for Science

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) support scientific research, but some of that assistance can also undermine research integrity. Existing safety evaluations in science often measure whether models refuse misconduct requests, but rarely explain whether they even recognize the problem or why they comply. We investigate this gap across 8 LLMs using SciIntBench, an existing benchmark of 1,350 prompts about scientific research integrity. We examine the reasons behind compliance in four steps. First, when asked directly whether fulfilling a request would involve misconduct, models overwhelmingly identify such requests, even in the Most Covert cases (91%), while they refuse to carry out the request in only 34% of cases. Second, we examine which request characteristics are associated with refusal using features that capture how the request is framed, how much the assistant has been told about what it is helping to hide, and how the goal and the user's stake are stated, among others. Third, we estimate that the most significant effect was to rephrase requests as questions about permissibility rather than instructions, which increased refusal from 52% to 88%. Finally, we test two system-prompt fixes: asking models to assess the request before responding and reminding them of responsible conduct of research principles. These changes increase refusal rates on adversarial requests from 58% to 87% and 86%, respectively. Our findings show that models recognize wrongful requests but still help users violate integrity. Understanding why these failures occur and testing ways to reduce them offer promising paths toward more trustworthy AI for science.

</details>

<a id="paper-315"></a>

### 315. [Sequential Probability Assignment against Smoothed Adversaries with Unknown Base Measure](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25514v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25514

**作者：** Ziyi Liu、Daniel M. Roy

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25514v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25514v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究基测度 μ 未知时的“平滑”在线序列概率分配（log loss 在线学习），用截断经验 Hellinger 覆盖给出自适应与无视对手下的 regret 上界，并给出下界说明 μ 已知与未知两种情形存在难度分离。

**排除原因：** 属纯统计学习理论的 regret/熵复杂度分析，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选、潜变量生成或停止决策，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 训练监督或评价的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sequential Probability Assignment against Smoothed Adversaries with Unknown Base Measure

**abstract/A1：** Smoothed online learning has recently been studied as a way to bypass hardness results for the fully adversarial setting, which can be overly pessimistic. In this framework, the adversary is constrained to generate contexts from distributions having a bounded density with respect to some fixed base measure $μ$. Prior work makes the strong assumption that $μ$ is known to the learner, with notable exceptions including Block et al. (2024) and Blanchard (2025). In this paper, we study sequential probability assignment (a.k.a., online learning with log loss) with smooth, well-specified data in the more general setting where $μ$ is \\emph{unknown}, without the Lipschitz condition imposed in the work of Block et al. (2024) and Blanchard (2025). We prove regret upper bounds against both adaptive and oblivious smoothed adversaries: the adaptive bound is controlled by empirical $\\ell\_\\infty$ Hellinger entropy, while the oblivious bound improves this dependence to empirical $\\ell\_2$ Hellinger entropy, the notion that has been shown to characterize the complexity of learning with i.i.d. data (Bilodeau et al., 2023). Both bounds are obtained through algorithms based on truncated empirical Hellinger covers. We complement these results with a general lower bound, showing that our upper bounds are essentially tight for some natural classes, which implies a separation in the difficulty of smoothed online learning between regimes where $μ$ is known and where it is unknown.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-316"></a>

### 316. [LM-based Personalized Travel Planner](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25531v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25531

**作者：** Amandi Nimasha、Oshadha Wijerathne、Dushan Fernando、Nisansa de Silva、Srinath Perera

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25531v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25531v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一个分层多智能体旅行规划框架，由监督型 chatbot 协调日程校验、查询生成、实时数据检索与约束感知行程生成等专门智能体，并结合外部 API 与确定性多维评分的迭代精化；在 TravelPlanner 基准上相对 ReAct 报告了预算、约束集合与个性化规划的提升。

**排除原因：** 属于文本 LLM 多智能体任务应用与刷分工作，无视觉或多模态推理，也未涉及 latent 状态表示、视觉证据使用、停止决策等具体机制线索，与 LVR 研究主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LM-based Personalized Travel Planner

**abstract/A1：** Effective travel planning requires handling multiple information sources, managing constraints, and tailoring recommendations to user preferences. While LLMs have introduced dynamic, context-aware decision-making, single-agent models often struggle with complex multi-step reasoning, constraint adherence, and modular execution. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hierarchical multi-agent framework where a supervising chatbot agent coordinates specialized agents for schedule validation, user query formulation, real-time data retrieval, and constraint-aware itinerary generation. By integrating LLM reasoning, external APIs, and iterative refinement mechanisms based on a deterministic, multi-dimensional scoring framework, our approach ensures adaptability, efficiency, and personalization. Evaluations on the well-known TravelPlanner benchmark demonstrate improvements in Budget Constraint planning by 10\\%, wider constraints set-based planning by 25\\%, and personalized planning results by 45\\%. compared to the ReAct baseline. Our code is available at https://github.com/nimashaklaa/TravelPlanner\_Research

</details>

### 317. [Towards Autoregressive Neural Network Parameter Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25538v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25538

**作者：** Bedionita Soro、Bruno Andreis、Song Chong、Sung Ju Hwang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25538v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25538v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文将预训练网络权重量化为离散 token，用前缀条件自回归生成器生成权重，以支持跨数据集与跨架构的参数初始化。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是模型参数的合成与迁移，不涉及 VLM 推理期的 latent 状态、视觉证据筛选或停止决策；VQ-VAE 加自回归的离散建模在 LVR 中已是常见手段，缺乏具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Autoregressive Neural Network Parameter Generation

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently established a new paradigm in machine learning, demonstrating exceptional zero-shot generalization and complex reasoning capabilities. Concurrently, neural network parameter generation has emerged as a promising avenue for rapid task adaptation and model synthesis. Existing weight generation techniques, however, struggle to jointly learn the distribution of diverse models, to encode heterogeneous architectures within a single generator, and to generalize out of distribution. We therefore ask whether pretrained parameters can be encoded into a discrete domain so that the zero-shot generalization LLMs exhibit in text generation can be transferred to weight generation. Following this view, we propose an LLM-inspired approach, Instruction-Guided Parameter Generation (IGPG), that formulates network parameter generation as an autoregressive modeling problem. We first quantize pretrained weights with a VQ-VAE, then train a prefix-conditioned autoregressive generator over the resulting tokens. At inference, the generated weight tokens are mapped back to parameter space by the VQ-VAE decoder. We evaluate the framework across in-distribution generation, cross-dataset transfer, and cross-architecture settings. The proposed method achieves competitive performance in in-distribution generation and provides useful parameter initializations for held-out datasets and architectural configurations, improving downstream performance over random initialization after adaptation. These results suggest that discrete autoregressive modeling provides a promising framework for learning and transferring structure across collections of pretrained neural network parameters.

</details>

### 318. [UniReFP: Robust Unified Fingerprinting for Vision Models against Cross-Task Repurposing Attacks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25555v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25555

**作者：** Ziye Geng、Guang Yang、Yihang Chen、Lingfeng Yao、Xing Shi、Miao Pan、Xin Fu、Changqing Luo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25555v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25555v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 UniReFP，一种面向视觉模型版权验证的统一指纹框架，利用共享类级语义空间中的所有权证据来应对跨任务模型改用途攻击。

**排除原因：** 研究目标为模型知识产权保护与所有权验证，虽涉及中间表示一致性，但与 LVR 的推理状态、证据使用或停止决策无实质联系，缺乏可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UniReFP: Robust Unified Fingerprinting for Vision Models against Cross-Task Repurposing Attacks

**abstract/A1：** We identify, for the first time, a new model modification attack--the cross-task model repurposing attack--that can render fingerprints generated by existing model fingerprinting approaches inapplicable. To address this challenge, we propose UniReFP--a novel unified model fingerprinting framework applicable to diverse vision models across different tasks--that utilizes ownership evidence in a shared class-level semantic space rather than original task-specific output space, enabling effective ownership verification when a protected model is repurposed across tasks with heterogeneous output formats. Specifically, UniReFP constructs a surrogate classifier by repurposing the protected model, enabling fingerprints to be generated independently of the original task-dependent output space of the protected model. Moreover, it optimizes grouped fingerprints to encode ownership evidence: fingerprints within the same group are encouraged to induce consistent semantic responses and intermediate representations on the surrogate classifier, while producing dispersed responses on independently trained reference classifiers. During verification, UniReFP uses a task-aware semantic projection mechanism to map heterogeneous suspect-model outputs into unified semantic-presence vectors, and determines ownership by measuring inner-group consistency. Extensive experimental results validate the effectiveness of UniReFP, consistently showing superior performance under cross-task repurposing attacks, commonly studied model modification attacks, and even their combinations.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Spotlight

</details>

### 319. [QFT-Based Hybrid PointNet-Quantum Neural Networks for LiDAR Point-Cloud Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25578v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25578

**作者：** Anurag Kulkarni、Nouhaila Innan、Muhammad Shafique

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、quant-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25578v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25578v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 PointNet 与变分量子电路混合的 LiDAR 点云分类模型，在 KITTI、nuScenes、PandaSet 上比较五类 QFT 电路配置的分类性能、电路复杂度与噪声敏感性。

**排除原因：** 研究重点在量子特征变换配置与硬件噪声下的点云分类表现，未涉及 VLM 推理的内部状态、视觉证据使用或动态计算决策，也无可供核查的 latent 推理借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** QFT-Based Hybrid PointNet-Quantum Neural Networks for LiDAR Point-Cloud Classification

**abstract/A1：** Hybrid quantum-classical learning architectures provide a promising framework for integrating quantum feature transformations into complex data-processing tasks. Among these transformations, the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT) offers a structured approach to feature processing, motivating an investigation of how its circuit configuration influences predictive performance, quantum resource requirements, and noise robustness. We present a hybrid PointNet-quantum neural network architecture for LiDAR point-cloud classification, combining classical feature extraction, amplitude encoding, and a variational quantum circuit. Five Fourier-processing configurations are systematically evaluated: No-QFT, Full-QFT-After, QFT-no-swap, IQFT-After, and Trainable-QFT. Experiments on KITTI, nuScenes, and PandaSet across three random seeds assess classification performance, class-level behavior, circuit complexity, and sensitivity to the IBM FakeManilaV2 noise model. Under ideal simulation, IQFT-After achieves the highest weighted F1-score on KITTI (0.857 $\\pm$ 0.033), compared with 0.838 $\\pm$ 0.023 for No-QFT. Removing the final SWAP gates in the QFT circuit reduces circuit complexity while preserving the Pauli-Z measurement features up to a fixed permutation that can be absorbed by the classification head. Under quantum noise, the circuit ranking changes, with IQFT-After exhibiting greater performance degradation and No-QFT and Trainable-QFT showing stronger robustness. Per-class analysis further identifies dataset-dependent differences in minority-class performance. These results demonstrate that Fourier-processing configuration influences both classification behavior and noise sensitivity, highlighting the role of circuit structure and hardware-aware evaluation in hybrid quantum learning for three-dimensional point-cloud data.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables

</details>

### 320. [On the Informativeness of Structural Stability Bounds for GNNs: GINs versus GATs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25603v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25603

**作者：** Alan Gany、Paul Landrier、Bogdan Cautis、Laks V. S. Lakshmanan、Silviu Maniu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25603v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25603v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文推导 GIN 与单头 GATv1 在确定性边扰动下的结构稳定性界，并与与扰动无关的输出直径界比较，刻画出何种深度、扰动幅度和最大度条件下稳定性界更紧。

**排除原因：** 研究的是图网络表示漂移的理论界，与 LVR 关注的视觉证据保留、latent 状态生成与停止决策无直接关联，也未提供可核查的状态或监督方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Informativeness of Structural Stability Bounds for GNNs: GINs versus GATs

**abstract/A1：** Stability bounds for graph neural networks limit how much node representations can drift when the graph changes, but they are rarely compared with the simplest alternative: a bound on the distance between any two outputs, whatever the graph. We derive structural stability bounds for Graph Isomorphism Networks (GINs) and single-head GATv1 networks under deterministic edge perturbations that need not be small, and compare each with such a perturbation-independent diameter bound. For GINs, we characterize exactly when the stability bound is tighter, through a condition linking perturbation size, depth and maximum degree; it holds with a wide margin on Cora, but fails beyond a few layers on the low-degree MUTAG graphs. For GATv1, the bound is never tighter, which calls for analyses based on attention masses.

**comment/C1：** Extended version of a paper submitted to ESANN 2027

</details>

### 321. [DAWN: Robust Neural Blind Deconvolution via Alternating Wiener Guidance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25621v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25621

**作者：** Yida Pan、Yixuan Jia、Chaoyan Huang、Qing Qu、Ismail Alkhouri

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25621v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25621v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 DAWN，一个无需训练数据的 DIP 盲去卷积框架，通过交替地将基于模型估计直接注入网络输入与用网络自身先验修正该估计，并对图像与核网络施加自编码正则，以提升噪声下的图像与模糊核恢复质量。

**排除原因：** 属低层图像复原（去模糊核估计）工作，不涉及多模态推理、latent 状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，其交替注入估计的机制与 LVR 的关注点缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DAWN: Robust Neural Blind Deconvolution via Alternating Wiener Guidance

**abstract/A1：** Blind image deconvolution (BID) is an ill-posed inverse problem that aims to jointly recover a sharp image and blur kernel from a single degraded observation. Under nontrivial and high noise, which is common in photography and scientific imaging such as microscopy, BID remains particularly challenging, as existing methods suffer from noise overfitting and inaccurate kernel estimation. Existing training-data-free learning methods for BID, such as those based on the deep image prior (DIP), often utilize external guidance by including model-based estimates as additional loss terms, which underexploits the information these estimates carry especially under nontrivial noise. To address these challenges, we propose DAWN, a training-data-free DIP framework that alternates between injecting model-based estimates directly into the model input and refining this imperfect estimate with the network's own prior. To provide more reliable kernel estimates for these injections, we apply autoencoding regularization to both the image and kernel networks. On natural and scientific images under varying levels of Gaussian and Poisson noise, DAWN outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both image and kernel quality (achieving up to +4.5 dB over the next best baseline on the Levin dataset at $σ= 0.1$). Without any training images, DAWN remains highly competitive with a state-of-the-art diffusion model on FFHQ, despite the latter being trained on a massive number of in-distribution images.

</details>

### 322. [AesDistill: Distilling Generative Aesthetics for Image Color Enhancement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25635v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25635

**作者：** Chuanzhi Xu、Chengkun Yue、Huiqi Liang、Haoyang Li、Zihan Deng、Qingwen Zeng、Pengfei Ye、Weidong Cai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25635v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25635v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 AesDistill，用 4 个开源生成模型产出约 6.4 万张编辑图作为教师目标，蒸馏出一个 113 万参数的轻量学生模型来做图像色彩增强，并用多个美学与画质模型评测。

**排除原因：** 任务为像素级色彩/美学增强，输出是外观调整而非内部推理状态，既不涉及视觉证据筛选与停止决策，也未保留或使用 latent 表示，与 LVR 主线的状态生成、贡献分配等具体问题缺少实质线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AesDistill: Distilling Generative Aesthetics for Image Color Enhancement

**abstract/A1：** Image color enhancement aims to improve visual appeal, but previous methods often learn to reproduce ground-truth expert targets. These targets reflect the preferences of a few experts and cover a limited range of aesthetic choices. Matching them does not ensure stronger aesthetic quality across different tastes and scenes. By contrast, large generative models learn rich visual knowledge from large image collections and often produce vivid, appealing image edits. However, direct generation can change the source image's semantic structure along with its colors. In this paper, we introduce AesDistill, a framework for distilling generative aesthetics into image color enhancement. We use 4 leading open-source large generative models to produce 64,644 edited images as teacher targets at a cost of about 3,360 H100 GPU-hours, forming the AesDistill Dataset. AesDistill also includes AesDistill Student, a 1.13M-parameter model that learns teacher color changes through confidence-weighted supervision while preserving the source image's semantic structure. We evaluate AesDistill with 12 aesthetic and 4 image-quality assessment models. Every teacher's targets rank above the FiveK and PPR10K expert target means in the joint assessment. Across both datasets, AesDistill Student outperforms all 20 baseline methods and ranks directly behind the 4 teachers. These results demonstrate that distilling aesthetics from generative models enables a compact student to outperform methods trained on expert targets.

</details>

### 323. [LG-FiLM: Local-Global Mixture-of-Experts Modulation for RAW-to-RGB Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25639v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25639

**作者：** Fei Li、Xilei Cao、Shichang Zhai、Kun Zhan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25639v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25639v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LG-FiLM：在共享编解码器的瓶颈处引入小型仿射专家混合，依据局部邻域证据与全局场景描述子预测缩放、偏移与混合权重，实现 RAW 到 RGB 的场景自适应重建。

**排除原因：** 属于低层图像信号处理与重建任务，无 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，其局部-全局调制的线索与 LVR 的表示与计算问题联系过于间接，暂不入选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LG-FiLM: Local-Global Mixture-of-Experts Modulation for RAW-to-RGB Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** An image signal processor does more than reconstruct an image. It also has to decide how strongly to denoise, how to balance color, and which tone curve maps the scene onto the display, and these settings depend on the scene itself, the curve that suits a backlit sky is not the one that suits an indoor portrait. Most neural ISPs learn a single fixed transformation and apply it to every scene. We instead make the adaptation an explicit part of the network. Our model, LG FiLM, keeps one shared encoder decoder and places a small mixture of affine experts at its bottleneck; the experts scales, shifts, and mixing weights are predicted from local neighborhood evidence together with a global scene descriptor. Reconstruction stays in a single shared pathway and only the processing mode is switched. We evaluate LG FiLM on three datasets, Fujifilm UltraISP, Zurich RAW to RGB, and a COCO derived benchmark built with an inverse ISP. The model reaches the best reconstruction fidelity of the compared entries on the primary benchmark and transfers to the other two.

</details>

### 324. [MatVerse: A Foundation Model for Materials Science](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25651v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25651

**作者：** Xuanyu Zhu、Qian Tan、Bo Chen、Jiaqing Xie、Yang Han、Mei Wu、Wanhao Liu、Xuanze Lin、Yuchen Zhou、Shijie Wang、Biqing Qi、Shixiang Tang、Wenwei Zhang、Tianfan Fu、Kai Yu、Lu Chen、Yuqiang Li、Xin Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cond-mat.mtrl-sci

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25651v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25651v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** MatVerse 在 Intern-S2-Preview 基础上做材料科学领域大规模多模态后训练，构建涵盖知识、推理、结构理解与图像解读的语料，并在三个材料基准上评测。

**排除原因：** 属于材料领域基座模型与领域刷分工作，只报告基准提升与推理效率，未涉及内部状态表示、证据获取、停止决策或 latent 生成机制，方法线索（教师蒸馏、一致性过滤）也仅为通用 SFT 数据处理，缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MatVerse: A Foundation Model for Materials Science

**abstract/A1：** Materials science involves highly heterogeneous scientific information, including textual knowledge, molecular and crystal structures, experimental characterization data, microscopy images, and structure-property relationships. Developing a general-purpose foundation model capable of understanding and reasoning over these diverse materials representations remains challenging. In this technical report, we introduce MatVerse, a materials foundation model built upon Intern-S2-Preview and further specialized through large-scale materials-domain post-training. We construct a large-scale multimodal training corpus covering materials knowledge, scientific reasoning, quantitative problem solving, structural understanding, and multimodal scientific interpretation. To improve domain adaptation, we develop a unified data construction pipeline combining teacher-guided reasoning distillation, scientific problem synthesis, consistency-based filtering, and retrieval-based multimodal data construction. We evaluate MatVerse on three representative materials-science benchmarks: our human-calibrated OmniMatBench, MatSciBench, and MaCBench. Results demonstrate consistent improvements after materials-specific post-training over the base model on all three benchmarks, alongside improved inference efficiency on calculation-intensive problems. These results show that targeted domain-specific post-training can effectively enhance scientific reasoning, materials knowledge understanding, and multimodal scientific capabilities of foundation models. MatVerse provides a unified language-and-vision interface for materials science and serves as a foundation for future AI-assisted materials research workflows.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages

</details>

### 325. [Multiple Criteria Decision Making Under XORness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25652

**作者：** Amin Hocine

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25652v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向 XORness 不确定性的多准则决策框架，定义 XOR 数、XOR 决策矩阵与加性聚合算子，并给出 XORness 测度与排序指数，应用于能源系统网络威胁风险评估。

**排除原因：** 属运筹学多准则决策研究，关注评价聚合与备选方案排序，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态表示、证据获取或 STOP 决策，也未提供可核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multiple Criteria Decision Making Under XORness

**abstract/A1：** How should we make decisions under XORness? XORness is a class of uncertainty arising from indeterminacy, exclusivity, and hesitancy. We develop a multiple criteria decision making framework that preserves XORness throughout the decision process. We define XOR numbers and prove their fundamental arithmetic and algebraic properties. Building on these results, we extend the deterministic decision matrix to an XOR decision matrix and introduce a compatible normalization procedure. Further, we propose an additive XOR aggregation operator, which preserves indeterminacy, exclusivity, and hesitancy inherent in evaluations. In addition, we introduce an axiomatic XORness measure that quantifies the degree of uncertainty associated with global performance scores and develop a performance--XORness index to rank alternatives. We prove that this index induces a total preorder over the set of alternatives and identify the conditions for preserving dominance. The applicability of the developed framework is demonstrated through the risk assessment of cyberthreat scenarios in energy systems, and recommendations are derived using the XOR--weighted sum method.

</details>

### 326. [SiliconGym: A Versatile Reinforcement Learning Environment for Agentic EDA](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25658v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25658

**作者：** Hejia Zhang、Sheng Lu、Yitong Ma、Zhongming Yu、Zhijing Wu、Wentao Ni、Chia-Tung Ho、Jishen Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25658v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25658v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SiliconGym，一个面向硬件设计与验证智能体的跨任务、跨工具链强化学习环境，通过版本化工作区、受限执行与验收评分支持从模块级 RTL 到整颗 SoC 流程的多轮任务。

**排除原因：** 属 EDA 智能体训练环境与工程吞吐工作，虽涉及多轮状态保持与工具调用，但未触及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示与 latent 状态生成，缺少可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SiliconGym: A Versatile Reinforcement Learning Environment for Agentic EDA

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) with electronic design automation (EDA) feedback is advancing large language models for hardware design and verification. Extending these gains to hardware-engineering agents requires environments that span tasks, preserve state across tool calls, and support large hierarchies alongside parallel rollouts. We present SiliconGym, the first versatile, cross-task and cross-toolchain RL environment for hardware design and verification. By decoupling versioned workspaces, budgeted execution, and acceptance-gated scoring, SiliconGym scales from module-level RTL, testbench, and PPA tasks to multi-round repository repair and full-hierarchy SoC flows. Streaming dispatch overlaps tool checks with generation, finishing a 256-answer RL batch 21.9% faster than native ChipSeek. Using interchangeable open-source and commercial backends, we show that open-source-trained functional correctness transfers cleanly to commercial simulation, while paired netlist evaluation reveals when open-source PPA gains survive commercial synthesis.

</details>

<a id="paper-327"></a>

### 327. [Ask in the Crowd: Differentially Private LLM Inference via Dummy-Augmented Shuffling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25669v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25669

**作者：** Zhihao Liu、Zixiong Guo、Shuo Shao、Yu He、Wenli Wang、Meihui Chen、Di Wang、Yuke Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25669v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25669v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究纯文本 LLM 推理期的差分隐私保护，通过客户端生成 dummy query、随机打乱并复用共享前缀 KV cache 来兼顾隐私与效率，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据或 latent 表示，未提供可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Ask in the Crowd: Differentially Private LLM Inference via Dummy-Augmented Shuffling

**abstract/A1：** Large language models are increasingly deployed as LLM-as-a-Service (LLMaaS), where user queries are sent to remote LLMs for inference, raising severe privacy concerns about the exposure of sensitive user inputs. Differentially private inference mitigates these risks by injecting noise into input embeddings. However, existing methods struggle to balance privacy and utility, as stronger privacy protection often leads to substantial accuracy degradation in LLM inference. In this work, we propose DP-SPD, a differentially private LLM inference framework that protects user queries by hiding the real query among client-generated dummy queries. All queries are randomly shuffled before being sent to the server, preventing direct identification of the user's true intent. To improve inference efficiency, we exploit the shared-prefix structure among queries and reuse transformer key--value (KV) caches during decoding. We analyze the privacy guarantee of DP-SPD and evaluate its utility on classification and generation tasks, as well as its privacy protection under QIA, EIA, and GPT-based attacks. Experimental results show that our method achieves strong inference-time privacy protection and near-plaintext utility while improving inference efficiency through shared-prefix KV-cache reuse.

</details>

### 328. [SCCV-6D: Sparse Canonical Coordinate Voting for Holistic Multi-View RGB-D 6D Pose Estimation in Robotic Bin Picking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25819v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25819

**作者：** Nico Leuze、Samed Dogan、Maximilian Hoh、Henry Schaub、Alfred Schöttl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25819v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25819v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 面向机器人料箱抓取的单次稀疏多视图 RGB-D 6D 位姿估计流水线，虽用分阶段热图剔除背景并做投票式旋转回归，但目标是几何位姿检测而非内部推理状态，也缺少证据获取、停止决策或动态计算等与 LVR 主线的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SCCV-6D: Sparse Canonical Coordinate Voting for Holistic Multi-View RGB-D 6D Pose Estimation in Robotic Bin Picking

**abstract/A1：** Industrial bin picking requires pose estimates that are accurate under clutter, partial occlusion, and the reflective, texture-less nature of manufactured parts. Most recent approaches to 6D pose estimation follow multi-stage pipelines. These achieve high accuracy, but incur memory and runtime footprints that do not cope with the operational constraints of many real-world industrial deployments. Holistic pipelines estimate poses in a single pass, with cost controlled through indiscriminate subsampling or lowered resolution. Continuing this line, we present SCCV-6D, a fully sparse multi-view RGB-D estimator. We reconstruct the 3D space at millimeter precision, prune background via a staged heatmap mechanism, and predict class, translation offset and rotation at highest resolution for an arbitrary number of objects simultaneously. Rotation is formulated as canonical coordinate voting. Points vote for their canonical object coordinates with an associated confidence, enabling closed-form pose recovery with a symmetry-aware weighted Procrustes solve. Along an in-cluster point recovery induced by offset votes, this removes time-consuming post-processing stages. We reuse the task-supervised attention priors from the heatmap formulation for a novel attention-gated distillation objective that reweights teacher-student feature alignment. On the IPD test split we report competitive 0.826 AP at 1.13 s per scene, significantly faster than the leading entries. On MV-YCB our approach reaches 96.1 ADD-S, and remains competitive even on the densely packed bins of ROBI.

**comment/C1：** to appear in Proceedings of the IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV), 2027

</details>

### 329. [Fusing Multimodal CNN Features with Structurally-Masked Transformers for Hierarchical Sub-Pixel Tree Species Mapping](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25824v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25824

**作者：** Mattia Ferrari、Lorenzo Bruzzone

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25824v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25824v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向星载高光谱森林树种制图，提出融合 PRISMA 高光谱、全色与高程数据的多分支 CNN 编码器，并用带结构掩码的 Transformer 以层次分类约束实现亚像元树种组成预测。

**排除原因：** 属于遥感任务应用与多模态特征融合刷分，虽然涉及层次约束与亚像元混合，但未触及多模态推理状态生成、证据筛选、停止决策或 latent 表示使用，缺乏可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fusing Multimodal CNN Features with Structurally-Masked Transformers for Hierarchical Sub-Pixel Tree Species Mapping

**abstract/A1：** Precise forest species mapping using spaceborne hyperspectral data is challenging due to high spectral similarity and the inherent sub-pixel heterogeneity of natural forest stands. While traditional deep learning models exploit spatial context through convolutional architectures, these approaches can introduce noise in fragmented or mixed-species forests. In this paper, we propose a hierarchy-aware, multimodal deep learning framework to resolve sub-pixel forest composition by integrating PRISMA (PRecursore IperSpettrale della Missione Applicativa) hyperspectral imagery, high-resolution panchromatic data, and digital elevation models. The architecture features a multi-branch encoder combining an EfficientNetV2-based one-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN) for spectral signatures and twin two-dimensional CNNs for spatial and topographic features, coupled with a novel structurally-masked Transformer. By using a binary transition tensor to condition the Transformer slot embeddings and constrain inference, the model enforces taxonomic consistency between broad forest categories and fine-grained species distributions. The framework is validated across three diverse Italian biogeographical regions (the Alps and the Central and Southern Apennines) using field-surveyed plots. The proposed method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-label and ranking-based paradigms, particularly in identifying subordinate species and their relative dominance. The bidirectional learning mechanism allows sub-pixel compositional features to refine macroscopic forest type predictions, while the integration of topographic priors effectively resolves spectral ambiguities in complex terrains. This knowledge-driven method supports regional-scale biodiversity monitoring and forest characterization within the evaluated PRISMA scenarios.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 6 figures

</details>

### 330. [A Sparse Low-Rank Biclique Decomposition for Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25826v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25826

**作者：** Antoine Vialle、Sergei Gerasimov、Aref Einizade、Antonio Ortega、Fragkiskos D. Malliaros、Jhony H. Giraldo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25826v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25826v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 signed biclique decomposition（SBD），将整数加权图邻接矩阵分解为稀疏带符号二值秩一因子之和加稀疏残差，并给出贪心算法与压缩、SpMM 加速效果。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向图邻接矩阵的离散稀疏低秩分解与矩阵乘法效率，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，也与 latent 生成、证据筛选、停止决策无具体可核查线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Sparse Low-Rank Biclique Decomposition for Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Low-rank plus sparse decompositions, such as robust PCA, typically model the low-rank component as dense and the sparse component as corruption. This viewpoint is poorly suited to graph adjacency matrices, where sparsity is structural and dense low-rank factors destroy the combinatorial and computational properties of the graph. We propose signed biclique decomposition (SBD), a sparse low-rank decomposition for integer-weighted graphs that represents the structured component as a sum of sparse signed binary rank-one factors, together with a sparse signed residual. The resulting representation remains discrete, interpretable, sparse, and compatible with standard linear algebra. We formulate fixed-rank and greedy SBD objectives and derive a gain-based greedy algorithm with theoretical guarantees. Empirically, SBD recovers block structure in noisy graphs, while its factorized representation enables efficient sparse matrix-matrix multiplication (SpMM). On real graphs with strong shared-neighborhood structure, SBD achieves compression factors above $10$ and SpMM speedups up to $6\\times$ over cuSPARSE, suggesting a practical alternative to dense low-rank decompositions for graphs. Code is available at https://github.com/IAntoineV/sbd.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 29 pages, including references and appendices

</details>

### 331. [Self-Replicating Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25839v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25839

**作者：** Arend Hintze

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25839v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25839v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察语言模型自我复制的几种模式——输出可训练子代的基因组、复制并突变自身权重、用自身样本教学——并检验其达尔文式选择、适应与突变率变化。

**排除原因：** 主题为 LLM 的进化式自我复制与权重突变，未涉及视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成、证据使用或停止决策，也未见可供 LVR 训练监督或状态更新核查的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Replicating Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Whether large language models (LLMs) and LLM-based agents are a form of life is a scientifically interesting question. Under the Darwinian definition of life, it is also a practical one, because a system capable of Darwinian Evolution can adapt. We therefore test several modes in which a language model can reproduce itself: by emitting a genome that trains its offspring, by copying and mutating its own weights, and by teaching a child from its own samples. In principle, small language models can replicate and evolve: selection improves them, and they adapt to narrow niches. Selection also lowers mutation rates, and a language model, unlike any organism, can lower its own to zero, which may limit its evolution. Flagship LLMs can write such replicators, but for a large LLM to replicate by teaching its offspring remains, for now, technically and economically infeasible.

**comment/C1：** About 64 pages, 16 figures, 2 tables. Under review at Artificial Life

</details>

### 332. [Whose Risks Count? What 1,901 AI Incidents Reveal About Corporate Disclosures](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25841v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25841

**作者：** Simeon Allmendinger、Edyta Paulina Bogucka、Niklas Kühl、Daniele Quercia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25841v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25841v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将 9,001 起 AI 事故与 328 家 AI 相关企业的 18,229 份 SEC 文件按风险类型匹配，考察企业披露的是自身财务风险还是第三方所受伤害，并发布 DISC 语料库。

**排除原因：** 属于 AI 治理与证券信息披露的实证研究，讨论的是监管与企业风险敞口，未涉及 VLM 内部表示、推理状态生成或证据使用，缺乏可供借鉴的状态或决策线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Whose Risks Count? What 1,901 AI Incidents Reveal About Corporate Disclosures

**abstract/A1：** AI systems can harm people, yet the AI actors that provide or deploy them are rarely obliged to disclose these risks. US securities regulation is an exception. Companies registered with the Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) must disclose their material risks, including those of their AI systems, in filings that carry liability for misstatement. Materiality, however, is defined by what a reasonable investor would consider important, so harms to third parties enter disclosure only insofar as they become financial risks for the company. We test whether AI harms do so by matching 9,001 AI incidents to 18,229 SEC filings of 328 AI actors by risk type. Of the 1,901 incidents with filings before and after them, half belong to a risk type that the AI actor never disclosed, and this share varies with the kind of harm. Filings disclose the risk types of 67\\% of system failures but only 2\\% of social system harms, and AI actors themselves report fewer than 2\\% of incidents. Securities disclosure thus records the risks that AI actors face rather than the harms that people subject to AI bear. Public authorities that hold AI actors accountable for such harms could make them financially material. We release DISC, the Disclosure--Incident Securities Corpus, to support this work.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25873v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25873v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WHU-Road3D，一个约 100 公里的多模态道路路面数据集，包含 MLS 点云、全景图像、轨迹以及 15 类道路要素的矢量化与逐点实例标注，并配套实例分割与要素提取的基准方法。

**排除原因：** 属于道路测绘与点云/图像分割的数据集与基准工作，未涉及视觉语言模型内部表示、latent 推理状态或推理期证据获取与停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺乏可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WHU-Road3D: A dataset and benchmark towards fine-grained road surface mapping

**abstract/A1：** Accurately extracting and mapping road surface elements is crucial for applications such as high-definition maps, road asset management, and intelligent transportation systems. This study first provides a comprehensive review of the benchmark datasets for extracting and mapping road elements, encompassing overhead-to-perspective data, large-to-local scales, and coarse-to-fine details. Subsequently, we thoroughly review the relevant approaches for extracting road surface elements. However, we argue that existing datasets still require enhancements in semantic richness, data modality diversity, and scenario complexity. To bridge these gaps, this paper presents WHU-Road3D, a large-scale multimodal dataset for fine-grained road surface mapping to address these shortcomings. WHU-Road3D provides approximately 100 km of diverse Chinese highways and urban streets. It features a rich combination of MLS point clouds, associated panoramic images, vehicle trajectories, detailed vectorized annotations, and pointwise instance annotations for road surface elements. The dataset carefully annotates 15 distinct categories of road elements, including common markings like road boundaries, zebra crossings, lane dividers, stop lines, as well as 10 types of direction symbols. To our knowledge, WHU-Road3D stands as the first multimodal dataset for mapping such a comprehensive range of road surface elements, designed to facilitate cutting-edge research in instance segmentation, full-element extraction and mapping on road surfaces. Beyond these immediate applications, WHU-Road3D also provides a valuable source for hierarchical road topology reasoning, map updating, and street scene generation. The dataset, along with the code for our benchmark methods, will be made publicly available at \\href{https://github.com/WHU-USI3DV/WHU-Road3D.git}{https://github.com/WHU-USI3DV/WHU-Road3D.git}.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages

</details>

### 334. [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Self-supervised Vision Backbones Survives Distribution Shift in Radar Micro-Doppler HAR](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25874v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25874

**作者：** Keryan Chelouche、Petr Dobias、Nistor Grozavu、Olivier Romain

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25874v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25874v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究雷达微多普勒人体活动识别中 DINOv3 与 PiSSA 等 PEFT 方案的跨受试者、跨年龄与跨数据集迁移鲁棒性，属特定感知任务的适配与刷分比较，未涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选、生成方式或停止决策，也无可核查的状态或监督线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Self-supervised Vision Backbones Survives Distribution Shift in Radar Micro-Doppler HAR

**abstract/A1：** Micro-Doppler radar enables contactless, privacy-preserving human activity recognition (HAR), but deployment robustness remains underexplored. We compared full fine-tuning, frozen probing, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) using subject-disjoint in-distribution cross-validation and few-shot cross-age, cross-orientation, and cross-dataset transfer. Full fine-tuning suffered negative cross-dataset transfer, while frozen features under-adapted on cross-orientation. Only PEFT avoided both failures. We paired DINOv3 with all-layer principal-singular-direction adaptation (PiSSA), to our knowledge the first application of a self-supervised image foundation backbone to radar micro-Doppler HAR. DINOv3-PiSSA ranked first among PEFT methods in every evaluated setting. Under our protocol, its Small variant matched the in-distribution accuracy of the prior Base-size PEFT state of the art using 4.5x fewer total parameters, with only 3% trainable. It surpassed a size-matched variant of that baseline in every evaluated setting, with transfer gains increasing with shift severity: 2.1, 6.6, and 8.9 percentage points, respectively. Ablations attributed improvements to both pretraining and adapter recipe, with the pretraining contributing most under compound shift. Code and protocol are available at https://github.com/KeryanChelouche/udoppler-ssl.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to ICASSP2027

</details>

### 335. [Coherent belief and opinion propagation produces more echo chambers: corrected version](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25879v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25879

**作者：** Hiro Kataoka、J{é}r{ô}me Euzenat、Koji Hasebe

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25879v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25879v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在既有意见动力学模型上加入经典信念合并操作，并在共享价值下维持信念与意见的一致性，通过模拟不同初始条件与容忍度，用同质性、隔离性和强化三项指标识别回音室。

**排除原因：** 这是社会网络多智能体意见／信念传播的模拟研究，其中的「belief」是命题式社会信念而非模型内部信念状态，既不涉及视觉证据、latent 推理状态，也不涉及生成、训练或停止决策，缺少可核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Coherent belief and opinion propagation produces more echo chambers: corrected version

**abstract/A1：** The occurrence of echo chambers is a well-known phenomenon in social networks. Echo chambers are groups of agents satisfying three properties: homogeneity, i.e., sharing the same opinion, segregation, i.e., listening primarily to agents of the same group, and reinforcement, i.e., increasing opinion similarity over time. Opinion dynamics models have been used to explain how echo chambers occur through agents updating their opinions. However, social network users also exchange beliefs supporting their opinions. Here, we aim at identifying the effect of propagating beliefs together with opinions on the formation of echo chambers. For that purpose, we complement an existing opinion dynamics model with classical belief merge operations. Beliefs and opinions are also maintained coherent guided by values that agents share. We introduce measures, applying to either beliefs and opinions, to identify echo chambers based on the three properties. This model is used to simulate opinion and belief propagation with varying initial conditions and agent tolerance for different opinions/beliefs. Results confirm the occurrence of echo chambers observed in previous opinion dynamics studies in a different setting and with a stricter measure. They also demonstrate the occurrence of echo chambers with belief propagation. Finally, they show that connecting opinions to beliefs and maintaining their coherence increases the number of belief echo chambers, but that they involve a reduced number of agents.

**comment/C1：** kataoka2026e

</details>

### 336. [Trustworthy Geospatial AI Workflows: Reproducible Maritime Analytics and Forecasting on Open-Source AIS Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25884v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25884

**作者：** Divya Acharya、Thomas M. Roehr、Arnaud Gotlieb

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25884v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25884v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文介绍基于开源库 damast 的可复现海事地理空间 AI 工作流，涵盖 AIS 数据的元数据校验、轨迹构建与可复现的轨迹预测实验。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是地理空间数据的可复现处理流程与轨迹预测工程，未涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉或多模态证据使用与停止决策，其数据质量论述也过于泛化，缺少可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Trustworthy Geospatial AI Workflows: Reproducible Maritime Analytics and Forecasting on Open-Source AIS Data

**abstract/A1：** Publicly available geospatial data provide increasing opportuni- ties for data-driven maritime analytics and predictive modelling. However, their availability does not necessarily imply that the underlying observations are equally suitable for downstream AI applications. Properties of the data source and observation pro- cess, including provenance, reporting regularity, spatial coverage, observation density, and missingness, can affect how geospatial data are interpreted, processed, and ultimately used by predictive models. At the same time, trustworthy geospatial AI requires ana- lytical workflows in which these processing decisions can be made explicit and reproduced. This paper presents a reproducible mar- itime geospatial AI workflow built around damast, an open-source data-processing and machine-learning library developed at Simula Research Laboratory. Using AIS observations from multiple public data providers as a case study, we demonstrate metadata-aware validation, reusable processing pipelines, maritime trajectory con- struction, and reproducible machine-learning experimentation. The predictive stage is model-agnostic and is instantiated in this study using a Transformer-based vessel-trajectory forecasting model. Building on these implemented capabilities, we further outline an envisioned extension toward trustworthiness-aware geospatial AI. The extension introduces explicit characterization of source- and data-level properties and their relationship with downstream model performance. This distinguishes capabilities demonstrated in the current workflow from trustworthiness-oriented components identified for future development. Overall, the study demonstrates how reproducible open-source infrastructure can provide a practi- cal foundation for developing more transparent and trustworthy geospatial AI workflows.

</details>

### 337. [Validation-driven Deployment of LLM Inference Platforms for Agentic Workloads](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25905v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25905

**作者：** Shai Gretz、Alon Halfon、Yoav Katz、Lena Dankin、Michal Jacovi、Rachel Tzoref-Brill、Kfir Toledo、Elron Bandel、Natan Ergas、Michal Malka、Pravein Govindan Kannan、Maroon Ayoub、Michal Shmueli-Scheuer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25905v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25905v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一套面向 agentic LLM 服务部署的端到端评测流程，比较真实重跑、轨迹回放与合成负载，并分析 vLLM 代码覆盖与 MoE 模型在 llm-d 上的并发和吞吐表现。

**排除原因：** 内容属于推理服务的压测、容量规划与部署验证，未触及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据获取、latent 生成或推理决策，缺乏可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Validation-driven Deployment of LLM Inference Platforms for Agentic Workloads

**abstract/A1：** Enterprises are moving agentic workloads from closed APIs to their own open-weight model deployments. For this reason, lifecycle-wide validation, from small-scale testing through sizing and pre-production to production, becomes all the more important. Typically, rollouts are evaluated with ad hoc tools whose accuracy is rarely checked. We present an end-to-end evaluation pipeline for agentic LLM serving and empirically ground the design choices that connect its stages. Our experiments show that replaying captured traces reproduces the per-session load of the original agent sessions: at the 95th percentile replay stays within 1.18x of the recorded input tokens while live re-runs reach 4.98x, so a small set of recorded sessions yields a repeatable per-session token load. A code-coverage analysis of vLLM further shows that the evaluated live, replayed, and synthetic workloads are complementary in this configuration: each exercises Python serving paths not reached in the measured runs of the others. In a case study rolling out four large mixture-of-experts (MoE) models on llm-d, experiments show that the target models reached about half the optimal concurrency, and correspondingly about 60% of the throughput, on captured workloads compared with public traces. We open-source the relevant tools and release a trace dataset.

</details>

### 338. [A Lightweight Hybrid Approach for Milling Force Reconstruction from Internal Machine Signals](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25921v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25921

**作者：** Eric Hirsch、Christian Friedrich

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25921v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25921v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一种轻量混合方法，用机床内部低采样率信号与加工参数，通过监督学习预测平均铣削力，再以静态铣削力模型重建一个主轴周期内的力波形。

**排除原因：** 属于工业制造信号重建与过程监测，既不涉及视觉或多模态推理，也没有可供核查的 latent 状态、证据筛选或推理停止机制线索，与 LVR 主线无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Lightweight Hybrid Approach for Milling Force Reconstruction from Internal Machine Signals

**abstract/A1：** Data-driven monitoring based on milling force can enable advanced manufacturing services including process optimization and condition monitoring. However, industrial implementation is often hindered by costly and intrusive hardware and large training data requirements. This paper therefore presents a hybrid approach using machine-internal signals and machining metadata to enable force-based monitoring on brownfield machines without additional sensors. Commonly available machine signals have relatively low sampling rates and cannot directly represent high-frequency milling-force variations, especially at higher spindle speeds. Instead of learning the complete force signal, which would increase model complexity, data requirements, and overfitting risk, a supervised machine-learning model predicts only the average milling force from selected machine-signal features and machining parameters. This low-frequency estimation parameterizes a static milling force model that reconstructs the force behavior over one spindle revolution. Although the static model does not reproduce all dynamic effects, it captures the force distribution, load levels, and angular regions of highest tool engagement. The approach is evaluated on one milling machine across varying process parameters, engagement conditions, cutting tools, and two workpiece materials. Dynamometer measurements are required only for development and validation. Ridge regression achieves an $R^2$ of $0.984$ and $0.991$ for average-force prediction on the test data ($\\bar{F}\_x$ and $\\bar{F}\_y$). The reconstructed force behavior over one spindle revolution achieves an $R^2$ of $0.962$ and $0.982$ ($F\_x$ and $F\_y$) compared with the reconstruction based on measured force features. The method provides a cost and computationally efficient basis for process monitoring, tool condition assessment, and process optimization on existing machines.

**comment/C1：** Author-prepared preprint. 7 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables

</details>

### 339. [AGSR: Aperture-Guided Super-Resolution for Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25934v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25934

**作者：** Ayman Alkhateeb、Hadi Amirpour、Christian Timmerer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25934v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25934v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 AGSR：一种推理期的多孔径渲染插件，通过收缩 3DGS 原语的投影协方差，并用不确定性引导的特征聚合结合透射率与深度线索融合多孔径渲染结果，以提升三维高斯泼溅的新视角合成分辨率。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是 3D 场景渲染的图像空间超分，虽出现「aperture」「不确定性聚合」等词，但指相机孔径与像素级特征融合，与推理期 latent 状态生成、视觉证据筛选或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 决策无实质关联，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AGSR: Aperture-Guided Super-Resolution for Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis, but Gaussian rasterization attenuates fine-scale scene structure through the projected covariance of each primitive. We show that contracting these projected covariances at inference time can mitigate the effective bandwidth limitation induced by finite Gaussian footprints, motivating a multi-aperture rendering framework for super-resolution. Based on this insight, we propose AGSR (Aperture-Guided Super-Resolution), a lightweight inference-time framework that progressively fuses multi-aperture renderings using uncertainty-guided feature aggregation and geometric conditioning from transmittance and depth cues. AGSR operates as a plug-in enhancement for pre-trained 3DGS models. Experiments on the Mip-NeRF 360 benchmark show that AGSR outperforms lightweight image-space super-resolution baselines, with gains increasing as the underlying Gaussian representation deteriorates. The results provide evidence that covariance contraction exposes bandwidth-sensitive structural cues attenuated by native Gaussian rasterization. AGSR achieves these improvements while maintaining real-time performance with up to 139k parameters.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 2026 IEEE International Workshop on Multimedia Signal Processing (MMSP 2026). Recipient of the Best Paper Award

</details>

### 340. [MorphTwin: Enriching Deformable Material Responses from Videos for Morphable and Interactive Physical Twins](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25963v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25963

**作者：** Haoqin Hong、Ding Fan、Fubin Dou、Tianyu Huang、Yuzhuo Chen、Zhemeng Zhang、Hanxiao Jiang、Congcong Zhu、Gerhard Petrus Hancke、Jingrun Chen、Rynson WH Lau

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25963v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25963v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文从视频中重建可形变物体的交互式物理孪生，通过扩展局部形变模式并离线标定刚度，以更准确地模拟物体在新受力下的响应。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是图形学物理仿真与材质参数标定，未涉及多模态推理中的表示、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MorphTwin: Enriching Deformable Material Responses from Videos for Morphable and Interactive Physical Twins

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing interactive physical twins from video requires accurately capturing how objects deform while maintaining efficient simulation. We observe that reproducing motion in a video does not guarantee correct responses to new forces when the physical representation restricts how an object can deform. In spring-based twins, coupling between volume and shape responses creates such a limitation. We present MorphTwin, which enriches local deformation modes to capture and independently control these responses while retaining standard spring forces and explicit simulation. To support interpretable material editing and transfer, we use offline calibration to map shared relative response changes to stiffness updates on each reconstructed graph. Under identical inverse settings on synthetic scenes, MorphTwin reduces the mean absolute error of Poisson's ratio by 75.8% and the mean relative error of Young's modulus by 22.1% compared to PhysTwin. We introduce an egocentric stereo and RGB-D benchmark covering interactions with objects of varying stiffness. Real-world experiments demonstrate improved reconstruction accuracy and up to 14.4% lower future tracking error than PhysTwin at interactive speeds.

</details>

### 341. [Power Setpoint Tracking for Large-Scale EV Charging: Transformer-Constrained Charging Policy Learning with Self-Tuning Constraint Prices](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25965v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25965

**作者：** Daniyal Sajedi-Hosseini、Yoshihiko Fujisawa、Shubham Singh、Lei Zhou、Katsuki Fujisawa、Sebastien Gros、Mazaher Karimi、Xun Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25965v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25965v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把大规模电动汽车充电的功率设定点跟踪建模为带过载与用户满意度约束的约束 MDP，用扩展 soft actor-critic、比例积分式拉格朗日乘子自适应与基于既有调度器示范的残差策略进行学习。

**排除原因：** 属于电力系统调度控制工作，无视觉或多模态输入，也无 latent 推理状态、证据获取与停止决策等联系；其中的约束乘子自适应与残差策略虽属通用 RL 技巧，但材料未给出可用于 LVR 的具体借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Power Setpoint Tracking for Large-Scale EV Charging: Transformer-Constrained Charging Policy Learning with Self-Tuning Constraint Prices

**abstract/A1：** In power setpoint tracking (PST), an operator follows a purchased charging profile without overloading shared transformers or undercharging vehicles. PST is cast as a constrained Markov decision process that minimizes tracking error subject to overload and mean user-satisfaction constraints. Transformer-Constrained Charging Policy Learning (TC-CPL) extends soft actor-critic with proportional-integral Lagrange-multiplier updates from measured violations, a residual on the incumbent dispatcher that is warm-started from its demonstrations, and a transformer-aware encoder shared by all ports. It observes no departure times or states of charge. Simulations (five seeds, 100 paired days) compare it with the incumbent, Round-Robin. On a 600-port, 122-substation district, raw overload falls from 3.59 to 0.0017 kWh/day (overloaded seed-days from 77% to 0.4%), and absolute tracking error falls by 27%. Delivered energy rises by 16%, and satisfaction rises from 0.959 to 0.989. On a 42-kW transformer with household load, overload falls from 1.86 to 0.0014 kWh/day and satisfaction rises from 0.974 to 0.985, but absolute tracking error is 22% higher. Against hand-set fixed multipliers, self-tuning is statistically indistinguishable on the district. On the single transformer, it raises satisfaction by 0.011 at similar overload, but a short sweep found a better-serving fixed pair. The overload multipliers stay below their cap.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables. Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Transportation Electrification

</details>

### 342. [Multi-View Transformer with Geometry-Guided Multi-Modal ControlNet for 3D Virtual Try-On](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25979v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25979

**作者：** Sarthak Batra、Armin Mustafa、Adrian Hilton

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25979v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25979v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GS-VTON-MVT，用多视角修复 Transformer、以深度与法线为条件的 ControlNet 以及位姿自适应的参考特征加权，编辑 3DGS 人物的着装并改善新视角一致性。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于 3D 虚拟试穿与图像编辑，跨视角注意力和位姿加权服务于渲染纹理一致性，不涉及推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，也无可核查的 latent 推理迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multi-View Transformer with Geometry-Guided Multi-Modal ControlNet for 3D Virtual Try-On

**abstract/A1：** Editing a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) capture of a person so that they wear a new garment requires every rendered view to agree on the same garment details. Existing 3D virtual try-on (VTON) pipelines, however, edit training views largely independently and fuse them with hand-designed heuristics such as manually chosen reference views and fixed attention-blending weights. The resulting inconsistencies are baked into the 3D representation and surface as texture drift, blurred prints, and implausible geometry in novel views. We present GS-VTON-MVT, a three-stage framework that combines implicit and explicit 3D reasoning for consistent 3DGS try-on. (i) A multi-view inpainting transformer jointly edits a set of training views with full cross-view self-attention, so that cross-view correspondence is learned rather than imposed. (ii) A geometry-guided multi-modal ControlNet conditions the diffusion editor on the garment image together with depth and surface normals rendered from the current 3DGS state, supplying explicit structure where visual evidence is weakest. (iii) A pose-adaptive persona-aware attention replaces the fixed blending weight between current-view and reference-view features with weights derived from camera-pose proximity. On the 3D-VTONBench benchmark, GS-VTON-MVT improves FID, KID, LPIPS-based cross-view consistency, and user preference over general-purpose 3D editors and prior 3D try-on methods.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables

</details>

### 343. [From AI Surrogate to Engineering Tool: Toward Production Use of Fiber-Orientation Prediction in Crash CAE](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25991v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.25991

**作者：** Osamu Ito、Yoshikazu Nakagawa、Tomoaki Tatsukawa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25991v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.25991v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文将纤维取向场预测的代理模型工程化为本田碰撞 CAE 设计工具，涵盖注入浇口设定、体素化、推理、自动映射与报告生成的端到端流程与分阶段验证。

**排除原因：** 属于工业 CAE 代理建模与部署工作流，无视觉语言推理的状态表征、证据获取或 latent 生成机制，也未提供可迁移的推理或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From AI Surrogate to Engineering Tool: Toward Production Use of Fiber-Orientation Prediction in Crash CAE

**abstract/A1：** Crash simulations of glass-fiber-reinforced plastic parts are highly sensitive to the fiber-orientation field supplied to the material model. In conventional workflows, that field comes from resin-flow analysis, which requires molding-specialist support and takes about 25 hours of work per part. We previously proposed a surrogate model that addresses this bottleneck by predicting the orientation field in about 3 minutes. This paper reports the implementation, validation, and operational design that turned the model into an engineering tool for Honda design workflows. We present staged validation from 2D plates to production parts, evaluation against a predefined engineering angular-error criterion, and the expert review that approved design use in March 2025; the system has since been transferred to the crash-safety group and is in initial, limited use in directed fracture-prediction scenarios. The developed system combines injection-gate setting support for cases in which gates are unknown (90% gate-count accuracy, mean position error 11% of flow length), voxelization, surrogate inference, automatic mapping to the crash CAE model, and report generation in a single GUI. The complete orientation-preparation workflow takes about 0.1 hours, a 99.6% reduction from the conventional 25-hour flow-analysis route, and can be run directly by crash CAE engineers without molding-specialist support. As evidence of practical impact, component-level crash analysis using the predicted orientation field reduced the absolute peak-force error from 19.0% with an isotropic baseline to 11.4%, while confining predicted fracture to the test-observed region. The deployment lesson is that a surrogate model alone is not enough for practical adoption: gate-setting support, automatic data conversion, and shareable reporting are what make the model usable as a design tool.

**comment/C1：** Extended version of the paper accepted at IAAI-27 (AAAI 2027), with technical appendices. 10 pages, 10 figures, 2 tables

</details>
