### 344. [Fixing the Twin, Not the Detector: Sim-to-Real Roadside LiDAR Detection from Digital-Twin Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26035v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26035

**作者：** KuanLin Chen、Feng-jui Kuo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26035v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26035v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是路边 LiDAR 3D 检测的 sim-to-real 数据对齐与无标签后处理工程，关注点云密度、标注约定和静态结构，不涉及 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，缺少与 LVR 主线的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fixing the Twin, Not the Detector: Sim-to-Real Roadside LiDAR Detection from Digital-Twin Data

**abstract/A1：** Roadside LiDAR detectors are expensive to train on real labels, and digital twins promise to replace these labels, but a detector trained on a twin meets a different sensor, different objects and different annotation conventions on the real site. We describe the pipeline that placed first in the LUMPI track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge (IEEE BigData 2026 Cup, 0.5541 combined score) and fifth on its V2X-Real track (0.8102). Every detector is a CenterPoint model trained only on synthetic data, on a single 8 GB laptop GPU. Most of the gain came from correcting differences between the twin and the real site rather than from the detector. We align the twin's coordinates, point density and box convention with the test sensor, generate the pedestrians and riders that the twins lack, plus street clutter as unlabeled negatives, and rebuild twin objects at real size and real point density. A non-learned resampling to the per-cell point density of unlabeled real frames lifts LUMPI realism from 0.589 to 0.947, against 0.949 between real frames. Label-free post-processing then exploits what a fixed roadside sensor guarantees: static structures recur in every frame, and real objects have characteristic point statistics. Reporting the validation and test gain of each component side by side, we find that corrections of systematic sim-to-real differences transfer to the test set, typically keeping a quarter to three quarters of their validation gain, while choices that fit the few objects of a validation set do not.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 1 figure, 5 tables. Accepted to the IEEE BigData 2026 Cup (UCF UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge); first place, LUMPI track. Code: https://github.com/dipper13579/urbantwin-sim2real-lidar

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PaveSplat: Resolution-Robust Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting with Residually Corrected Surface-Aligned Covariances

**abstract/A1：** We present PaveSplat, a feed-forward Gaussian splatting method that adds a learned residual to the analytic surface-aligned covariance, keeping surfels paving the visible surface across rendering resolutions. Pixel-aligned feed-forward Gaussian splatting predicts one primitive per pixel from a few images in a single pass, and its rendering quality depends on how well these primitives cover the surface. Recent surfel-based methods compute the surface-aligned covariance analytically from predicted depth. However, this covariance has no way to correct the surfel orientation where the depth-derived frame is ill-posed. The surfels then cover the surface loosely; at the training resolution each surfel's one-pixel minimum footprint covers the gaps, but they show up as holes when the same prediction is re-rendered at a higher resolution. To address this, we keep the analytic covariance as an initialization, with its axes aligned to the surface tangents, and add an identity-initialized unit-quaternion residual that reorients a surfel only where this improves rendering, turning surfels that the analytic frame mis-aims back onto the surface. On RealEstate10K, PaveSplat achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS among single-Gaussian-per-pixel methods at every scale (1x-8x). The native-resolution gain is small; the benefit appears when the same prediction is rendered at higher display resolutions without re-running the network. On zero-shot DL3DV, it leads PSNR within this group at every scale and ranks first or second on SSIM/LPIPS. A coverage-hole analysis shows fewer uncovered pixels than both baselines under high-resolution re-rendering.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to ACCV 2026. 16 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://janghunn.github.io/pavesplat/ ; code: https://github.com/janghunn/pavesplat

</details>

### 346. [Studying AI Act Mentions: A first look at the impact of the European Union's AI regulation on Hugging Face](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26050v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26050

**作者：** Anamaria Mojica-Hanke、Tessi Aigbovbiosa-Igbinovia、Steffen Herbold

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26050v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26050v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文对 Hugging Face 上超五百万条记录做实证分析，人工编码约 1500 条显式提及欧盟《人工智能法案》的内容，考察合规声明在模型卡、讨论与提交中的分布与表述方式。

**排除原因：** 研究主题为 AI 监管合规与平台治理，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、生成与训练，也未提供可迁移至 LVR 的方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Studying AI Act Mentions: A first look at the impact of the European Union's AI regulation on Hugging Face

**abstract/A1：** The European Union's Artificial Intelligence Act (AI Act) entered into force in August 2024, and its obligations apply in stages. Obligations for providers of general-purpose AI models have applied since August 2025, and transparency rules since August 2026. These obligations affect developers who share and deploy models and applications on public platforms such as Hugging Face (HF), the largest platform for distributing AI artifacts. However, little is known about how practitioners respond to the regulation in practice. To address this gap, we conducted an empirical study of explicit references to the AI Act on HF. We collected over five million records, including repository cards, discussions, and commits from Models, Datasets, and Spaces, and identified over 1,500 records that explicitly mention the Act. Two coders manually labeled these records using a primarily closed coding approach, and we analyzed the resulting categories per source. We find that explicit references to the AI Act remain rare across all repository types. Repository cards mainly contain claims of compliance and responsibility, like ensuring compliance or assigning responsibility to downstream users, and some claim that the Act does not apply because the model is not a general-purpose AI model. Discussions are used to report alleged violations of the Act, while commits mainly update artifacts that support compliance. Overall, compliance statements focus on who is responsible rather than how compliance is achieved. We believe that this calls for direct platform support, e.g., automated checks and documentation templates, as well as clearer guidance on the scope of the AI Act.

</details>

### 347. [Complete-SVD: A Functional Norm Approach to Low-Rank LLM Compression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26059v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26059

**作者：** Alper Kalle、Theo Rudkiewicz、Mohamed-Oumar Ouerfelli、Mohamed Tamaazousti

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26059v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26059v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Complete-SVD：对原始与压缩模型 logits 的 L2 距离做线性化与 Kronecker 分解近似，化为带闭式解的加权 SVD，并用随机探针无偏估计输出侧因子，用于 LLM 低秩压缩。

**排除原因：** 该工作关注权重低秩近似与困惑度、指令跟随等压缩指标，未涉及视觉信息保留、latent 推理状态生成或推理期证据使用，也无可供核查的状态或决策类方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Complete-SVD: A Functional Norm Approach to Low-Rank LLM Compression

**abstract/A1：** Post-training low-rank compression of neural networks must not only approximate the original weights but also preserve the model's input-output behavior. Recent output-aware methods measure the compression error beyond the compressed layer, at the model output, weighting logit changes by the observed next token or by the model's predicted probabilities. We propose Complete-SVD, a method for large language models that minimizes the $L\_2$ distance between the logits of the original and compressed models, weighting all logit changes equally. Linearizing the network around the original weights, together with a Kronecker-factored approximation, reduces this objective to a weighted SVD with a closed-form solution. Its output-side factor is intractable to compute; we estimate it without bias from random probes, at a cost of $K$ backward passes per sequence, independent of vocabulary size and sequence length. At matched settings with uniform ranks and WikiText-2 calibration, Complete-SVD achieves the lowest C4 and PTB perplexity in every setting, and at ratio 0.7 under LEMS rank allocation. Replacing the $L\_2$ distance by the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KL-SVD), with the same estimation framework, lowers calibration perplexity in most settings and raises downstream accuracy, but raises cross-corpus perplexity. Complete-SVD also improves IFEval instruction-following accuracy over the strongest prior output-aware methods throughout.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 3 figures, 15 tables

</details>

### 348. [DfD-GS: Defocus Image Prior for Metric-Scale Single-View Depth Estimation and View Synthesis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26072v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26072

**作者：** Shiori Ueda、Rinto Yagawa、Hideo Saito、Shohei Mori

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26072v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26072v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DfD-GS，利用全聚焦—离焦图像对中的离焦模糊作为物理度量线索，估计度量尺度视差以校准单目深度先验，并前馈预测可见与遮挡区域的 3D Gaussian 场景，用于单视图新视角合成。

**排除原因：** 属于三维重建与新视角合成任务，关注物理离焦线索与深度尺度校准，不涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、latent 生成或证据获取与停止决策，缺少可供 LVR 借鉴的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DfD-GS: Defocus Image Prior for Metric-Scale Single-View Depth Estimation and View Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** Recovering metric-scale geometry from observations at a single viewpoint remains fundamentally ill-posed, limiting the reliability of novel view synthesis. Recent methods rely on learned monocular depth priors, which provide strong structural estimates but can suffer from scale ambiguity and dataset-dependent biases. We propose DfD-GS, a feed-forward framework that predicts a metric-scale 3D Gaussian scene representation from a single-view all-in-focus--defocused image pair. The key idea is to exploit defocus blur as a physical metric depth cue. DfD-GS first estimates a defocus-based metric disparity map from the all-in-focus--defocused image pair and the corresponding defocus strength. This estimate is used to align and refine a monocular depth prior to metric scale. Visible and hidden 3D Gaussians are then predicted for occlusion-aware novel view synthesis. Experiments on synthetic-defocus datasets constructed from Booster and Middlebury show that DfD-GS outperforms single-image baselines in metric depth estimation and novel view synthesis. On WildRGB-D, DfD-GS achieves lower AbsRel and LPIPS but higher depth RMSE than the single-image baselines. Additional analyses show that DfD-GS maintains a more stable metric scale under field-of-view changes. Qualitative results on a real dataset show recovery of the main scene structure under optical defocus.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://sh1027.github.io/dfd-gs/

</details>

### 349. [Pareto-Optimal and Strategyproof Data-Sharing Between Competitors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26108v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26108

**作者：** Morgan Colin-Mourre、Felipe Garrido-Lucero、Patrick Loiseau

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.GT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26108v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26108v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究同一市场竞争企业间的数据共享契约：以预测模型精度与市场份额刻画利润，刻画 Pareto 最优契约，提出使相对份额不变且对虚报初始数据量具有策略防伪性的契约族，并用真实数据做实证检验。

**排除原因：** 属于机制设计与数据市场的经济学分析，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、生成、训练监督或停止决策，材料中也没有可供迁移到 LVR 的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pareto-Optimal and Strategyproof Data-Sharing Between Competitors

**abstract/A1：** We study data-sharing among firms competing in the same market through the quality of their predictive models. We model firms' profit through two main components, precision and market share, where the former depends on the firm's own number of datapoints and the latter depends on the endowments of all firms. We characterize Pareto-optimal contracts as those in which at least one firm receives all of the other firms' datapoints. We then propose a parametric family of data-sharing contracts and show that, under any of them, the relative market shares of all firms remain constant, eliminating the negative effect of sharing data with competitors and equalizing all firms' gains relative to the no-sharing scenario. We further show that one contract within this family satisfies both Pareto-optimality and strategyproofness against firms attempting to under-declare the size of their initial endowment. Finally, using a real-world dataset, we empirically test the gain in profit for all firms under the contract described above, and show that data-sharing is particularly beneficial when the machine learning task is harder or when firms' initial endowments are closer to homogeneous.

</details>

### 350. [MultiBinder: Preventing Multi-Subject Identity Leakage in Personalized Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26164v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26164

**作者：** Wei Li、Yiheng Zhang、Zhaofan Qiu、Ting Yao、Xiaoyan Sun、Tao Mei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26164v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26164v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究个性化视频生成中多主体身份泄漏，提出「先绑定后叙事」的两阶段条件注入以对齐实体短语与参考图身份特征；虽涉及实体—视觉绑定与解耦表示，但属于生成任务的条件控制，没有推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策等与 LVR 相关的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MultiBinder: Preventing Multi-Subject Identity Leakage in Personalized Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Personalized video generation faces a significant scalability challenge: as the number of custom subjects increases, existing methods suffer from severe identity leakage and semantic entanglement, resulting in videos with blended attributes and incoherent narratives. We identify that this stems from a lack of explicit binding between textual entity phrases and their corresponding visual patterns prior to fusion in diffusion models. To address this, we propose MultiBinder, a novel framework built on a \`\`bind-then-narrate'' paradigm. Our approach introduces a two-stage conditioning process before video diffusion. First, Cross-modal Entity Binding devises an entity-aware Q-Former to extract identity-centric features from each subject's reference image and tightly align them with its unique entity phrase, yielding disentangled \`\`identity tokens.'' Second, a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) acts as a \`\`narrative director'' in the Narrative-Aware Subject Arrangement stage, capturing the action of each subject from the text prompt and synthesizes contextualized subject features that encapsulate \`\`who is doing what'' and preserve role-specific distinctions. These features are then injected into a video diffusion model via dedicated conditioning pathways, ensuring precise attribute allocation. Extensive experiments on both general and newly curated multi-subject benchmarks show that MultiBinder significantly outperforms existing methods in multi-subject identity fidelity.

**comment/C1：** ACM Multimedia 2026

</details>

### 351. [StreamEgo: Streaming Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26165v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26165

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26165v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26165v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** StreamEgo 提出实时流式的第三人称到第一人称视频生成框架，通过双分支先验、区域感知损失课程与分块自回归蒸馏实现无界长视频合成。

**排除原因：** 该工作是视角转换的视频生成与扩散蒸馏，其中的「状态」是视觉帧流而非推理状态，未涉及证据获取、latent 推理或停止决策，区域感知损失等仅服务于生成保真度，缺乏可供 LVR 核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StreamEgo: Streaming Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Exocentric-to-egocentric (Exo2Ego) video generation transforms third-person recordings into first-person viewpoints, unlocking immersive AR/VR experiences and scalable demonstration synthesis for embodied robotics. Extending it to a streaming setting is expected to further enable live applications. However, existing methods suffer from degraded manipulation regions, overfitting and confinement to fixed-length clips with prohibitive latency. To address these limitations, we present StreamEgo, a real-time framework for unbounded, generalizable Exo2Ego video generation. To resolve intricate manipulation dynamics while supporting the streaming pipeline, a streaming dual-branch prior provides pixel-aligned guidance for translating appearance and skeletal kinematics. We also introduce a region-aware loss curriculum to improve generalization and stabilize multi-objective diffusion training. Furthermore, we distill the model into a chunk-wise autoregressive model, augmented with a region-aware corruption mechanism and a first-chunk curriculum, enabling high-quality, generalizable real-time streaming Exo2Ego generation at 24.3 FPS on a single H100 GPU.

</details>

### 352. [The FrontierOR Challenge: Agentic Algorithm Design for Operations Research](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26171v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26171

**作者：** Hai Wang、Bretislav Hajek、Chonghe Jiang、Minwei Kong、Ao Qu、Huatian Gong、Bo Dong、Cynthia Barnhart、Jinhua Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26171v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26171v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 FrontierOR 挑战赛，让团队构建 LLM agent 工作流，自动为运筹优化问题设计、测试并改进算法，并按可行性校验与目标值比进行排名。

**排除原因：** 材料为运筹优化算法设计竞赛，未涉及视觉或多模态内部表示、latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，也无具体方法线索可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The FrontierOR Challenge: Agentic Algorithm Design for Operations Research

**abstract/A1：** Operations research (OR) relies on algorithms that experts tailor to each problem: a new routing, scheduling or network design problem can take weeks of specialist work before it is solved well at scale. Large language model (LLM) agents now write, test and improve code, but whether they can design algorithms that scale to realistic instances is open: in the FrontierOR benchmark's one-shot evaluation, the strongest model matches Gurobi, with an objective within 1% of Gurobi's and no slower, on only 39% of the large instances. The FrontierOR Challenge asks teams to build agentic workflows that design, test, and improve algorithms for many optimization problems at once. Stage 1 releases 60 of the benchmark's 180 audited problems, spanning six application categories and five formulation types, in weekly rounds of 15; Stage 2 introduces new problems outside the benchmark, with unseen instances. Two tracks are ranked separately: in the Main track we run the submitted algorithms in a network-isolated sandbox with open-source dependencies only; in the Self-hosted track teams solve the instances on their own machines, with any software, within a timed window. Every solution is checked for feasibility, and a feasible solution scores the ratio of its objective to a frozen known reference, averaged over hidden instances.

</details>

### 353. [FlexVSR: Continuous-Scale Super-Resolution for Streaming Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26178v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26178

**作者：** Ji Wang、Haonan An、Jiasheng Zhang、Renlong Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26178v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26178v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向流式视频生成超分的连续尺度 latent 适配，latent 仅服务于重建画质与延迟，未涉及推理状态、证据获取、答案使用或停止决策；与 LVR 的共同点仅为「latent」术语，缺乏可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FlexVSR: Continuous-Scale Super-Resolution for Streaming Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Real-time streaming video generation has gained rapid momentum in live content creation and interactive media. Deploying such systems in practice requires flexible scaling factors to accommodate diverse source resolutions, target displays, and computational budgets. However, extending fixed-scale generative video super-resolution (VSR) to varying target scales poses two challenges: downsampling from a larger fixed scale incurs redundant cost, while re-optimizing the diffusion transformer (DiT) is costly and alters the shared generative prior. To address both, we propose FlexVSR, a decoupled framework for continuous-scale streaming generative VSR with supervision at only a few scales. Our key insight is that naively resampling fixed-scale latents onto a new target grid induces scale-dependent input mismatch, which can be effectively addressed through parameter-efficient, scale-aware adaptation of the latent interface without updating the DiT. To this end, FlexVSR introduces ReLSA (Refined Latent Scale Adaptation), a lightweight latent adapter that constructs and refines target-grid latents before they enter the DiT, enabling a shared frozen VSR prior to generalize across continuous scales with only 0.274M trainable parameters and an adaptation cost of 7.3 H100 GPU-hours, while introducing no additional temporal dependencies and preserving the native causal streaming protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive or superior reconstruction and temporal quality, lower latency than most native-scale streaming baselines and offline methods, and effective generalization to unseen continuous scales, filling the continuous-scale gap in real-time streaming generative VSR.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 5 figures

</details>

### 354. [Thermodynamically Consistent Neural Networks for a Universal Fluid Gibbs Free Energy Equation of State](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26183v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26183

**作者：** Xurui Li、Zhiguo Gan、Jiaming Zhang、Hongxi Zeng、Diannan Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26183v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26183v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文以 Gibbs 自由能为起点，构建热力学一致的神经网络状态方程（GEoS），用于统一表征流体状态并预测气液相密度。

**排除原因：** 属流体热力学建模工作，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的表示、证据筛选与停止决策，也无可迁移的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Thermodynamically Consistent Neural Networks for a Universal Fluid Gibbs Free Energy Equation of State

**abstract/A1：** Over the past three centuries, many thermodynamics researchers have worked to develop fluid equations of state with both high accuracy and broad applicability, proposing various theoretical frameworks and models. Building on these efforts, this study uses Gibbs free energy as the starting point and introduces thermodynamically consistent neural networks to construct a fluid equation of state based on Gibbs free energy (GEoS). This approach establishes a novel machine learning framework that provides a unified representation of fluid states. GEoS performs well on the vapor- and liquid-phase density test sets, with average absolute relative deviations (AARDs) of 5.25% and 3.96%, respectively. It outperforms conventional volume-translated cubic equations of state and existing machine learning models, demonstrating its effectiveness. Further analysis using the UMAP dimensionality reduction method shows that most molecules in the test set belong to extrapolation systems, confirming the strong generalization ability of the model. Finally, the test results show that the PC-SAFT equation fitted using GEoS maintains high predictive accuracy, providing a reliable basis for its practical application in industry.

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Backbone Matters: Benchmarking Encoder-Decoder Networks for Semantic Segmentation of Driving Scenes on BDD100K

**abstract/A1：** Semantic segmentation gives an autonomous vehicle a dense, pixel-level description of its surroundings and is a core part of scene understanding. Most segmentation networks consist of an encoder (the backbone) and a decoder, yet the backbone is often chosen without a systematic comparison. This work studies this choice on the BDD100K driving dataset. Four widely used decoders (U-Net, FPN, LinkNet and PSPNet) are combined with 13 backbones from six families (ResNet, VGG, DenseNet, Inception, MobileNet and EfficientNet), and all 52 models are trained with one common protocol. The best model, an FPN decoder with an EfficientNet-B4 backbone, reaches 62.6% mean intersection over union (mIoU), 89.8% accuracy and a loss of 0.092. Only two of the 52 models, both FPN with EfficientNet, exceed the 56.9% mIoU reported with the BDD100K benchmark. The effect of the backbone depends strongly on the decoder: the same EfficientNet-B4 gives 42.6% mIoU with PSPNet but 62.6% with FPN, and the gap between the best and the worst backbone ranges from 12.0 points (U-Net) to 42.5 points (FPN). The results and the scope of this study are discussed.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A version of the Cloude/van Zyl decomposition for full/quad pol synthetic aperture radar data

**abstract/A1：** Based on the Cloude/van Zyl decomposition of reflection symmetric polarimetric SAR data we present a version for full/quad polarisation data. We demonstrate both versions on X-band F-SAR data over Vejers, Denmark. Differences between the original and our version of the decomposition occur especially for very bright man-made targets. These differences come with lower entropy for our version giving an improved focus on a more pronounced dominant scattering mechanism for these objects. Interpretation of the scattering mechanism related to the full/quad pol version of the decomposition is facilitated by means of the Cloude/Pottier $\\barα$ angle.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 5 figures

</details>

### 357. [Physics-Informed Feature Representations and Pretrained Tabular Foundation Models for Generalizable Melt Pool Geometry Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26235v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26235

**作者：** Rafid Enayet、Imtiaz Ahmed、Md Habibor Rahman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26235v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26235v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究在激光粉末床熔融中用无量纲物理特征（归一化焓、Peclet 数）配合经典 ML 与预训练表格基础模型，预测熔池宽度、深度与熔化模式，并评估跨材料泛化。

**排除原因：** 属材料增材制造的表格回归/分类应用，无视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选与停止决策等关联，物理无量纲特征虽涉及表示设计但不构成对 LVR 的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physics-Informed Feature Representations and Pretrained Tabular Foundation Models for Generalizable Melt Pool Geometry Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Developing generalizable, data-efficient machine learning (ML) models for advanced manufacturing remains challenging because experimental datasets are limited and process-property relationships vary across materials and operating conditions. In laser powder bed fusion (LPBF), melt pool width, depth, and melting mode (conduction, transition, or keyhole) govern process stability and defect formation. Conventional ML models typically use dimensional process parameters, which represent laser-material interactions only implicitly and may limit cross-material transfer. This study evaluates whether two physics-informed dimensionless features, normalized enthalpy and Peclet number, combining laser processing conditions with material thermophysical properties, improve melt pool prediction over raw process parameters. We systematically benchmark seven models: four classical ML methods (Gaussian process, support vector machine, random forest, and XGBoost) and three pretrained tabular foundation models (TabPFN, TabDPT, and TabICLv2). Experiments span 316L stainless steel, IN625, IN718, and combined-material datasets, covering regression of melt pool width and depth and classification of melting modes. Dimensionless features yield comparable or superior predictive performance in most settings, with the largest gains in cross-material depth prediction (R^2 = 0.96). Tabular foundation models generally achieve stronger classification performance than classical baselines, particularly for multi-material datasets. These findings indicate that combining physically meaningful dimensionless representations with pretrained tabular models can improve data-efficient melt pool modeling and support more transferable process-window identification in LPBF.

</details>

<a id="paper-358"></a>

### 358. [Beyond Covers and Titles: Evidence-First Ranking for Robust Cold-Start Recommendation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26249v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26249

**作者：** Fuyi Wang、Yuxin Cao、Yifan Liao、Renyang Liu、Lin Li、Wei Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26249v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26249v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究短视频冷启动推荐中的「包装信息」对抗攻击与防御，核心指标是排序名次与 NDCG，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据获取或生成式推理；虽有视频内容塔与门控残差，但目标是排序鲁棒性而非推理表示，缺少具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Covers and Titles: Evidence-First Ranking for Robust Cold-Start Recommendation

**abstract/A1：** Short-video platforms rank new uploads before anyone has watched them, using content towers that read a video's cover, title and frames. Existing attacks on content-aware recommenders perturb pixels or features and ignore platform re-encoding, and existing defenses train against norm-bounded perturbations that neither match real edits nor preserve recommendation quality. We show with REAP, a realizable attack through {a simulated platform channel (H.264 transcoding, rescaling, cropping and JPEG)}, that packaging edits raise a cold video by 32.0 rank-percentile points in unrelated audiences. We then observe that cold-start ranking does not need the packaging at all: a tower that sees only the video body ranks cold items best. Building on this, we propose Evidence-First Ranking (EFR), which scores cold videos from their body, admits packaging only through a support-gated residual bounded by $γ$, and merges cold and warm rankings by rank percentile, yielding an exact certificate against any packaging edit. On MicroLens-100K, EFR cuts the attack gain by 81% and raises cold-start Recall@100 from 0.1133 to 0.170, while keeping the merged NDCG@10 within 4% of the undefended system.

</details>

<a id="paper-359"></a>

### 359. [Prism: Hierarchical Text Detection with Minimalist Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26254v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26254

**作者：** Yuchen Su、Xuesheng Wang、Zhilong Ji、Jinfeng Bai、Shijian Lu、Zhineng Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26254v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26254v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Prism，一种面向分层文本检测的极简框架：在单尺度主干特征上并联轻量头分别检测词、行、段落，并用几何包含算法重建层级结构。

**排除原因：** 属于场景文本检测的架构轻量化与效率刷分工作，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据筛选、停止决策或 VLM 推理训练；其层级解析线索与 LVR 的视觉证据组织关联过远，缺少可核查的迁移点。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Prism: Hierarchical Text Detection with Minimalist Design

**abstract/A1：** Hierarchical text detection aims to localize words, text lines, and paragraphs while parsing their structural relationships. It provides essential structural cues for visual text understanding. To maximize accuracy, existing methods typically rely on complex multi-scale feature fusion necks and heavy-weight heads, resulting in bloated architectures and low inference efficiency. In this paper, we argue that a sufficiently pre-trained backbone can effectively replace these sophisticated external modules, while simple geometric inclusion suffices for hierarchical text structural parsing. Thus, we propose Prism, a minimalist framework for hierarchical text detection. Prism performs multi-granularity detection by attaching parallel lightweight heads to a single-scale backbone feature map, covering words, text lines, and paragraphs, and reconstructs the structural hierarchy via an efficient geometric inclusion algorithm. In addition, we systematically compare representative lightweight head archetypes and show that pixel-level regression offers the best accuracy-efficiency trade-off in this setting. Experiments on multiple public benchmarks show that our significantly simplified architecture achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance with remarkable efficiency. Specifically, it runs $1.7\\times$ faster than LRANet on CTW1500 while achieving higher accuracy, and surpasses the current SOTA method, OmniParser V2, by over 6 PQ points on the hierarchical HierText dataset. Code will be released soon.

</details>

### 360. [Similarity-based OSF Logic for Flexible SPARQL Query Answering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26261v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26261

**作者：** Gian Carlo Milanese、Gabriella Pasi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DB、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26261v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26261v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作是语义网本体推理的演示系统：用模糊 OSF 逻辑的相似关系与约束归一化重写查询，使 SPARQL 能返回近似答案；其相似度定义在 sort 的模糊包含关系上，而非模型习得的内部表示，也不涉及视觉证据获取、推理状态更新或计算停止决策，缺乏可供核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Similarity-based OSF Logic for Flexible SPARQL Query Answering

**abstract/A1：** We present an updated version of fosf, a reasoner for fuzzy order-sorted feature (OSF) logic, with support for flexible SPARQL query answering over RDF knowledge graphs. Fuzzy OSF logic is a knowledge representation and reasoning language where feature symbols denote functions and sort symbols denote fuzzy sets. Sorts are organized in a fuzzy subsumption relation that extends to OSF terms, record-like structures representing classes of entities. The unification algorithm for these structures provides a calculus of fuzzy type subsumption. The latest release of fosf introduces support for similarity-based reasoning by extending the OSF language with a similarity relation among sorts, alongside a translation engine for compiling OSF terms into standard SPARQL. Queries expressed as OSF terms undergo fuzzy ontology-aware rewriting prior to translation. This process incorporates an OSF theory of sort definitions, feature domain and range declarations, and fuzzy subsumption and similarity relations through fuzzy OSF constraint normalization. This rewriting enables flexible query answering by returning approximate solutions, allowing queries that would otherwise be inconsistent with the underlying ontology to yield relevant results.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ISWC 2026 (Demo)

</details>

### 361. [Digitization of Analogue Gauges using Computer Vision](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26262v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26262

**作者：** Erico Kayumba Mutonkole、Olasupo Ajayi、Omowunmi Isafiade

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26262v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26262v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向工业压力表读数自动化，比较传统 OpenCV/Scikit-Image 流程与 OpenCV 结合 ResNet18、VGG19 的混合方案，在自建 1,848 张仪表图像上测试，报告 ResNet18 达 97.3% 准确率。

**排除原因：** 这是工业仪表图像的识别与读数应用，属于一般视觉任务落地，未涉及多模态推理状态、证据获取或停止决策等具体联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Digitization of Analogue Gauges using Computer Vision

**abstract/A1：** Analogue gauges remain prevalent in manufacturing industries for measuring physical quantities, yet manual monitoring by operators introduces human error and operational inefficiency. This research developed and evaluated an automated gauge reading system using computer vision and deep learning techniques. Three hybrid approaches were implemented and compared: OpenCV with Scikit-Image (traditional computer vision), OpenCV with ResNet18, and OpenCV with VGG19 (deep learning approaches). A comprehensive dataset of 1,848 pressure gauge images (0-10 BAR range) was curated through web scraping, synthetic generation, and augmentation techniques. The experimental results demonstrate that ResNet18 significantly outperforms the alternatives, achieving an accuracy of 97.3%, compared to VGG19's 64.4% and Scikit-Image's 63.8%. The study validates the effectiveness of residual learning architectures for industrial gauge digitization, providing a robust foundation for Industry 5.0 automation applications.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 12 figures. Presented at SATNAC 2025

</details>

### 362. [C-LiTE: A Lightweight Foundation Model for Cellular feature learning in Histopathology](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26316v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26316

**作者：** Esha Sadia Nasir、Jiaqi Lv、Gozde N. Gunesli、Shan E Ahmed Raza

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26316v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26316v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一个仅2200万参数的轻量细胞级病理基础模型，采用原生分辨率 tokenisation 与预训练 ViT 权重伪逆适配，并用6000万细胞裁剪图自监督预训练，在冻结特征下评估细胞分类等任务。

**排除原因：** 属于病理图像表征学习与刷分型应用，没有涉及 latent 推理状态、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，与 LVR/VLM 推理主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** C-LiTE: A Lightweight Foundation Model for Cellular feature learning in Histopathology

**abstract/A1：** Characterising individual cells is fundamental to computational pathology, as cellular morphology informs tasks such as tumour grading, mitotic assessment and immune profiling. However, most pathology foundation models are trained on tissue patches and capture cellular features only implicitly, while existing cell-level models are either restricted to specific cell populations or rely on computationally expensive architectures with hundreds of millions of parameters. Applying ViTs directly to small cellular crops also presents an architectural challenge: upsampling increases computational cost without adding morphological information and may introduce interpolation artefacts. To address these limitations, we introduce C-LiTE, a lightweight foundation model for cellular representation learning in H&amp;E-stained histopathology, with only 22 million parameters. C-LiTE employs a native-resolution tokenisation strategy tailored to cellular inputs, processing 64 x 64 px cell-centred crops using a 4 x 4 patch embedding to produce a 16 x 16 token grid without image upsampling. To transfer visual knowledge from large-scale pre-training, we adapt the patch embedding weights of a pre-trained ViT-5-S using a pseudoinverse-based transformation, enabling weight initialisation at the finer patch resolution. We further assemble a pre-training dataset of 60 million cell-centred crops from TCGA, balanced across 19 cancer types and 16 tissue types. To our knowledge, this represents the largest public dataset for self-supervised pre-training on H&amp;E images at the cellular level. We evaluate C-LiTE on seven public datasets spanning mitotic figure recognition, nucleus and cell-type classification, and immune cell phenotyping. Under frozen-feature evaluation, C-LiTE achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.670, approaching CytoFormer (0.682) with approximately 30x fewer parameters.

</details>

### 363. [Contactless Estimation of Respiratory Frequency Using 3D-CNN on Thermal Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26367v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26367

**作者：** Federica Gioia、Filippo Pura、Alberto Greco、Dario Piga、Arcangelo Merla、Marco Forgione

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26367v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26367v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究提出用 3D-CNN 直接从原始热成像视频估计呼吸频率，借助数据增强与合成数据迁移学习，跳过复杂预处理与 ROI 跟踪。

**排除原因：** 属于生理信号的无接触监测应用，既不涉及 VLM 推理状态，也没有证据筛选、停止决策或 latent 表示使用等可供核查的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Contactless Estimation of Respiratory Frequency Using 3D-CNN on Thermal Images

**abstract/A1：** Monitoring physiological parameters such as respiratory rate ($f\_R$) is essential for diagnosing and managing various pathological conditions. Thermal imaging offers a promising contactless alternative to traditional methods, which often rely on partially invasive sensors or obtrusive wearable systems. However, existing approaches for $f\_R$ estimation from thermal signals typically require extensive pre-processing and manual or semi-automatic region-of-interest (ROI) tracking, limiting their practical applicability. This study proposes a deep learning-based method for estimating $f\_R$ directly from thermal videos, eliminating the need for complex pre-processing and ROI tracking. A 3D Convolutional Neural Network (3D-CNN) is developed to operate on raw thermal video data. To address challenges related to small datasets, the model is trained using data augmentation and transfer learning from synthetic datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves a validation $R^2$ score of approximately 0.61 on both pre-processed and raw thermal videos. By simplifying the workflow, this method holds promise for enhancing the feasibility of thermal imaging in real-world applications, such as remote healthcare and driver monitoring in automotive applications.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26369

**作者：** Jiangjie Qiu、Chengrui Xu、Xiaonan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26369v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26369v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文比较13个药物-靶点相互作用系统在FDA药物与人蛋白上的双向推荐，用Top-10重合度、命中率与换种子重训稳定性，考察模型预测能否支撑实际实验筛选决策。

**排除原因：** 该工作属药物发现领域的评测研究，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或停止决策，其模型间推荐重合与重训波动分析也缺少可迁移到LVR的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Benchmarks to the Bench: Preference, Correspondence and Selectivity in Bidirectional Drug-Target Screening

**abstract/A1：** Identifying active molecules for known targets and potential targets for existing drugs is central to drug discovery and repurposing. Drug-target interaction (DTI) models can rank vast numbers of drug-target pairs, but limited experimental capacity restricts testing to a small number of candidates. We compared the bidirectional recommendations of 13 released systems across 711 molecular structures of FDA-approved drugs and 614 human proteins. For the same target or drug, pairs of models produced completely disjoint Top-10 molecule and protein lists in 77.8% and 70.1% of comparisons, respectively. Even where recommendations overlapped, agreement between some systems largely reflected recurring preferences for the same candidates. Published inhibition measurements for 79 molecules and 65 kinases revealed that some systems enriched active candidates, yet their hit yields changed little when recommendations were matched to different drugs or targets, whereas other systems lost substantially more hits. Some of the former systems also rarely ranked both molecules and kinases correctly when two drugs favoured different kinases. We further retrained the same architecture on the same data, with the training objective fixed and random seeds varied. Despite similar test performance, mean pairwise Top-10 overlap on a separate screening catalogue was about two candidates. Our study moves DTI model evaluation beyond benchmark performance to assess whether model predictions can guide meaningful experimental decisions in drug discovery and target identification.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 5 figures and 1 table

</details>

### 365. [Fast Self-Supervised Generative CT Denoising via Few-Step Sampling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26370v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26370

**作者：** Xingyi Yao、Zhengtao Ma、Zhennong Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26370v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26370v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究低剂量或薄层 CT 的自监督生成式去噪，用 few-step mean flow 采样加多输出平均提升效率，属医学图像增强应用；其加速与多采样平均针对像素噪声而非推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系，也不构成可迁移的 latent 生成或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fast Self-Supervised Generative CT Denoising via Few-Step Sampling

**abstract/A1：** Low-dose and thin-slice computed tomography (CT) images suffer from elevated noise that obscures anatomical detail, while clean references for supervised denoising are often unavailable. Although Noise2Noise permits self-supervised training from noisy pairs, direct regression tends to oversmooth fine structure. Averaging multiple independently generated samples mitigates this blur, though repeating multi-step diffusion sampling is costly. We introduce Noise2Noise with Improved Mean Flow (N2N-iMF) to accelerate this process via few-step sampling. N2N-iMF learns to generate one noisy image conditioned on its paired counterpart; at inference, independent outputs are sampled and averaged to suppress noise. We evaluated image quality and sampling efficiency on simulated thin-slice brain and low-dose abdominal CT across four sampling methods and averaging counts from 1 to 20, and validated the approach on real-world photon-counting CT (PCCT) brain scans. With ten samples per conditioning input and three network evaluations per sample, N2N-iMF achieved a mean absolute error of 2.60 HU on brain CT and 14.02 HU on abdominal CT, outperforming 50-evaluation denoising diffusion implicit model (DDIM) sampling (2.91 and 14.53 HU). On the same GPU, per-output generation time dropped from 7.29 to 0.43 s. Across eight PCCT cases, N2N-iMF reached a mean gray-white matter contrast-to-noise ratio of 1.341, versus 0.916 for Noise2Noise and 1.176 for DDIM. N2N-iMF accelerates self-supervised generative CT denoising without distillation while keeping the number of averaged outputs adjustable at inference.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables. Xingyi Yao and Zhengtao Ma contributed equally to this work. Zhennong Chen is the corresponding author

</details>

<a id="paper-366"></a>

### 366. [FFGaussType: Feed-Forward Conversion of Glyph and SVG Geometry into Compact Gaussian Assets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26431v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26431

**作者：** Haoan Feng、Tianfu Wang、Peimeng Guan、Dae Yeol Lee、Guan-ming Su

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26431v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26431v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将挤出的字形与 SVG 轮廓通过单次前向传播转换为紧凑的 Gaussian 资产，用共享解码器预测带法向的 neural Gaussians，并支持特征平面压缩传输。

**排除原因：** 属于 3D 内容表示与资产生成方向，不涉及多模态推理状态、视觉证据使用或推理期计算决策，未提供可供 LVR 借鉴的状态更新或评价线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FFGaussType: Feed-Forward Conversion of Glyph and SVG Geometry into Compact Gaussian Assets

**abstract/A1：** Immersive 3D content allows text and graphical elements, such as subtitles, annotations, and icons, to be placed inside a scene rather than displayed as flat overlays. Placed this way, they respect occlusion and perspective and take on user-assigned materials and lighting while preserving their original shapes. In Gaussian scenes, however, each new glyph or icon requires its own iterative optimization, which limits scalable content creation. We present FFGaussType, which converts extruded glyph and SVG outlines into Gaussian assets in a single forward pass. A point encoder maps anchors sampled on the known surface to a single feature plane, from which a decoder shared by all assets predicts neural Gaussians carrying surface normals. This separates geometric conversion from material and lighting choices, and the feature plane supports image and video coding for compact delivery. We construct a paired dataset of 27,221 glyph and SVG icon assets with anchors and calibrated normal and silhouette renderings, holding out font families for evaluation. Conversion from prepared anchors takes 9.1 ms per asset, at a fraction of the compute and memory of prior baselines. On unseen font families and test icons, FFGaussType achieves $1.62^\\circ$ normal error and $0.985$ silhouette IoU, the best among the compared feed-forward converters, matching the fidelity that per-asset fitting needs thousands of steps to reach. The code and dataset will be released after publication.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages. Project page: https://fengyee.github.io/FFGaussType/

</details>

<a id="paper-367"></a>

### 367. [AdaPixFlow: Learning Continuous-Time 3D Dynamics Across Scenes from Sparse Multi-view Observations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26433v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26433

**作者：** Hrishikesh Viswanath、Aniket Bera

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26433v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26433v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 AdaPixFlow，将多视角 RGB-D 观测融合到世界坐标体素网格，学习跨场景的连续时间速度场算子，以预测物体在观测间和观测后的 3D 位置。

**排除原因：** 属于 3D 场景物理动力学/世界模型预测，虽含连续时间状态积分，但不涉及 VLM 推理中的视觉证据筛选、latent 状态生成或计算停止决策，与 LVR 主线无具体可核查联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AdaPixFlow: Learning Continuous-Time 3D Dynamics Across Scenes from Sparse Multi-view Observations

**abstract/A1：** Understanding how objects move in dynamic scenes requires recovering their 3D motion from visual observations and predicting their positions at unobserved times. Existing methods either optimize scene-specific velocity fields or impose predefined physical and kinematic assumptions. We instead study learning a continuous-time dynamics operator across scenes, mapping multi-view visual observations to 3D object kinematics. We introduce Adaptive Pixel Flows (\\ours{}), which learns this operator from multi-view RGB-D observations without per-scene optimization or predefined motion models. The method fuses observations from all cameras into a shared voxel grid in world coordinates, from which the network predicts a continuous-time velocity field. Because the operator is trained across scenes, it learns motion priors that carry over to unseen scenes. The field can then be integrated from observed states to predict object positions between observations and beyond the observed sequence. On held-out PhysInOne scenes, \\ours{} improves F1 over the strongest competing method by 2.5\\% for in-between prediction and 15\\% for future prediction. Compared with per-scene velocity-field methods, the improvements are 30\\% and 85\\%, respectively. Applied zero-shot to Spring-Gaus, it improves future-prediction F1 by 12\\%.

</details>

<a id="paper-368"></a>

### 368. [Asuka-FM: A System-Architectural Co-Design Framework for Efficient Text-to-Image Pretraining on Consumer Hardware](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26437v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26437

**作者：** Víctor Rodrigo González、Pablo M. Olmos、Ignacio Peis、Alejandro Lancho

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26437v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26437v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Asuka-FM，一个面向消费级单卡（RTX 4090）的文本到图像预训练系统与架构协同设计框架，通过无 AdaLN 双流 DiT、稀疏-稠密残差融合注意力、连续宽高比归一化 RoPE 与轻量 Patch Detailer Head，在约 160 GPU 小时内从零训练约 206M 参数模型。

**排除原因：** 该工作是图像生成的可及性与系统效率优化，不涉及视觉推理中的内部状态、证据获取或停止决策；稀疏注意力与位置编码属通用工程手段，未提供与 LVR 状态生成或视觉信息保留的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Asuka-FM: A System-Architectural Co-Design Framework for Efficient Text-to-Image Pretraining on Consumer Hardware

**abstract/A1：** Frontier text-to-image (T2I) foundation models predominantly rely on multi-billion parameter Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) pretrained across massive compute clusters, creating an accessibility barrier for academic laboratories. When training DiTs from scratch on single consumer GPUs (e.g., an NVIDIA RTX 4090 with 24\\,GB VRAM), practitioners encounter three primary bottlenecks: quadratic attention sequence costs, Just-In-Time (JIT) compilation graph breaks induced by variable aspect-ratio bucketing, and an autoencoder throughput wall where frozen Variational Autoencoder (VAE) operations dominate step latency. In this work, we introduce \\textbf{Asuka-FM}, an end-to-end system and architectural framework demonstrating that competitive dual-stream DiTs ($\\sim$206M parameters) can be pretrained from scratch on a single consumer GPU in $\\sim$160 GPU hours for under \\$55. Asuka-FM integrates four core designs: (i) an AdaLN-free dual-stream DiT backbone reducing parameter count; (ii) structured sequence sparsification via Sparse-Dense Residual Fusion (SPRINT) to cut attention FLOPs by over 50\\%; (iii) continuous aspect-normalized Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) coupled with shifted square crop training, guaranteeing static tensor shapes while unlocking zero-shot virtual camera control; and (iv) a pixel adaptation phase utilizing a lightweight (10M parameter) Patch Detailer Head operating under velocity regression ($v$-prediction). Evaluated on open-domain benchmarks without benchmark-specific fine-tuning, Asuka-FM achieves GenEval 0.588 / DPG 78.96 scores at 256px and GenEval 0.609 / DPG 78.51 scores at 512px, setting a new Pareto-optimal efficiency standard for democratic foundation modeling.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages, 19 figures, 17 tables. Project page: https://deeplearningcafe.github.io/projects/asuka-fm ; Code: https://github.com/deeplearningcafe/asuka-fm

</details>

### 369. [Hybrid Retrieval for Government Scheme Search in English and Marathi](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26514v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.26514

**作者：** Aishwarya Gunjal、Kanchan Khemnar、Pawan Poojary、Pranav Kulkarni、Raviraj Joshi、Manish Khodaskar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26514v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26514v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建面向马哈拉施特拉邦政府项目的英—马拉地双语检索系统，将 BM25 与句向量相似度等权融合，并在人工构建的双语查询集上对比 BM25、稠密检索与混合检索。

**排除原因：** 属于跨语言文档检索的应用与基准分析，未涉及 VLM 内部表示、latent 推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，缺乏可供 LVR 借鉴的具体状态或训练线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hybrid Retrieval for Government Scheme Search in English and Marathi

**abstract/A1：** Retrieving government schemes requires matching citizens' information needs to formal administrative descriptions, often across languages and terminology. We present a bilingual search system for Maharashtra government schemes and public services that combines BM25 retrieval with sentence-embedding similarity through equal-weight score fusion. The system indexes aligned English-Marathi scheme descriptions and supports multilingual, Marathi-focused, and translation-mediated semantic configurations. Evaluation on manually curated bilingual queries compares BM25, dense retrieval, and hybrid retrieval using IndicSBERT and MahaSBERT. BM25 provides a strong baseline with a mean reciprocal rank (MRR) of 0.864. Hybrid retrieval increases MRR to 0.872 with IndicSBERT and 0.879 with MahaSBERT, whereas the corresponding dense-only systems achieve 0.663 and 0.655. MahaSBERT-based fusion also increases precision from 0.781 to 0.808 while matching BM25 recall at 0.946. These results support the complementary use of lexical and semantic signals in this collection, while showing that improvements over a strong lexical baseline are modest and metric-dependent. The study provides an application-focused analysis of hybrid retrieval for bilingual public-service information access.

</details>

</details>
